### [1] [Symmetric Thin-Shell Wormholes from a Regularized String-Cloud Black Hole: Energy Conditions, Multi-Fluid Stability, and Optical Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00131)
*Zijun Zhong,Jonathan A. Reboucas,Edson Otoniel,Minyong Guo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 构建了有理正则化Letelier–Alencar弦云黑洞外部的对称薄壳虫洞，分析其表面应力、能量条件、守恒律与稳定性，并研究光学成像特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以具有有限曲率反de Sitter核和string-cloud渐近的有理正则化弦云黑洞为母体，构造可穿越的对称薄壳虫洞，检验正则化对能量条件、径向稳定性及光学外观的影响。

Method: 采用Darmois–Israel形式推导表面应力、能量条件组合、守恒律与有效势；建立适用于密度和半径相关物态方程的通用局部稳定性判据并应用于五种表面流体模型，每个静态喉部校准一个参数；与未正则化的精确对照解比较；并用逆向光线追踪分析成像。

Result: 所有可容许静态喉部均具有负表面能量密度，违反弱和主导能量条件；切向零能量条件及内禀强能量条件仅在内采样外部成立。体积源的径向真空关系消除了外部动量通量。五个模型中，正压壳不稳定，其他闭合模型可出现参数相关的稳定分支。未正则化对照显示前三类模型的能量条件和径向稳定性符号保持，而cosmic-Chaplygin稳定符号可变，所选校准分支仍为实且简单。逆向光线追踪表明喉部位置主要重组高阶和穿喉像，而弦云强度与倾角控制环状结构和多普勒不对称性；单双侧源对比可分离真正的远侧像，中心暗区依赖源分布。

Conclusion: 该有理正则化弦云黑洞可支持对称薄壳虫洞，但需负表面能量密度因而违反经典能量条件；其径向稳定性与光学成像强烈依赖表面流体模型、弦云参数、倾角和源分布。

Abstract: We construct a symmetric thin-shell wormhole from two copies of the positive-lapse exterior of a rationally regularized Letelier--Alencar string-cloud black hole with a finite-curvature anti--de Sitter core and string-cloud asymptotics. Using the Darmois--Israel formalism, we derive the surface stresses, energy-condition combinations, conservation law, and effective potential. Every admissible static throat has negative surface energy density, so the weak and dominant energy conditions are violated, whereas the null condition restricted to directions tangent to the shell, and consequently the intrinsic strong condition, holds only in the inner sampled exterior. The radial-vacuum relation of the bulk source removes the external momentum flux. We derive a general local-stability criterion for density- and radius-dependent equations of state and apply it to five surface-fluid models, calibrating one parameter at each static throat. Across the sampled families, barotropic shells remain unstable, whereas the other closures can admit parameter-dependent stable branches. An exact unregularized control shows that the energy-condition structure and the radial-stability signs of the first three closures persist without the rational regulator, whereas the cosmic-Chaplygin stability sign can change while the selected calibration branch remains real and simple. Backward ray tracing shows that throat location primarily reorganizes higher-order and through-throat images, while string-cloud strength and inclination control annular structure and Doppler asymmetry. Comparisons of one- and two-sided source configurations isolate genuine far-side images and show that the central dark region depends on the source distribution.

</details>


### [2] [Normalizing Flow Emulator for Gravitational Wave Detection Probability in the LIGO/Virgo/KAGRA O4 Observing Run](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00306)
*Julius Gassert,Ignacio Magaña Hernandez,Ariel J. Amsellem,Antonella Palmese,Barnabas Póczos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出基于归一化流（NF）的机器学习方法，用于快速、精确估计引力波事件的探测概率，替代昂贵的蒙特卡洛注入过程，并在O4类探测器网络上验证其有效性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在给定源参数下，精确估计引力波事件的探测概率通常依赖计算代价高昂的Monte Carlo注入活动，难以高效完成。作者希望用机器学习方法替代传统流程，实现高效且准确的探测概率估计。

Method: 采用归一化流（Normalizing Flows, NFs）构建探测概率的仿真器（emulator），面向LIGO/Virgo/KAGRA O4-like探测器网络配置；使用公开注入数据集作为训练与测试数据；通过考察NF作为生成模型恢复被探测事件正确参数分布的能力来评估原始NF性能；并将NF emulator预测的探测效率与Monte Carlo重要性采样结果进行对比。

Result: NF emulator预测的探测效率与Monte Carlo重要性采样结果吻合良好；对于包含尖锐特征（sharp features）等多种族群模型，其探测效率估计可比Monte Carlo重要性采样更精确；NF作为生成模型能够恢复被探测事件的正确参数分布。

Conclusion: NF emulator可用于引力波源的族群分析，效果类似于先前的神经网络方法；训练代码与训练好的模型已公开，具备可复现性与实际应用价值。

Abstract: Efficient and accurate estimation of the detection probability of a gravitational wave event given its source parameters remains a challenging problem because it conventionally requires expensive Monte Carlo injection campaigns. We present a machine learning-based approach using Normalizing Flows (NFs) to estimate the detection probability of gravitational wave events for a LIGO/Virgo/KAGRA O4-like detector network configuration. We utilize the publicly available injection dataset as training and test data. We test our method and implementation by evaluating the raw NF performance as a generative model based on its ability to recover the correct parameter distributions of detected events. In addition, we compare the detection efficiencies predicted by the NF emulator with those from Monte Carlo importance sampling and find good agreement. Furthermore, estimating the detection efficiency for a wide variety of population models, including those with sharp features, can be more precise than estimates obtained via Monte Carlo importance sampling. We show that the NF emulator can be used in population analysis of gravitational wave sources, similar to previous neural network approaches. The training code and trained models from this work are publicly available.

</details>


### [3] [Quasinormal Modes of Reissner-Nordström Black Holes in Modified Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00393)
*Xia-Tong Tan,Yu Zhang,Zhi-Hong Nie*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在标量-张量-矢量引力(STVG)框架下，研究Reissner-Nordström修正引力(MOG)黑洞在无质量标量、电磁和引力扰动下的准正规模与时间演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用黑洞谱学检验修正引力理论需要系统的理论参考，本文旨在为MOG黑洞的准正规模与铃宕特征提供系统分析。

Method: 采用扩展的3至6阶WKB方法并结合13阶Padé近似计算准正规模频率，同时基于光锥坐标的有限差分法获得完整时间域波形。

Result: 增大MOG参数α会提升黑洞有效质量，使阻尼率|ω_I|减小、铃宕衰减变慢；增大电荷Q产生类似效应，近极端极限下阻尼趋近于零。三种扰动的铃宕衰减时间由长到短依次为引力场、电磁场、标量场。时间域波形与频率域结果定性一致，晚期拖尾幂律指数与渐近平直时空相符。

Conclusion: 该研究为利用黑洞谱学检验修正引力理论提供了系统的理论参考。

Abstract: In the framework of Scalar-Tensor-Vector Gravity (STVG), this paper investigates the quasinormal modes and time-domain evolution of Reissner-Nordström Modified Gravity (MOG) black holes under massless scalar, electromagnetic, and gravitational perturbations. Quasinormal frequencies are calculated by extending the 3rd- to 6th-order WKB method and combining it with Padé approximation to the 13th order, while complete time-domain waveforms are obtained using a finite-difference method based on light-cone coordinates. The results indicate that increasing the MOG parameter $α$ raises the effective mass of the black holes, leading to a decrease in the damping rate $|ω_I|$ and a slower ringdown decay. An increase in charge $Q$ produces a similar effect, with the damping approaching zero in the near-extremal limit. Regarding the ringdown decay times for the three types of perturbations, the gravitational field has the longest duration, the electromagnetic field is intermediate, and the scalar field is the shortest. The oscillations and decay observed in the time-domain waveforms are qualitatively consistent with the frequency-domain results, and the power-law exponent governing the late-time tail aligns with that of asymptotically flat spacetimes. This study provides a systematic theoretical reference for testing modified gravity theories using black hole spectroscopy.

</details>


### [4] [Fermionic Particle Creation in Curved Spacetime: Physical Bloch-Vector Dynamics and Exact de Sitter Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00410)
*Samak Boonpan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 将弯曲时空中的费米子粒子创生表述为物理Bloch矢量的精确演化，每个Dirac动量模式化为二能级系统，并在de Sitter时空中给出解析结果，重现Gibbons-Hawking Fermi-Dirac平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Bogoliubov描述虽能给出粒子数，但缺乏对有限时间态轨迹的紧凑几何刻画，且费米子占据数界限的微观起源易被错误归因于几何本身，因此需要一种既等价于标准描述又能保留完整动力学轨迹的表述。

Method: 将每个Dirac动量模式约化为二能级系统，用物理Bloch矢量描述其精确演化，通过将物理态投影到瞬时能量基得到瞬时占据数；该构造与标准Bogoliubov描述等价，并针对de Sitter时空中的有质量Dirac场进行解析求解。

Result: 动力学约化为以Hubble单位表示的物理动量和质量-哈勃比值的普适函数；识别出质量控制的非绝热尺度以及奇异的小质量边界层，解决了质量为零极限与晚期极限不可交换的问题；独立的笛卡尔Bloch、角向Bloch和Bogoliubov积分验证了解析结果，并重现标准Gibbons-Hawking Fermi-Dirac平台。

Conclusion: 该Bloch矢量表述在保持与Bogoliubov描述等价的同时，以紧凑实几何完整刻画有限时间态轨迹，使费米子占据数上限自然显现；de Sitter情形下的解析结果揭示了质量控制的非绝热尺度与小质量边界层结构。

Abstract: We formulate fermionic particle creation in a time-dependent curved spacetime as the exact evolution of a physical Bloch vector. Each Dirac momentum mode reduces to a two-level system, and its instantaneous occupation is obtained by projecting the physical state onto the instantaneous energy basis. This construction is equivalent to the standard Bogoliubov description while retaining the complete finite-time state trajectory in a compact real geometry. It also makes the fermionic occupation bound manifest without attributing its microscopic origin to geometry alone. For a massive Dirac field in de Sitter spacetime, the dynamics reduces to a universal function of the physical momentum in Hubble units and the mass-to-Hubble ratio. We identify a mass-controlled nonadiabatic scale and a singular small-mass boundary layer that resolves the noncommuting massless and late-time limits. Independent Cartesian Bloch, angular Bloch, and Bogoliubov integrations verify the analytical results and recover the standard Gibbons--Hawking Fermi--Dirac plateau.

</details>


### [5] [Black hole sensitivity in Einstein--aether theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00503)
*Morgan Kidnew,Andrew Kovachik,Sergey Sibiryakov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文数值求解了Einstein–aether理论中缓慢运动的Schwarzschild黑洞解，并计算其与aether场耦合的敏感度参数，给出敏感度对理论参数的简洁解析近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Einstein–aether这类洛伦兹破缺引力理论中，黑洞与aether场的耦合强度由敏感度参数刻画，该参数是点粒子有效理论描述黑洞运动所需的关键量，此前缺乏对缓慢运动黑洞情形下其取值及对理论参数依赖的定量结果。

Method: 采用数值方法求解缓慢运动Schwarzschild黑洞在Einstein–aether理论中的场方程解，基于所得解在点粒子有效理论框架内计算敏感度参数，并拟合推导其依赖于Einstein–aether理论参数的解析近似表达式。

Result: 获得了缓慢运动Schwarzschild黑洞的数值解，并由此得到敏感度参数的数值结果；进一步给出敏感度随Einstein–aether参数变化的简单解析近似公式。

Conclusion: 该工作为Einstein–aether理论中黑洞的类点粒子描述提供了定量输入，所得敏感度的解析近似便于后续在黑洞动力学与引力波等研究中直接使用。

Abstract: We numerically find the solutions for slowly moving Schwarzschild black holes in Einstein--aether theory. We use these solutions to compute the sensitivity parameter which describes the coupling of the black hole to the aether field within point-particle effective theory. A simple analytic approximation for the dependence of the sensitivity on the Einstein--aether parameters is derived.

</details>


### [6] [Dynamics of Regge calculus with torsion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00593)
*Ruijue Yan,You Ding,Yongge Ma,Cong Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在n维单纯形流形上研究离散几何，将挠率引入Regge calculus，构造了3维和4维（欧氏与洛伦兹号差）的单纯形Einstein–Cartan作用量，并验证其连续极限与运动方程性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Regge calculus是广义相对论的离散化框架，但通常只处理无挠情形，无法描述Einstein–Cartan理论中的挠率。作者希望将挠率自然地纳入离散几何，从而建立离散Einstein–Cartan理论的对应形式。

Method: 在每个单纯形中，将边向量赋予各边以编码长度与方向信息；用内禀规范群元素表示穿过相邻单纯形界面曲线的和乐（holonomy）。挠率表现为界面某边向量与其经和乐平行移动后属于相邻单纯形的同一边向量之差。据此构造3维和4维（分别对应欧氏与洛伦兹号差）单纯形Einstein–Cartan作用量，作为边向量与和乐的函数。

Result: 所构造的作用量在无挠情形下退化为相应的Regge作用量。对4维离散作用量分别关于边向量和和乐变分，得到两个运动方程：前者与连续理论中的对应方程一致；无挠和乐满足后一方程，与连续情形相同。

Conclusion: 所得理论可作为Einstein–Cartan理论在单纯形流形上的离散类比。

Abstract: The discrete geometry on an $n$-dimensional simplicial manifold are studied, in order to incorporate torsion into Regge calculus. In each simplex, the edge vectors are assigned to the edges to encode the information of their lengths and directions. In addition, the holonomies along the curves across the interfaces of two adjacent simplices are represented by the internal gauge group elements. The torsion manifests itself as the difference between an edge vector on an interface belonging to one simplex and the parallel transported edge vector, via the holonomy, of the same edge but belonging to the adjacent simplex. The simplicial Einstein--Cartan actions are then constructed as functions of the edge vectors and holonomies in three and four dimensions with Euclidean and Lorentzian signatures, respectively. It is shown that they return to the corresponding Regge actions for the torsion-free cases. The variations of the 4-dimensional discrete action with respect to the edge vectors and holonomies, respectively, give two equations of motion. It is shown that the former is consistent with the corresponding equation in the continuum theory, and the torsion-free holonomies satisfy the latter equation as in the continuous case. Thus, the resulting theory on the simplicial manifold can be regarded as a discrete analogue of Einstein--Cartan theory.

</details>


### [7] [Naked Singularities and Black Hole Thermodynamics from Shadows and Photon Rings in the Quantum Oppenheimer-Snyder Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00640)
*Jose Miguel Ladino,Anuar Idrissov,Carlos E. Romero-Figueroa,Hernando Quevedo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究量子Oppenheimer-Snyder（qOS）时空在黑洞与裸奇点情形下的光学与热力学性质，通过光线追踪与半解析方法刻画阴影、光子环等光学特征，并借助几何热力学（GTD）建立光子环可观测量与热力学稳定性及微观相互作用结构之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子修正时空（qOS）兼具黑洞与裸奇点两种可能结局，其可观测特征及热力学稳定性尚缺乏系统性刻画，需要给出可区分二者的观测判据并揭示光学与热力学的内在关联。

Method: 分析测试粒子运动并对薄吸积盘模型进行数值光线追踪；采用半解析方法计算高阶光子环碰撞参数与间隔参数（gap parameter）；在黑洞情形下应用几何热力学（GTD）分析比热发散（Davies点）及曲率标量。

Result: 量子修正参数增大会减小视阴影尺寸；qOS黑洞呈现暗内阴影，而裸奇点因无限排斥核势垒产生中心亮环，但仍保留光子环等关键光学特征；半解析得到的高阶光子环碰撞参数与间隔参数与数值结果吻合良好；qOS裸奇点的间隔参数区间偏离Schwarzschild情形，可作为观测检验判据；GTD在Davies点识别出比热发散，光子环可观测量与GTD曲率标量相关联。

Conclusion: qOS时空的光学特征可有效区分黑洞与裸奇点，其间隔参数偏离可作为观测判据；光子环可观测量与GTD曲率标量之间的联系揭示了黑洞阴影、热力学稳定性与微观相互作用结构之间的直接关联。

Abstract: We investigate the optical and thermodynamic properties of quantum Oppenheimer-Snyder (qOS) spacetimes in both the black hole and naked singularity regimes. By analyzing test-particle motion and employing numerical ray tracing of thin accretion disk models, we identify their characteristic optical signatures. We find that increasing the quantum correction parameter reduces the apparent shadow size. While qOS black holes exhibit dark inner shadows, naked singularities produce central bright rings, driven by an infinite repulsive core barrier, yet still retain key optical features such as photon rings. Furthermore, using a semi-analytical approach, we obtain higher-order photon-ring impact and gap parameters that closely align with our numerical findings. Notably, the gap parameter intervals for qOS naked singularities deviate from the Schwarzschild case, providing a concrete criterion for observational tests. Finally, in the black-hole sector, the application of Geometrothermodynamics (GTD) identifies the specific-heat divergence at the Davies point. By linking photon-ring observables to GTD curvature scalars, we reveal a direct connection between qOS black hole shadows, thermodynamic stability, and microscopic interaction structures.

</details>


### [8] [Reconstruction of $f(Q)$ Gravity from Geodesic Congruence Dynamics: Raychaudhuri Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01254)
*Tuhina Ghorui,Prabir Rudra,Farook Rahaman*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 利用Raychaudhuri方程作为几何工具，在对称远平行引力框架下重构$f(Q)$引力的函数形式，探究其解释宇宙加速膨胀的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否在不引入额外暗能量组分的情况下，用$f(Q)$引力解释观测到的宇宙加速膨胀，并借助Raychaudhuri方程建立$Q$与宇宙运动学演化之间的联系以实现逆重构。

Method: 以Raychaudhuri方程为核心，通过膨胀标量与测地线同余动力学，将非度规标量$Q$与宇宙运动学演化关联；在FLRW时空中分别假设常数减速度参数和常数jerk参数进行重构分析，并检验广义相对论极限、Friedmann一致性与背景物理合理性。

Result: 获得了$f(Q)$的显式形式，可在无需额外暗能量组分的情况下解释观测到的宇宙加速膨胀；重构模型在特定情形下可延拓至无限过去以描述早期宇宙。

Conclusion: Raychaudhuri方程作为刻画宇宙学测地线同余聚焦/散焦的方程，可作为$f(Q)$引力逆重构的核心方程。

Abstract: In this study, we use the Raychaudhuri equation as the essential geometrical tool to reconstruct the functional form of $f(Q)$ gravity within the context of symmetric teleparallel gravity. The expansion scalar and the congruence dynamics recorded in the Raychaudhuri equation are used to relate the non-metricity scalar $Q$ with the kinematical evolution of the Universe in order to establish the reconstruction method. We obtain explicit forms of $f(Q)$ that can explain the observed accelerated expansion of the Universe without the need for an additional dark energy component by taking into account appropriate cosmological assumptions. Moreover, the reconstructed model can also be extended to the infinite past to model the early universe for certain scenarios. In an FLRW spacetime we have considered a constant deceleration parameter and a constant jerk parameter to facilitate our reconstruction analysis. We further discuss the, general relativity limit, Friedmann consistency and background physical viability of the reconstructed models. The analysis indicates that Raychaudhuri equation, interpreted as an equation governing the focusing/defocusing of cosmological geodesic congruences, can be used as the central equation in an inverse reconstruction of $f(Q)$ gravity.

</details>


### [9] [Charged Hawking Mass on CMC Collars with Charged Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01255)
*Sehong Park*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究三维时间对称带电初值中严格稳定极小视界，证明带电Hawking质量的一阶变分恒等式，并在电荷密度单号条件下建立单调性与局部刚性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨含宇宙常数、允许带电物质的三维时间对称带电初值集中，严格稳定极小视界的刚性；将Hawking质量变分方法推广到带电情形，以刻画源自由常曲率Reissner–Nordström–(反)de Sitter模型的视界数据。

From this we recover the local rigidity results under a local maximality condition. In the Friedmann equation, both scalaron and radiation components gravitate, thereby accounting for the backreaction of the generated radiation.

Despite continuous radiation production, the radiation component remains subdominant, and the slow-roll conditions persist throughout inflation, yielding approximately 60 e-folds. Following inflation, the scalaron enters a damped oscillatory phase accompanied by rapid radiation growth. This transition provides a graceful exit from inflation and a smooth entry into reheating. This indicates the onset of a hot, radiation-dominated phase, satisfying the Kofman-Yi criterion for successful reheating. Our results demonstrate that the coupled scalaron-radiation dynamics provides a self-consistent realization of graceful exit and the transition to radiation domination in the Starobinsky model.

Abstract: We study closed timelike curves (CTCs) in three rotating spacetimes arising in modified gravity: the tidally charged braneworld black hole, the Kerr-Newman black hole in Rastall gravity, and the charged Johannsen-Psaltis spacetime. In Rastall gravity, the extent of the CTC region depends jointly on the Rastall coupling and the surrounding matter. For each matter type we find a finite range of the coupling for which the CTC region is absent. In the charged Johannsen-Psaltis spacetime, the deformation parameter can either increase or decrease $g_{φφ}$ depending on the radial position, and we derive a window of its values for which the CTC region is removed from the radial domain considered.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: A long-time Poincaré section flattens a chaotic orbit into one static blur, erasing the order in which the orbit visits its own local structures. We introduce the sliding-window frequency ratio (SWFR), built from nothing but radial and polar turning events, to recover that order along individual relativistic orbits. Each window spans a fixed number of radial cycles and simply counts the polar events inside it; because event times are kept, any candidate interval can be sliced out and replotted as its own Poincaré section. No spectrum, no reference center, no basis functions. Integrable Kerr benchmarks recover the known frequency ratios, and for regular motion with uniformly bounded count deviations the counting-error bound falls off as $O(W^{-1})$. The same counts then pay off in chaos. In magnetized Kerr spacetime a single charged orbit dwells near $3/5$ and later near $4/7$, and exactly those intervals open into fivefold and sevenfold section structures; a second orbit dwells near $1/2$ with two lobes. In Schwarzschild--Melvin spacetime a photon holds $4/5$ across a fivefold pattern. In every case the section covers exactly the interval SWFR selected: integer counts and phase-space geometry agree that a globally chaotic orbit is paying a temporary visit to a resonance.

</details>

*David Bambague,Miguel Bezares,Katy Clough,Steven Tobias*

TL;DR: 本文提出利用自动微分（auto-differentiation）高效求解数值相对论中感兴趣的动力学解，并以标量临界坍缩为验证案例，在2D参数空间中用35次模拟定位临界解线，显著优于二分法的270次模拟。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Numerical relativity is a term used to describe the solution of the full non-linear Einstein Equations as an initial value problem using numerical methods. Individual simulations are computationally expensive and in many applications the high complexity of the parameter space and non-linear sensitivity of the solutions makes locating particular outcomes of interest challenging. In this work, we demonstrate how auto-differentiation can efficiently find dynamical solutions of interest, using the classic problem of scalar critical collapse as a proof of principle. We locate a line of critical solutions in a 2D parameter space using 35 runs, compared to the 270 runs required to locate 10 critical points using bisection, and directly calculate the unstable mode, confirming its universality along the line. The method is straightforwardly extendable to higher-dimensional parameter spaces, offering a promising method to efficiently target more complex solutions in strong dynamical gravity regimes.

</details>

Abstract: We determine the dimensions of all derivative loci and all higher multiplicity loci of an elementary symmetric polynomial over an algebraically closed field of positive characteristic. The common zero locus of its order-q derivatives has dimension d - q - 1 + 1_{Q>d-q}, whereas the locus of zeros of multiplicity at least s has dimension d - s + 1_{Q>d}. The order-two case settles the dimension question in a conjecture of Orzel, with a necessary correction when d is a power of p. We classify all components attaining the larger dimension and prove that they are generically smooth. Along the coordinate components in the smaller-dimension cases, we determine the local rings and obtain generic multiplicity exactly Q. These results distinguish dimension jumps from infinitesimal thickening and give a uniform description of their dependence on the characteristic.

This yields epsilon-optimal leading relative-entropy and posterior-overlap bounds for the fine posterior and the sharper fixed-dimensional asymptotic scale $(d/ε)\log(d/ε)$. Abstract: Recent work has shown that quantum double models can be used to circumvent the Bravyi-Koenig bound. This yields a natural class of beyond-Pauli stabilizer models, including many twisted quantum double phases. In particular, when the stabilizers of a state require operators from level $k$ of the Clifford hierarchy, we show that the diagonal gates required to produce the phases in the wavefunction sit one level higher. Finally, we demonstrate that a pair of qudit toric codes may be deterministically prepared in a logical magic state by measuring $X\mathcal C^{(k)}_d$ stabilizers. Together, these results provide a concrete stabilizer framework for topological codes beyond Pauli stabilizer models and their logical gates.

Abstract: Neural quantum states (NQS) have emerged as powerful representations of quantum states with rapidly expanding applications across quantum many-body physics. Yet our understanding of when neural networks can efficiently represent physical quantum states remains limited, in part due to the nonlinear parameterization of NQS, the intricate sign structure of quantum states, and the sensitive dependence on basis choice. Our results provide an intrinsic complexity metric for quantum states with provable connections to their neural-network representability.

Abstract: The Hamiltonian of a natural quantum system is usually determined indirectly, by proposing microscopic models and comparing their predicted observables with experiment. A more direct approach is to extract information about the Hamiltonian from measurements on the system itself. In many settings, however, direct access to the full system is unavailable and measurements can be performed only through controllable probes, as in quantum sensing and boundary spectroscopy. Existing Hamiltonian learning protocols under such restricted access typically require a special symmetry, a known parametric form, or controlled operations beyond free evolution. We introduce Quantum Krylov Learning (QKL), a restricted-access framework that avoids these requirements. QKL uses single temperature probe autocorrelators and their operator Krylov structure to extract model-independent spectral information about the unknown system. Rather than fitting a parametrized Hamiltonian, it reconstructs the operator Krylov Jacobi matrix representing the dynamical modes accessible to the probe. The method assumes that the unobserved system is at, or near, infinite temperature. Under this condition, the Jacobi matrix can be reconstructed directly from the autocorrelation of a probe observable, without oracle access to the Hamiltonian. The required operations are free evolution under the unknown $H$, together with probe state preparation and measurement. The learning core therefore requires no symmetry, no parametric model, and no controlled-unitary gates. We show a Lieb--Robinson bound for the operator Krylov chain and derive a data-driven criterion for the required observation window. We also establish an end-to-end sample complexity guarantee: under generic conditions on the spectral gaps and weights, the number of measurement shots required to achieve spectral precision $\varepsilon$ is polynomial in $1/\varepsilon$.

</details>

Abstract: Quantum state tomography provides a foundation for characterizing state preparation and predicting measurement outcomes. Under an explicit sufficient block size condition for the two layer periodic Clifford ensemble, we establish statewise expected trace norm oracle inequalities for operator norm minimum distance and measurement weighted projected least squares, with bounds that depend on each state's actual spectral tail, while the estimators require no prior knowledge of the state's structural characteristics, including its rank, eigenbasis, or spectral structure. In contrast, the diagonal model exhibits Hilbert-space fragmentation and, within its largest irreducible sector, signatures of quantum many-body scar dynamics including towers of highly localized, low-entanglement eigenstates, and long-lived coherent revivals. These results establish a direct link between Hamiltonian design, Hilbert-space network structure, and emergent non-ergodic dynamics.

These transitions are largely insensitive to the host environment due to effective shielding by the filled outer shells. In this work, we isolate neutral Er atoms in solid Ne and Ar at 6 K and characterize their emission by steady-state and time-resolved photoluminescence (PL) spectroscopy. In both hosts, we observe several near-infrared transitions between 1000--1500 nm, including inner-shell emission in the telecom O and E bands. The $4f \rightarrow 5d$ transition near 1299 nm forms a multiplet of narrow components, consistent with crystal-field splitting, and decays on a microsecond timescale. The $4f \rightarrow 4f$ transition near 1437 nm shows a matrix shift below 1 cm$^{-1}$ in both hosts. In solid Ne, its inhomogeneous linewidth is bounded by the spectrometer resolution to < 22 GHz, and its PL lifetime reaches up to 78.9 ms, close to the estimated radiative limit. Thermal annealing extends the 1437 nm lifetime in Ne and suppresses the phonon sidebands relative to the zero-phonon lines in Ar. These results identify RG solids as promising hosts for long-lived telecom-band Er emission and provide a foundation for future spin-photon interfaces and hybrid quantum architectures.

For any fixed rank-one measurement and $n\le c_2d^2/κ_ν$, the minimax mean-squared error is at least $c_1\log^2\{d^2/(κ_νn)\}$. The results concern a fixed measurement repeated independently across copies; adaptive and collective protocols, and protocols that reuse common randomness across copies, are not covered.

Conclusion: Kochen-Specker语境性并非普遍洛伦兹不变：仅在完整希尔伯特空间成立，限制到自旋后普遍破缺。各量子资源按Δ的不同阈值依次丧失，且非局域性、GHZ及瞄准源结果可通过q-plate在无运动情形下实验检验。

Abstract: We ask whether Kochen-Specker contextuality is Lorentz invariant. On the full Hilbert space, it is, since boosts act unitarily. Restricted to spin, it is not: momentum acts as an environment applying momentum-dependent Wigner rotations, leaving spin observables unsharp and breaking the operator identities behind state-independent contextuality. For spin $1/2$ one scalar governs the loss, the packet average $Δ$ of $\sin^2(θ_W/2)$ (where $θ_W$ is the Wigner angle); for any spin, a ray degrades at a rate set by its spin variance transverse to the boost. In the spin-only account, the Free Will Theorem's SPIN axiom fails for every $Δ>0$; the Yu-Oh set loses contextuality between $Δ\approx0.008$ and $0.016$, with state independence going first; the Peres-Mermin square loses contextuality at $Δ\approx0.059$ or $0.074$ by orientation; singlet CHSH loses nonlocality for $Δ\geq0.159$; and a GHZ-state OR gate loses Raussendorf's contextuality certificate at $Δ\approx0.206$. A $q$-plate implements the channel exactly on photon polarisation, with a retardation in place of the rapidity, so the nonlocality, GHZ and aimed-source results are testable without motion.

Abstract: We prove that a quasi-local quantum Gibbs sampler rapidly mixes to the Gibbs state of any 1D finite-range Hamiltonian at all finite temperatures in $\mathcal{O}\left(\log (n/\varepsilon)\right)$ time. </details>

Abstract: We establish duality relations for conditional entropies relative to subalgebras of arbitrary von Neumann algebras, including the Umegaki relative entropy as well as the sandwiched- and Petz-Rényi divergences. Abstract: Collective spin systems in solid-state materials are promising platforms for quantum-enhanced sensing. In systems such as nitrogen-vacancy centers in diamond, donor spins in silicon, and magnetic materials, interactions among many spins can generate collective quantum correlations that improve measurement precision beyond the standard quantum limit. This tutorial develops an intuitive, materials-oriented framework for understanding these effects through nonlinear collective-spin dynamics. We show how nonlinear interactions reshape collective spin states in phase space, redistribute quantum fluctuations, and generate metrologically useful states such as spin-squeezed and entangled states. We then connect these ideal collective dynamics to microscopic interactions and realistic solid-state systems, discussing how interaction strength, disorder, decoherence, and experimental control affect sensing performance. Different platforms and experiments are compared to highlight key material properties, advantages, and limitations. The tutorial focuses on a materials-oriented understanding of nonlinear collective-spin sensing, rather than a comprehensive comparison of experimental platforms or a practical guide to sensing experiments.

</details>

By contrast, free-fermion systems are nonchaotic, and their quantum states are constrained to remain Gaussian. Motivated by this distinction, we introduce the fermionic Gaussian Scrooge ensemble to describe deep thermalization in generic free-fermion systems. This ensemble is defined as a distortion of the Gaussian Haar ensemble by the average density matrix, and its moments reveal an enlarged symmetry of Gaussian states in the replicated Hilbert space. We demonstrate the emergence of the fermionic Gaussian Scrooge ensemble in two complementary settings: (1) we analytically prove that the projected ensemble of the SYK$_2$ model follows the fermionic Gaussian Scrooge ensemble at arbitrary evolution times; (2) we provide numerical evidence that it emerges at sufficiently long times in random Gaussian circuits with charge conservation. Our results establish the fermionic Gaussian Scrooge ensemble as a universal description of deep thermalization in free-fermion systems.

Abstract: We study the interplay between local stroboscopic measurements and the unitary quantum circuit dynamics of the deterministic Floquet-quantum East model. The resulting statistics exhibit two regimes set by the competition between the measurement probing time and the relaxation timescale of the unitary circuit dynamics: while infrequent monitoring leads to geometric first-detection statistics, frequent monitoring induces correlations that manifest as deviations from this geometric behavior. These results provide a rare exact characterization of how local measurement backaction reshapes detection statistics in an interacting quantum many-body system. In our local protocol, return probabilities are controlled by the size of the monitored subregion and can therefore be accessed on digital quantum simulators via local mid-circuit measurements.

Abstract: We determine the optimal success probability for transmitting a fixed number of classical messages through a finite number of uses of the quantum erasure channel, assisted by noiseless classical feedback and without initial shared entanglement. For an input dimension $d$, an erasure probability $p$, a blocklength $n$, and $M$ equiprobable messages, the optimal success probability is equal to $E[\min\{1,d^K/M\}]$, where $K$ is binomial with parameters $n$ and $1-p$. The converse allows arbitrary adaptive quantum encoders, quantum memories, and receiver instruments. Its main ingredient is an elementary dimension bound for noiseless quantum communication with classical feedback, proved by fixing the classical controls without conditioning the sender's state on the observed transcript. A classical protocol that transmits the base-$d$ digits of an integer representing the message, repeating each digit until the receiver acknowledges its reception or the prescribed blocklength is reached, attains the bound for every integer $M$. We also establish a relative-majorization property of erasure-channel outputs and exactly evaluate a hypothesis-testing converse, recovering the same numerical bound without feedback. That converse need not be achievable without feedback: four uses of the qubit erasure channel and four messages give a strict gap. A product-state code employing tetrahedral qubit states nevertheless outperforms every classical binary erasure code with these parameters. We give an explicit message-size formula and show that the bounded-remainder normal approximation and the average-success strong-converse exponent are unchanged without feedback. We also determine the feedback-assisted error exponent below capacity, prove that it agrees with the no-feedback exponent above a critical rate, and give no-feedback bounds at lower rates.

### [79] [Causal Trade-Offs in Superluminal Extensions of Relativity. A no-go theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00494)

TL;DR: 本文构建因果建模框架，证明一个no-go定理：允许超光速参考系的理论要避免超光速信号，必须严重限制可操作协议可访问的时空区域，或使参考系选择具有可观测后果，或使自由变量不存在／其存在依赖参考系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dragan与Ekert近期提出包含超光速参考系的相对论扩展，试图用客观不确定性调和相对论对称性与量子不确定性。本文旨在形式化检验该提议是否真能避免超光速信号这一根本冲突。

Method: 发展因果建模（causal modelling）框架，对超光速参考系提议进行形式化分析，并证明相应no-go定理，考察避免超光速信号所需的代价与假设松弛。

Result: no-go定理表明：允许超光速参考系的理论仅在以下条件下可避免超光速信号——严重限制协议可访问的时空区域、参考系选择产生可观测后果、或自由变量不存在／其存在依赖参考系。该结论超出“超光速变换可能致超光速信号”的直觉，说明客观不确定性本身不足以消除冲突。

Conclusion: 引入客观不确定性并不能单独解决超光速参考系与超光速信号的矛盾；由于可通过松弛其他基本假设来避免信号，所提客观不确定性可能根本不必要。结果揭示了基于不确定性解决方案的局限及超光速扩展面临的因果权衡。

Abstract: Extensions of relativity that include superluminal reference frames have recently been proposed by Dragan and Ekert as a way to reconcile relativistic symmetry with quantum uncertainty. In this work, we develop a causal modelling framework to formally study this proposal and prove a no-go theorem demonstrating that a theory permitting superluminal reference frames can avoid superluminal signalling only if it severely constrains which spacetime regions are accessible to operational protocols, if the choice of reference frame has observable consequences or if free variables do not exist or their existence depends on the reference frame. Crucially, our result goes beyond the familiar intuition that superluminal transformations may lead to superluminal signalling; our no-go result shows that Dragan and Ekert's proposal of introducing objective uncertainty does not, by itself, eliminate this conflict. In fact, because superluminal signalling might instead be avoided by relaxing other fundamental assumptions in the presence of superluminal reference frames, the proposed objective uncertainty may not be necessary at all. Our results clarify the limitations of uncertainty-based resolutions and the causal trade-offs faced by superluminal extensions of relativity.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究多副本量子秘密共享（QSS），证明其在阈值情形下超越单副本no-cloning障碍，并给出与k-着色相关的可达界与Clique Path不可行障碍，k=2时上下界完全匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典秘密共享可实现所有访问结构，量子情形受no-cloning限制，单副本阈值QSS仅当t>n/2可行。作者希望考察提供多份输入态副本、仅需恢复一份的新变体是否能突破该限制，并判断no-cloning是否仍精确刻画可行性。

Method: 提出k副本QSS框架；利用从访问结构导出的图的k-着色性质，构造阈值t>(n-k+1)/2时的QSS方案，并给出适用于任意访问结构的Clique Path不可行障碍，阈值下t≤(n-1)/k不可行。

Result: 多副本可略微超越单副本障碍；阈值k副本下可达t>(n-k+1)/2，而Clique Path障碍给出t≤(n-1)/k不可行，k=2时上下界精确匹配；k≥3仍存在间隙。

Conclusion: no-cloning不再精确刻画多副本量子秘密共享可行性；结果可推广至擦除信道下的量子纠错，k≥3的界闭合是未来方向。

Abstract: Secret sharing is ubiquitous throughout cryptography. All possible access structures are classically feasible, and in the case of threshold access structures, the protocols are even very efficient. However, when moving to the quantum setting, the no-cloning theorem shows that many access structures are impossible. In fact, no-cloning exactly characterizes feasibility: for thresholds, quantum secret sharing is possible if and only if the threshold $t$ is strictly more than $n/2$.
  In this work, we propose a variant of quantum secret sharing (QSS) where two or more identical copies of the input state are provided, but the output is only required to recover one copy. This notion circumvents the simple one-copy no-cloning obstruction, though the natural $k$-copy generalization still gives much milder obstructions. For thresholds using $k$ copies, no-cloning implies that QSS is impossible whenever $t\leq n/(k+1)$.
  It is tempting to hypothesize that no-cloning continues to exactly characterize the many-copy case. However, we show that this is not the case. We give positive results showing that multiple copies allow for going slightly beyond the single-copy obstruction: for thresholds, we construct QSS whenever $t>(n-k+1)/2$. On the other hand, we give a novel obstruction we call the Clique Path obstruction, which applies to arbitrary access structures. For thresholds, it shows that QSS is impossible whenever $t\leq (n-1)/k$. Our upper and lower bounds exactly match for $k=2$. Both our results leverage connections to the $k$-colorability of certain graphs derived from the access structure. We leave closing the gap for $k\geq 3$ copies as a fascinating direction for future work.
  Secret sharing is closely related to error correction, which can also be considered in the many-copy setting. Our results imply similar obstructions for quantum error correction for erasure channels.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭合玻色格点系统一般不满足与初态无关的算符范数Lieb–Robinson界，因为信息传播速度随局域玻色占据数增长，可达宏观量级。本文旨在说明局域耗散能够消除这一障碍，使玻色系统获得与自旋系统类似的、对输入态一致成立的动力学局域性。

Method: 考虑由Lindbladian描述的耗散Bose–Hubbard模型，含在位ℓ-光子损耗（ℓ>2）。耗散快速耗尽高占据格点，从而在局域粒子矩的Sobolev型标度上正则化状态。利用由此得到的矩界（t→0时发散但在足够低阶上对t的近零区域可积），建立近乎弹道的Lieb–Robinson界；并将方法推广到cat-code耗散情形。

Result: 对ℓ>2的在位损耗耗散Bose–Hubbard模型，证明了近乎弹道的算符范数Lieb–Robinson界；获得对总粒子数/体积一致的局域绝热近似（初态位于code space时）；并给出局域信道近似、热力学极限的存在性以及高效数字量子模拟等推论，均对输入态一致成立，区别于闭合Bose–Hubbard系统。

Conclusion: 局域耗散可正则化玻色系统的信息传播，恢复算符范数Lieb–Robinson界，使耗散玻色格点模型在态无关意义下具备与量子自旋系统相同的动力学局域性及一系列标准应用，而闭合玻色系统不具备此性质。

Abstract: Closed bosonic lattice systems do not generally admit operator norm Lieb--Robinson bounds uniformly in the initial state. The reason is that the information propagation velocity can grow with the local boson occupancy, which can be macroscopically large. Here, we show that local dissipation restores an operator-norm Lieb--Robinson bound. We consider the dissipative Bose--Hubbard model described by a Lindbladian operator with on-site $\ell$-photon loss, $\ell>2$ and, for our main result, we establish an almost-ballistic Lieb-Robinson bound. Dissipation rapidly depletes highly occupied sites, thus regularizing the state on the Sobolev-type scale of local particle moments. The resulting moment bounds diverge as time approaches zero but remain integrable near zero at sufficiently low orders. We extend these ideas to treat cat-code dissipation and, for initial states in the code space, we prove a local adiabatic approximation uniform in the total volume. Further consequences include local channel approximation, a thermodynamic limit, and efficient digital quantum simulation. The point is that these applications are now available \textit{uniformly in the input state}, as for quantum spin systems, but in contrast to closed Bose--Hubbard systems.

</details>


### [82] [Robust classical and quantum communication over almost-iid channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00442)
*Liuhang Ye,Bjarne Bergh,Nilanjana Datta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：沿信道 Φ 的 Choi-几乎-iid 信道序列，其通用无辅助经典容量与量子容量均等于 Φ 本身的容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信道渐近通信的 iid 范式假设 n 次使用等价于张量幂 Φ^{⊗n}，但精确张量幂行为只是理想化。因此需要研究"几乎-iid"信道序列是否仍能达到 Φ 的渐近容量。

Method: 定义 Choi-几乎-iid 信道序列：其归一化 Choi 态沿 Φ 的归一化 Choi 态为 Mazzola-Sutter-Renner (MSR) 几乎-iid，缺陷参数 r_n=o(n)。MSR 几乎-iid 类源自 Renner 量子态指数 de Finetti 定理中的近似混合，Choi-几乎-iid 信道在伴随论文的信道指数 de Finetti 定理中扮演对应角色。

Result: 证明该类信道序列的通用无辅助经典容量与量子容量均等于 Φ 的相应容量，且具通用性：对每个低于相关容量的速率，存在仅依赖 Φ 与速率（而非具体信道序列）的编码序列，使该类中每个序列的误差趋于零。

Conclusion: 几乎-iid 信道序列的渐近容量与精确张量幂情形一致，表明 iid 范式的容量结果对 Choi-几乎-iid 扰动具有稳健性，并提供了在该类内通用的编码方案。

Abstract: The independent and identically distributed (iid) paradigm for asymptotic communication through quantum channels describes $n$ uses of a channel $Φ$ by $Φ^{\otimes n}$. Since exact tensor-power behaviour is an idealization, we ask whether the asymptotic capacities of $Φ$ remain achievable for channel sequences that are almost-iid along $Φ$. We consider sequences of Choi-almost-iid channels whose normalized Choi states are Mazzola-Sutter-Renner (MSR) almost-iid along the normalized Choi state of $Φ$, with defect parameters $r_n=o(n)$. In Renner's exponential de Finetti theorem for quantum states, almost-iid states appear in the approximating mixture and motivate the MSR almost-iid class. Choi-almost-iid channels play the corresponding role in an exponential de Finetti theorem for quantum channels established in a companion paper. In this paper, we prove that the universal unassisted classical and quantum capacities of the class of Choi-almost-iid channels along $Φ$ equal those of the channel $Φ$. Here universality is within this class: for each rate below the relevant capacity, a code sequence depending on $Φ$ and the rate, but not on the particular channel sequence, achieves vanishing error for every sequence in the class.

</details>


### [83] [Approximation theorems for fermionic Gaussian states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01860)
*Amir-Reza Negari,Farzin Salek,Zoltán Zimborás,Aram Harrow,Patrick Hayden,Jens Eisert*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在Majorana协方差矩阵层面证明了费米子高斯态中"充分分散的相互作用导致有效平均场描述"这一原理的定量版本，并由此导出高维图上的乘积态近似、有限费米子高斯de Finetti定理以及空间关联衰减与可恢复性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体物理中一个反复出现的原理是：充分分散的相互作用或关联会导致有效的平均场（乘积态）描述。作者希望针对费米子高斯态，将这一原理在Majorana协方差矩阵层面给出定量刻画，从而统一解释乘积态近似、de Finetti型结论与可恢复性估计。

Method: 核心观察是费米子协方差矩阵的可容许性条件对两点关联施加了定量的monogamy（一夫一妻）约束：固定区域只有有限的"协方差预算"可分配给许多不相交区域。作者以该原理为工具，分三个方向推导结论：(1) 在高次有限简单图上的二次费米子哈密顿量；(2) 在分次费米子拷贝置换下n-可交换的k拷贝高斯态；(3) 具有指数聚类互信息的空间费米子高斯态。

Result: (1) 对高次有限简单图上的二次费米子哈密顿量，得到基态能量与有限温度自由能的乘积态近似，D-正则图的误差为O(D^{-1/2})。(2) 证明了有限费米子高斯de Finetti定理：在分次费米子拷贝置换下n-可交换的k拷贝高斯态，在迹范数下与单拷贝边缘态的乘积相差O(k/n)；特别地，高斯类内的无穷可交换性蕴含精确的乘积结构。(3) 对具有指数聚类互信息的空间费米子高斯态，证明跨分离缓冲区的条件互信息以O(r^{-1})衰减，进而由可恢复性得到该态可由恢复态以O(r^{-1/2})迹范数逼近。

Conclusion: 费米子高斯系统的乘积态近似、de Finetti近似以及可恢复性估计均可直接由协方差层面的结构导出，表明协方差矩阵层面的monogamy约束是理解这些现象的统一来源。

Abstract: A recurring principle in many-body physics is that sufficiently distributed interactions or correlations lead to an effectively mean-field description. For fermionic Gaussian states, we establish a quantitative version of this principle directly at the level of Majorana covariance matrices. The main observation is that the admissibility condition for fermionic covariance matrices imposes a quantitative monogamy constraint on two-point correlations: a fixed region has only a bounded covariance budget to distribute among many disjoint regions. We use this principle in three directions. First, for quadratic fermionic Hamiltonians on finite simple graphs of high degree, we obtain product-state approximations to both the ground-state energy and the finite-temperature free energy, with error of order \(D^{-1/2}\) for \(D\)-regular graphs. Second, we prove a finite fermionic Gaussian de Finetti theorem: a \(k\)-copy Gaussian state that is \(n\)-exchangeable under graded fermionic copy permutations is \(O(k/n)\)-close in trace norm to the product of its one-copy marginal. In particular, infinite exchangeability within the Gaussian class implies exact product structure. Third, for spatial fermionic Gaussian states with exponentially clustering mutual information, we prove that the conditional mutual information across a separating buffer decays as \(O(r^{-1})\), and hence, by recoverability, the state admits an \(O(r^{-1/2})\) trace-norm approximation by a recovered state. These results show that product and de Finetti approximations and recoverability estimates for fermionic Gaussian systems can all be derived directly from covariance-level structure.

</details>


### [84] [Flexible rigidity: Compiling generic trusted-preparation protocols into sequential device-independent protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00444)
*Amir Arqand,Srijita Kundu,Ernest Y. -Z. Tan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出通用编译器，将一类带可信态制备的设备相关协议转为顺序设备无关协议，并给出基于Rényi散度的新型刚性定理，实现恒定速率与恒定噪声容忍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有刚性定理基于迹距离或保真度，面临根本性标度障碍，难以在容忍每设备恒定噪声的同时保持恒定速率，限制了设备无关协议的实际可行性与效率。

Method: 构造通用编译器：要求可信制备协议具备纠缠实现，且该实现对应满足弱刚性条件的非局域博弈最优策略。技术核心是建立基于Rényi散度的一类新型刚性定理。通用顺序情形证明依赖Fawzi–Fawzi（2021）的锐散度链式法则；在无记忆设备特例中，通过推导修正信道夹层散度的新次可加性提升速率；并证明迭代均值散度的链式法则。

Result: 编译器成功应用于不可克隆加密、点函数防复制和盲随机性扩展，得到相应顺序设备无关协议。所提刚性定理使编译器达到恒定速率（可信态制备与设备使用之比），同时容忍每设备使用的恒定噪声水平，突破以往刚性定理的标度障碍。

Conclusion: 基于Rényi散度的刚性定理为设备无关协议提供了高效编译工具，在保持恒定速率与噪声容忍方面优于迹距离/保真度方法；无记忆情形下速率进一步改进，迭代均值散度链式法则为未来优化提供方向。

Abstract: We give a generic compiler for turning a wide class of device-dependent protocols with trusted state preparation into sequential device-independent protocols. In order for our compiler to apply, the trusted-preparation protocol needs to satisfy certain minimal conditions: having an entanglement-based realization in the first place, and the entanglement-based realization corresponding to the optimal strategy of any non-local game that satisfies a fairly weak rigidity condition. We apply our generic compiler to give sequential device-independent protocols for uncloneable encryption, copy-protection of point functions, and blind randomness expansion.
  Our technical contribution is a new class of rigidity theorems based on Rényi divergences, with flexible cryptographic applications. They allow our compiler to achieve a constant rate (i.e., the ratio of trusted states prepared to device uses), while tolerating a constant level of noise per device usage. This overcomes a fundamental scaling obstacle faced by previous rigidity theorems for trace distance or fidelity. For the generic sequential case, our proof is based on the sharp-divergence chain rule by Fawzi and Fawzi (2021), which suffices for a constant but fairly low rate. In the special case when the devices retain no memory, we improve the rates by deriving a novel subadditivity property for a modified channel sandwiched divergence, which may be of independent interest. Finally, as a prospect for future improvements, we also prove a chain rule for the iterated-mean divergences from Brown, Fawzi and Fawzi (2021).

</details>


### [85] [Floquet-Universal Hamiltonian Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01878)
*Emilio Onorati,Harriet Apel,Michael M. Wolf,Toby Cubitt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了 Floquet 模拟理论：利用周期性驱动的哈密顿量合成本征定常哈密顿量，证明集合 $S$ 可 Floquet 模拟 $\mathrm{Lie}(S)$ 中所有哈密顿量，并给出 Floquet-通用哈密顿量的完整构造性刻画，仅需 $O(1)$ 局域相互作用强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟量子模拟（analogue）与数字量子模拟（digital）分属两个极端：前者用本征定常哈密顿量，需不切实际的多尺度局域相互作用强度；后者用完全可控的时变量子线路。介于两者之间的周期性哈密顿量尚未有系统理论，作者旨在填补这一中间区间的理论空白。

Method: 以李代数 $\mathrm{Lie}(S)$ 为框架，研究由周期驱动哈密顿量对目标定常哈密顿量的合成（Floquet 模拟）。证明相互作用集合 $S$ 能够 Floquet 模拟其李代数中的所有哈密顿量，并据此对 Floquet-通用哈密顿量给出完整且构造性的刻画；构造仅使用 $O(1)$ 的局域相互作用强度及其比值。

Result: 证明 $S$ 可 Floquet 模拟 $\mathrm{Lie}(S)$ 中任意哈密顿量，得到 Floquet-通用哈密顿量的完整构造性表征；构造仅需 $O(1)$ 局域相互作用强度，规避了模拟模拟中的多尺度强度要求；对 $k$-局域晶格哈密顿量等重要类别，所需驱动频率范围随系统规模多项式增长；并由此导出 Floquet 物理中自然物理性质与量的若干 BQP-完全性与 QMA-困难性结论。

Conclusion: 周期性驱动哈密顿量构成了介于模拟与数字量子模拟之间的有效中间体制；Floquet 模拟能以常数局域强度与多项式驱动频率实现通用合成，并带来复杂性理论上的直接推论。

Abstract: Analogue and digital Hamiltonian simulation represent two distinct approaches to making one quantum system replicate the physical properties of another, with consequences ranging from practical applications to complexity theory and quantum gravity. They are rooted in two distinct regimes of quantum dynamics: time-independent Hamiltonians for the former; fully controllable time-dependent quantum circuits in the latter. Time-periodic Hamiltonians represent an intermediate regime. In this work, we establish a theory of Floquet simulation where we use periodically driven Hamiltonians to synthesise time-independent ones.
  We show that a set of interactions $S$ can Floquet-simulate every Hamiltonian in the Lie algebra $\mathrm{Lie}(S)$, and consequently we give a complete and constructive characterisation of Floquet-universal Hamiltonians that are able to produce any target Hamiltonian. Notably, our construction uses only $O(1)$ local interaction strengths and ratios thereof, avoiding the impractical multi-scale local interaction strengths often required in analogue simulation via time-independent Hamiltonians. Instead, the construction requires a range of driving frequencies which scales polynomially with system size for important classes such as $k$-local lattice Hamiltonians. This has immediate complexity-theoretic implications, including numerous BQP-completeness and QMA-hardness results for natural physical properties and quantities studied in Floquet physics.

</details>


### [86] [Learning Local Fermionic Lindbladians under Parity Superselection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00448)
*Tim Möbus,Daniel Stilck França,Cambyse Rouzé*

Main category: quant-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇称超选择禁止直接测量奇马约拉纳可观测量，因此标准方法无法直接获取奇数马约拉纳算符的信息，需要设计仅使用偶制备/测量的替代学习方案。

Method: 利用偶制备与测量、不间断短时演化学习时间无关、宇称协变、k模局域的Lindbladian；通过内部标记将奇探针转为偶可观测量，并用三个分离标记间的配对测量校准动力学贡献；采用带符号Fierz反演恢复规范系数，半定拟合得到合法生成元。

Result: 在已知有界度图上的有限程模型，给定强度界ᾱ与标记访问，逐元素误差ε需Õ(ᾱ²ε⁻²log(m/δ))样本；达到钻石范数误差ε额外乘m²，与短时新系统下界匹配至对数与固定几何因子。无图时，每模一个空闲辅助模及可能非局域配对操作，在给定近似系数度界d与控制弱系数尾下，每模总绝对系数误差≤ε，样本量Õ_k(ᾱ²d²m^{⌊k/2⌋}ε⁻²log(m/δ))。几何模型中固定支撑偶可观测量可在固定时间与精度下以对数系统规模样本复杂度预测。所有保证以至少1−δ概率成立，并给出有限体积与指数尾扩展。

Conclusion: 该工作为宇称约束下的马约拉纳Lindbladian学习提供了可行框架，给出在偶测量限制下最优或近最优的样本复杂度，并厘清了有图与无图、有界度与几何模型等情形的代价差异。

Abstract: Parity superselection forbids direct measurement of odd Majorana observables. We learn time-independent, parity-covariant, $k$-mode-local Lindbladians on $m$ modes using even preparations and measurements with uninterrupted short-time evolution. Internal markers turn odd probes into even observables, and pair measurements among three separated markers calibrate their dynamical contributions. Signed Fierz inversion recovers canonical coefficients, while semidefinite fitting yields a valid generator. For finite-range models on known bounded-degree graphs with suitable marker access and a supplied weighted-strength bound $\barα$, entrywise error $\varepsilon$ is achieved using $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\barα^2\varepsilon^{-2}\log(m/δ))$ samples, without external modes or known nonzero coefficient locations. Recovery to diamond-norm error $\varepsilon$ costs an additional factor $m^2$, matching lower bounds for short-time experiments on fresh systems up to logarithmic and fixed geometric factors. Without a supplied graph, one idle ancillary mode per system mode and potentially nonlocal pair operations give total absolute coefficient error at most $\varepsilon$ per mode using $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}_k(\barα^2\mathsf d^2m^{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}\varepsilon^{-2}\log(m/δ))$ samples, under a supplied approximate coefficient-degree bound $\mathsf d$ and controlled weak-coefficient tails. All guarantees hold with probability at least $1-δ$. For geometric models, fixed-support even observables can be predicted with logarithmic system-size sample complexity at fixed time and accuracy. We also give finite-volume and exponential-tail extensions.

</details>


### [87] [Universal Bound and Phase Transition in Many-Body Fermionic Non-Gaussianity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02075)
*Masahiro Hoshino,Ryota Matsuda,Yuto Ashida*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 以magic Rényi熵(MRE)度量费米非高斯性,证明其普适上界与Haar均值(最大密度ln(4/3)每Majorana),并揭示SYK演化下MRE密度的一阶转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米非高斯性是通用量子计算的资源,可由量子多体相互作用产生,但缺乏对其定量刻画、上界及动力学增长规律的理解。

Method: 采用magic Rényi熵作为非高斯性度量,推导普适上界与Haar随机态均值;在SYK哈密顿量下对高斯态作虚时与实时演化,并考察用于计算MRE的四副本置换对称性。

Result: 推导出MRE的普适上界与Haar均值,证明大系统极限下典型Haar随机态达到每Majorana ln(4/3)的最大MRE密度;发现协方差矩阵相同态的MRE可广延地不同;识别出MRE密度拐点与四副本置换对称性自发破缺标志的一阶转变,该转变不反映在热力学自由能中。

Conclusion: MRE给出了费米非高斯性的严格定量框架,其动力学增长呈现热力学自由能无法捕捉的一阶定性转变。

Abstract: Fermionic non-Gaussianity is a resource for universal quantum computation that can be generated by interactions in quantum many-body systems. Using the magic Rényi entropy (MRE) as a measure of non-Gaussianity, we derive its universal upper bound and Haar mean, proving that typical Haar-random states attain the maximal MRE density of $\ln(4/3)$ per Majorana in the large-system limit. We also show that the MRE can differ extensively between states with identical covariance matrices. To investigate how non-Gaussianity grows toward this maximal density, we evolve a Gaussian state in imaginary and real time under the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev Hamiltonian. By varying the imaginary- and real-time durations, we identify a first-order transition marked by a kink in the MRE density and spontaneous breaking of the permutation symmetry among the four copies used to evaluate the MRE. This transition represents a qualitative change in the non-Gaussianity of the state that is not reflected in the thermal free energy.

</details>


### [88] [Avoiding Exponentially Large Groups with Open Quantum System Technology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01932)
*Jihong Cai,Advith Govindarajan,Marius Junge*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出基于开放量子系统的高效量子态制备与信道实现方法：在少量辅助比特环境中识别低维李群G（李代数维度为poly(n)），结合部分迹与环境态制备及群操作，可从任意固定输入态逼近任意量子态；并进一步给出单一开放系统"魔术"相互作用哈密顿量以实现密度矩阵空间传递性，进而用更大环境实现n比特信道的信道普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统态制备方法通常依赖在n比特上操作整个酉群，所需资源随比特数呈指数增长，成本高。作者希望利用开放量子系统的全部能力（部分迹、环境态制备、非酉相互作用）降低资源需求，探索用小的、廉价的李群结构代替全局酉群，从而建立一套关于"廉价态"与"昂贵态"的新分类框架。

Every state can be approximated starting from any fixed input state by repeatedly using partial trace and state preparation in the environment alongside group operations. Moreover, we also identify a single (open system magic) interaction Hamiltonian whose unitary group can be combined with open system quantum technology mentioned above to achieve transitivity on the space of all densities. Similarly, we show that all channels on $n$ qubits can be approximated by using a larger environment, achieving channel universality. Abstract: For a quantum code defined by an isometry $T:H_{\rm code} \to H_{\rm phys}$, an operator map is a *-homomorphism $\varphi: B(H_{\rm code}) \to B(H_{\rm phys})$ from the space of operators on the logical Hilbert space to the space of operators on the physical Hilbert space satisfying $\varphi({\cal O}) T = T {\cal O}$ for every operator ${\cal O}$ on $H_{\rm code}$. In this case, $\varphi(B(H_{\rm code})$ is a von Neumann factor subalgebra of $B(H_{\rm phys})$ and we call the pair $(T,\varphi)$ a {\it factor code}.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 无参数保留库仑载体 $-δ/e$ 与有限-$Z$ 插入 $8δ/9$ 紧密复现了独立的类氦系数与能量。显式Bethe–Goldstone计算展示出宽广的非单分母回归谱，然而其标量能量恰好是矩可压缩的。这表明微观谱复杂性与单模态能量充分性是可以分离的两个层面。

Conclusion: 定态量子演化在reg-HF框架下通过单模态排除公理实现了标量能量的单模态充分描述。氦原子检验表明，即使响应谱结构复杂（非单分母、宽广回归谱），其标量能量仍可由单模态机制精确刻画。研究将微观谱复杂性与单模态能量充分性明确区分开来，为理解定态量子态"单一动力学相位+标量能量"这一特征提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: A stationary quantum state has one dynamical phase factor and one scalar energy even when its internal response spans a large Hilbert space. Our argument concerns not the value or observability of an absolute phase, but the one-ness of stationary time evolution. In regularized Hartree--Fock (reg-HF), the connection to the stationary mean field is established before any one-mode reduction: the primitive-product relative-coordinate Gaussian measure induced by the Gaussian HF representation fixes a covariance and, at that covariance, is simultaneously the minimum-Fisher-information and maximum-Shannon-differential-entropy distribution and the equality case of the Stam bound. The Coulomb operator admits a compatible Gaussian scale resolution, and virial completion converts the resulting regularized pair displacement into a stationary-compatible response. Stationarity motivates a one-mode constraint on the terminal scalar correlation response. The decisive reg-HF hypothesis is a one-mode exclusion axiom: covariance carried by response components orthogonal to the retained pair mode is excluded from the terminal scalar energy property, without asserting that the unrestricted response is one dimensional. Helium provides the minimal nontrivial test. The parameter-free retained Coulomb carrier $-δ/e$ and finite-$Z$ insertion $8δ/9$ closely reproduce independent He-like coefficients and energies, while an explicit Bethe--Goldstone calculation displays a broad non-single-denominator return spectrum even though its scalar energy is exactly moment-compressible. The result separates microscopic spectral complexity from one-mode energetic sufficiency.

</details>

Abstract: Electromagnetic duality in the toric code has order two at the level of anyon types and acts as a Hadamard-type logical transformation on the encoded quantum information. Here, we ask whether it can likewise be realized microscopically as an order-two Clifford operation. For the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ toric code, we prove that any locality-preserving Clifford realization of electric-magnetic exchange cannot have order two. Our proof is fully general and requires neither translation symmetry with restricted families of system sizes nor a fixed pairing between vertex and plaquette stabilizers. Geometrically, the proof locally emulates the unavoidable crossing between electric and magnetic strings, similar to a cross-cap in a non-orientable manifold. Extending the problem to the $\mathbb{Z}_N$ toric code, we find that an order-two Clifford realization exists for odd $N$, whereas it is impossible for even $N$. Our results show that the group law of an emergent electromagnetic duality need not lift faithfully to its microscopic Clifford realization.

Abstract: Many quantum systems are believed to thermalize slowly at low temperatures due to the existence of metastable phases. However, there are cases where further cooling can restore polynomial-time thermalization: we demonstrate that the Davies dynamics of the mean-field interchange model with local dimension $d\geq 3$ and single-site generalized Pauli couplings exhibits this behavior. At low and high temperatures surrounding the interval $(β_{\mathrm{min}}(d),d)$, the spectral gap of the Davies generator is bounded below by an inverse polynomial in system size $n$. TL;DR: 该论文证明任意置换不变混合态的酉Schur采样可仅用成对SWAP测试实现，误差ε下需O(n·min{n,d}³·log²(n/ε))次随机SWAP测试，并将其应用于量子纯度放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电路实现Schur采样依赖Clebsch-Gordan变换、广义相位估计或对称群量子傅里叶变换等相干表示论操作，实现复杂。作者希望找到更简单、仅用基本成对测量的方案。

Method: 核心思想是反复识别并提取最大的反对称子系统，将酉Schur采样转化为对反对称性的搜索，算法可解释为Young图的随机遍历。仅使用双量子比特投影到对称与反对称子空间的成对SWAP测试。协议保持SU(d)不可约表示态，并在重数子系统中制备规范纯态。

Result: 对n个d维qudit上任意置换不变混合态，协议以O(n·min{n,d}³·log²(n/ε))次随机SWAP测试达到误差ε（以diamond距离度量）。作为应用，在量子纯度放大中，最优纯化态可通过保留单个指定输出qudit并丢弃其余量子比特获得。

Conclusion: 成对SWAP测试足以实现酉Schur采样，无需相干表示论操作；该方案为Schur采样提供了搜索反对称性的新视角，并给出量子纯度放大的简洁最优策略。

Abstract: Schur sampling is a fundamental primitive for extracting permutation-invariant information from many-body quantum systems and has broad applications in quantum information science. Circuit-based implementations typically rely on coherent representation-theoretic operations such as Clebsch-Gordan transforms, generalized phase estimation, or quantum Fourier transforms over the symmetric group. We show that unitary Schur sampling of arbitrary permutation-invariant mixed states on $n$ $d$-dimensional qudits can instead be implemented using only pairwise SWAP tests, namely, two-qudit projective measurements onto the symmetric and antisymmetric subspaces. Our protocol achieves error $ε$ in diamond distance using $O(n\min\{n,d\}^{3}\log^2(n/ε))$ random SWAP tests. The central idea is to repeatedly identify and extract the largest antisymmetric subsystem. This turns unitary Schur sampling into a search for antisymmetry and gives the algorithm a natural interpretation as a stochastic traversal of a Young diagram. The protocol preserves the $\mathrm{SU}(d)$-irrep state while preparing a canonical pure state in the multiplicity subsystem. As an application, we consider quantum purity amplification (QPA), in which multiple noisy copies of a pure quantum state are combined to produce a state of higher purity. We show that the optimal purified state can be obtained by retaining a single designated output qudit and discarding the rest.

</details>


### [96] [Geometry of Knill-Laflamme Coefficients for Pauli Error Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01992)
*Baisong Sun,Ningping Cao,Yiu Tung Poon,Bei Zeng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Knill-Laflamme系数可达向量集的几何结构，证明表示重数足以将活跃期望数据提升为精确标量压缩，从而该系数范围为凸连通的活跃期望体，其签名谱为闭区间；并给出若干反例说明码维数与检测约束的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Knill-Laflamme系数通过误差算子在码空间上的标量压缩刻画精确量子纠错检测。给定Pauli可观测量与固定码维数，可达系数向量构成联合高阶数值域，其欧氏范数像为签名谱。作者希望厘清算子表示、码维数与检测约束如何支配这一几何结构，并给出系数范围凸性、连通性及签名谱为区间的充分条件。

Method: 以联合高阶数值域（joint higher-rank numerical range）为框架，将可达Knill-Laflamme系数向量刻画为活跃期望体的像；核心定理利用表示重数（每个占据块的重数至少为码维数乘以相应活跃态秩）作为提升条件，把活跃期望数据提升为精确标量压缩。随后应用于交换Pauli族（足够公共本征空间简并给出多胞形）与子系统稳定子码（受保护逻辑子系统提供重数空间以处理非交换规范可观测量），并通过显式例子与父哈密顿量构造进行验证。

Result: 当重数足以实现所有活跃态时，系数范围等于完整活跃期望体，故为凸且连通，签名谱为闭区间。交换Pauli族在足够公共本征简并下产生多胞形；子系统稳定子码中逻辑子系统为重数提供空间。显式例子表明：增大码维数可使范围收缩、塌缩或变空，而增大重数可将Bloch球变成实心球。三量子比特例子中，额外的非交换检测约束把四面体范围约化为其重心与四个顶点，签名谱不连通。

Conclusion: 该工作为Knill-Laflamme系数几何提供了统一的结构框架：重数充分性保证系数范围的凸性与连通性以及区间型签名谱，而码维数与检测约束可导致范围收缩、塌缩或非连通。结果为结构化误差模型下系数范围连通性与签名谱区间性的更精细判据提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Knill-Laflamme coefficients characterize exact quantum error detection through scalar compressions of error operators to the code space. For prescribed Pauli observables and a fixed code dimension, attainable coefficient vectors form a joint higher-rank numerical range, whose Euclidean norm image is the signature spectrum. We study how operator representation, code dimension, and detection constraints govern this geometry. Our main theorem identifies representation multiplicity as sufficient to lift active expectation data to exact scalar compressions: a lift exists whenever each occupied block has multiplicity at least the code dimension times the rank of the corresponding active state. When multiplicity suffices to realize all active states, the coefficient range is the full active expectation body and is therefore convex and connected, with a closed interval as its signature spectrum. This framework unifies commuting Pauli families, where sufficient common-eigenspace degeneracy yields polytopes, and subsystem stabilizer codes, where protected logical subsystems supply multiplicity spaces for noncommuting gauge observables. Explicit examples show how increasing the code dimension can make a range shrink, collapse, or become empty, while increasing multiplicity can turn a Bloch sphere into a filled ball. In the subsystem setting, parent-Hamiltonian constructions realize exact detecting codes as degenerate ground spaces, selecting distinguished vectors and continuous paths within these ranges. A final three-qubit example shows additional noncommuting detection constraints reduce a tetrahedral range to its barycenter and four vertices, producing a disconnected signature spectrum. The results provide a structural framework for Knill-Laflamme coefficient geometry and motivate sharper criteria for connected coefficient ranges and interval signature spectra under structured error models.

</details>


### [97] [Quantum Algorithms for OPI Variants Beyond Locality and Classical Decodability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00502)
*Seyoon Ragavan,Noah Shutty*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Regev量子约化框架下突破两项限制：一是用量子解码器处理非坐标型约束（通过码的"两重乘法性质"）；二是保留经典解码器但允许直方图局部（全局频率）约束，证明其随机重采样稳定性并应用于最优多项式交集（OPI）变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Regev约化是寻找满足非线性约束码字的量子算法框架，通过解码对偶码实现。现有未被去量子化的应用依赖两个限制：高效经典解码器，以及逐坐标约束（每坐标的允许取值集合）。作者希望分别突破这两个限制，扩展框架的适用范围，解决更一般的约束问题。

Method: 第一项贡献：引入量子解码器，求解B y=0、y∈(F_q\{0})^m的问题（B∈F_q^{n×m}）。对固定素数q>2，将Chen-Liu-Zhandry（Eurocrypt 2022，针对m=Ω(n^2)随机矩阵）的模板推广到具有"两重乘法性质"的码（B的行张成空间），即两两码字逐坐标乘积张成的空间维数远小于m。第二项贡献：保留经典解码，但允许对符号频率的全局约束；研究"直方图局部"约束（规定每个符号允许出现次数），证明对广泛族类，均匀随机满足向量在随机重采样一个坐标后以常数概率仍满足（稳定性），从而在可解码Hamming重量处提供足够Fourier质量。

Result: 第一项贡献给出了在具有两重乘法性质的码上，用量子解码器求解B y=0且各分量非零问题的方法，超越了此前的随机矩阵体制。第二项贡献证明了直方图局部约束的随机重采样稳定性（常数概率保持满足），并据此得到针对最优多项式交集（OPI）若干变体的高效量子算法。

Conclusion: 通过分别引入量子解码器与允许全局频率约束，本文扩展了Regev约化的能力边界：前者将量子解码推广至具两重乘法性质的码，后者利用直方图局部约束的稳定性获得OPI变体的高效量子算法，展示了该框架在超越逐坐标约束与经典解码方面的潜力。

Abstract: Regev's reduction is a quantum algorithmic framework for finding codewords satisfying nonlinear constraints by decoding the dual code. To date, applications that have not been dequantized have relied on efficient classical decoders and coordinate-wise constraints specifying a set of allowed values for each coordinate. We push past these two restrictions separately via two separate contributions.
  Our first contribution uses a quantum decoder to find solutions $\mathbf{y}\in(\mathbb{F}_q\setminus\{0\})^m$ to $\mathbf{B}\mathbf{y}=0$, where $\mathbf{B}\in\mathbb{F}_q^{n\times m}$. For fixed prime $q>2$, Chen, Liu, and Zhandry (Eurocrypt 2022) solve this problem for random matrices with $m=Ω(n^2)$, a regime now covered by classical algorithms. We adapt their template to codes (spanned by the rows of $\mathbf{B}$) with a ``two-fold multiplication property'': the coordinate-wise products of pairs of codewords span a space of dimension much smaller than $m$.
  Our second contribution retains classical decoding but allows global constraints on symbol frequencies. We study ``histogram-local'' constraints, which specify the allowed numbers of occurrences of each symbol. For broad families of these constraints, we show that a uniformly random satisfying vector remains satisfying with constant probability after resampling a uniformly random coordinate. This stability provides sufficient Fourier mass at decodable Hamming weights, yielding efficient quantum algorithms for variants of optimal polynomial intersection (OPI).

</details>


### [98] [Local random quantum circuits converge to the Porter-Thomas distribution in polynomial depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02125)
*Aniruddha Sen,Nicholas Hunter-Jones*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了多项式深度的砖墙随机量子电路（由最近邻Haar随机门构成）的输出分布在全变差距离上以逆多项式速率收敛到Porter-Thomas分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Porter-Thomas统计是随机量子态及混沌量子多体系统输出分布的特征，其在随机电路采样和量子优势实验验证中起着核心作用。然而，低深度随机量子电路输出统计近似服从Porter-Thomas这一预期缺乏严格的收敛性证明，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 作者结合了近似设计（approximate designs）给出的矩界、特征函数的解析估计，以及针对逆矩的局域反集中（anticoncentration）性质进行证明。

Result: 对于由最近邻Haar随机门构成的局域随机量子电路，固定比特串上的输出概率分布满足：对任意m≥0，深度为O(n^{2m+1}log(n))的电路所对应的分布与Porter-Thomas分布之间的全变差距离至多为O(1/n^m)。

Conclusion: 多项式深度砖墙随机电路的输出分布在总变差距离上以逆多项式速率收敛到Porter-Thomas分布，为随机电路采样及量子优势实验中的Porter-Thomas统计假设提供了严格的理论支持。

Abstract: Porter-Thomas statistics are a characteristic feature of the output distribution of random quantum states and, more broadly, chaotic quantum many-body systems. Convergence to Porter-Thomas plays a central role in random circuit sampling and experimental demonstrations of quantum advantage, where the output statistics of low-depth random quantum circuits are expected to be approximately Porter-Thomas, despite the absence of a rigorous proof of convergence. We show that the output distribution of polynomial-depth brickwork random circuits converges inverse-polynomially in total variation distance to the Porter-Thomas distribution. Specifically, consider the output probability distribution over a fixed bitstring of a local random quantum circuit, constructed from nearest-neighbor Haar random gates. Then, for any $m \geq 0$, the distribution corresponding to circuits of depth $O(n^{2m+1}\log(n))$ is at most $O(1/n^m)$ far in total variation distance from the Porter-Thomas distribution. Our proof uses moment bounds from approximate designs, analytic estimates for characteristic functions, and a local anticoncentration property for inverse moments.

</details>


### [99] [Graph Neural Post-selection for Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00504)
*Conor Carty,Tamas Noszko,Joschka Roffe,Roberto Bondesan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出图神经网络后选择方法，仅用综合征（及BP后验）预测译码失败，实现高效量子纠错后选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算中后选择通过丢弃可能逻辑失败的采样提升逻辑可靠性，但传统方法需额外执行译码器或依赖专门的互补间隙方法，代价高。本文希望在不额外运行译码器的前提下预测译码失败。

Method: 使用图神经网络（GNN）作为神经后选择器，仅基于综合征预测译码器失败；对高码率量子低密度奇偶校验（qLDPC）码，还利用现有置信传播（BP）后验信息。在均匀去极化电路级噪声下评估旋转表面码与双变量自行车（BB）码存储。

Result: 保留90%采样时，在真实物理错误率下，[[72,12,6]]和[[144,12,12]] BB码逻辑错误率分别降低约3700倍和740倍；距离5表面码在物理噪声p=0.003时，神经后选择器在未运行译码器的情况下达到与领先互补间隙方法相当的逻辑错误抑制效果。

Conclusion: 图神经网络后选择器可在不额外执行译码器的情况下有效提升逻辑可靠性，对高码率qLDPC码和表面码均表现优异，为量子纠错后选择提供了高效新途径。

Abstract: Post-selection improves the logical reliability of quantum computation by rejecting shots which are likely to result in logical failure. We introduce graph neural networks that predict decoder failure without additional decoder executions, using only syndromes, and for high-rate quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes, existing belief-propagation posteriors. We evaluate rotated surface and bivariate bicycle (BB) code memories under uniform depolarising circuit-level noise. Retaining 90% of shots gives approximately 3700x and 740x reductions in logical error rates for [[72,12,6]] and [[144,12,12]] BB codes at realistic physical error rates. For the distance 5 surface code at physical noise p=0.003, our neural post-selector achieves logical error suppression comparable to the leading complementary gap method without running the decoder.

</details>


### [100] [Beyond Light Cones: State Preparation Complexity in Quantum Spin Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02166)
*Omar Al-Ghattas,David Gamarnik,Bobak T Kiani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出用“有效 profile 复杂度”（基于 Pauli profile 的度量熵）刻画稠密量子 p-spin 哈密顿量的态制备复杂度，突破仅依赖光锥的界限。研究给出能量分离、基准类比较、门数下界、深度-宽度权衡、纠缠深度与 MPS bond dimension 下界，以及 magic 层级各层级的阻碍结果；并证明 Clifford+T 电路在 o(n) 个 T 门时相对乘积 stabilizer 态无主导阶能量优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统态制备复杂度下界多基于电路光锥，难以处理稠密相互作用或浅层电路后接非局域操作（如 Clifford 电路）的情形，因此需要一种不依赖小光锥论证的复杂度度量。

Method: 引入有效 profile 复杂度，由 Pauli profile（恰好支撑在 p 个量子比特上的 Pauli 算子期望）的度量熵导出；证明结合非对称量子 de Finetti 定理（Berta 等）与高斯过程熵界。应用于 p-spin 基态能量问题，并分析 Parham magic 层级各固定层级及第一级 reverse magic 设定。

Result: 二次有效 profile 复杂度有界的类与基态能量保持正比于 sqrt(n) 的间隔；次二次复杂度类不能以主导阶超越合适基准类（乘积态为通用基准）。达到近基态能量需 Ω(n^2/log n) 个一/二比特门，即使允许任意可丢弃 ancilla。还得到深度-宽度权衡、纠缠深度与 MPS bond dimension 下界，以及 magic 层级各层阻碍。第一级 reverse magic 中，浅层电路后接无限制 Clifford 电路会扩散局部可观测量，故允许任意多干净 ancilla；Clifford+T 电路若只有 o(n) 个 T 门，即使有无限制 Clifford 和任意可丢弃 ancilla，也无主导阶能量优势。

Conclusion: 有效 profile 复杂度提供了超越光锥界限的态制备复杂度分析框架，统一导出稠密 p-spin 基态制备的门数、深度、纠缠与 magic 资源下界，并刻画了非局域后处理下基准优势的消失条件。

Abstract: We introduce a method for studying state preparation complexity in dense quantum $p$-spin Hamiltonians on $n$ qubits, going beyond bounds based only on circuit lightcones. The key input is the class's effective profile complexity, which is derived from the metric entropy of its Pauli profiles. These profiles record expectations of all Pauli operators supported on exactly $p$ qubits. Classes with uniformly bounded quadratic effective profile complexity remain separated from the ground-state energy by a positive multiple of $\sqrt n$ for sufficiently large fixed $p$. At subquadratic effective profile complexity, the class cannot outperform a suitable benchmark class at leading order, with product states providing a universal benchmark. The proof combines an adaptation of a nonsymmetric quantum de Finetti theorem of Berta et al. (arXiv:1810.12197) with Gaussian process entropy bounds.
  Applying this framework, we show that attaining near-ground-state energy requires $Ω(n^2/\log n)$ one- and two-qubit gates, even with arbitrary discardable ancillas. We also obtain depth-width tradeoffs, entanglement-depth and matrix product state bond-dimension lower bounds, and obstructions for both orientations at every fixed level of Parham's magic hierarchy (arXiv:2504.19966), with total circuit width $O(n)$. In first-level reverse magic, a shallow circuit is followed by an unrestricted Clifford circuit. The latter can spread local observables across the system, preventing a direct application of small-lightcone bounds. For this first-level class, our bounds also allow arbitrarily many clean ancillas at fixed shallow-circuit depth. A sharper benchmark shows that Clifford+$T$ circuits with $o(n)$ $T$-gates have no leading-order energy advantage over product stabilizer states, even with unrestricted Clifford operations and arbitrary discardable ancillas.

</details>


### [101] [A computational phase diagram for the transverse field Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02079)
*Thuy-Duong Vuong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了横场Ising模型配分函数的经典可计算性，给出了一个基于谱宽与温度/横场强度比值的复杂度相变判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望刻画横场Ising模型在何种参数区间内其配分函数与Gibbs态可观测量可被高效经典算法近似，并明确其计算复杂度的硬边界，从而理解该类量子多体问题相对经典计算的难易分界。

Method: 定义Hamiltonian H = 1/2 Σ J_ij Z_i Z_j + Σ h_i^z Z_i + η Σ X_i，令Δ(J)=λ_max(J)-λ_min(J)。当Δ(J)·tanh(βη)/η ≤ 1时，构造随机化经典算法近似配分函数Z(β)=Tr(e^{-βH})；当该量大于1时，通过复杂性归约证明近似Z(β)（在exp(o(n))乘法因子内）是NP-hard。此外在≤1区间内给出近似Gibbs态Pauli弦观测量的随机化经典算法。

Result: 当Δ(J)·tanh(βη)/η ≤ 1时，算法在关于n、β、模型参数及ε^{-1}的多项式时间内，以给定相对误差ε∈(0,1)近似配分函数；当该量>1时，以exp(o(n))乘法因子近似Z(β)为NP-hard，在标准复杂度假设下不太可能有多项式时间经典或量子算法。在≤1区间内，还可将Pauli弦观测量近似到任意小加性误差；当观测量在X基下对角（P∈{I,X}^{⊗n}）时进一步达到任意小相对误差。

Conclusion: 该工作以Δ(J)·tanh(βη)/η = 1为阈值，建立了横场Ising模型配分函数经典可近似性的清晰相变：阈值以下存在高效随机化经典算法，阈值以上近似问题NP-hard，并给出Gibbs态观测量的相应近似保证。

Abstract: We study the transverse field Ising model, defined by the Hamiltonian $H =\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i, j\in [n]} J_{ij} Z_i Z_j +\sum_{i=1}^n h_i^z Z_i + η\sum_{i} X_i$ where $J $ is the symmetric interaction matrix, and $η$ is the transverse field strength. Let $Δ(J)=λ_{\max}(J)-λ_{\min}(J)$ be the spectral width of $J.$ When the inverse temperature $β\geq0$ satisfies $Δ(J)\cdot\frac{\tanh(βη)}η\leq1$, we give a randomized classical algorithm that approximates the partition function $Z(β)=\operatorname{Tr}(e^{-βH})$ to a given relative error $ε\in(0,1)$ in time polynomial in $n$, $β$, the model parameters, and $ε^{-1}$. When $ Δ(J) \cdot \frac{\tanh(βη)}η > 1 ,$ we show that approximating $ Z(β)$ within an $\exp(o(n))$-multiplicative factor is $\textbf{NP}$-hard, and thus unlikely to admit an efficient classical or quantum algorithms under standard complexity theoretic assumptions. Furthermore, in the regime $Δ(J)\cdot \frac{\tanh(βη)}η\leq 1,$ we provide an efficient randomized classical algorithm that approximates Pauli string observables of the Gibbs state $ ρ_β= \frac{e^{-βH}}{\operatorname{Tr}(e^{-βH})}$ within an arbitrarily small additive error. In the special case when the observable is also diagonal in the $X$-basis, i.e. $P \in \{I, X\}^{\otimes n}$, the algorithm further achieves arbitrarily small relative error.

</details>


### [102] [Polynomial-time classical and quantum simulation of quantum impurity models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02167)
*Jiaqing Jiang,Nathan Ju,Ojas Parekh,Chaithanya Rayudu,Andrew Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究量子杂质模型的计算复杂性，证明其静态性质（基态能量、配分函数）可在经典计算机上多项式时间高效计算，而动力学性质（非平衡格林函数）的模拟对经典计算机是困难的，但在量子计算机上容易，从而划定了该问题的经典与量子可解性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子杂质模型既是相互作用量子物质的典型模型，也是现代电子结构方法的核心计算原语，描述与环境耦合的小型相互作用费米子系统。明确其模拟在经典与量子计算之间的复杂性边界，对理解该类问题的可解性和量子优势来源具有重要价值，作者希望刻画这一边界。

Method: 从计算复杂性角度进行分析。针对静态性质：设计经典算法，(1) 以 poly(n, δ^{-1}) 时间将基态能量估计到加性精度 δ；(2) 以 poly(n, β, δ^{-1}) 时间将逆温度 β 下的配分函数估计到相对精度 δ，n 为系统规模。针对动力学性质：以计算非平衡格林函数为典型例子，论证其捕捉量子计算的完整能力，即便在有限温度下。

Result: 静态性质可在经典计算机上高效计算：基态能量估计复杂度由拟多项式改进为多项式时间，并首次为热平衡下模拟杂质模型建立严格多项式时间保证。动力学性质模拟对经典计算机困难，但对量子计算机容易——计算非平衡格林函数可捕获量子计算的完整能力，即使有限温度亦然。

Conclusion: 这些结果排除了计算静态性质时存在超多项式量子加速的可能性，但为在非平衡杂质物理模拟中获得量子优势提供了途径，从而界定了量子杂质模型静态与动力学性质之间的经典/量子可解性边界。

Abstract: Quantum impurity models are paradigmatic models of interacting quantum matter, as well as key computational primitives for modern electronic-structure methods. They describe a small subsystem of interacting fermions coupled to a large, noninteracting bath. We perform a comprehensive study of the computational complexity of simulating impurity models, delineating the boundary between classical and quantum tractability for this class of problems. Our main finding is that static properties of quantum impurity models can be calculated efficiently on a classical computer. Specifically, we give classical algorithms that (1) estimate the ground-state energy to additive precision $δ$ in time $\mathrm{poly}(n,δ^{-1})$, and (2) estimate the partition function at inverse temperature $β$ to relative precision $δ$ in time $\mathrm{poly}(n,β,δ^{-1})$, where $n$ is the system size. These results improve the previous best-known complexity for ground-state energy estimation from quasipolynomial to polynomial time, while establishing for the first time rigorous polynomial-time guarantees for simulating impurity models in thermal equilibrium. On the other hand, we find that simulating dynamical properties of impurity models is hard for classical computers but easy on a quantum computer. As a canonical example, we show that computing their nonequilibrium Green's functions captures the full power of quantum computation, even at finite temperature. Taken together, our results rule out superpolynomial quantum speedups for computing static properties, but provide an avenue for quantum advantage in simulating impurity physics out of equilibrium.

</details>


### [103] [Noisy Quantum Query Complexity via Fractional Block Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00506)
*Mehil Agarwal,Shravas Rao,Fang Song*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究多种不完美预言机模型下的量子查询复杂度，以分数块敏感度（fbs）为统一框架给出下界，并提出时变退相干模型与变分资源量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际量子算法中的预言机访问往往不完美（如以一定概率出错、混合相干/经典查询、逐次退相干噪声），而既有下界技术多针对理想预言机，难以刻画这些噪声模型下的量子优势是否仍存在，因此需要一个统一的复杂度下界框架。

Method: 以分数块敏感度 fbs 为核心度量：对疏忽型预言机（以概率1−p返回正确结果）用 fbs(f,0^n) 建立下界；通过构造部分函数 f' 保持 f 的量子查询复杂度来研究疏忽是否破坏量子加速；对混合算法证明 C+Q²=Ω(fbs(f)) 权衡；对 IID 退相干噪声模型证明需要 Ω(p·fbs(f)) 次查询；进一步引入时变退相干模型族与一个总被 fbs(f) 下界的变分资源量，将资源计算归约为凸优化。

Result: 疏忽预言机下用 fbs(f,0^n) 给出下界，并证明可将任意 f 变换为部分函数 f' 使疏忽查询复杂度基本保持 f 的量子查询复杂度，从而构造出疏忽查询下仍具指数量子加速的部分函数，排除了部分函数上基于 fbs(f) 的通用下界；对全体布尔函数，混合模型得到 C+Q²=Ω(fbs(f))，IID 退相干模型得到 Ω(p·fbs(f))；变分资源量可由凸优化计算，据此重新导出上述两个界，并确定退相干率随时间增长时无序搜索的查询复杂度。
