Conclusion: 分数块敏感度 fbs 可作为不完美预言机量子查询复杂度的统一下界工具，但部分函数上不存在仅依赖 fbs(f) 的通用下界；时变退相干模型可由一个受 fbs(f) 下界的变分资源量刻画，其计算归约为凸优化，为新的噪声时间表提供简便的下界推导途径。

Abstract: We study quantum query complexity under several models of imperfect oracle access, and develop lower bounds through a common framework based on fractional block sensitivity (\(\fbs\)).
  For a negligent oracle that applies the correct query with probability \(1-p\), we prove a lower bound in terms of \(\fbs(f,0^n)\). We also show, perhaps surprisingly, that negligence need not destroy quantum speedups: any function \(f\) can be transformed into a partial function \(f'\) whose negligent query complexity essentially preserves the quantum query complexity of \(f\). This gives partial functions with exponential quantum speedups even under negligent queries, and in particular rules out a general lower bound in terms of \(\fbs(f)\) for partial functions in this model.
  For two other models, we obtain general lower bounds in terms of \(\fbs(f)\) for all Boolean functions. For hybrid algorithms using \(Q\) coherent and \(C\) classical queries, we prove the tradeoff \(C+Q^2=Ω(\fbs(f))\). For an IID dephasing noisy model where each query dephases the query-index register at rate \(p\) independently, we prove \(Ω\!\left(p\,\fbs(f)\right)\) queries are necessary.
  Finally, we introduce a broader family of time-varying dephasing models and identify a variational resource that is always lower bounded by \(\fbs(f)\). Computing this resource reduces to a convex optimization problem, providing a simple way to derive lower bounds for new noise schedules. As applications, we recover the hybrid and IID dephasing bounds and determine the query complexity of unstructured search when the dephasing rate grows over time.

</details>


### [104] [Blind Unforgeability implies Plus-One Unforgeability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00517)
*Mehil Agarwal,Calliope S. Reimann,Fang Song*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了量子盲不可伪造性蕴含量子加一不可伪造性，从而确立前者严格强于后者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子盲不可伪造性与量子加一不可伪造性之间的关系是该领域的公开问题：已知加一不可伪造性不能推出盲不可伪造性，但反方向是否成立尚不明确。

Method: 提出一种新的"平滑"技术，以黑盒方式对量子查询算法的查询历史进行相干缩放，将任意成功的加一攻击者转化为盲不可伪造性攻击者，仅带来逆多项式损失。

Result: 证明了量子盲不可伪造性蕴含量子加一不可伪造性；结合已知的反向不蕴含关系，得出盲不可伪造性在量子情形下严格强于加一不可伪造性。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了文献中的公开问题，并指出仅提取有用查询历史的旧思路一般必然失败，转而相干地重构查询历史间的干涉方式，构成概念上的转变。

Abstract: We prove that quantum blind-unforgeability implies quantum plus-one unforgeability, settling an open question in the literature. Since plus-one unforgeability is known not to imply blind-unforgeability, our result establishes that blind-unforgeability is \emph{strictly stronger} than plus-one unforgeability in the quantum setting.
  The implication is proven using a new \emph{smoothing} technique that coherently rescales the query histories of a quantum-query algorithm in a black-box manner. This enables us to transform any successful plus-one attacker into a blind-unforgeability attacker with only inverse-polynomial loss. Our proof represents a conceptual shift from merely extracting a useful query history as in earlier attempts, which we show inevitably fails in general, to first coherently reshaping how query histories interfere.

</details>


### [105] [One-Shot any Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02137)
*Andrew C. Yuan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明单次码具有自纠错性，并给出将任意 [[n,k]] CSS QLDPC 码转换为 [[nm,k]] 单次量子纠错码的构造，配备 O(log m) 并行时间的高效局部解码器，并分析其阈值与误差抑制增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次量子纠错（SS QEC）可避免测量噪声累积，是容错量子计算的重要方向。作者希望给出一般性结论：单次码是自纠错的，并为任意 CSS QLDPC 码提供一种可提升误差抑制、保持阈值且本地可解码的通用转换方法。

Method: 1) 给出“任意单次码是自纠错”的简单证明；2) 将任意 [[n,k]] CSS QLDPC 码通过构造转换为 [[nm,k]] CSS QLDPC 码，使其具备单次纠错能力与运行时间为 O(log m) 并行时间的局部解码器；3) 在联合局部随机物理噪声与测量噪声下分析阈值行为与误差抑制。

Result: 在联合局部随机物理与测量噪声的常数阈值 p_RG>0 以下，T 轮纠错后的逻辑失败概率为 O(Tn)exp[−Ω(m^α)]（α>0 为常数），即阈值为 p_RG、误差抑制为 Ω(m^α)。若输入码本身具有阈值 p_c>0 与误差抑制 Ω(n^β) 的单次解码器，则输出码将误差抑制增强为 Ω(m^α n^β)，同时保持解码器无关的阈值 p_RG，并在 p_c<p_RG 时称为“增强的”。

Conclusion: 该工作统一并推广了单次量子纠错理论：证实单次码的自纠错性质，并给出一类通用 LDPC 码提升方案，在保持常数阈值的同时以并行局部解码实现指数级误差抑制，且可在原码基础上进一步放大误差抑制。

Abstract: We first provide a simple proof showing that any single-shot code is self-correcting. We then show that any $[[n,k]]$ CSS quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) code can be transformed into an $[[nm,k]]$ CSS QLDPC code with single-shot (SS) quantum error correction and an efficient local decoder which runs in $O(\log m)$ parallel time. Below a constant threshold $p_{\rm RG}>0$ for joint local stochastic physical and measurement noise, the logical failure probability over $T$ correction rounds is bounded by \begin{equation*}
  O(T n)\exp[-Ω(m^α)] \end{equation*} for a constant $α>0$; we say that the \textit{threshold} is $p_{\rm RG}$ and \textit{error suppression} is $Ω(m^α)$. Moreover, if the input code is equipped with a single-shot decoder with threshold $p_c>0$ and error suppression $Ω(n^β)$, then the output code \textit{enhances} the error suppression to $Ω(m^α n^β)$, while maintaining the decoder-independent threshold $p_{\rm RG}$, and thus \textit{enhanced} if $p_c < p_{\rm RG}$.

</details>


### [106] [Near-Optimal Ground-State Preparation without Controlled Hamiltonian Evolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00520)
*Dhrumil Patel,Steven T. Flammia,Raul Garcia-Patron*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究仅通过无受控哈密顿量演化访问模型下的基态制备、能量估计与性质估计问题，提出"谱下降原理"框架，给出两种算法并证明其复杂度达到已知下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基态制备算法依赖块编码或受控哈密顿量演化达到近最优复杂度，但尚不清楚受控演化是否必要，或能否在仅有无受控演化访问的较弱模型下达到相同标度，该问题此前未解决。

Method: 仅通过查询 $U_H$ 及其逆 $U_H^\dagger$ 访问哈密顿量 $H$。提出"谱下降原理"：算法从输入态 $|\psi\rangle$ 出发，沿能谱逐步下降逼近基态直至达到目标保真度。第一种算法使用输入态的新鲜副本；第二种利用输入态制备酉及其逆的相干访问以改进复杂度。并将框架推广至基态性质和能量估计。

Result: 设输入态基态权重至少 $\eta$、谱隙至少 $\gamma$、$\|H\|\le\alpha_H$、$\tau=\Theta(1/\alpha_H)$，第一种算法需 $\widetilde O(\alpha_H/(\gamma\eta))$ 次 $U_H$ 与 $U_H^\dagger$ 查询；第二种改进为 $\widetilde O(\alpha_H/(\gamma\sqrt\eta))$，与已知下界仅差对数因子。同时给出性质估计与能量估计的显式复杂度保证。

Conclusion: 受控哈密顿量演化访问对基态制备的查询复杂度无法带来超越对数因子的改进，即无受控演化已足以达到近最优标度；谱下降原理为基态制备、性质估计与能量估计提供了统一且简洁的算法框架。

Abstract: Ground-state preparation, energy estimation, and property estimation are fundamental tasks in quantum simulation. Existing ground-state preparation algorithms achieve near-optimal complexity using block encodings or controlled Hamiltonian evolution, raising a natural question: is controlled Hamiltonian evolution necessary, or can the same scaling be attained in the weaker access model that provides access to only uncontrolled Hamiltonian evolution? We resolve this question by giving two algorithms that access the input Hamiltonian $H$ only through queries to $U_H$ and its inverse $U_H^\dagger$, where $\|H\|\leα_H$ and $τ=Θ(1/α_H)$. Given an input state with ground-state weight at least $η$ and a spectral gap of $H$ of at least $γ$, our first algorithm uses fresh copies of the input state and requires $\widetilde O(α_H/(γη))$ queries to $U_H$ and $U_H^\dagger$, while our second algorithm uses coherent access to the input-state preparation unitary and its inverse to improve this complexity to $\widetilde O(α_H/(γ\sqrtη))$. The latter bound matches the known lower bound up to logarithmic factors, showing that algorithms with additional access to controlled Hamiltonian evolution cannot improve the query complexity for ground-state preparation beyond logarithmic factors. Both algorithms are based on a simple mechanism that we call the spectral descent principle, in which the algorithm starts from the input state $|ψ\rangle$ and progressively descends through the energy spectrum, moving closer to the ground state until the desired fidelity is reached. We also extend this framework to ground-state property and energy estimation and give explicit complexity guarantees for both tasks.

</details>


### [107] [First-quantized quantum simulation with non-local potentials by matrix-product-state encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00521)
*Kazuki Tsuoka,Taichi Kosugi,Masari Watanabe,Hirofumi Nishi,Yu-ichiro Matsushita*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用矩阵乘积态（MPS）编码高效实现第一量子化模拟中的非局域赝势，并给出一维氢原子超短激光脉冲电离模型的验证结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一量子化量子模拟相比第二量子化具有更低的空间与门复杂度，而赝势可进一步减少模拟电子数、降低门需求，但赝势的非局域性质使其在量子线路中难以实现，因此需要一种高效实现非局域势的方法。

Method: 采用矩阵乘积态（MPS）编码来高效实现非局域势，并据此构造Trotter步算子；其线路深度为O(deg(V_loc)·n^{deg(V_loc)} + 8L·n_atom·χ² + 2n²)，其中deg(V_loc)为多项式局域势的阶数，n_atom为原子数，L为所有原子中投影函数的最大数目，χ为MPS键维数，n为一维空间二值编码波函数的量子比特数。

Result: 将该方法应用于一维氢原子在超短激光脉冲驱动下的电离模型，结果表明MPS编码相比直接幺正分解可显著降低所需线路深度。

Conclusion: MPS编码为在量子线路中实现非局域赝势提供了高效途径，可显著降低第一量子化模拟的线路深度，为实际可行的第一量子化量子模拟铺平道路。

Abstract: Quantum simulations based on first-quantized approaches can offer lower space and gate complexities than second-quantized approaches. The gate requirements for these simulations can be further reduced by employing pseudopotentials, a technique widely used in classical quantum chemistry calculations to decrease the number of simulated electrons. However, implementing pseudopotentials in quantum circuits remains challenging due to their non-local nature. In this study, we proposed a method to efficiently implement non-local potentials using matrix product state (MPS) encoding. MPS encoding allows us to efficiently implement a Trotter step operator with a circuit depth of $O(\mathrm{deg}(V_{\mathrm{loc}}) n^{\mathrm{deg}(V_{\mathrm{loc}})} + 8Lnn_{\mathrm{atom}}χ^2 + 2n^2)$, where $\mathrm{deg}(V_{\mathrm{loc}})$ is the degree of the polynomial local potential, $n_{\mathrm{atom}}$ is the number of atoms in the system, $L$ is the maximum number of projector functions across all atoms, and $n$ is the number of qubits used to represent the binary-encoded wave function in one-dimensional space. We demonstrated the effectiveness of this approach by applying it to a model of the ionization of a one-dimensional hydrogen atom driven by an ultrashort laser pulse. Our results showed that MPS encoding significantly reduces the required circuit depth compared to a direct unitary decomposition, providing a pathway for practical first-quantized simulations.

</details>


### [108] [Good Quantum Locally Testable Codes from Lossless Cubical Complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00525)
*Itay Cohen,Itai Leigh,Assaf Reiner,Amnon Ta-Shma,Elad Tzalik*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展高维"无损扩张"方法，提出"层级链复形"并证明其局部到全局定理：方向图中的一维无损扩张可推出全局层级复形的小集上边界扩张，进而由足够不平衡的双侧无损四维立方复形得到渐近好量子局部可测码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sipser与Spielman从二部谱扩张图或单侧无损扩张图构造LDPC码；高维情形下谱扩张在Dinur等及Panteleev与Kalachev的渐近好经典LTC与qLDPC构造中居核心地位，Lin与Hsieh则用二维无损立方复形构造LTC与qLDPC码。作者欲发展高维无损扩张这一替代路线。

Method: 不直接构造所需的高维无损立方复形，而是研究其存在性蕴含什么。为高维立方复形关联一个"层级链复形"，其链群支撑在布尔立方体的层级集（而非胞腔）上；核心技术贡献是给出该结构的局部到全局定理：方向图中适当的一维无损扩张蕴含全局层级复形的小集上边界扩张。

Result: 证明局部到全局定理；作为推论，足够不平衡的双侧无损四维立方复形可产生渐近好量子局部可测码（qLTC）。

Conclusion: 建立了高维无损扩张的局部到全局原理，将一维无损扩张与全局层级复形的小集上边界扩张联系起来，并由此连接四维无损立方复形与渐近好qLTC；作者预计该局部到全局原理有进一步应用。

Abstract: Sipser and Spielman constructed LDPC codes from either bipartite \emph{spectral} expanders or one-sided \emph{lossless} expanders. In higher dimensions, \emph{spectral} expansion similarly played a central role in the constructions of asymptotically good classical LTCs and qLDPC codes by Dinur, Evra, Livne, Lubotzky, and Mozes and by Panteleev and Kalachev. Alternatively, Lin and Hsieh constructed classical LTCs and qLDPC codes from two-dimensional \emph{lossless} cubical complexes.
  In this work we develop the higher-dimensional \emph{lossless} approach. We do not construct the required high-dimensional lossless cubical complexes; rather, we investigate what their existence would imply. We associate with a high-dimensional cubical complex a \emph{level chain complex}, whose chain groups are supported on the level sets of the Boolean cube rather than on its cells. Our main technical contribution is a clean local-to-global theorem for this structure: suitable one-dimensional lossless expansion in the directional graphs implies small-set coboundary expansion of the global level complex. As a consequence, sufficiently imbalanced, two-sided lossless four-dimensional cubical complexes give rise to asymptotically good quantum locally testable codes. We expect the local-to-global principle developed here to have further applications.

</details>


### [109] [Unifying and Extending Strong Simulation of Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00547)
*Floris Geerts,Rihan Hai,Matthias Lanzinger,Reinhard Pichler,Emanuel Sallinger,Daniel Unterberger*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将功能聚合查询（FAQ）确立为量子电路精确经典模拟的统一语言，通过改变因子表示与求值方案，在同一InsideOut框架内恢复并推广了张量网络、相位多项式等多类可模拟性保证，并提出了新的"张量布局对称宽度"判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电路经典模拟存在多条相互独立的技术路线（如树宽、团宽度、Clifford仿真等），其可模拟性来源各不相同，缺乏统一的理论与算法基础。本文旨在用一个统一语言刻画"是什么使模拟变得可行"，从而系统性地整合并扩展已有的结构性、代数性可模拟性结论。

Method: 将电路模拟表述为和-积查询（FAQ）：因子编码门，内部线变量被聚合，自由边界变量索引转移振幅。核心思想是在统一的InsideOut求值框架下同时利用不同的可处理性来源——查询规定"算什么"，而求值计划、半环以及中间因子的表示方式决定代价。通过选择显式因子表示或压缩因子表示、选择相应半环，可导出不同保证。

Result: （1）显式因子表示下，FAQ求值恢复张量网络收缩的树宽界，并经由分数覆盖得到更精细的稀疏性敏感界；（2）在形式化相位半环上，压缩中间因子恢复基于秩宽的模拟（适用于兼容的二次相位表示）；（3）对Clifford电路，仿射-二次因子在乘法与边缘化下封闭且规模保持多项式，从而在无任何有界宽度假设下实现多项式时间精确振幅计算；（4）提出新的可处理性判据——张量布局对称宽度，结合局部割秩与分离子对称性，给出构造性求值界，并构造出对称宽度有界但相位图秩宽与线路图树宽均无界的电路族。

Conclusion: 表示感知的FAQ求值构成经典模拟的共同算法基础，并提供了系统化发现新可处理机制（如张量布局对称宽度）的路径。

Abstract: We establish functional aggregate queries (FAQs) as a unifying language for exact classical simulation of quantum circuits. A circuit becomes a sum-product query: factors encode gates, internal wire variables are aggregated, and free boundary variables index transition amplitudes. The central insight is that distinct sources of simulation tractability can be exploited within the same InsideOut evaluation scheme. The query specifies what is computed; the evaluation plan, semiring, and representation of intermediate factors determine the cost.
  This view unifies structural and algebraic simulation guarantees. With explicit factor representations, FAQ evaluation recovers the treewidth bound for tensor-network contraction and yields finer sparsity-sensitive bounds via fractional covers. Over a formal phase semiring, compressed intermediate factors recover rank-width-based simulation for compatible quadratic phase representations. For Clifford circuits, affine-quadratic factors are closed under multiplication and marginalization and remain polynomial in size, yielding polynomial-time exact amplitude computation without any bounded-width assumption.
  Beyond these recoveries, the framework yields a new tractability criterion: tensor layout symmetry width. This parameter combines local cut-rank with separator symmetry through exact tree-tensor representations. We give a constructive evaluation bound and exhibit a circuit family with bounded tensor layout symmetry width but unbounded phase-graph rank-width and circuit line-graph treewidth. These results establish representation-aware FAQ evaluation as a common algorithmic foundation for classical simulation and a systematic route to new tractable regimes.

</details>


### [110] [A polynomial-time classical sampler for noisy quantum circuits from statistical mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00548)
*Jon Nelson,Joel Rajakumar,Chao Yin,Yifan F. Zhang,Michael J. Gullans*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：对含单量子比特退极化噪声的几何局域幺正电路，当深度达到 Ω(p^{-1} log p^{-1}) 时，即可在多项式时间内经典近似采样，突破了以往电路深度需随系统规模对数增长的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对通用电路的经典采样算法要求电路深度随系统规模对数增长，才能让噪声将全局输出态推向平凡态。本文旨在证明噪声的局部累积在系统规模无关的深度下就已足够，从而更精确地刻画量子优势的经典模拟界限。

Method: 将电路输出态映射为统计力学中的聚合物模型（polymer model），并耦合收敛的集团展开（cluster expansion）与退极化信道的超压缩性（hypercontractivity）。

Result: 证明任何由幺正操作（unital operations）构成的几何局域电路，在每层后插入强度为 p 的单量子比特退极化噪声，深度达到 Ω(p^{-1} log p^{-1}) 后，均可由多项式时间经典计算机近似采样。

Conclusion: 噪声的局部累积在独立于系统规模的深度下即足以使经典模拟可行，给出了量子优势边界更紧的刻画。

Abstract: Developing classical simulation algorithms for noisy quantum circuits is essential to delineating the limits of quantum advantage. Existing classical sampling approaches for general circuits require circuit depths to grow logarithmically with system size, so that noise drives the global output state close to a trivial state. Here we show that local accumulation of noise at a depth independent of system size is already sufficient. Specifically, we prove that any geometrically local circuit composed of unital operations and interspersed with single-qubit depolarizing noise of strength $p$ after $Ω(p^{-1} \log p^{-1})$ depth can be approximately sampled from a polynomial-time classical computer. Our proof maps the output state of the circuit to a polymer model in statistical mechanics, and combines a convergent cluster expansion with hypercontractivity of the depolarizing channel.

</details>


### [111] [Optimal two-mode bosonic loss codes from finite group symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00561)
*Argyris Giannisis Manes,Mahadevan Subramanian,Liang Jiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究双玻色模式下固定总光子数编码的量子纠错，发现最优编解码器具有有限群结构，给出有限群不变码的Knill-Laflamme充要条件，并构造出达到最大损耗距离的解析码族，覆盖并超越数值搜索的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子损耗是玻色量子硬件（如超导腔）中的主要噪声。固定总光子数的双模编码兼具双轨比特的损耗探测优势与纠错能力，但如何在任意编解码器下最优地提升纠缠保真度、并理解最优码的代数结构尚不清楚。

Method: 在4≤n≤25范围内对任意编码器和解码器优化纠缠保真度，观察到所有最优码具有有限群结构；据此推导任意有限群不变码的Knill-Laflamme充要条件，将构造约化为表示重数空间上的方程；对SU(2)的每个有限子群和每个张量秩，显式构造施加全部损耗纠错约束所需的最少算符数；并用MacWilliams-Farkas证书验证损耗距离。

Result: 最优码中11个对应二维不可约表示、11个对应可约表示。对22个扇区中的21个，证书证明码达到最大可能损耗距离，且距离上界对总光子数单调。识别出光子数n_d=⌈(3d²+1)/4⌉的二元多面体序列，构造到距离d=10；(n,d)=(28,6),(49,8),(76,10)为相应距离下已知最小n；d≤9的所有成员均达到固定n的LP距离界。

Conclusion: 这些解析构造、距离证书与对称性约化的约束计数共同支持存在一个无限码族，猜想其在最小光子数下可达任意损耗距离。

Abstract: Photon loss is a dominant noise process in bosonic quantum hardware, including superconducting cavities. Fixed-total-photon-number qubit encodings in two bosonic modes retain the loss-detection advantage of dual-rail qubits while supporting photon loss correction. Optimizing entanglement fidelity in this setting for $4\leq n\leq25$ over arbitrary encoders and decoders reveals finite-group structure in every best-found code: 11 correspond to two-dimensional irreducible representations and 11 to reducible ones. Motivated by this emergence, we derive the necessary-and-sufficient Knill-Laflamme conditions for arbitrary finite-group-invariant codes, reducing their construction to equations on representation multiplicity spaces. For every finite subgroup of $SU(2)$ and every tensor rank, we explicitly construct the minimum number of operators required to impose all corresponding loss-correction constraints. These results produce analytic counterparts to most numerical codes and predict constructions missed by the initial search. Exact MacWilliams-Farkas certificates show that our codes achieve the maximum possible loss distance in 21 of the 22 sectors, and we prove that the resulting distance bound is monotone in total photon number. Beyond the scan, we identify a binary-polyhedral sequence with photon number $n_d=\lceil(3d^2+1)/4\rceil$ and construct each corresponding code through distance $d=10$. To our knowledge, the constructed $(n,d)=(28,6),(49,8),(76,10)$ codes give the smallest reported $n$ for their respective distances; all members through $d=9$ attain the fixed-$n$ LP distance bound. Together, these constructions, certificates, and a symmetry-reduced constraint count provide evidence for an infinite code family conjectured to attain every distance at the minimum photon number.

</details>


### [112] [Characterizing unitaries via quasi-process functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00579)
*Nasra Daher Ahmed,Ravi Kunjwal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明任意n量子比特酉算子的受控单量子比特分解可自然定义一个准过程函数，从而给出酉算子的标准形式qPFU。基于此，作者提出MECC与Unambiguity两个充分非必要条件刻画过程函数酉算子（PFU），并研究其电路深度与量子因果性、无纠缠量子非局域性之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望将不定因果序研究中出现的过程函数概念与量子电路的受控分解联系起来，用因果性视角统一理解量子非局域性（QNLWE）、因果序与局域性的权衡，并为酉算子提供一种标准表示与电路深度分析工具。

Method: 首先证明任意n量子比特酉算子的受控单量子比特酉分解中的控制条件天然定义一个准过程函数，从而得到qPFU表示；然后引入MECC（控制条件互斥）与Unambiguity两个充分条件来判定PFU；进一步证明二者互不蕴含，并将MECC酉算子的电路深度刻画为Hamming图上的团划分问题，由此给出电路深度界；最后利用MECC构造相关过程函数的酉纯化。

Result: 得到酉算子的标准qPFU表示；给出PFU的两个充分非必要判据MECC与Unambiguity，并证明二者互不蕴含；将MECC酉算子电路深度刻画为Hamming图团划分问题并给出n量子比特MECC酉算子的电路深度界；由MECC构造出过程函数的酉纯化，得到一类在量子因果性中感兴趣的酉过程。

Conclusion: 该因果性启发的酉算子表示可作为分析量子电路结构的通用工具，将量子非局域性、因果序与局域性权衡、过程函数及电路深度等概念统一在一个框架下，并有望推广到其他领域。

Abstract: It is well-known that every n-qubit unitary can be decomposed into a product of controlled single-qubit unitaries. We show that the control conditions in such a decomposition naturally define a quasi-process function, an object that originally came up in the study of indefinite causal order. Here quasi-process functions yield a representation of every n-qubit unitary in a canonical form, namely, as a quasi-process function unitary (qPFU). We use this representation to show how quantum nonlocality without entanglement (QNLWE) can be understood in terms of unitaries that encode the trade-off between causal order and locality noted in R. Kunjwal and Ä. Baumeler, arXiv:2202.00440. Specifically, we obtain two sufficient (but not necessary) criteria---mutual exclusivity of control conditions (MECC) and Unambiguity---for a unitary to be a process function unitary (PFU), i.e., it admits a qPFU representation where the quasi-process function is a process function, an object that can model paradox-free classical causal loops. We also show the inequivalence of these criteria, neither implying the other. We then show that the circuit depth of MECC unitaries admits a characterization in terms of clique-partitioning problems on Hamming graphs and use this correspondence to provide bounds on the circuit depth of n-qubit MECC unitaries. Finally, we show how MECC unitaries provide a recipe for constructing a unitary purification of the associated process function, thereby providing a large class of unitary processes of interest in quantum causality. The generality of this causality-inspired representation of unitaries makes it a versatile tool for analyzing the structure of quantum circuits in other domains.

</details>


### [113] [Quantum key distribution using generalized contextuality against post-quantum eavesdroppers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00595)
*Daniel Centeno,Roberto D. Baldijão,Maria Ciudad Alañón,Yujie Zhang,Pedro Lauand,Elie Wolfe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Bell非局域性的设备无关密钥认证思想推广到单系统prepare-and-measure场景下的广义语境性，构建对抗性模型证明密钥率可由有限线性规划求解，并指出语境性对正密钥率是必要但非充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bell非局域性允许在无需信任设备内部操作、甚至仅受无信号原理约束的窃听者存在时认证密钥。作者探究在单系统prepare-and-measure实验中可用的非经典性概念——广义语境性——能否扮演类似角色，从而实现更弱假设下的密钥认证。

Method: Alice与Bob先建立本地制备与测量程序间的操作等价关系，将其视为任何对通信信道的物理干预（含对手干预）都必须遵守的理论无关约束；Eve除须遵守这些等价关系外不受其他限制，其干预不假设由量子理论描述。在此对抗模型下，作者将Eve针对个体攻击的最优猜测概率刻画为有限线性规划的解，并据此由观测统计与操作等价关系导出最坏情况渐近密钥率。

Result: 证明语境性对正密钥率是必要的；但进一步证明其并不充分：已知可对量子窃听者产生密钥的(3,2)-奇偶无感随机存取码，对后量子窃听者却产生不出可认证的密钥率。反之，某些在无信号约束中无对应物的操作等价关系能够严格提升渐近密钥率，作者用带对齐密钥生成设置的CHSH协议的prepare-and-measure版加以说明。

Conclusion: 广义语境性可作为单系统下设备无关式密钥认证的资源，但语境性本身既不充分也不完全等同于无信号类约束的作用；操作等价关系的具体结构会显著影响可认证密钥率，为单系统抗后量子窃听者的密钥分发提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Bell nonlocality allows secret key to be certified without trusting the internal operation of the devices, and even against eavesdroppers constrained only by the no-signaling principle. We ask whether generalized contextuality, a notion of nonclassicality available in prepare-and-measure experiments on a single system, can play an analogous role. In our approach, Alice and Bob first establish operational equivalences among their local preparation and measurement procedures, which are then treated as theory-independent constraints that any physical intervention on the communication channel, including that of an adversary, must respect. Eve is otherwise unrestricted, that is, her intervention on the transmitted system is not assumed to be described by quantum theory. Within this adversarial model, we show that Eve's optimal guessing probability against individual attacks is the solution of a finite linear program, and hence that a worst-case asymptotic key rate follows from the observed statistics and the operational equivalences alone. We prove that contextuality is necessary for a positive key rate. However, we also show that it is not sufficient. Concretely, the (3,2)-parity-oblivious random access code, known to yield secret key against a quantum eavesdropper, yields none certifiable key rate against a post-quantum one. Conversely, operational equivalences with no analogue among no-signaling constraints can strictly increase the asymptotic key rate. We illustrate this with the prepare-and-measure version of the standard CHSH protocol with an aligned key-generating setting.

</details>


### [114] [Late-Time Power Laws in Fluorescence Decays: Quantum Decay or Alternative Mechanisms?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00603)
*Francesco Giacosa,Anna Kolbus,Krzysztof Kyziol,Magdalena Plodowska,Milena Piotrowska,Karol Szary,Arthur Vereijken*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了量子力学与非量子（非广延统计、连续寿命分布、能量迁移）模型对时间分辨荧光光谱中长时幂律衰变的解释，发现后者的参数化无法重现量子描述预期的指数到幂律的锐利转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不稳定系统的生存概率P(t)在长时行为上预期出现幂律，但复杂系统中非广延统计、连续寿命分布及荧光能量迁移等模型也能产生类似效应。作者旨在通过实验数据判别这些竞争性解释，澄清长时幂律的真正来源。

Method: 以赤藓红B（erythrosine B）和伊红Y（eosine Y）为荧光团，开展时间分辨荧光光谱实验；将量子力学描述与特定的非广延统计、连续寿命分布及能量迁移参数化模型对同一数据进行拟合与对比，考察能否复现指数衰减到幂律的锐利转变。

Result: 拟合结果显示，所采用的非量子和能量迁移参数化无法再现量子力学描述预期的关键特征——从指数衰减到幂律的锐利转变。

Conclusion: 在所考察的实验体系和参数化范围内，非量子模型难以解释观测到的长时幂律行为，量子力学框架对指数-幂律转变的描述更具说服力。

Abstract: In quantum mechanics, the properties of an unstable system are closely related to its corresponding energy distribution. One of the predictions of the theory is the emergence of a late-time power law in the survival probability $P(t)$ of the unstable state. However, in complex systems, similar effects may emerge due to other phenomena. This is the case for nonextensive statistical models, for some continuous lifetime distribution models, and also for certain energy-migration models applied to fluorescence. In this work, these alternative interpretations are confronted with data from time-resolved fluorescence spectroscopy experiments with erythrosine B and eosine Y as fluorophores. It turns out that some of the features expected within the quantum-mechanical description, such as the sharp transition from the exponential to the power-law regime, cannot be reproduced by the specific statistical and energy-migration parametrizations fitted in the analysis.

</details>


### [115] [Operator-language Feynman rules for driven-dissipative quantum systems: from mean field to non-Gaussian photon correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00667)
*Peter Ehlers,Phi Hung Nguyen,Daniel Soh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在量子光学的算符语言下为Lindblad主方程构建Feynman规则，将微扰展开分为运动学部分（Wick序本征算符上的移位代数与顶点表）与动力学部分（仅通过未扰生成元的预解式进入），并应用于驱动Kerr腔、强驱动二能级原子、Kerr环与链以及计数场倾斜生成元等情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为开放量子系统（Lindblad主方程）提供一个系统、可计算且能超越Gaussian近似与常规累积量闭合的微扰框架，以便处理非Gaussian关联（如三光子关联）、光子计数累积量及多体（环/链）体系，并在强驱动与强耦合区域获得高精度且成本可控的结果。

Method: 构建Lindblad主方程的Feynman规则：定义一套固定的基本算符移动代数与闭式传播子，围绕可精确求解（通常为Gaussian）的生成元做微扰论。展开分为两部分：(1) 运动学部分，在Wick序本征算符O_mn=:(c†)^m c^n:上的移位代数，据此为给定相互作用组装顶点表；(2) 动力学部分，仅通过未扰生成元L_0的预解式进入，对任意稳定、可对角化的对角Gaussian L_0（含热库、压缩库、关联衰减）以及对二能级发射体（驱动或未驱动）均为对角且可加。方法进一步将规则扩展至计数场倾斜的生成元。

Result: (1) 驱动Kerr腔中圈展开即半经典展开，级数为渐近级数。(2) 强驱动二能级原子中，缀饰线以一个深达饱和区的精确结果替代有限收敛半径的级数，每个Mollow分量对应一条线。(3) Kerr环与链在固定微扰阶下代价随格点数呈多项式增长。(4) 三格点环上，图形恢复了Gaussian累积量闭合所缺失的三光子关联的非Gaussian部分；展开至十阶时，在U≤0.25κ下给出所比较方法中最小的g^(3)误差，且代价低于四阶闭合，而三阶与四阶闭合在中等耦合下更准确。(5) 同一套规则扩展至计数场倾斜生成元后，可给出无序八格点链的光子计数累积量，超越直接对角化能力。(6) 量子跳跃轨迹在三种耦合下与上述结果在1.6个标准误内一致，在最强耦合下在一个标准误内，此时第三累积量高出Gaussian值六个标准误。

Conclusion: 该Feynman规则框架为Lindblad主方程提供了统一、高效且可系统扩展的微扰工具，能够精确刻画非Gaussian效应与光子计数统计，在多体与强驱动regime下以多项式代价取得优于现有闭合方法的结果，并与量子跳跃轨迹数值一致，展现了良好的准确性与实用性。

Abstract: We construct Feynman rules for Lindblad master equations in the operator language of quantum optics: a fixed algebra of elementary operator moves and closed-form propagators for perturbation theory about an exactly solvable, in most cases Gaussian, generator. The expansion separates into a kinematic part (the shift algebra on the Wick-ordered eigenoperators $O_{mn}=:(c^\dagger)^m c^n:$, from which the vertex table for a specified interaction is assembled) and a dynamical part that enters only through the resolvent of the unperturbed generator, diagonal and additive for any stable, diagonalizable Gaussian $L_0$, including thermal and squeezed reservoirs and correlated decay, and equally available for a two-level emitter, driven or undriven. In the driven Kerr cavity the loop expansion is the semiclassical expansion and the series is asymptotic. In a strongly driven two-level atom, dressed lines replace a series of finite radius by one accurate deep into saturation, one line per Mollow component. For Kerr rings and chains the cost is polynomial in the number of sites at fixed perturbative order. On a three-site ring the diagrams recover the non-Gaussian part of the three-photon correlation that the Gaussian cumulant closure lacks; carried to tenth order they give the smallest $g^{(3)}$ error of the methods compared for $U\leq0.25κ$ at a cost below that of a fourth-order closure, and third- and fourth-order closures are more accurate at moderate coupling. The same rules, extended to the counting-field-tilted generator, give the photon-counting cumulants of a disordered eight-site chain beyond direct diagonalization; quantum-jump trajectories at three couplings agree with them to within $1.6$ standard errors, within one at the strongest coupling, where they place the third cumulant six standard errors above the Gaussian value.

</details>


### [116] [Quantum Circuit Pruning: From NISQ Architectures to Fault-Tolerant Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00669)
*Pau Escofet,Carmen G. Almudéver,Sergi Abadal,Eduard Alarcón*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种路由感知的量子线路剪枝策略，通过比较省略参数化双量子比特门的保真度下界与其路由执行成本，选择性删除低贡献门，在NISQ与FTQC场景下均实现门数减少与保真度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子线路剪枝多关注门的计算贡献，忽视了路由执行带来的保真度开销。作者希望将"该门是否值得执行"的判断与硬件路由成本结合，并统一适用于NISQ与容错量子计算两种架构。

Method: 对线路中每个参数化双量子比特门，将架构无关的"省略该门所导致的保真度损失下界"与硬件相关的路由执行成本模型进行比较，据此决定是否剪枝；并给出剪枝边界随旋转角度、比特距离和硬件噪声变化的解析刻画。分别在NISQ与FTQC架构下进行建模和评估。

Result: 在基准线路仿真中，NISQ场景下门数最多减少48.6%，保真度最多提升47.7%；FT硬件可行性分析表明，在不同码距与物理错误率范围内，剪枝仍然有效。

Conclusion: 路由感知剪枝是一种跨NISQ与FTQC的实用统一编译策略，对量子计算全栈具有推广价值。

Abstract: We present a routing-aware pruning strategy for quantum circuits that selectively removes parametric two-qubit gates whose computational contribution is outweighed by the fidelity cost of their execution, and assess it across both Noisy Intermediate-Scale Quantum (NISQ) and fault-tolerant quantum computing (FTQC) architectures. Each two-qubit gate of a circuit is evaluated by comparing an architecture-independent lower bound on the fidelity cost of omitting the gate against a hardware-specific model for the routing execution cost in each setting. We additionally provide an analytical characterization of the pruning boundary as a function of rotation angle, qubit distance, and hardware noise. Simulations on benchmark circuits demonstrate gate count reductions of up to 48.6% and fidelity improvements of up to 47.7% in the NISQ regime, while a feasibility analysis on FT hardware indicates that pruning is expected to remain relevant across a range of code distances and physical error rates. These results establish routing-aware pruning as a practical and unified compilation strategy across the quantum computing stack.

</details>


### [117] [Beyond Feasibility: Finite-Depth Accessibility in Constrained QAOA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00736)
*Rushikesh Ubale,Gregory T. Byrd,Yasar Mulani,Sangram Deshpande*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究受约束QAOA在实际执行电路（指定可行初态、有限深度、有限混合器相互作用时序）层面的可达性，定义有限深度可达集及其目标上限和归一化可达质量诊断量Q_R，并通过资源匹配的块局部XY混合器实验证明Q_R与浅层QAOA实际性能关联最强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束保持混合器虽能将QAOA限制在可行子空间内，但可行性与全局混合器连通性并不能决定某一浅层电路实际可获得哪些可行构型，因此需要从实际执行电路层面刻画有限深度可达性。

Method: 定义有限深度可达集R_p(M,x_0)，证明理想QAOA电路的计算基支撑包含于其中；据此给出参数无关的目标上限f_R*与归一化可达质量诊断量Q_R，将可达体积与其中目标质量分离。采用资源匹配的块局部XY混合器时序，在约束组合优化与合成块约束二次问题上评估，并进行仅编译层面的资源分析。

Result: 在207个有利尾部CVaR QAOA配置中，Q_R与实现的浅层QAOA性能关联最强，Pearson与Spearman相关系数分别为0.7003和0.8057，且在受控与依赖感知分析下保持正向；该区分在可行初始化、组合规模与目标族变化下依然存在。编译研究表明，对稠密组合哈密顿量，相位分离器路由主导编译后的双量子比特资源规模，而编译双量子比特成本相近的混合器时序可呈现显著不同的有限深度可达质量。

Conclusion: 目标相关的有限深度可达性可作为资源受限约束QAOA的补充诊断量，弥补仅靠可行性与混合器连通性无法区分浅层电路可获得构型的不足。

Abstract: Constraint-preserving mixers keep QAOA within a feasible subspace, but feasibility and global mixer connectivity do not determine which feasible configurations are available to a particular shallow circuit. We study constrained QAOA at the level of the circuit actually executed: a specified feasible initial state, finite depth, and finite schedule of mixer interactions. We define the finite-depth accessible set $R_p(M,x_0)$ and show that the computational-basis support of the ideal QAOA circuit is contained within it. This gives a parameter-independent objective ceiling $f_R^\star=\max_{x\in R_p}f(x)$ and a normalized reachable-quality diagnostic $Q_R$, separating accessible volume from the objective quality contained within it.
  We evaluate this framework using resource-matched block-local XY mixer schedules for constrained portfolio optimization and synthetic block-constrained quadratic problems. Across 207 favorable-tail CVaR QAOA configurations, $Q_R$ shows the strongest association with realized shallow-QAOA performance among the structural diagnostics considered, with Pearson and Spearman correlations of $0.7003$ and $0.8057$; the relationship remains positive under controlled and dependence-aware analyses. The distinction persists across changes in feasible initialization, portfolio size, and objective family.
  A compilation-only study further shows that, for the dense portfolio Hamiltonians considered here, phase-separator routing dominates the compiled two-qubit resource scale, while mixer schedules with comparable compiled two-qubit cost can exhibit markedly different finite-depth accessible quality. These results motivate objective-relevant finite-depth accessibility as a complementary diagnostic for resource-bounded constrained QAOA.

</details>


### [118] [Negative quasiprobabilities redistribute (and complex ones reduce) the information in unabsorbed photons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00740)
*Jonte R. Hance*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文检验了"Kirkwood–Dirac准概率负性带来量子优势"这一说法，针对交互自由测量（IFM）的原始目标——在尽量少吸收光的前提下获取样品信息。结果表明：单光子单次通过任意干涉仪时，每吸收一个光子所获透射率Fisher信息不超过直接透射，负性无本质作用；真正的优势来自相位响应或"无物体时全暗"的输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kirkwood–Dirac（KD）准概率分布的负性常被认为是交互自由测量和弱后选择测量量子优势的来源。作者希望以IFM最初设计的目标（让样品尽量少吸收光以获取其信息）为基准，严格检验负性是否真正带来优势。

Method: 利用路径与输出构成的Kirkwood–Dirac分布刻画弱样品下的线性响应：实部对应吸收、虚部对应相位。对纯单光子单次通过任意干涉仪的情形，计算每吸收一个光子关于透过率（transmittance）的Fisher信息，并与直接透射（无干涉仪）情形比较；同时分析虚部对输出基的依赖，以及临界耦合谐振腔等多程方案。

Result: 对纯单光子单次通过任意干涉仪，每吸收一个光子的Fisher信息等于直接透射值减去各虚部平方和，即任何干涉仪与读出方案都无法超越直接透射；负性只起把信息集中到弱照明输出上的作用。KD分布的虚部仅依赖输出基而非样品，故总存在一个基使所有项为实，且该基即最优。单光子只有在通过相位响应（需厚样品或非均匀展宽样品，或多次通过且不计数吸收，如临界耦合谐振腔）时才能提升每吸收光子的信息。探测弱吸收体时，负性只提供单次测量的优势，且不随试验次数累积；优势累积出现在"无物体时全暗"的输出情形，而这种情形不需要负性。

Conclusion: Kirkwood–Dirac负性与IFM/后选择测量的量子优势之间并无必然因果联系。对于弱吸收探测，负性仅带来不累积的单次优势；真正可累积的优势源于无物体时全暗的输出，这与负性无关。该工作澄清了负性在量子度量中的实际作用。

Abstract: Negative Kirkwood--Dirac quasiprobabilities are credited with the quantum advantage of interaction-free measurement and postselected metrology. We test that claim on the task interaction-free measurement was designed for: learning about a sample letting it absorb as little light as possible. The Kirkwood--Dirac distribution of path and output gives the linear response to a weak sample: its real part responds to absorption, its imaginary part to a phase. For a pure single photon passing once through any interferometer, the Fisher information about transmittance per absorbed photon is the direct-transmission value minus a sum of squared imaginary parts: no interferometer or readout beats direct transmission. Negativity plays no role beyond concentrating information in weakly lit outputs. The imaginary parts of the Kirkwood-Dirac distribution depend on the output basis here, not on the sample, so a basis exists in which every term is real, which is optimal. A single photon gains information per absorbed photon only through a phase response, which for an optical line needs a thick or inhomogeneously broadened sample, or through many passes when absorptions go uncounted, as shown by a critically coupled resonator. For detecting a weak absorber, negativity gives a single-shot advantage that does not accumulate over multiple trials; advantage instead accumulates when there are outputs which are dark without the object, a case which needs no negativity.

</details>


### [119] [esQueranto: Differentiable Structured Quantum Light for Automated Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00750)
*Marcello Armezzani,Tareq Jaouni,Pontus Lindgren,Sören Arlt,Mario Krenn,Xuemei Gu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出 esQueranto，一个基于 JAX 的可微分仿真器，将光子数量子光学与结构光传播统一描述，支持自动微分与硬件加速，用于自动化实验设计与光学仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来实验设计主要依赖人工；随着AI与计算方法介入实验设计，需要足够表达力的仿真器来表征多样物理过程及其耦合，尤其是量子光学与结构光传播的联合模拟。

Method: 采用 JAX 实现 esQueranto，将光子数量子光学与结构光传播纳入统一的可微分框架，使光的空间演化直接影响非经典量子态及其干涉；利用自动微分与硬件加速支持优化和自动化搜索。

Result: 在广泛的量子光学应用中验证了该框架，涵盖其物理描述中互补的多个方面。

Conclusion: 将量子物理与结构光物理集成于单一可微分仿真器，向构建物理学基础性仿真器的目标迈出重要一步。

Abstract: Uncovering new phenomena in nature requires experiments. For centuries, their design has been exclusively a human endeavor. Today, a new paradigm is emerging in which artificial intelligence and computational methods can design experiments themselves. Realizing this form of automated scientific discovery requires simulators that are sufficiently expressive to represent the diverse physical processes and couplings from which new experiments can be constructed. In this direction, we introduce \textsc{esQueranto}, a differentiable software that brings photon-number quantum optics and structured-light propagation into a common description, allowing the spatial evolution of light to directly influence non-classical quantum states and their interference. \textsc{esQueranto} is implemented in JAX, providing automatic differentiation and hardware-accelerated evaluation for optimization and automated search. We demonstrate the framework across a broad range of quantum-optical applications that exercise complementary aspects of its physical description. By combining quantum and structured-light physics within a single differentiable simulator, \textsc{esQueranto} takes an important step towards the dream of a foundational simulator in physics.

</details>


### [120] [Real-Time Adaptive Filtering and the Boxcar Limit in Superconducting Qubit Readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00783)
*Hans Johnson,Tanay Roy,Leonardo Bove,David van Zanten,Silvia Zorzetti,Jafar Saniie*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在超导量子比特色散读出中，文章系统评估了实时LMS自适应FIR滤波、逐样本加权与序列检测相对经典boxcar平均的增益，发现波形去噪不必然带来判别保真度提升，主要增益来自读出窗口的起点与时长优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: boxcar均值为色散读出的事实基线，但数字后处理（自适应滤波、逐样本加权、序列检测）何时能真正提升保真度尚不清晰；作者希望区分"波形去噪"与"判别改进"两类效应。

Method: 在FPGA量子控制器上用LMS算法实时训练自适应FIR滤波器，并对测得的读出shot做离线回放分析；与boxcar平均对比，同时测试固定FIR、逐样本加权以及序列检测（含匹配滤波模板），覆盖2D transmon（Q1）与3D ancilla（Q2）两个工作点。

Result: 固定FIR滤波后的求和等价于boxcar和乘一个复数（kernel collapse），信号与噪声等比缩放，故存在boxcar极限、判别无法改善；冻结LMS滤波降低trace噪声但在测试读出长度下未带来可分辨的保真度提升，8 μs工作点处滤波shot仅保留boxcar判别SNR的99.4%。Q1上优化读出窗口起点与时长使对比度读出保真度提升+2.4%~+5.6%（四个读出长度）；逐样本加权在全窗口积分上再增最多+3.0%，但在8 μs调优窗口上仅再增+0.33%。Q2位于早决交叉点之上（Q1在其下），硬件扫描定出交叉窗口约1 μs；离线序列检测对多数shot提前判决，保真度与boxcar相差在0.5个百分点内；约30个带标签shot/类即可构建接近数百shot水平的匹配滤波模板。

Conclusion: 结果构成一份工作点记录，明确区分波形去噪与判别改进：滤波主要去除噪声而非提升判别能力，真正的保真度增益来自读出窗口调优及（调优后的）有限逐样本加权；早决序列检测在具备足够信噪裕度的ancilla工作点上有效。

Abstract: In dispersive readout of superconducting qubits, the common baseline is a boxcar averager: a uniformly weighted average over a fixed window followed by threshold-based state assignment. We ask when additional digital processing improves fidelity. We evaluated an adaptive finite-impulse-response (FIR) filter trained in real time by the least-mean-squares (LMS) algorithm on an FPGA quantum controller, and analyzed measured readout shots offline to compare the frozen filter with boxcar averaging and to test per-sample weighting and sequential detection.
  For the fixed FIR filters tested, summing the filtered samples gives the boxcar sum multiplied by one complex number, apart from a small edge correction (the kernel collapse). This scales state separation and noise equally, so discrimination cannot improve (the boxcar limit).
  The frozen LMS filter reduces trace noise without a resolved fidelity improvement at the tested readout lengths. Replayed on the same shots offline, each filtered shot carries 99.4% of the boxcar shot's discrimination signal-to-noise ratio at the 8 $μ$s operating point of the 2D transmon qubit (Q1).
  On Q1, optimizing the readout window's start and duration improves contrast readout fidelity by +2.4% to +5.6% across four readout lengths. Per-sample weighting adds up to +3.0% over full-window integration, and only +0.33% beyond the tuned window at 8 $μ$s.
  Remaining results are from a 3D ancilla qubit (Q2), which sits above the early-decision crossover that Q1 sits below. Hardware sweeps locate the crossover window near 1 $μ$s. Offline, the sequential test decided most shots early, with fidelity within 0.5 percentage points of the boxcar. About 30 labeled shots per class build a matched-filter template nearly as good as one from hundreds.
  The results form an operating-point record that separates waveform denoising from decision improvement.

</details>


### [121] [Remote state preparation of a single-spin state via hybrid spin-photon entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00832)
*Yunge Jiang,Yehan Yu,Lili Song,Yan Mu,Chaoyun Peng,Mingfeng Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于电子自旋与相干态光脉冲混合纠缠的远程态制备协议，通过辅助自旋耦合、零差探测和自旋投影测量规避非正交相干态叠加测量，仅需1比特经典通信即可确定性制备一类目标态，并解析量化了损耗与退相干对保真度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程态制备利用对目标态的先验知识降低量子态分发所需的通信资源。相比单光子编码，相干态编码天然容忍光子损耗——损耗仅衰减相干态振幅，导致制备保真度逐渐下降而非协议概率性失败。但传统协议需要投影到非正交相干态的叠加上，这一操作难以实现。

Method: 利用自旋腔系统中自旋相关反射产生的电子自旋—相干态光脉冲混合纠缠；将传输脉冲与辅助自旋耦合，随后进行零差探测与自旋投影测量，从而避免直接测量相干态叠加和光子数分辨探测。

Result: 理想极限下，仅用1比特经典通信即可确定性制备特定类目标态。解析量化了相干态非正交性、光纤损耗和自旋退相干对保真度的影响：增大相干态振幅提升态可区分度从而提高保真度，但同时增强光子损耗导致的支路信息泄漏，因此在每个传输距离上存在最优振幅。在实验相关参数下，优化后的平均保真度在数十公里距离上仍显著高于经典基准。

Conclusion: 该协议为基于相干态编码的远程态制备提供了可行方案，并讨论了利用金刚石氮-空位色心实现的可能性。

Abstract: Remote state preparation is a fundamental quantum-information protocol that exploits prior knowledge of a target state to reduce the communication resources required for quantum-state distribution. Here, we propose a remote-state-preparation protocol for a stationary electron-spin qubit using hybrid entanglement between the electron spin and a coherent-state light pulse, generated by spin-dependent reflection from a spin-cavity system. Unlike single-photon encodings, the coherent-state encoding is naturally tolerant of photon loss: a loss channel attenuates the coherent-state amplitude, leading to a gradual reduction in preparation fidelity rather than probabilistic protocol failure. The central difficulty---implementing the projection onto superpositions of nonorthogonal coherent states required in the conventional protocol---is circumvented by coupling the transmitted pulse to an auxiliary spin, followed by homodyne detection and a spin projective measurement. The protocol therefore avoids direct coherent-state-superposition measurements and photon-number-resolving detection. In the ideal limit, deterministic preparation of a particular class of target states can be achieved using only one bit of classical communication. We analytically quantify the effects of coherent-state nonorthogonality, fiber loss, and spin dephasing on the preparation fidelity. Increasing the coherent-state amplitude improves state distinguishability and hence the preparation fidelity, but also enhances which-branch information leakage caused by photon loss, resulting in an optimal amplitude at each transmission distance. For experimentally relevant parameters, the optimized average fidelity remains well above the classical benchmark over distances of tens of kilometers. We also discuss a possible implementation using diamond nitrogen-vacancy centers.

</details>


### [122] [Neural Fourier Surrogates for Data Reuploading Quantum Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00841)
*Oliver Knitter,Jonathan Mei,Sang Hyub Kim,Chi Chen,Masako Yamada,Martin Roetteler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 Neural Fourier Surrogates (NFS)，一种在同一有限傅里叶级数支撑上学习系数的经典随机神经网络架构，作为评估量子神经网络性能的自然经典基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习中经典与量子优势的确切边界尚不清晰。直接将 QNN 与现有经典模型比较存在根本困难：两者的函数类本质不同，难以提供对二者差异的普遍性洞见。因此需要一种与 QNN 处于相同函数空间、可直接对照的经典基线。

Method: 受 Neural Quantum States 与 Random Fourier Features 技术启发，设计 NFS：一种随机经典神经网络架构，在与 QNN 相同的有限傅里叶级数支撑上高效学习傅里叶系数。

Result: 在多个表格基准数据集上，NFS 是有效的分类器架构，整体上可与既有经典基线竞争，包括与之相当的 Random Fourier Features 模型，并且性能与 data-reuploading QNN 可比；结合在合成数据上对 QNN 与 NFS 所学傅里叶谱的额外分析，进一步支持上述结论。

Conclusion: NFS 可作为评估 QNN 性能的自然经典基线，为厘清经典与量子优势边界提供更公平、可解释的比较框架。

Abstract: For quantum machine learning, the exact boundary between classical and quantum advantage is still poorly understood. Direct comparison between quantum neural networks (QNNs) and existing classical models, which encompass fundamentally different function classes, often fails to provide broader insight into the difference between the two. Inspired by the techniques of Neural Quantum States and Random Fourier Features, this work introduces Neural Fourier Surrogates (NFS), a stochastic classical neural network architecture for efficiently learning coefficients over the same finite Fourier series support as quantum neural networks. Testing on a selection of tabular benchmark datasets, we find that NFS is an effective classifier architecture broadly competitive with established classical baselines, including a comparable Random Fourier Features model, and possessing comparable performance to data-reuploading QNNs; combined with additional analysis comparing the learned Fourier spectra of QNNs and NFS on synthetic data, these results establish NFS as a natural classical baseline for evaluating QNN performance.

</details>


### [123] [Beyond IP = PSPACE and QIP = PSPACE: Interactive Proofs in Arbitrary Physical Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00847)
*Kishor Bharti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究交互式证明的能力在多大程度上依赖底层物理理论，并在一般概率论框架下考察其计算能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典与量子情形下均有 IP = PSPACE 及三消息 QIP = PSPACE，这引出一个基本问题：交互式证明的能力有多少来自底层物理理论本身。

Method: 在一般概率论（GPT）中研究交互式证明，涵盖经典与量子理论；按证明者与验证者交换的内容以及理论如何定义高效操作进行分类讨论。

Result: 若仅交换经典消息，在满足标准假设的任意理论中，协议判定的恰为 PSPACE；若验证者与消息均为量子、而证明者可使用任何包含量子理论的更一般理论，最大接受概率不增加，故三消息 PSPACE 结果仍成立；若消息可为任意系统，则交互式证明类可严格超出 PSPACE。

Conclusion: 交互式证明的能力与所允许的物理理论及消息类型密切相关：经典消息下不超越 PSPACE，量子验证者加量子消息亦维持该界限，但允许任意物理系统作为消息时则可严格超越 PSPACE。

Abstract: The equalities IP = PSPACE and QIP = PSPACE, the latter achievable with three messages, raise a basic question: how much of an interactive proof's power comes from the underlying physical theory? We study interactive proofs in general probabilistic theories, which include classical and quantum theory. The answer depends on what the prover and verifier exchange and how the theory specifies efficient operations. When they exchange only classical messages, protocols in every theory satisfying our standard assumptions decide exactly PSPACE. For protocols with a quantum verifier and quantum messages, allowing a prover to use any theory containing quantum theory does not increase the maximum acceptance probability. Thus, the three-message PSPACE result remains valid against such provers. When messages may be arbitrary systems, the interactive-proof class can strictly exceed PSPACE.

</details>


### [124] [From Steane to A7: Quantum Codes from Invariant States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00865)
*Ian Teixeira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 构造了一类具有横向 $\tilde{A_7}$ 对称性的单错纠正七量子四元码 $((7,4,3))_4$，通过"不变量父态"方法而非直接搜索码空间，实现双量子比特超黄金门集的有限分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希望实现非Clifford操作（超黄金门）的横向容错实现，需要具备特定横向对称性、能够编码逻辑量子比特且纠正单错的量子纠错码；直接搜索码空间困难，故转而寻找不变父态。

Method: 对 $n$ 个局域维度 $q$ 的系统，设局域对称为酉 $t$-群、置换对称为 $t$-传递。将至多 $t$ 个格点上所有边缘分布的最大混合等价于 Schur--Weyl 扇区权重的线性方程；每个可行解给出纯度距离至少 $t+1$ 的父态，删除一个格点得到距离至少 $t$ 的 $q$ 维码并具指定横向局域对称性。通过 Steane 码与非稳定子 Fake Steane 码分析连续自由度的来源（二者八量子比特纯化位于一个圆上，纠错固定两个不变扇区范数、保留相对相位自由），将置换群保持、把 Clifford 对称替换为 $\tilde{A_7}\leq SU(4)$，得到三个固定扇区范数与两个自由相对相位。

Result: 得到两参数族的 $((7,4,3))_4$ 单错纠正七量子四元码，具横向 $\tilde{A_7}$ 对称性，编码两个逻辑量子比特，对每个物理四元系统施加相同门即可支持非Clifford操作，实现双量子比特超黄金门集的有限分量。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于不变父态与 Schur--Weyl 扇区权重线性方程的一般性构造框架，可将横向局域对称性与置换对称性要求转化为可解线性条件，成功推广至非稳定子的高维 ququart 情形，并揭示了 Steane、Fake Steane 与新 ququart 族之间连续自由度的代数来源。

Abstract: We construct a two-parameter family of single-error-correcting seven-ququart codes with transversal $\tilde{A_7}$ symmetry, realizing the finite component of a two-qubit super-golden gate set. These $((7,4,3))_4$ codes encode two logical qubits and support non-Clifford operations by applying the same gate to each physical ququart.
  Our method constructs invariant parent states rather than searching directly for a codespace. For $n$ systems of local dimension $q$, suppose the local symmetry is a unitary $t$-group and the permutation symmetry is $t$-transitive. Maximal mixing of all marginals on at most $t$ sites is then equivalent to linear equations in Schur--Weyl sector weights. Every feasible solution yields parents of pure distance at least $t+1$; puncturing one site produces a $q$-dimensional code of distance at least $t$ with the prescribed local symmetry acting transversally.
  Steane and the nonstabilizer Fake Steane code reveal why the construction leaves continuous freedom. They share the same prescribed transversal Clifford and permutation symmetries, and their eight-qubit purifications lie on a circle: error correction fixes two invariant-sector norms but leaves the relative phase free. Keeping the same permutation group and replacing Clifford symmetry by $\tilde{A_7}$ $\leq SU(4)$ gives three fixed sector norms and two free relative phases, producing the new ququart family.

</details>


### [125] [A Gate-Based Quantum Computing Framework for Codon Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00887)
*Fatemeh Ghasemi,Kion Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于门电路的量子计算框架用于密码子优化，将优化目标表述为Ising哈密顿量并映射为Pauli算符，采用VQE/SVQE/QAOA求解，在IBM量子处理器上实现并复现精确基态解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码子优化是合成生物学、蛋白表达和生物技术中的重要组合优化问题。此前量子退火已被探索用于该问题，但基于门电路的量子算法仍基本未被研究，因此需要探索门模型量子计算在该问题上的可行性和实现方案。

Method: 将密码子优化目标函数表述为Ising哈密顿量，并转换为适合门模型量子处理器的Pauli算符形式；采用变分量子本征求解器（VQE）、采样变分量子本征求解器（SVQE）和量子近似优化算法（QAOA）进行求解；以精确对角化、经典优化和量子退火作为基准对比；在三氨基酸基准问题上测试，并在IBM量子处理器上进行硬件演示。

Result: 在三氨基酸基准上，所提实现成功复现了精确基态解；同时确定了优化的拟设（ansatz）选择、优化策略、初始化方案以及硬件执行设置，从而在当前的噪声量子硬件上获得可复现的性能；并在IBM量子处理器上完成了硬件验证。

Conclusion: 作为一项进行中的工作，本研究为基于门电路的量子密码子优化建立了一个可复现的计算框架，并为未来随着量子硬件和算法进步而开展更大蛋白质序列的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Codon optimization is a challenging combinatorial optimization problem with important applications in synthetic biology, protein expression, and biotechnology. While quantum annealing has previously been explored for this problem, the application of gate-based quantum algorithms remains largely unexplored. In this work, we present a gate-based quantum computing framework for codon optimization by formulating the optimization objective as an Ising Hamiltonian and expressing it in terms of Pauli operators suitable for gate-based quantum processors. The proposed framework is investigated using the Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE), Sampling Variational Quantum Eigensolver (SVQE), and the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA), with benchmarking against exact diagonalization, classical optimization, and quantum annealing. Using a three-amino-acid benchmark, the proposed implementations successfully reproduce the exact ground-state solutions while identifying optimized ansatz selections, optimization strategies, initialization schemes, and hardware execution settings that provide reproducible performance on current noisy quantum hardware. Hardware demonstrations are performed on IBM Quantum processors to evaluate the practical implementation of the proposed workflow. As a work in progress, this study establishes a reproducible computational framework for gate-based quantum codon optimization and provides the foundation for future investigations of larger protein sequences as quantum hardware and algorithms continue to advance.

</details>


### [126] [TATVA: A Reinforcement Learning Framework for Quantum Circuit Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00945)
*Om Bhamare,Aryan Jadhav,Manas Shinde,Prithvi Shinde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出强化学习系统TATVA用于量子态制备电路合成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态制备的保真度直接影响计算结果的正确性，而传统电路设计依赖预设规则与数学分解，难以为不同且复杂的目标态设计电路，因此需要一种能以更少门数高保真地自动合成量子电路的方法。

Method: 将电路合成建模为序贯决策问题，智能体每次选择单个量子门，并在每次应用后计算保真度，选取产生最高保真度的门加入电路；系统采用并行架构，结合DQN与PPO两种强化学习智能体以及Qiskit的statevector模拟器；达到目标保真度后，进行事后优化以在保持保真度的前提下降低电路深度。

Result: 系统可合成多达5个量子比特的电路，保真度达到0.999999，并生成紧凑电路；同时通过训练阶段未使用的目标态测试系统的泛化能力；最终电路以保真度、成功率、门数和电路深度进行评估。

Conclusion: TATVA证明了利用强化学习实现自动、高保真且电路紧凑的量子电路合成是可行的。

Abstract: One of the most important initial steps in quantum computing is high-fidelity quantum state preparation, because errors in it can affect the final computational result. Therefore, one of the major challenges is to design a quantum circuit that can achieve the desired target state accurately with a smaller number of gates. Traditionally, circuits are designed using predefined rules and mathematical decompositions, but this makes it difficult to design circuits for different and complex target states. This paper presents TATVA, a reinforcement learning (RL) system for synthesizing quantum circuits. TATVA views circuit synthesis as a sequential decision problem in which the agents select one gate at a time and calculate the fidelity after each applied gate, with the gate producing the highest fidelity selected for the circuit. The system introduces a parallel architecture that uses two RL agents, Deep Q-Network (DQN) and Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), along with Qiskit's statevector simulator. The system can synthesize up to 5 qubits while achieving a fidelity of 0.999999 and producing compact circuits. After achieving the desired fidelity, it performs a post-hoc optimization step that reduces the circuit depth while preserving the achieved fidelity. The circuit is finalized using fidelity, success rate, gate count, and circuit depth. Target states that are not used during training are used to test whether the system has learned effectively and can generalize beyond the training states. This approach aims to enable automatic quantum circuit synthesis with high fidelity using reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [127] [Sublinear-depth Quantum Simulation of Electrons with Atomic Orbitals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00950)
*Jakob Günther,Aram W. Harrow*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明原子轨道基组的电子结构模型可在量子计算机上以 $\mathcal{O}(\mathrm{polylog}(N))$ 深度、无辅助比特地实现 Trotter 步，总门数 $N^{5/3+o(1)}$ 达到任意基组已知最优标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子轨道是分子电子结构建模的默认紧凑选择，但其哈密顿量项数可达 $\mathcal{O}(N^4)$ 且结构稀疏，难以在量子模拟复杂度上匹敌平面波和实空间网格方法。

Method: 结合严格的轨道局域化处理、基于快速多极子方法（FMM）的层级哈密顿量分解，以及浅层量子傅里叶算术电路，实现无辅助比特的 Trotter 步。

Result: Trotter 步深度为 $\mathcal{O}(\mathrm{polylog}(N))$；通过限定 Trotter 误差获得次线性模拟深度；总门数为 $N^{5/3+o(1)}$。

Conclusion: 原子轨道基组下的量子模拟复杂度可达到与其他任意基组（如平面波、实空间网格）相同的最优标度，弥补了该基组的长期劣势。

Abstract: Discretizing electronic degrees of freedom into atomic orbitals is the default choice to accurately model electrons in molecules in a compact and flexible way. We consider the task of simulating such electronic structure models on a quantum computer for $N$ orbitals, keeping the number of orbitals per atom constant. Atomic orbitals give rise to Hamiltonians with potentially up to $\mathcal{O}(N^4)$ terms and little structure, which made it challenging to match the complexity of methods based on plane waves and real-space grids. In this work, we show that a Trotter step for atomic orbitals can be implemented in depth $\mathcal{O}(\mathrm{polylog}(N))$ without ancillas by combining a rigorous treatment of orbital localization, a hierarchical Hamiltonian decomposition based on the Fast Multipole Method, and shallow quantum Fourier arithmetic circuits. By bounding the Trotter error, we obtain sublinear simulation depth, and our total gate count $N^{5/3 + o(1)}$ matches the best scaling known for any basis.

</details>


### [128] [Exact $T$-counts of Toffoli layers from an isotropy bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01024)
*Arul Rhik Mazumder*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文证明：在无Hadamard的Clifford+T电路中，实现m个不相交CCZ门的一层至少需要6m+1个T门，并给出基于各向同性约束的通用下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T-count是容错Clifford+T计算的主要开销；此前Campbell与Howard给出了m个不相交CCZ层的匹配构造（6m+1），但只在m=1、m=2有最优性依据，缺乏对一般m的最优性证明。

Method: 将T门对每个量子比特的"触碰"记录为向量，对纯立方相位证明这些向量张成完全各向同性子空间，由此推出各向同性下界δ≥2(n−d*)−r，可用相位多项式在多项式时间内计算；并结合稳定子零度ν及奇偶性论证。

Result: 证明m个不相交CCZ层恰需6m+1个T门且为最优；该下界在受控酉算子上与其精确计数相差至多1，并复现扇出情形的4m+3；在非Clifford纯立方门上提升为2ν+1；在TODD优化器的24个基准电路上，该下界为其311个合并相位多项式块中的193个证明最优（ν仅为113）。

Conclusion: 各向同性约束给出了通用的T-count下界，对无Hadamard电路在特定条件下有效（含仅以单位性未计算临时AND块为内部Hadamard的情形），但在自适应前馈下仅能证明t≥ν。

Abstract: The $T$-count is the dominant cost of fault-tolerant Clifford+$T$ computation. We prove that a layer of $m$ disjoint CCZ gates, the diagonal core of a parallel Toffoli layer, needs exactly $6m+1$ $T$ gates in every Hadamard-free Clifford+$T$ circuit with clean ancillas. Campbell and Howard gave the matching construction. To our knowledge this is the first proof that it is optimal for general $m$ (for $m=1$ the value $7$ is classical, and Campbell and Howard state the value $13$ at $m=2$). On every controlled unitary the same floor comes within one of their exact count, and it recovers their $4m+3$ for a fan-out of $m$ Toffolis from one control. The proof rests on an isotropy constraint: for a pure-cubic phase, the vectors recording which $T$ gates touch each qubit span a totally isotropic subspace. In general the constraint gives the isotropy floor $δ\ge2(n-d^{\ast})-r$ for every diagonal level-three gate, computed from the phase polynomial in polynomial time. The floor is never below stabilizer nullity $ν$, which equals $n-d^{\ast}$ on this class, and a separate parity argument raises it to $2ν+1$ on non-Clifford pure-cubic gates. On the output of the TODD optimizer for the $24$ benchmark circuits it completes, the floor certifies $193$ of its $311$ merged phase-polynomial blocks optimal for their Hadamard layering ($186$ to $193$ across five optimizer seeds), against $113$ for nullity. The floor also holds, under stated conditions, for circuits whose only internal Hadamards form unitarily uncomputed temporary AND blocks, while under adaptive feedforward only $t\geν$ is proved.

</details>


### [129] [Device-Independent Conference Keys from Parity-Extended Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01025)
*Suvradip Chakraborty,Ronak Ramachandran,Aniruddha Sen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出Parity-$G$游戏框架，将任意两方非局域游戏扩展至$N$方并保持量子/经典值不变，据此构造首个基于伪心灵感应游戏的设备无关会议密钥协商(DI-CKA)协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DI-CKA协议均基于单一Bell不等式（通常是CHSH博弈的多方变体），而DI-QKD已利用丰富的非局域游戏库；如何将该游戏库迁移到多方会议场景尚不明确。核心动机是扩展DI-CKA可用的非局域游戏种类，进而提升协议性能。

Method: 引入Parity-$G$游戏：在任意两方游戏$G$存在一方的Pauli观测量最优策略时，将其扩展为$N$方游戏，且保持$G$的量子值与经典值不变，安全性分析可仅依赖原两方游戏。该框架以Ribeiro等人的Parity-CHSH为特例；应用于Mermin–Peres Magic Square Game，得到新$N$方伪心灵感应游戏Parity Magic Square Game（理想设备每轮必胜），并用其构造DI-CKA协议，证明其抗相干攻击安全性。

Result: 该协议是首个基于伪心灵感应游戏的DI-CKA协议，可抗相干攻击；每轮产生最多2比特密钥，且在低噪声下密钥率超过基于Parity-CHSH的DI-CKA协议。

Conclusion: Parity-$G$框架成功把丰富两方非局域游戏库迁移到多方会议场景，统一并推广了现有DI-CKA构造，并借助伪心灵感应游戏实现更高密钥率，为DI-CKA设计提供了通用且可扩展的途径。

Abstract: Device-independent conference key agreement (DI-CKA) lets a group of parties establish a shared secret key from untrusted quantum devices, with security certified by non-locality. Existing DI-CKA protocols are each built around a single Bell inequality, typically a multiparty variant of the CHSH game. DI-QKD protocols, in contrast, have been built from a much richer landscape of non-local games, and it has remained unclear how to carry this landscape over to the conference setting. We introduce $\textit{Parity-$G$ games}$, which extend any two-player game $G$ to $N$ players, for every $N$, provided $G$ has an optimal strategy in which one player measures Pauli observables. The extension preserves the quantum and classical values of $G$, and the security of the resulting $N$-party protocol follows from an analysis of the two-player game alone. Our framework recovers the Parity-CHSH game of Ribeiro, Murta and Wehner (Phys. Rev. A, 2018) as a special case. Applied to the Mermin--Peres Magic Square Game, it yields a new $N$-player pseudo-telepathy game, the $\textit{Parity Magic Square Game}$, which ideal devices win in every round. We use it to construct the $\textit{first}$ DI-CKA protocol based on a pseudo-telepathy game. We prove the protocol secure against coherent attacks. It produces up to two key bits per round, and at low noise its key rate exceeds that of the DI-CKA protocol based on the Parity-CHSH game.

</details>


### [130] [Function-like pseudorandom unitaries generate pseudorandom quantum processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01038)
*Zitai Xu,Rahul Arvind,Kishor Bharti,Tobias Haug,Dax Enshan Koh,Wai-Keong Mok,Andrew Tanggara,Alexey V. Gorshkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出"伪随机函数型酉算符"(PRFU),用单个短密钥即可生成一整个可复用的、由公开标签索引的随机化量子操作族,并分别针对经典标签与相干标签访问建立自适应量子查询下的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Haar随机酉算符是通用随机量子演化的标准模型,但其描述复杂度和电路复杂度均为指数级。伪随机酉算符(PRU)虽能高效模拟单个Haar随机酉算符,但许多任务需要一整个可复用的、彼此独立且看起来随机的操作族,单个PRU无法满足这一需求。

Method: 作者定义PRFU(由公开标签索引、仅需单个短密钥的酉算符族),区分经典标签访问与相干标签访问,并在自适应量子查询下建立安全性。经典标签情形:基于后量子伪随机函数与伪随机酉算符给出通用构造。相干标签情形:使用带索引的路径记录框架分析跨标签干涉,并基于量子安全的函数与置换原语证明构造的安全性。

Result: 证明PRFU可生成伪随机信道以及带私有记忆的量子梳(quantum comb),且能抵抗自适应干预与并发会话攻击。将PRFU与酉算符粘合(unitary gluing)结合,得到单密钥的伪随机酉算符族,其支持寄存器宽度可在密钥生成之后选取。

Conclusion: 进一步应用包括nonce解析的量子认证、相干掩码QRAM查询以及随机多时刻动力学的高效模拟。该工作将量子伪随机性从单个操作扩展至可高效生成的、看似随机的量子动力学族。

Abstract: Haar-random unitaries provide a canonical model of generic random quantum evolution, but they typically have exponential description and circuit complexity. Although pseudorandom unitaries efficiently emulate a single Haar-random unitary, many tasks require an entire reusable family of independently random-looking operations. We introduce pseudorandom function-like unitaries (PRFU), which generate such a family indexed by public labels using only a single short key. We distinguish classical- and coherent-label access and establish security under adaptive quantum queries. For classical labels, we provide a generic construction from a post-quantum pseudorandom function and a pseudorandom unitary. For coherent labels, we use an indexed path-recording framework to analyze interference across labels and prove security of a construction based on quantum-secure function and permutation primitives. We further show that PRFUs generate pseudorandom channels and quantum combs with private memory, which are secure against adaptive interventions and concurrent sessions. Combining PRFUs with unitary gluing yields a one-key family of pseudorandom unitaries whose supported register widths can be chosen after key generation. Further applications include nonce-resolved quantum authentication, coherently masked QRAM queries, and efficient emulation of random multi-time dynamics. Our results extend quantum pseudorandomness from individual operations to efficiently generated families of random-looking quantum dynamics.

</details>


### [131] [Learned Parallel Bit-Flipping Sequential Belief Propagation Decoding of Quantum LDPC Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01068)
*Mohsen Moradi,Taejoon Kim,Remi A. Chou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种学习型并行比特翻转顺序BP解码器：先运行固定迭代的学习型顺序BP，若校验子未满足，则构造四元比特翻转候选（每个对应翻转一个量子比特的Pauli判决），并用新评分（校验子增益+量化Pauli对数似然惩罚+假设翻转态Q表前瞻）选择候选，从同一解码器状态并行发起独立的学习型顺序BP续算，从而以并行资源换取低延迟并提升可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QLDPC码是实现低开销容错量子计算的有力候选，但实用化需要快速、低复杂度且可靠的解码器。标准flooding BP因短环、简并性和对称解码轨迹易收敛失败；学习型顺序BP虽通过强化学习策略选择变量节点更新顺序改善收敛，但单一学习轨迹仍对不利的局部Pauli判决敏感。

Method: 1) 先执行固定迭代次数的学习型顺序BP；2) 若校验子未满足，构造四元比特翻转候选，每个候选对应改变一个量子比特当前的Pauli判决；3) 设计新的四元评分，融合校验子增益、量化Pauli对数似然惩罚以及在假设翻转后状态上的Q表前瞻；4) 用该评分选择候选，并从同一解码器状态出发为每个候选初始化独立的学习型顺序BP续算；5) 由于各续算相互独立，可并行执行，测试多个候选主要增加并行硬件资源而非顺序解码延迟。

Result: 在去极化信道下对代表性QLDPC码的仿真表明，所提解码器在保持并行低延迟结构的同时，提升了学习型顺序BP的可靠性。

Conclusion: 该工作通过将学习型顺序BP与并行四元比特翻转候选搜索结合，在低延迟并行架构下缓解了单一学习轨迹对局部Pauli判决的敏感性，提高了QLDPC码解码可靠性，为低开销容错量子计算提供了有前景的解码方案。

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) codes are promising candidates for low-overhead fault-tolerant quantum computation, but their practical use requires fast, low-complexity, and reliable decoders. Belief propagation (BP) is attractive because of its local message-passing structure, yet standard flooding BP often suffers from convergence failures on QLDPC codes due to short cycles, degeneracy, and symmetric decoding trajectories. Learned sequential BP improves convergence by using a reinforcement-learning policy to choose variable-node update orders, but a single learned trajectory can still be sensitive to unfavorable local Pauli decisions. We propose a learned parallel bit-flipping sequential BP decoder with a new quaternary score combining syndrome gain, a quantized Pauli log-likelihood penalty, and Q-table lookahead at hypothetical post-flip states. Our decoder first runs learned sequential BP for a fixed number of iterations. If the syndrome is not satisfied, it constructs quaternary bit-flipping candidates, each corresponding to changing the current Pauli decision of one qubit. The selected candidates initialize independent learned sequential BP continuations from the same decoder state. Since these continuations are independent, they can be executed in parallel, so testing several candidates mainly increases parallel hardware resources rather than sequential decoding latency. Simulations on representative QLDPC codes over the depolarizing channel show that the proposed decoder improves the reliability of learned sequential BP while having a parallel low-latency structure.

</details>


### [132] [The stationarity test: a framework for learning quantum many-body systems from their thermal states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01074)
*Thiago Bergamaschi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"平稳性检验"算法，利用量子Gibbs采样的详细平衡马尔可夫链，从热态样本中学习哈密顿量，实现对晶格哈密顿量的结构学习、从亚稳态学习系数，并改进了热亚稳态的面积律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从量子系统的热态（Gibbs态）样本中学习支配系统的哈密顿量相互作用，是量子学习理论与多体物理交叉领域的根本问题。该问题此前缺乏在所有温度下进行结构学习、以及仅凭热亚稳态样本学习系数的有效算法。

Method: 提出"平稳性检验"算法：先"猜测"哈密顿量，再在与详细平衡量子马尔可夫链（[CKG23]）相关联的动力学下，测量局部可观测量随时间的变化率。技术核心是建立这些Gibbs采样算法的量子Fisher信息的新近似局域性与凸性性质。

Result: 1. 首个在任意温度下、仅凭Gibbs态副本进行晶格哈密顿量底层相互作用图（结构学习）的学习算法；2. 首个仅凭热亚稳态（建模为自由能的"局部极小"）副本学习晶格哈密顿量系数的算法；3. 对近期热亚稳态面积律（[BCV25]）的改进，在热力学极限下成立。算法严格、时间高效，在系统规模和精度上接近样本最优。

Conclusion: 平稳性检验将量子Gibbs采样文献与哈密顿量学习相联系，为热态下的哈密顿量学习提供了统一且近乎最优的框架，其基础是量子Fisher信息的近似局域性与凸性等新技术结果。

Abstract: The task of learning the Hamiltonian interactions governing a quantum system, given samples of its thermal (or 'Gibbs') states, is a foundational question at the intersection of quantum learning theory and many-body physics. In this paper, we draw connections to the quantum Gibbs sampling literature to introduce a natural learning algorithm we call the stationarity test: which simply "guesses" the Hamiltonian, and measures the rate-of-change of local observables, under the associated detailed-balanced quantum Markov chain [CKG23]. We leverage the stationarity test to address the following applications:
  1. To give the first learning algorithm for the underlying interaction graph, i.e. structure learning, of lattice Hamiltonians at all temperatures, given copies of their Gibbs states.
  2. To give the first learning algorithm for the coefficients of a lattice Hamiltonian, given only copies of its thermal metastable states, modeled as the "local minima" of the free energy.
  3. In addition, we present a refinement to the recent area law for thermal metastable states [BCV25], which holds in the thermodynamic limit.
  Our learning algorithms are rigorous, time-efficient, and nearly sample-optimal in system size and accuracy. At a technical level, our arguments are based on new approximate locality and convexity properties for the quantum Fisher information of these Gibbs sampling algorithms.

</details>


### [133] [Quantum synchronization in atom-cavity coupled systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01094)
*Katha Haldar,Anushree Dey,Saikat Ghosh,Bimalendu Deb*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究受经典场驱动的自旋-1原子系综与两个驱动腔场的相互作用，揭示原子子系统和双模腔场在不同驱动强度下呈现极限环或量子同步现象，并给出可视化与多角度验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索驱动耗散相互作用量子系统中极限环与量子同步之间的涌现现象，并为利用耦合原子-腔系统在量子计量中获取量子优势提供理论基础。

Method: 采用腔量子电动力学（CQED）模型：自旋-1原子系综经经典场驱动，与两个驱动腔场模式相互作用。用SU(2)相干态构造广义双模场Husimi-Q函数，用自旋-1 Husimi-Q函数可视化原子自旋态；并通过U(1)对称性破缺、Wigner准概率分布、约化场密度矩阵的相干性以及两模间Wehrl互信息进行分析。

Result: 原子与双模腔场子系统依腔驱动强度及系统参数呈现极限环或量子同步；场同步可由U(1)对称性破缺、Wigner准概率分布和相干性佐证；发现场同步与Wehrl互信息互斥。

Conclusion: 驱动耗散相互作用量子系统中极限环与量子同步存在有趣竞争与涌现行为，该研究对借助耦合原子-腔系统在量子计量中提取量子优势具有重要意义。

Abstract: We consider a cavity quantum electrodynamics (CQED) setup where an ensemble of spin-1 atoms driven by a classical field interacts with two driven cavity field modes. We show that the two sub-systems - the atoms and the two-mode cavity field, exhibit limit-cycle or quantum synchronization depending on the cavity drive strengths and other system parameters. In order to visualize the limit cycle and synchronization in the field, we introduce generalized two-mode field Husimi-Q function using SU(2) coherent states. For atomic spin states, we make use of spin-1 Husimi-Q function to visualize the same. We further corroborate the occurrence of field synchronization and limit cycle by analyzing $U(1)$ symmetry breaking, Wigner quasi-probability distribution, coherence properties of reduced field density matrix. Our results reveal intriguing interplay between the limit-cycle and quantum synchronization behavior, highlighting emergent quantum phenomena in driven dissipative interacting quantum systems. Additionally, we calculate Wehrl mutual information between the two modes and find that the field synchronization and Wehrl mutual information are mutually exclusive. Our study would be important to extract quantum advantages in quantum metrology using coupled atom-cavity systems.

</details>


### [134] [Constant-Per-Layer-Depth MPS-Pretrained Ansatz for Noisy Distributed Quantum Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01099)
*Seongpyo Hong,Woodo Lee,Yong-Su Kim,Seung-Sup B. Lee,Junghyun Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作通过比较MPS预训练电路的三种实现方式（ladder、mixed-canonical、brick-wall），发现更浅的brick-wall结构在噪声和ZNE辅助下性能更优；并将其扩展到模块化多QPU系统，证明在通信空闲时间远短于相干时间时分布式电路可媲美甚至超越单处理器方案，确立了张量网络预训练、电路调度与通信拓扑的硬件感知协同设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式量子处理器有望将变分算法扩展到单设备之外，但电路深度、通信开销和噪声限制了其性能。因此需要研究如何在噪声模块化硬件上实现高效的变分量子计算，并明确张量网络预训练、电路调度与通信拓扑之间的协同设计关系。

Method: 在每层双量子比特块资源匹配的条件下，对比MPS预训练的ladder、mixed-canonical与brick-wall三种实现；随后将MPS预训练电路扩展到每个QPU配备一个通信量子比特的模块化处理器，并采用最近邻QPU路径调度使每层深度随QPU增加保持恒定。

Result: 三种实现具有可比的理想VQE性能与梯度尺度，但更浅的brick-wall架构降低了电路持续时间、空闲时间退相干与ZNE开销，从而在有噪及ZNE辅助场景下表现更优。当通信空闲时间远短于相干时间时，实现目标哈密顿量长程相互作用的分布式电路在噪声下达到或超过单处理器brick-wall方案。

Conclusion: 结果确立了面向噪声模块化硬件的硬件感知协同设计原则：将张量网络预训练、电路调度与通信拓扑联合优化，是变分量子计算的有效途径。

Abstract: Distributed quantum processors could scale variational algorithms beyond single devices, but circuit depth, communication overhead, and noise limit their performance. We compare ladder, mixed-canonical, and brick-wall realizations of matrix-product-state (MPS) pretraining with matched per-layer two-qubit-block resources. Despite comparable ideal variational quantum eigensolver performance and gradient scales, the shallower brick-wall architecture reduces circuit duration, idle-time decoherence, and zero-noise-extrapolation (ZNE) overhead, yielding superior noisy and ZNE-assisted performance. We then extend MPS-pretrained circuits to modular processors with one communication qubit per quantum processing unit (QPU); a nearest-neighbor QPU-path schedule keeps the per-layer depth constant as QPUs are added. Distributed circuits whose inter-QPU links realize long-range interactions of the target Hamiltonian match or outperform the single-processor brick-wall under noise when communication idle time is short compared with the coherence time. These results establish hardware-aware co-design of tensor-network pretraining, circuit scheduling, and communication topology as a principle for variational quantum computation on noisy modular hardware.

</details>


### [135] [Multivariate Quantum Signal Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01125)
*Guang Hao Low*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出多变量量子信号处理框架，利用多端口IIR滤波器结构设计矩阵值变换，给出一系列多oracle问题的近优或最优查询复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单变量QSP/QSVT只能处理单一非正规算子的标量/多项式变换，难以直接刻画多个非对易非正规块编码矩阵的联合变换，限制了Hamiltonian simulation、线性系统求解等多oracle问题的查询复杂度。

Method: 建立Multivariate QSP框架，将量子换能器赋予多端口无限脉冲响应(IIR)滤波器的解析结构，构建传输函数设计方法论；提供可高效计算的解析界、可达传输函数的完整刻画以及模块化解析IIR组件库。

Result: 在块编码归一化λ与代价C下，加权拟范数Hamiltonian simulation代价约为t⟨λ,C⟩_{1/2}+O(||C||_1 log(1/ε))；稀疏模拟达最优时间-范数Θ(√d||H||_{1→2}t)；线性系统、微分方程、基态制备与能量估计、广义特征值问题在最小矩阵假设下取得最优加权态制备与多项代价；并应用于downfolded Hamiltonian模拟、多尺度图分类(Kron reduction)及在线各向异性Kalman滤波。

Conclusion: 多变量QSP为多oracle量子算法提供了统一、可模块化的传输函数设计范式，误差对数依赖且成功概率近1，达到与单变量QSP/QSVT相当的近似理论保证，并改进或解决了多个开放问题的per-oracle查询复杂度。

Abstract: We develop Multivariate Quantum Signal Processing for designing matrix-valued transformations of multiple noncommuting nonnormal block-encoded matrices. Our framework equips quantum transducers with the analytic structure of multiport Infinite-Impulse-Response (IIR) filters and establishes a transfer-function methodology for designing quantum query algorithms. Efficiently computable analytic bounds that hold uniformly across the oracle promise yield efficient quantum circuits with logarithmic error dependence and near-unity success probability, paralleling the approximation-theoretic guarantees of univariate QSP/QSVT. We provide a complete characterization of achievable transfer functions and a principled library of analytic IIR components. Our modular designs greatly improve or resolve the per-oracle query complexity of open multi-oracle problems: (1) Hamiltonian simulation under $H=\sum_j H_j$ with weighted quasinorm cost $\approx t\langle\vecλ,\vec C\rangle_{1/2}+\mathcal{O}(\|\vec C\|_1\log(1/ε))$ for block-encoding normalizations $\vecλ$ and costs $\vec C$; (2) sparse simulation with optimal time--norm $Θ(\sqrt{d}\|H\|_{\rm 1\to2}t)$ and additive error dependence; (3) solving linear systems and (4) differential equations under minimal matrix assumptions with optimal weighted state-preparation and multi-term cost; (5) ground-state preparation and energy estimation; (6) generalized eigenvalue problems. We apply these to exactly simulate downfolded Hamiltonians with leading cost $t\langle\vecλ_{\rm eff},\vec C_{\rm eff}\rangle_{1/2}$ further reduced by fractional dynamic correlation energy contributions from coarse-grained Hamiltonians, and show similar classical-data-input-reduction results for multiscale graph classification via Kron reduction. We also show the online generalization with anisotropic Kalman filtering of streaming quantum data.

</details>


### [136] [Classical Hardness of Learning Functions of Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01141)
*Sota Hashimoto,Akinori Kawachi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文严格证明了Morohoshi等人提出的两个分布于Hamiltonian函数学习问题的平均情形经典困难性，该结论基于随机RSA模数因式分解的平均情形困难性假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Morohoshi等人提出了Hamiltonian函数学习问题，即从Hamiltonian H和量子态ρ的经典描述中预测Tr[f(H)ρ]，虽构造了高效量子学习算法，但平均情形经典困难性的严格证明仍悬而未决。

Method: 针对f_{cos,π}(λ)=cos(πλ)和f_{exp,β}(λ)=e^{-βλ}两个具体分布问题，在随机RSA模数因式分解平均情形困难性假设下进行归约证明。

Result: 证明若存在对任一问题在平方损失下、输出假设可在经典多项式时间内求值的高效经典随机学习器，则可构造出因式分解随机RSA模数的经典随机多项式时间算法。

Conclusion: 所研究的两个Hamiltonian函数学习问题在随机RSA模数因式分解平均情形困难性假设下，不存在上述意义上的高效经典学习算法，从而填补了经典困难性证明的空白。

Abstract: Morohoshi, Nakayama, Manabe, and Mitarai proposed a physically motivated quantum machine learning problem in which the goal is to predict quantities of the form $\operatorname{Tr}[f(H)ρ]$ from classical descriptions of a Hamiltonian $H$ and a quantum state $ρ$, where $f$ is an unknown function. We call this problem Hamiltonian function learning in this paper. They constructed an efficient quantum learning algorithm under suitable conditions, while leaving a rigorous proof of average-case classical hardness open. In this paper, we rigorously prove the average-case classical hardness for two distribution-specific Hamiltonian function learning problems for $f_{\cos,π}(λ)=\cos(πλ)$ and $f_{\exp,β}(λ)=e^{-βλ}$ discussed in the paper of Morohoshi et al. under the assumption of the average-case hardness of factoring random RSA moduli. More specifically, we show that an efficient classical randomized learner under squared loss whose output hypotheses are evaluable in classical polynomial time for either problem would yield a classical randomized polynomial-time algorithm for factoring random RSA moduli.

</details>


### [137] [Robustness hierarchy of bipartite quantum correlations under noisy dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01163)
*Shakib Daryanoosh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个鲁棒性框架，用于刻画Bell非局域性、EPR导引和纠缠的层级，建立相应鲁棒性度量的排序，并将其与噪声诱导的转变时间及Lindblad动力学下的瞬时鲁棒性衰减（方向导数）相联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子关联在噪声下十分脆弱，因此需要不仅追踪其存在性、还能追踪其在物理演化过程中退化程度的定量度量。

Method: 构建针对Bell非局域性、EPR导引和纠缠层级的鲁棒性框架；建立相应鲁棒性度量的排序；将鲁棒性与噪声诱导的转变时间关联；把Lindblad动力学下的瞬时鲁棒性衰减表述为由演化和鲁棒性泛函共同确定的方向导数。

Result: 通过各向同性退极化噪声和振幅阻尼两个应用实例，分别展示了所得的资源转变时间与导引鲁棒性的方向依赖性。

Conclusion: 该框架为刻画噪声动力学下双体量子关联的退化与寿命提供了一种途径。

Abstract: Quantum correlations are fragile under noise, motivating quantitative measures that track not only their presence but also their degradation during physical evolution. We develop a robustness framework for the hierarchy of Bell nonlocality, EPR steering, and entanglement, establishing an ordering of the corresponding robustness measures and relating robustness to noise-induced transition times. We further formulate the instantaneous robustness decay along Lindblad dynamics as a directional derivative determined by both the evolution and the robustness functional. Applications to isotropic depolarizing noise and amplitude damping illustrate, respectively, the resulting resource transition times and the directional dependence of steering robustness. The framework provides an approach to characterizing the degradation and lifetime of bipartite quantum correlations under noisy dynamics.

</details>


### [138] [Experimental quantification of quantum coherence for a set of quantum states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01227)
*Tianle Zheng,Liangsheng Li,Wenting Zhou,Chengjie Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对一组量子态的量子相干性进行了基无关的直接实验验证，发现理论与实验结果完全吻合，并证明BB84协议所用态具有最大集合相干性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子相干性是量子信息核心资源之一，但以往研究多针对单个态或依赖特定基，缺乏对"一组量子态"整体相干性的基无关实验验证，且其在BB84、量子安全直接通信等协议中的实际作用有待实验确认。

Method: 采用萨格纳克（Sagnac）干涉仪，对两组实验制备的量子态进行基无关的集合相干性量化测量，并与集合相干性理论预测进行对比分析，进一步考察BB84协议态的集合相干性表现。

Result: 实验结果与集合相干性理论完美吻合；成功量化了两组实验态的集合相干性；发现BB84协议所用态具有最大集合相干性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次以基无关方式实验验证了量子态集合的相干性理论，拓展了集合相干性在量子密钥分发及概率量子克隆等协议中的应用前景。

Abstract: We present a direct experimental verification of quantum coherence for a set of quantum states in a basis-independent manner.
