We find that the theory of quantum coherence for a set of quantum states matches our experimental results perfectly and can be applied to protocols like BB84 and quantum secure direct communication. Using a Sagnac interferometer, we experimentally quantify the quantum coherence for two sets of experimental states. Moreover, we introduce a novel application of set coherence, illustrating that the states employed in the BB84 protocol exhibit maximal set coherence. Our results pave the way for wider applications of quantum coherence for a set of quantum states, including quantum key distribution protocols and probabilistic quantum cloning.

</details>


### [139] [Linear-Time Encodable Quantum Codes near the CSS GV Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01277)
*Rachel Yun Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一类量子CSS码，其码率-距离权衡逼近CSS GV界，可用对数深度、线性门数的量子线路编码，可视为经典重复-多次累加（RMA）码的量子类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典重复-多次累加（RMA）等累加类码能以极低编码复杂度逼近容量，但量子领域缺乏具有类似低深度、低门数编码结构且性能逼近CSS GV界的构造。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 受Brehm与Resch工作启发，构造量子CSS码。编码器结构简单：先施加一个常数深度的外码量子线路，后接若干经典累加层与逆累加层，整体为对数深度、线性门数的量子编码线路。

Result: 所得量子CSS码的码率-距离权衡逼近CSS GV界，且编码可由对数深度、线性门数的量子线路实现，无需高深度或高门数开销。

Conclusion: 该构造给出了低复杂度、高性能的量子编码方案，建立了经典累加码与量子CSS码之间的对应关系，为构造逼近CSS GV界的实用量子码提供了新思路。

Abstract: We construct quantum CSS codes with rate-distance tradeoff approaching the CSS GV bound, which can be encoded by a quantum circuit of logarithmic depth and a linear number of gates. Our construction is heavily inspired by a work of Brehm and Resch and can be viewed as a quantum analogue of classical repeat multiple accumulate codes. The encoder has a particularly simple structure, consisting of a single constant depth outer quantum circuit followed by several classical accumulation and inverse-accumulation layers.

</details>


### [140] [Preservability of Measurement Incompatibility: Purification, Activation, and a No-Go Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01327)
*Chao-Hsien Wu,Franco Nori,Huan-Yu Ku*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在测量不相容可保存性资源理论中引入基于鲁棒性的资源单调量，证明预滤波无法从不兼容湮灭信道中激活测量不相容可保存性（不可行定理），但后滤波可实现随机激活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量不相容是支撑密码学与通信等量子信息任务的重要量子资源，但系统与环境的相互作用会削弱甚至完全破坏它（即测量不相容湮灭信道）。近期虽已在测量不相容可保存性的资源理论框架下刻画了噪声动力学保持测量不相容的能力，但如何纯化该可保存性、以及如何从不兼容湮灭信道中激活它仍缺乏研究。

Method: 在测量不相容可保存性的资源理论中，首先引入基于鲁棒性的资源单调量作为度量指标（figures of merit）；随后分别考察预滤波操作与后滤波操作对该资源的作用。

Result: 预滤波操作能够增强测量不相容可保存性，但无法从不相容湮灭信道中将其激活，从而给出一条不可行定理（no-go theorem）；同时显式证明不相容湮灭信道可通过后滤波操作被随机激活。

Conclusion: 研究为在量子信息处理中利用测量不相容提供了实用框架，明确了预滤波与后滤波在纯化和激活可保存性方面的不同能力边界。

Abstract: Measurement incompatibility is a fundamental quantum resource that enables advantages in many quantum information tasks, including cryptography and communication. However, unavoidable interactions between a system and its environment can degrade or even completely destroy measurement incompatibility; such a process is referred to as a measurement-incompatibility-annihilating channel. Recently, the capability of noisy quantum dynamics to preserve measurement incompatibility has been characterized within the resource theory of measurement incompatibility preservability. This motivates studying how to purify the preservation of measurement incompatibility and how to activate it from a measurement-incompatibility-annihilating channel. To this end, we first introduce our figures of merit as robustness-based resource monotones within this resource theory. We demonstrate that while pre-filtering operations can strengthen measurement incompatibility preservability, they cannot activate it from an incompatibility-annihilating channel, establishing a no-go theorem. Furthermore, we explicitly demonstrate that an incompatibility-annihilating channel can be stochastically activated via post-filtering operations. Our results provide a practical framework for exploiting measurement incompatibility in quantum information processing.

</details>


### [141] [Optimal initial states for quantum Fisher information in linearized cavity optomechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01417)
*Wangjun Lu,Qing Yu,Ying Li,Cuilu Zhai,Rui Zhang,Zhao-Hui Peng,Shiqing Tang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用量子Fisher信息求解线性化腔光力学系统中单光子耦合估计的最优初态，在红失谐（分束器）区给出闭式最优解、Heisenberg型二次界及其饱和态（双模NOON态），并在截断Fock空间中数值验证；进一步讨论蓝边带、腔损耗及实际量子相干耦合实验三类扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子Fisher信息是估计参数精度的核心度量，而腔光力学系统的单光子耦合（如辐射压耦合）测量对量子传感与精密测量具有重要意义。作者希望确定在给定能量或光子数约束下，何种初态能够最大化对单光子耦合的估计精度，并厘清纠缠、压缩等量子资源在其中的作用，同时检验理想结果在非理想条件（蓝失谐、损耗、实际实验）下的适用性。

Method: 1. 对线性化腔光力学系统在红失谐（分束器）机制下进行分析，利用纯输入时Fisher信息等于激发交换算符方差四倍的结论，将优化问题化为方差最大化并求得闭式解。2. 对机械模为Fock态、任意固定机械参考等情形分别求解最优探针，其中后者通过对相干性的Lagrange对偶论证给出精确上界，最优态为相位匹配压缩真空。3. 在固定总激发数下联合优化两模，得到量子Fisher信息满足能量的二次Heisenberg界，并识别其饱和态为生成元两极端本征态的等权叠加（等价于经平衡分束器后的双模NOON态）。4. 采用截断Fock空间中的精确对角化对全部解析结果进行数值验证。5. 分别推演蓝边带（双模压缩谱无界）、腔损耗（信息积累在腔寿命尺度转为线性，最优时长为约2.5个腔寿命）以及实际量子相干耦合实验（每小时相对精度在百万分之一到十万分之一之间）三种扩展情形。

Result: 红失谐区Fisher信息为激发交换算符方差四倍，最优态有闭式解：机械模为Fock态时探针简并，任何满足光子数约束的态均最优，且多声子Fock参考使单光子灵敏度线性增强；固定机械参考下最优为相位匹配压缩真空（由Lagrange对偶给出相干性上界）。固定总激发数下量子Fisher信息服从能量的二次Heisenberg界，由生成元两极端本征态等权叠加（双模NOON态）饱和，平衡积Fock态仅达线性增强；单光子输入为最简饱和情形。截断Fock空间精确对角化确认上述结果。扩展情形：蓝边带双模压缩谱无界，纠缠最优态不可实现，定能下平衡积Fock态恰为最优；腔损耗下信息积累在腔寿命尺度转为线性，最优时长约2.5个腔寿命；实际量子相干耦合实验中每小时相对精度介于百万分之一到十万分之一，数态与热态参考最稳健。

Conclusion: 该工作系统刻画了线性化腔光力学系统中单光子耦合估计的最优量子初态：红失谐区存在闭式最优解并由双模NOON态实现二次（Heisenberg型）增强，超越了平衡积Fock态的线性增强；单光子输入是该类饱和态的最简特例。结果经精确对角化验证，并推广到蓝边带、腔损耗与实际实验参数，表明数态与热态参考在真实条件下最具鲁棒性，为腔光力学精密测量提供了初态设计的理论指导。

Abstract: We find the optimal initial states of a linearized cavity-optomechanical system for estimating the single-photon coupling by quantum Fisher information. In the red-detuned (beam-splitter) regime the interaction exchanges excitations between the two modes, and for pure inputs the Fisher information is four times the variance of the excitation-exchange operator, reducing the optimization to variance maximization with a closed-form solution. With the mechanical mode in a Fock state the probe is degenerate: any state saturating the photon-number constraint is optimal, and a Fock reference with many phonons enhances the per-photon sensitivity linearly. For an arbitrary fixed mechanical reference it becomes a phase-matched squeezed vacuum, from an exact bound on that coherence obtained by a Lagrange-dual argument. Jointly optimizing both modes at fixed total excitation number, the quantum Fisher information obeys a Heisenberg bound quadratic in the energy, saturated by equal superpositions of the two extremal eigenstates of the generator, equivalently a two-mode NOON state after a balanced beam splitter; balanced product Fock states reach only a linear enhancement. Exact diagonalization in truncated Fock spaces confirms all the results, the familiar single-photon input being the simplest saturating case. Three extensions are worked out: the blue sideband has an unbounded two-mode-squeezing spectrum, so the entangled optimum becomes impossible and the balanced product Fock state is exactly optimal at definite energy; under cavity loss the information accumulation turns linear on the cavity-lifetime timescale, at a renewal-optimal duration of about two and a half lifetimes; and for a state-of-the-art quantum-coherent coupling experiment the per-hour relative precision lies between one part in a million and ten parts in a million, number and thermal references being the most robust.

</details>


### [142] [Robust and leakage-resilient device-independent oblivious transfer in MiniQCryp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01421)
*Zhili Chen,Rahul Jain,YaoNan Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在假设后量子单向函数存在的前提下，本文构造了设备无关（DI）的茫然传输（OT）与比特承诺协议：诚实方仅用可信经典计算操作不可信量子设备，安全性基于模拟、可抵御量子多项式时间敌手并可顺序组合；同一协议骨架在两种测量/泄漏机制下工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DI 密码学旨在不信任量子设备的前提下获得安全性，但已有 DI-OT/承诺在容错率、泄漏模型、组合安全性或模拟器效率上存在限制。本文希望在仅假设后量子单向函数的最弱计算假设下，给出可组合、模拟安全、并在更现实的设备模型下仍能容忍噪声的 DI-OT 与 DI-比特承诺，并进一步编译出通用安全计算协议。

Method: 提出统一的协议骨架，通过每个基本 DI 调用使用全新、隔离的 polylog 数量设备坐标来隔离相关性与泄漏；在隔离实验室且诚实接收方设备进行坐标局部测量的机制下容忍常数速率设备故障，在实验室间存在 polylog 比特自适应泄漏且允许任意联合测量的机制下容忍反多项式速率故障。承诺的提取器通过经典模棱两可通过改变公开奇偶关系实现，保持设备执行及其泄漏不变；用提交并证明功能、分离审计与仿射一致性检查将认证关联连接到理想 OT；发送方安全基于从 Kundu–Tan 两轮阈值定理推导的、针对 Magic Square 博弈的选择器感知并行重复界。

Result: 构造了模拟安全的 DI-OT 与 DI-比特承诺，承诺对双方均有高效模拟器，并可产生带中止的 DI 抛硬币；编译后的 OT 总设备使用为多项式。由于 OT 对安全计算是完备的，该构造给出对任意有限参与方上高效可计算经典功能、在静态腐坏任意真子集下安全、带中止的 DI 协议。

Conclusion: 在后量子单向函数假设下，DI-OT 与比特承诺可在两种现实设备模型中以可组合方式实现，并编译为通用 DI 安全计算；关键在于连接认证关联与理想 OT 的机制以及选择器感知的并行重复安全性证明。

Abstract: Assuming post-quantum one-way functions, we construct device-independent (DI) oblivious transfer (OT) and bit commitment: honest parties use only trusted classical computation to operate untrusted quantum devices, which may share arbitrary entanglement and behave non-IID. Security is simulation-based against quantum polynomial-time adversaries and composes sequentially with efficient simulators. One protocol skeleton serves both, in two regimes. With isolated laboratories and coordinate-local measurements in the honest receiver's device, it tolerates a constant rate of honest-device faults. With polylogarithmically many qubits of adaptive leakage between the laboratories and arbitrary joint measurements, it tolerates an inverse-polylogarithmic rate. Each elementary DI call uses a fresh, isolated batch of polylogarithmically many device coordinates, and total device use in the compiled OT protocol is polynomial. The commitment has efficient simulators against both parties and yields DI coin tossing with abort.
  Because OT is complete for secure computation, the construction yields a DI protocol, with abort, for every efficiently computable classical functionality on a fixed number of parties, secure against static corruption of any proper subset of them.
  The commitment's extractor changes a public parity relation through classical equivocation and leaves the device execution, hence its leakage, unchanged. A commit-and-prove functionality, disjoint audits, and an affine consistency check link the certified correlations to ideal OT. Sender security rests on a selector-aware parallel-repetition bound for the Magic Square game, which we derive from the two-round threshold theorem of Kundu and Tan.

</details>


### [143] [Unflattening by Flattening -- How Input Distributions Shape Output Variance in Angle-Encoded Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01446)
*Melvin Strobl,Gabriel Mejia,Eileen Kuehn,Achim Streit*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究角度编码乘积态下输入分布如何通过"代数输入纯度"影响参数化量子电路的输出方差，证明matchgate电路保持~1/n的输出方差，给出off-diagonal族的零输出判据与经典预处理证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贫瘠高原（barren plateaus）使参数化量子电路的损失梯度随量子比特数指数衰减，阻碍训练。论文旨在揭示输入态分布如何影响输出变化，并寻找可预先判定的、能够避免贫瘠高原的电路与编码设计。

Method: 引入"代数输入纯度"这一概念，即输入态与电路动力学李代数的重叠量，用以刻画输入分布对输出方差的作用。在指定读出方式与Haar或精确群2-design采样下，分析matchgate电路及off-diagonal族的输出方差，用对角Pauli串计数刻画零输出端点，并构造基于共享随机旋转、以数据集平均纯度为门限的经典预处理证书。

Result: 在Haar或精确群2-design采样下，n量子比特matchgate电路对任意纯乘积输入保持量级1/n的输出方差；off-diagonal族在任意深度、任意参数下对计算基输入输出为零；独立均匀角度给出与matchgate同量级的平均方差。对角Pauli串计数可识别这些零输出端点。对任意至少2n个坐标的非零输入固定数据集，可构造经典预处理证书：当数据集平均纯度超过可计算阈值时接受共享随机旋转，从而在电路执行前证明off-diagonal族平均输出方差为1/n量级，且期望试验次数为常数。二进制与三进制加权编码也能由单个均匀标量输入恢复独立角度的平均纯度。

Conclusion: 论文给出了输出方差（对输入平均）的保证，说明成功的可训练性同时依赖任务本身以及编码所保留的信息；数值实验考察了训练行为及向更大代数的推广。该保证仅针对平均输出方差，而非学习任务的端到端可解性。

Abstract: Barren plateaus hinder training of parameterized quantum circuits by making loss gradients exponentially small in the number of qubits. For angle-encoded product states, we show how the input distribution affects output variation through algebraic input purity, i.e. the input's overlap with the circuit's dynamical Lie algebra. With the specified readouts and Haar or exact group 2-design sampling, matchgate circuits on $n$ qubits retain output variance of order $1/n$ for every pure product input. The off-diagonal family instead has zero output on computational-basis inputs at any depth and for every parameter choice. Independent uniform angles yield mean variance of the same order as matchgates. A count of diagonal Pauli strings identifies these zero-output endpoints. For any fixed dataset of nonzero inputs with at least $2n$ coordinates, we construct a classical preprocessing certificate. A shared random rotation is accepted only when the dataset's mean purity passes a computable threshold. This certifies mean output variance of order $1/n$ for the off-diagonal family before circuit execution, with a constant expected number of trials. Binary and ternary weighted encodings also recover the independent-angle mean purity from one uniform scalar input. Numerical experiments examine training behavior and extensions to larger algebras. The guarantee concerns output variance averaged over inputs, while successful learning also depends on the task and information preserved by the encoding.

</details>


### [144] [Unbounded separation between definite and indefinite causal order in finite-dimensional quantum metrology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01462)
*Yanglin Hu,Zi-Shen Li,Giulio Chiribella,Yuxiang Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明在有限维系统中，对于有限样本情形，不定因果序可实现无界优势。具体在基于离散位置与动量算符的几何相位估计中，存在参数N和d=Ω(N²)使得不定序策略所需初始探针能量比任何确定序策略少R倍（R任意大），前提是测量次数ν=O(exp(πd/16)/poly(d))。核心技术工具是离散高斯波包的近似Weyl关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不定因果序在量子计量学中已知能带来优势，包括谐振子几何相位测量中的无界优势。然而该优势局限于无限维情形，有限维类比一直不明确，近期研究甚至暗示有限维中的优势本质上受限于有界常数因子。本文旨在澄清这一问题，证明有限维系统在有限样本情形下同样可以出现任意大的优势。

Method: 作者研究d维量子系统中由离散位置和动量算符生成的两组N个位移所关联的几何相位估计问题。他们建立了离散高斯波包的近似Weyl关系这一技术工具，并据此构造和分析不定因果序策略与确定因果序策略之间的分离。分析在有限测量次数ν=O(exp(πd/16)/poly(d))的样本约束下进行，比较达到相同均方误差时两种策略所需的初始探针能量。

Result: 对于任意给定常数R，存在参数值N和d=Ω(N²)，使得不定序策略使用能量比确定序策略少R倍的初始探针即可达到相同均方误差。即不定序带来的能量节省随问题参数可任意增大，从而在有限维系统中建立了确定序与不定序之间的无界分离，反驳了有限维优势受限于有界常数的观点。

Conclusion: 有限维量子系统在有限样本情形下，不定因果序可实现任意大的计量学优势，表现为能量节省随参数增长而无界。这一结果拓展了不定因果序优势的适用范围，并表明其不局限于无限维设定。所建立的离散高斯波包近似Weyl关系具有独立的技术价值。

Abstract: Indefinite causal order is known to offer enhancements in quantum metrology, notably including an unbounded advantage in the measurement of a geometric phase of a harmonic oscillator. This advantage, however, is specific to the infinite dimensional setting, and its finite dimensional analogue remains elusive, with recent findings suggesting that advantages in finite dimensions may be fundamentally limited to bounded constant factors. Here we show that, in fact, arbitrarily large advantages arise for finite dimensional systems in the finite sample regime. Specifically, we establish an unbounded separation between definite and indefinite causal order in the estimation of a geometric phase associated to two sets of $N$ displacements generated by discrete position and momentum operators on a $d$-dimensional quantum system: for any given constant $R$, there exist values of $N$ and $d=Ω(N^2)$ such that a strategy with indefinite order uses an initial probe with $R$ times less energy than the probe required by every strategy with definite order achieving the same mean squared error, whenever the number of measurement shots $ν$ is bounded as $ν=\mathcal{O}(\exp(πd/16)/\mathrm{poly}(d))$. In other words, indefinite order offers an energy saving that grows arbitrarily large with the parameters of the problem. To prove this result, we establish an approximate Weyl relation for discrete Gaussian wavepackets, which is of independent technical interest.

</details>


### [145] [Zero- Versus Infinite-Temperature Damping in Variational Quantum Circuits: Feature Scale, Sampling Cost, and Frame Gauge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01466)
*Vu-Quoc-Minh Nguyen,Tuan-Vu Truong,Hoang-Long Nguyen,Trung-Khanh Le*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将振幅阻尼(AD)的Pauli twirl理解为无限温下的广义振幅阻尼，通过变分电路对比二者以隔离零温偏置；零温偏置主要作用于特征尺度。随机参数下，AD下特征趋于由末层决定的平台，强阻尼时该平台有闭式且随温度按tanh(ℏω/2kBT)下降，而twirl下特征每层等比例收缩（至多八比特）。可训练输出尺度可消除大部分精度差异，AD最多领先约3个百分点；被消除的差异转为测量代价：10^3次测量/图像时，四比特分类器在p=0.3下AD仍保持在无噪精度1.5个点内，而twirl版本最多损失33个点。弱阻尼下分离深度约按(np)^{-1}ln(1/p)增长。当阻尼跟随完全纠缠层且电路边界可训练时，阻尼方向是规范；在特征值求解器中，它在分解的双方门内部变为物理可观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文动机是厘清振幅阻尼(AD)中非幺正收缩项与零温偏置对变分量子电路的独立影响，并阐明其Pauli twirl（等价于无限温广义振幅阻尼）与AD在训练与测量资源上的差异来源。作者希望隔离零温偏置的作用机制，并解释为何可训练输出尺度会掩盖精度差异却将其转化为测量次数代价。

Method: 方法包括：将AD的Pauli twirl推广/解释为无限温广义振幅阻尼，比较二者在变分电路中的表现；分析随机参数下特征随层数的收缩或平台行为；引入可训练输出尺度；在四比特分类器等任务上用每图像10^3次测量的采样进行训练与测试；并考察阻尼方向在完全纠缠层后与可训练边界情形下的规范性质，以及在分解双方门中特征值求解器里的物理性。

Result: 结果：随机参数下AD的特征趋于平台，强阻尼平台由末层设定并有闭式，固定T1时随温度按tanh(ℏω/2kBT)下降；twirl下特征每层按常数因子收缩（至多八比特）。可训练输出尺度消除大部分精度差距，AD相对twirl最多领先约3个百分点。测量代价上，四比特分类器在p=0.3、10^3次测量/图像时，AD保持在无噪精度1.5个点内，而twirl分类器最多损失33个点。弱阻尼下分离所需深度约按(np)^{-1}ln(1/p)增长。阻尼方向在阻尼跟随完全纠缠层且边界可训练时为规范；在分解双方门内的特征值求解器中变为物理可观测。

Conclusion: 结论：AD与其Pauli twirl的差异可归结为零温偏置对特征尺度的作用；可训练输出尺度能吸收精度差异，但代价转移到测量采样上，使twirl在有限测量下显著劣于AD。阻尼方向的规范/物理属性依赖于是否跟随完全纠缠层、边界是否可训练，以及在具体分解门结构（如特征值求解器）中的实现方式。

Abstract: The Pauli twirl of amplitude damping (AD) is generalized amplitude damping at infinite temperature: it keeps the contraction of AD and removes its non-unital term, so comparing the two in variational circuits isolates the zero-temperature bias, which acts mainly through the scale of the features. For random parameters, features under AD settle on a floor, which at strong damping is set by the last layer, has a closed form, and at fixed $T_1$ falls with temperature as $\tanh(\hbarω/2k_BT)$; under the twirls they shrink by a constant factor per layer, up to eight qubits. A trainable output scale removes most of the resulting accuracy differences, leaving AD ahead of its twirls by at most about three percentage points in our simulations; what it removes reappears as a cost in measurement shots: trained and tested with $10^3$ shots per image, a four-qubit classifier under AD at $p=0.3$ stays within 1.5 points of noiseless accuracy, while the twirled classifiers lose up to 33. At weaker damping the separation depth grows roughly as $(np)^{-1}\ln(1/p)$. The damping direction is a gauge when the damping follows complete entangling layers and the circuit boundaries are trainable; in an eigensolver it becomes physical inside a decomposed two-qubit gate.

</details>


### [146] [Continuous-Process Randomized Compilation for Quantum Process Tensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01521)
*He Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将离散随机编译(RC)推广到连续时间记忆环境，在连续过程张量(cPT)框架下构建连续过程随机编译(CPRC)，统一随机幺正控制轨迹、点态协变有限时长仪器与环境动力学，并给出协变性、误差历史对角化及总变差误bound等理论结果与数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离散随机编译仅在逻辑门边界施加随机控制帧，无法刻画仪器内部的控制演化或持续的系统-环境耦合；在带记忆环境中，误差在时间上相关而非局限于单门。因此需要将RC从离散电路推广至连续时间、带记忆的量子过程。

Method: 在连续过程张量(cPT)框架下构建连续过程随机编译(CPRC)，将随机幺正控制轨迹、点态协变有限时长仪器与环境动力学统一到同一时间轴；研究四种协议：固定Pauli刷新、有界Cayley路径、幺正布朗运动、Ornstein-Uhlenbeck驱动；分析点态协变性、区间独立Pauli共轭对角化误差历史，并推导全自适应输出记录的总变差误差界。

Result: (1) 点态协变性保证无系统-环境耦合时每条轨迹精确复现目标逻辑，闭帧反例证明仅端点补偿会导致一阶仪器误差；(2) 全区间独立Pauli共轭在边界对角化误差历史，但经典标签保留环境诱导关联，并由精确静态浴解验证；(3) 在有界居中耦合与混合条件下得到全自适应输出记录的总变差误差界；证明固定粒子扇区中轨迹平均cPT系数在有限非高斯环境及具时间可积协方差的高斯浴(含未截断Drude谱)下收敛；并在qubit模型上以输出概率、算子条件互信息及双粒子系数数值验证(qubit与热Drude浴)。

Conclusion: 该工作将随机编译从离散电路拓展到连续时间带记忆量子过程，为非马尔可夫误差定制提供了统一框架、定量工具、可实施方案与基准。

Abstract: Traditional discrete randomized compiling (RC) tailors errors with random control frames at logical-gate boundaries, but fails to capture intra-instrument control evolution or persistent system-environment coupling. In memory-bearing environments, errors correlate across times, not just single gates. We build continuous-process randomized compilation (CPRC) in the continuous process tensor (cPT) framework, unifying random unitary control trajectories, pointwise-covariant finite-duration instruments, and environmental dynamics on one time axis. We study four protocols: fixed Pauli refresh, bounded Cayley paths, unitary Brownian motion, Ornstein-Uhlenbeck driving. We obtain three key results: (1) Pointwise covariance ensures each trajectory exactly reproduces the target logic without system-environment coupling; a closed-frame counterexample proves endpoint-only compensation causes first-order instrument errors. (2) Independent Pauli conjugations over full intervals diagonalize error histories at boundaries, but classical labels retain environment-induced correlations, confirmed by an exact static-bath solution. (3) We derive a total-variation error bound for full adaptive output records under bounded centered coupling and mixing conditions. We prove convergence of trajectory-averaged cPT coefficients in fixed particle sectors for finite non-Gaussian environments and Gaussian baths with time-integrable covariance, including the uncut Drude spectrum. We numerically validate the theory in a qubit model via output probabilities, operator conditional mutual information, and all two-particle coefficients for both qubit and thermal Drude baths. This work extends RC from discrete circuits to continuous-time memory-bearing quantum processes, offering a unified framework, quantitative tools, implementable schemes, and benchmarks for non-Markovian error tailoring.

</details>


### [147] [Efficient learning of quantum interactions from thermal metastable states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01538)
*Bingrun Wang,Qi Ye,Chi-Fang Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究从有限温度多体系统的亚稳态（而非理想Gibbs态）中学习量子相互作用，提出了物理与算法一致的替代框架，并达到了近最优的样本与计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从有限温度多体系统学习量子相互作用是新兴量子平台的核心任务。尽管已有从晶格量子Gibbs态学习的严格高效协议，但精确Gibbs态作为输入前提在计算上不可准备，且未必能忠实刻画一般有限温度量子系统；而与热浴耦合的系统往往在真正平衡前长期停留在近似稳定的亚稳态。因此需要一个更具物理一致性的学习框架。

Method: 本文将学习问题形式化为从由系统-热浴相互作用产生的细致平衡主方程（Lindbladian）的亚稳态中学习，提炼Gibbs态学习背后的算法机制与结构条件，并将其完整推广至亚稳态情形；同时细化亚稳态的概念，建立有限温度学习的统一框架。

Result: 所提方法在系统规模和精度上均达到近最优的样本复杂度与计算复杂度，实现了对亚稳态学习的严格高效协议。

Conclusion: 研究为从亚稳态进行有限温度量子学习提供了物理与算法一致的框架，拓展了Gibbs态学习理论，并统一了有限温度学习的相关概念。

Abstract: Learning quantum interactions from finite-temperature many-body systems is a central task in emerging quantum platforms. Recently, the problem of learning from lattice quantum Gibbs states has found rigorous, efficient protocols. Nevertheless, exact Gibbs states, as the input premise, are in fact computationally intractable to prepare and may not faithfully represent generic finite-temperature quantum systems. In contrast, a system coupled to a heat bath can be stuck at an approximate stationary state (metastable state) long before it truly equilibrates. Here, we formulate a physically and algorithmically consistent alternative: learning from such metastable states of detailed-balanced master equations (Lindbladians) arising from system-bath interactions. We distill the algorithmic mechanism and structural condition underlying Gibbs-state learning and extend it in full to metastable states, attaining nearly optimal sample and computational complexity (in the system size and the precision). More broadly, we sharpen notions of metastability and develop a unified framework for finite-temperature learning.

</details>


### [148] [Molecular Dynamics with Nuclear Effects on Quantum Computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01549)
*Lukas Haßfurth,Juliane Heitkämper,Elias Walter,Birger Horstmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种结合核电子轨道方法的混合量子-经典从头算分子动力学算法，用量子计算机上的变分量子本征求解器计算基态能量与力、经典更新几何构型，在H₂、H₂O和Zundel离子上的模拟表明纳入核量子效应后谱频误差显著改善，且紧凑硬件高效线路可达到UCCSD水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核量子效应对描述质子转移和氢键至关重要，但在经典硬件上将其纳入量子化学计算通常计算代价过高。量子计算机因空间需求随体系规模线性增长而成为有前景的替代方案，因此需要发展能有效利用量子硬件的从头算分子动力学方法。

Method: 提出一种新颖的混合量子-经典从头算分子动力学算法，通过核电子轨道（NEO）方法纳入核量子效应；在量子计算机上使用变分量子本征求解器（VQE）评估基态能量和力，分子几何构型则在经典端更新；并采用紧凑的硬件高效拟设（ansatz）线路。

Result: 通过对H₂、H₂O和Zundel离子H₅O₂⁺的模拟验证该方法，将模拟振动谱与实验数据对比：纳入核量子效应后，Zundel离子中的质子穿梭运动变为有效无势垒，模拟频率误差显著改善；使用紧凑硬件高效拟设线路可获得与更精确的UCCSD拟设相当的结果。

Conclusion: 该混合量子-经典NEO-VQE分子动力学算法能有效纳入核量子效应并显著提升振动谱精度，且紧凑硬件高效线路的表现接近UCCSD，表明该算法有望在近期量子设备上执行。

Abstract: Nuclear quantum effects are critical for describing proton transfer and hydrogen bonding, but their incorporation into quantum chemistry calculations is often computationally prohibitive on classical hardware. A promising alternative are quantum computers due to their linear scaling in the space requirements with system size. We introduce a novel hybrid quantum-classical algorithm for ab-initio molecular dynamics that incorporates nuclear quantum effects via the nuclear-electronic orbital method. The proposed algorithm evaluates ground state energies and forces on the quantum computer using a variational quantum eigensolver, while the molecular geometries are updated classically. We validate our approach through simulations of $\text{H}_2$, $\text{H}_2\text{O}$ and the Zundel ion $\text{H}_5\text{O}_2^+$, comparing the simulated vibrational spectra with experimental data. Upon inclusion of nuclear quantum effects, the proton shuttling movement in the Zundel ion becomes effectively barrierless, and errors in the simulated frequencies improve significantly. Employing compact hardware-efficient ansatz circuits we achieve results comparable to the more accurate UCCSD ansatzes, which hints towards the feasibility of executing our algorithm on near-term quantum devices.

</details>


### [149] [Efficient certification of time-reversal symmetry requires entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01555)
*Zhenhuan Liu,Zhenyu Du,Yifan Tang,Zi-Wen Liu,Jens Eisert,Ingo Roth*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅使用前向访问与可信量子操作的类Bell不等式时间反演对称性检验，将时间输入-输出关系通过纠缠转化为可测的空间交换对称性，并证明在n量子比特幺正动力学中区分时间反演对称圆系综与Haar随机动力学需要Ω(min{2^{n/2},2^{n-e}})次查询，而最大纠缠探针与SWAP测量可将其降至常数次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间反演对称性是物理学基本原理，描述物理定律在时间方向反转下的不变性，但如何在仅具备前向访问能力、无法直接反转时间的实验条件下对其进行检验，是一个基础且具有挑战性的问题。作者希望建立纠缠与时间反演对称性之间的定量联系，将量子信息科学中的纠缠资源与基础物理中的对称性概念相桥接。

Method: 作者将时间反演这一反幺正对称性形式化为类Bell不等式的检验：利用纠缠把时间的输入-输出关系映射为空间上的交换对称性，从而仅借助前向访问和可信量子操作即可测量。针对n量子比特幺正动力学，作者分析区分时间反演对称圆系综与Haar随机动力学所需的查询复杂度，并给出任意经典自适应协议下的下界Ω(min{2^{n/2},2^{n-e}})，其中e=min{e_s,e_m}，e_s与e_m分别为探针和测量的对数纠缠负度。此外，作者发展了针对任意固定、相容探针与测量的时间反演对称性检验，并将其查询复杂度与对数纠缠负度相关联，通过优化探针与测量在高纠缠区域匹配下界标度。

Result: 对于n量子比特幺正动力学，任意经典自适应协议可靠区分时间反演对称圆系综与Haar随机动力学需要Ω(min{2^{n/2},2^{n-e}})次查询，其中e由探针与测量的对数纠缠负度决定；采用最大纠缠探针与SWAP测量时，该查询代价可降至常数次。对于任意固定且相容的探针与测量，作者给出其时间反演对称性检验并建立查询复杂度与对数纠缠负度的关系，且在高纠缠区域通过优化探针与测量达到与下界一致的标度。

Conclusion: 本文建立了纠缠与时间反演对称性之间的定量联系，表明纠缠是检验时间反演对称性的关键资源，从而桥接了量子信息科学与基础物理中的两个核心概念。该框架为在仅有前向访问的量子系统中检验反幺正对称性提供了可操作的类Bell不等式方法，并给出了查询复杂度的严格下界与可达上界。

Abstract: Time-reversal symmetry is a fundamental principle of physics describing the invariance of physical laws under reversal of the direction of time. We formulate a Bell-inequality-like test of this antiunitary symmetry using only forward access and trusted quantum operations: entanglement converts temporal input--output relations into measurable spatial exchange symmetry. For $n$-qubit unitary dynamics, we prove that reliably distinguishing the time-reversal-symmetric circular ensembles from Haar-random dynamics requires $Ω(\min\{2^{n/2},2^{n-e}\})$ queries for any classically adaptive protocol. Here, $e=\min\{e_{\mathrm s},e_{\mathrm m}\}$ with $e_{\mathrm s}$ and $e_{\mathrm m}$ representing the probe and measurement logarithmic entanglement negativities, respectively. Maximally entangled probes and SWAP measurements reduce this cost to a constant number of queries. Furthermore, we develop a time-reversal symmetry test for arbitrary fixed, compatible probes and measurements, relate its query complexity to their logarithmic negativities, and match the lower-bound scaling in the high-entanglement regime by optimizing the probe and measurement. Our results establish a quantitative connection between entanglement and time-reversal symmetry, bridging two central concepts in quantum information science and fundamental physics.

</details>


### [150] [Adversarial Robustness in Fake Quantum Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01574)
*Marc Maußner,Volker Reers*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究噪声模型仿真器上量子机器学习模型的性能扩展性与对抗鲁棒性，分两阶段：基准测试Qiskit Aer仿真引擎在不同硬件架构下的计算吞吐量；评估在真实噪声条件下PGD攻击与对抗重训练策略的效果，量化中等规模仿真（最高8量子比特）的运行时权衡与扩展行为，并证明50/50的高对抗样本重训练比例对4量子比特分类器实现实用鲁棒性至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习模型在真实量子硬件上受噪声影响，对抗鲁棒性存疑；同时大规模量子仿真受计算资源限制，需明确仿真性能的扩展边界。因此需在噪声模型仿真器环境下同时考察QML的计算可扩展性与对抗攻击下的鲁棒性，为实际部署提供依据。

Method: 采用双阶段实验设计：第一阶段在多种硬件架构上对Qiskit Aer仿真引擎进行基准测试，测量计算吞吐量与运行时权衡；第二阶段在真实噪声条件下，对量子分类器实施投影梯度下降（PGD）对抗攻击，并采用对抗重训练策略（对比不同对抗样本与良性样本比例）。

Result: 量化了中等规模仿真（最高8量子比特）的运行时权衡与扩展行为；发现在真实噪声条件下，4量子比特分类器需要50/50的高对抗与良性样本重训练比例，才能实现实用的模型鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 噪声模型仿真器上的QML模型在可扩展性上受仿真规模制约，而对抗鲁棒性高度依赖重训练中对抗样本比例；高比例对抗重训练（50/50）是4量子比特分类器在真实噪声条件下获得实用鲁棒性的必要条件。

Abstract: This paper investigates the performance scalability and adversarial robustness of Quantum Machine Learning (QML) models deployed on noise-model-based fake simulators. We conduct a dual-phased study, first benchmarking the computational throughput of Qiskit's Aer simulation engine across varying hardware architectures, and second, evaluating the effectiveness of Projected Gradient Descent (PGD) attacks and adversarial retraining strategies under realistic noise conditions. Our results quantify the runtime tradeoffs and scaling behavior for medium-scale simulations (projected up to 8 qubits) and demonstrate that high adversarial-to-benign retraining ratios (50/50) are essential for achieving practical model robustness for 4-qubit classifiers under realistic noise conditions.

</details>


### [151] [Electromagnetic Side-Channel Vulnerability in QKD Equipment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01609)
*Mikio Fujiwara,Katsumi Fujii,Fumie Ono,Masakazu Ono,Akihisa Tomita*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 测量了基于时间仓诱饵态BB84协议（Y、Z基）的QKD原型设备在0 m和3 m处的电磁泄漏，发现发射端为主要泄漏源，且在1241.6 MHz时钟三次谐波处可区分不同量子态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际QKD网络节点可能遭受电磁泄漏等侧信道攻击，需评估原型设备的电磁辐射并判断其是否暴露量子态信息。

Method: 在微波暗室内对实现时间仓诱饵态BB84协议（Y、Z基）的QKD原型设备进行电磁测量，分别采集距发射端和接收端0 m与3 m处的电磁频谱及波形。

Result: 发射端是主要的电磁泄漏源；在1241.6 MHz时钟的三次谐波频率上，不同量子态产生的电磁辐射存在明显差异。

Conclusion: QKD原型设备存在可被利用的电磁侧信道，时钟谐波处的态相关泄漏可能威胁实际QKD网络节点的密钥安全。

Abstract: Actual quantum key distribution network nodes may be vulnerable to side-channel attacks such as electromagnetic leakage. In this study, we measured electromagnetic emissions from a prototype quantum key distribution device implementing a time-bin decoy-state BB84 protocol with Y and Z bases. We measured the electromagnetic spectra and waveforms at distances of 0 m and 3 m from the transmitter and receiver inside an anechoic chamber. The results showed that the transmitter was the primary source of electromagnetic leakage, and distinct differences were observed between the quantum states generated at the third harmonic of the 1241.6 MHz clock.

</details>


### [152] [A Code-Agnostic Graph Neural Network Decoder from the Detection Error Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01683)
*Federico Alberto Astolfi,Guido Pupillo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出PolyMechanon——一种仅以检测错误模型（DEM）为输入的GNN量子纠错解码器，统一解码任意稳定子码，性能优于相关MWPM与BP+OSD，且解码时间与物理错误率无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子纠错解码器通常针对特定码型与噪声模型定制（如MWPM依赖匹配图结构、BP+OSD依赖码的Tanner图），缺乏跨码型、跨噪声模型的通用性，且部分解码器（如BP+OSD）解码时间随物理错误率增长，难以满足实时解码需求。作者希望设计一种"以码为数据、而非设计选择"的通用解码架构，仅需更换DEM即可适配新码与新噪声模型。

Method: 将量子码在给定噪声模型下的DEM表示为"探测器—错误机制—逻辑观测量"的三部图，输入特征全部由量子码数据计算而非人工设计；采用图神经网络作为解码器，同一架构对任意可表达为DEM的稳定子码与噪声模型通用，只需在对应DEM上训练。

Result: 在旋转表面码上，现象学与电路级噪声下均优于相关MWPM，逻辑失败最多减少25%；在高码率qLDPC码族上，小码匹配BP+OSD、大码超越之，[[130,4,6]]上逻辑失败减少16%；解码时间不依赖物理错误率（BP+OSD则随之增长），[[130,4,6]]上单GPU单次有效开销为几毫秒，而单CPU核BP+OSD为几十毫秒，固定计算图适合中性原子处理器的实时解码；跨码族单一模型在训练所见图状码上优于非相关MWPM，在其余码上距BP+OSD 10%以内，但对未见码（尤其更大码）泛化较差；概率输出支持基于置信度的后选择，表面码上逻辑错误率降低一个数量级以上且保留90%以上样本。

Conclusion: 解码器仅依赖DEM作为输入，新码型与新噪声模型无需重新设计架构，仅替换DEM即可解码，展现出通用性、实时解码潜力与后选择能力；主要局限是跨码族泛化性不足，对未见过的更大码易失效。

Abstract: We present POLYMECHANON, a graph neural network (GNN) decoder for quantum error correction whose only input is the detection error model (DEM) of a quantum code under a given noise model. We represent the DEM as a tripartite graph of detectors, error mechanisms and logical observables, where every input feature is computed by using the quantum code as data rather than design choice. In this way, the same architecture decodes in principle any stabiliser code, under any noise model that can be expressed as a detection error model, once trained on the DEM. We test this approach along four directions. Firstly, on the rotated surface code the decoder outperforms correlated MWPM under both phenomenological and circuit-level noise, with up to $25\%$ fewer logical failures. Secondly, on a family of high-rate qLDPC codes it matches BP+OSD on the smaller codes and surpasses it on the larger ones, with up to $16\%$ fewer logical failures on $[\![130,4,6]\!]$. Thirdly, its decoding time does not depend on the physical error rate, whereas that of BP+OSD grows with it: on $[\![130,4,6]\!]$ its effective cost per shot is of the order of a few ms on a single GPU against a few tens of milliseconds for BP+OSD on a single CPU core, and its fixed computational graph makes it a candidate for real-time decoding on neutral-atom processors. Finally, a single model trained across codes of different families outperforms uncorrelated MWPM on the graph-like codes seen during training and stays within $10\%$ of BP+OSD on the others, while it tends to fail to generalize to unseen codes, particularly larger ones. Its probabilistic output enables confidence-based post-selection, lowering the logical error rate by more than an order of magnitude on the surface code while keeping more than $90\%$ of the shots. Since only the DEM changes, new codes and noise models can be decoded without redesigning the decoder.

</details>


### [153] [Mentor-Initiated Asymmetric Bidirectional Quantum Teleportation Protocol for Arbitrary Qubit States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01684)
*Sugata Adhya,Benchao Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由导师发起的双向非对称量子隐形传态协议，突破了现有方案对被传两量子比特态形式的限制，允许任意两量子比特态从Bob传至Alice。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导师发起方案将被传输的两量子比特态限定为Bell类形式α|00⟩+β|11⟩，通用性受限，无法处理任意两量子比特态，因此需要推广至更一般的传输情形。

Method: 在导师发起框架下设计双向非对称量子隐形传态协议；利用标准量子门构造所需纠缠资源；并通过Qiskit中的量子线路实现对该协议进行说明。

Result: 协议能够将任意两量子比特态在导师发起框架下从Bob隐形传态至Alice，且所需纠缠资源可由标准量子门构造，并给出了Qiskit量子线路实现。

Conclusion: 该协议推广了导师发起双向非对称量子隐形传态中可传输态的范围，从Bell类态扩展至任意两量子比特态，增强了方案的通用性与可实现性。

Abstract: In this paper, a mentor-initiated bidirectional asymmetric quantum teleportation protocol is proposed. Unlike existing schemes that restrict the two-qubit state transmitted from Bob to Alice to a Bell-like form $α|00\rangle+β|11\rangle$, our protocol allows an arbitrary two-qubit state to be teleported from Bob to Alice within the mentor-initiated framework. The required entangled resources are constructed using standard quantum gates, and the protocol is illustrated through its quantum-circuit implementation in Qiskit.

</details>


### [154] [Predicting electric-field noise in ion traps using fluctuation electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01693)
*Markus Teller,Da An,Alberto M. Alonso,Philip C. Holz,Philipp Schindler,Hartmut Häffner,Tracy E. Northup*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种预测离子阱中由介质与金属材料体内涨落引起的电场噪声的方法，仅需材料能量损耗作为参数，适用于任意几何阱体；应用于带悬浮电极的离子阱并与典型表面阱设计对比分析电极屏蔽效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微加工表面离子阱中的电场噪声会导致囚禁离子量子计算机的门操作错误，但该噪声此前难以预测，缺乏可用的理论模型。

Method: 构建从介质与金属材料体内涨落出发的电场噪声预测模型，模型适用于任意阱几何结构，且唯一相关材料属性为能量损耗；将该方法应用于含电学悬浮电极的离子阱，并推广到典型表面阱设计，分析电极结构的噪声屏蔽作用。

Result: 在含悬浮电极的离子阱中，介质的贡献占主导；模型预测噪声与实验观测值相差在四倍以内；对典型表面阱设计的分析给出电极结构对噪声的屏蔽规律。

Conclusion: 该模型能够以较少材料参数预测任意几何离子阱的电场噪声，并可用于评估与优化电极设计对噪声的屏蔽效果。

Abstract: Electric-field noise in microfabricated surface ion traps contributes to gate errors in trapped-ion quantum computers, but this noise has been challenging to predict. Here, we present a method to predict the electric-field noise arising from fluctuations in the bulk of dielectric and metallic materials. The method is valid for arbitrary trap geometries, and the only relevant material property is energy loss. We apply it to an ion trap with an electrically floating electrode and find the contribution from dielectrics to be dominant. Our model predicts the observed noise for this trap within a factor of four. In addition, we apply our model to typical surface trap designs and analyze how the electrode structures shield the noise.

</details>


### [155] [Efficiently Optimizing the Quantum Value of Bell Inequalities using Batched Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01699)
*Xinyu Xu,Ping Zhu,Weikang Li,Pierre Pocreau,Dalu Ding,Dawei Ding*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于批量梯度下降(BGD)的贝尔不等式量子值优化器,可高效处理超千输入/输出的贝尔不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究大规模贝尔不等式依赖可随参与方、输入、输出数量有效扩展的数值方法,可用于超越解析方法的大规模不等式研究、实验设计及随机性生成、受限通信多智能体协调等应用。然而现有通用优化器如see-saw法在仅十几个输入输出时即难以处理。

Method: 提出BGD优化器:在可行量子策略空间进行可微搜索,态与投影测量由无约束变量生成;贝尔表达式通过直接张量缩并求值,避免构造过于庞大的贝尔算子,使并行随机重启成本低廉,天然适配GPU。同时在GPU上枚举策略或将优化表述为混合整数线性规划以计算经典值。

Result: 与see-saw法对比,在多种贝尔不等式族上取得显著加速;GPU实现下,可在数分钟内优化含超千输入或输入的贝尔不等式。

Conclusion: 该优化器可作为贝尔实验及大规模输入输出贝尔不等式建模的多智能体协调问题的数值评估工具,适用于高频交易、分布式系统等实际场景。

Abstract: Exploring the world of Bell inequalities requires numerical methods that scale effectively with the number of parties, inputs, and outputs. Such methods can be used to study large-scale Bell inequalities beyond the reach of analytic methods, to design experiments for violating more complicated Bell inequalities, and to enable applications of Bell inequality violation. Such applications include randomness generation and multi-agent coordination with restricted communication. However, current general purpose optimizers such as the see-saw method struggle to handle Bell inequalities with only a dozen inputs and outputs. We introduce an optimizer for the quantum value of a Bell inequality based on batched gradient descent (BGD). More precisely, our BGD optimizer is a differentiable search over feasible quantum strategies in which states and projective measurements are generated from unconstrained variables. The Bell expression is evaluated by a direct tensor contraction without forming the prohibitively large Bell operator. This formulation makes parallel random restarts inexpensive and is thus naturally implemented by a GPU. We evaluate our optimizer against the see-saw method and find a significant speedup for a wide variety of Bell inequality families. Implemented on a GPU, our BGD optimizer can optimize Bell inequalities in our numerical experiments with more than a thousand inputs or outputs within minutes. For these large-scale Bell inequalities, we also efficiently compute the classical value by enumerating possible strategies using a GPU or expressing the optimization as a mixed-integer linear program. Our optimizers can serve as numerical evaluation tools for Bell experiments and multi-agent coordination problems modeled by Bell inequalities with many inputs and outputs, a common feature of real-world scenarios such as high frequency trading and distributed systems.

</details>


### [156] [Less precise but less noisy: local circuits for momentum-space state preparation and measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01704)
*Etienne Granet,Henrik Dreyer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文指出量子算法优化不应只看门数或电路深度，还需关注噪声敏感性；在H2量子计算机上，对于紧束缚链基态制备，当系统尺寸N超过某阈值后，绝热演化在相同门数和深度下比FFT达到显著更低能量；作者将FFT的高噪声敏感性归因于其高动量分辨率（1/N）需要实空间长程耦合，从而加速误差传播；并提出一种精度较低但成本更低、噪声更小的动量测量方案，在谱函数测量中表现优于FFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子算法优化通常以门数或电路深度为指标，但作者发现这些指标不足以刻画算法在含噪量子硬件上的实际性能。论文旨在揭示噪声敏感性是量子算法设计中被忽视的关键维度，并解释高精度（高动量分辨率）为何会带来更高的噪声敏感性。

Method: 在Quantinuum System Model H2量子计算机上，对紧束缚链基态制备问题，在相同门数和相同电路深度条件下，比较绝热演化与Fermionic Fourier Transform（FFT）的性能。通过分析FFT的动量分辨率（可分辨1/N的动量）与实空间长程耦合需求，建立高分辨率与误差快速传播之间的联系。进一步设计一种动量测量方案，其精度低于FFT但成本更低、噪声更小，并在谱函数测量任务上进行对比验证。

Result: 对于紧束缚链基态制备，存在系统尺寸N的阈值，超过该阈值后绝热演化在相同门数和电路深度下达到比FFT显著更低的能量。FFT的高噪声敏感性源于其高动量分辨率：分辨1/N的动量需要实空间长程耦合，导致误差传播更快；而绝热演化等局域物理电路动量分辨率较粗，但误差传播更慢。所提出的低成本、低噪声动量测量方案在H2量子计算机上的谱函数测量中性能优于FFT。

Conclusion: 量子算法设计应超越门数和电路深度的传统优化目标，将降低噪声敏感性作为核心考量。对于物理应用，高动量分辨率通常并非必需，往往值得用其换取更低的噪声敏感性。局域且物理的电路虽动量分辨率较粗，但在含噪硬件上可能更具优势。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms are usually optimized for gate count or circuit depth. We find on Quantinuum System Model H2 quantum computer that for a tight-binding chain ground state preparation, there is a system size $N$ beyond which the adiabatic evolution reaches significantly lower energies than the Fermionic Fourier Transform (FFT), with the same number of gates, and with the same circuit depth. We attribute this high noise sensitivity of the FFT to its high precision, being able to distinguish momenta by $1/N$. This high resolution in momentum space requires long-range couplings in real space, which propagates errors faster. In contrast, although local and physical circuits such as the adiabatic evolution have a coarser momentum resolution, they also propagate errors more slowly. For physical applications, high momentum resolution is rarely required and is often worth trading for low noise sensitivity. We also introduce a momentum measurement scheme that although less precise than FFT, is less costly and less noisy. We show that it achieves better performance than FFT for spectral function measurement on Quantinuum System Model H2 quantum computer. Our work emphasizes the importance of reducing the noise sensitivity of quantum algorithms, beyond the number of gates or circuit depth.

</details>


### [157] [Near-optimal quantum query lower bounds on bipartiteness and expansion testing in the bounded-degree graph model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01752)
*Chandrima Kayal,Sayantan Sen,Dániel Szabó*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在有界度模型下研究二部性和扩张性测试的量子查询复杂度，证明了这两个问题紧致的 Õ(N^{1/3}) 量子查询下界，完整刻画了其量子查询复杂度（至多对数因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在经典设定下，二部性和扩张性测试均需要 Õ(√N) 次查询；量子设定下前作给出 Õ(N^{1/3}) 算法及扩张性的 Õ(N^{1/4}) 下界，但二部性是否能获得指数级量子优势悬而未决，且十余年无进展。

Method: 沿用多项式方法，但引入与主问题通过归约相关联的中间问题，并对所产生的多项式进行更精确的分析，从而得到近最优下界。

Result: 证明了二部性和扩张性测试的 Õ(N^{1/3}) 量子查询下界，与已知上界匹配。

Conclusion: 两个问题的量子查询复杂度被完全刻画（至多对数因子），并否定了二部性测试存在指数级量子优势的可能。

Abstract: In this work, we study bipartiteness and expansion testing, two canonical problems in graph property testing in the bounded-degree model through the lens of quantum query complexity. In the classical setting, it is known that $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{N})$ queries are necessary and sufficient for both these testing problems (Goldreich and Ron, 1999, 2000 & 2002), where $N$ denotes the number of vertices of the input graph. Due to their significance, (Ambainis, Childs, and Liu, 2011) initiated the study of these problems in the quantum setting and designed quantum algorithms for bipartiteness and expansion testing that perform $\widetilde{O}(N^{1/3})$ queries, showing a polynomial speedup. They also proved that $\widetildeΩ(N^{1/4})$ queries are necessary for expansion testing, but the possibility of an exponential quantum advantage for bipartiteness testing remained open. Despite significant effort, there has been no improvement in these results in the last decade and a half. In this work, we prove essentially tight $\widetildeΩ(N^{1/3})$ quantum query lower bounds for both bipartiteness and expansion testing, thereby completely characterizing the quantum query complexity of these problems up to polylogarithmic factors. While our proofs use the polynomial method similarly to Ambainis, Childs, and Liu, we use intermediate problems that we relate to the main problems via reductions, and perform a more precise analysis of the resulting polynomials, leading to the near-optimal lower bounds.

</details>


### [158] [Efficient quantum phase estimation with adaptive entanglement-assisted Hadamard test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01772)
*Hengzhun Chen,Benchi Zhao,Yingzhou Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出自适应纠缠辅助Hadamard检验（AEHT）算法，通过迭代精化参考相位实现渐进增强的相位放大，在器件重启次数度量下高效完成高精度量子相位估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纠缠辅助Hadamard检验（EHT）通过放大相位信号估计量子相位，但其可实现的放大倍率根本上受限于参考相位的精度，导致在高精度区间效率低下；此外在非理想本征态制备情形下，传统EHT存在不可避免的系统性偏差。

Method: 提出自适应纠缠辅助Hadamard检验（AEHT）算法，迭代地精化参考相位，使放大强度随迭代逐步增强；针对本征态制备不完美的情形抑制系统性偏差；并从物理实现角度采用器件重启次数（device-restart count）而非采样次数（shot count）衡量相位估计代价。

Result: 数值实验证实，在该代价度量下，所提AEHT相对传统方法更为有效，能够充分发挥大纠缠态的放大能力。

Conclusion: AEHT为近期量子处理器上高效估计高精度量子相位提供了可行方案，释放了大纠缠态的全部放大潜力。

Abstract: The entanglement-assisted Hadamard test (EHT) is a practical method for estimating a quantum phase by amplifying the phase signal. However, the feasible amplification is fundamentally limited by the accuracy of the reference phase, such that the method is inefficient in the high-precision regime. In this work, we propose an algorithm, called adaptive entanglement-assisted Hadamard test (AEHT), that iteratively refines the reference phase, enabling progressively stronger amplification as the iteration goes by. We further consider the imperfect eigenstate preparation scenario, where a systematic bias is unavoidable when estimating the quantum phase with the conventional EHT. Such a bias can be suppressed by the proposed AEHT. Moreover, taking physical implementation into consideration, we adopt device-restart count to measure the cost of quantum phase estimation, rather than shot count. The numerical experiments confirm the effectiveness of the proposed AEHT compared with conventional methods under this measure. By unlocking the full amplification power of large entangled states, this work offers an efficient method to estimate high-precision quantum phase on near-term quantum processors.

</details>


### [159] [QUFIG: GNN-Based Prediction of Quantum Fault Injection Vulnerabilities with Gate-Level Precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01777)
*Shihan Zhao,Qiying Li,Ben Dong,Qian Wang,Yuntao Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出 QUFIG，将门级故障分析建模为受限保真度预算下的学习引导优先级排序问题，用基于电路 DAG 的 GNN 预测每个门-故障对的脆弱性得分并排序，从而更高效地定位高影响脆弱点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子硬件规模扩大与可访问性提升，暴露了云量子计算工作流中的可靠性安全问题，如运行时故障注入攻击。现有工作无法以门级精度识别脆弱性，也难以适应运行时环境。

Method: 将门级故障分析形式化为受限保真度预算下的学习引导优先级排序问题；框架采用基于电路 DAG 的 GNN 骨干网络，预测每个门对每种注入故障类型的脆弱性得分（定义为门-故障对对电路保真度的影响），随后按脆弱性得分对门-故障对进行排序。

Result: 在 QASMbench 和 HamLib MaxCut 上的实验表明，QUFIG 比随机和基于深度的启发式方法用更少的检查次数即可恢复高影响的脆弱门-故障对；在保持有效故障识别的同时，将需检查的门数量减少 2.9%–19.8%。

Conclusion: QUFIG 使量子电路设计者能够更高效地识别脆弱性并施加针对性防御。

Abstract: The growing scale and accessibility of quantum hardware exposed new reliability and security challenges in the quantum computing workflow, such as the run-time fault injection attacks in cloud-based quantum computing platforms. However, existing works fail to identify vulnerabilities with gate-level precision or adapt to run-time environments. In this work, we formulate gate-level fault analysis as a learning-guided prioritization problem under restricted fidelity budgets. The framework uses a circuit-DAG-based GNN backbone to predict the vulnerability score of each gate to each type of injected fault, defined as the impact of the gate-fault pair on circuit fidelity. The gate-fault pairs are then ranked by their vulnerability score. Experiments on QASMbench and HamLib MaxCut show that QUFIG recovers high-impact vulnerable gate-fault pairs with fewer inspections than random and depth-based heuristics. Our results show that QUFIG can reduce the number of gates requiring inspection by 2.9--19.8% while maintaining effective fault identification, allowing quantum circuit designers to identify vulnerabilities and apply targeted defenses more efficiently.

</details>


### [160] [Non-Markovian effects on informational steady states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01782)
*Jacob Werner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在连续监测碰撞模型框架下研究非马尔可夫效应对信息稳态（ISS）的影响，发现稳态单次测量信息增益与非马尔可夫度呈负相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息稳态（ISS）已在马尔可夫设定下得到研究，但记忆效应如何影响其形成及稳态性质仍不清楚，因此有必要探讨非马尔可夫效应对ISS的作用。

Method: 采用连续监测碰撞模型，将环境建模为一系列双量子比特辅助粒子（ancilla），并通过数值方法分析系统-辅助粒子及辅助粒子-辅助粒子相互作用参数的影响。

Result: 在该特定模型中，稳态单次测量的信息增益与非马尔可夫度呈负相关；数值结果进一步刻画了系统-辅助粒子与辅助粒子-辅助粒子相互作用参数如何同时影响非马尔可夫度和稳态信息增益。

Conclusion: 记忆效应（非马尔可夫性）会削弱该模型中的稳态信息增益，且其程度可通过碰撞模型的相互作用参数调控。

Abstract: Informational steady states (ISSs) arise when the rate at which information is gained from continuous measurements is exactly balanced by the rate at which information from earlier measurements is lost to the environment. While ISSs have been studied in Markovian settings, relatively little is known about how memory effects influence their formation and steady-state properties. In this work, we investigate non-Markovian effects on ISSs within the framework of continuously monitored collision models. We model the environment as a sequence of two-qubit ancillas and find that, for this specific model, the steady-state information gain per measurement is negatively correlated with the degree of non-Markovianity. Numerical results further characterize how the system-ancilla and ancilla-ancilla interaction parameters influence both the degree of non-Markovianity and the steady-state information gain.

</details>


### [161] [Learnt Attacks on Quantum Key Distribution under Channel Noise and Device Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01792)
*Marcel Mordarski,Benjamin Gras,Abdelrahman Shehata,Daniel Budina,Roberto Bondesan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将自适应窃听建模为带预算约束的马尔可夫决策过程，在信道漂移（Ornstein–Uhlenbeck 噪声）下联合搜索门结构与旋转角度的学习型攻击电路，量化了自适应窃听相对于最佳固定电路的增益上界，并在E91与BB84上验证其接近动态规划上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QKD安全性分析基于平稳信道假设，而实际设备在两次校准之间存在信道漂移。此前Decker等人仅在固定门模板与固定信道下训练参数化电路。对于无法获知模板的噪声模型（如振幅阻尼信道），且针对信道自身噪声不可篡改、但窃听者可跟随漂移的自适应攻击，其收益尚未被量化。

Method: 将自适应窃听表述为约束马尔可夫决策过程：攻击者每轮选择一个电路，噪声水平服从Ornstein–Uhlenbeck过程，中止条件为每个轮次块的预算约束。联合搜索门结构与旋转角度以生成足够紧凑的离散动作集，适用于无已知模板的噪声模型；用最佳固定电路给出下界，用动态规划给出上界，并进行强化学习攻击与噪声索引规则的对比。

Result: 在双边去极化噪声下的设备无关E91中，强化学习攻击者将Holevo信息从最佳固定电路的0.135提升至零检测下的0.348，达到上界的98%。在漂移比特翻转信道下的BB84中，其保真度比保守的噪声索引规则高出0.024，达到上界的99%。平稳噪声下，基不对称带来的增益在平均错误率约束与逐基错误率约束之间发生符号反转。从随机门序列出发的搜索复现了解析克隆器与集体攻击密钥率，并从上方满足Winick–Lütkenhaus–Coles目标的下界。

Conclusion: 自适应窃听可显著超越最佳固定电路并逼近动态规划上界，说明基于平稳信道假设的安全性分析会低估攻击者能力；该方法无需已知门模板即可推广至振幅阻尼等噪声模型，并可作为评估QKD在信道漂移情形下安全裕度的工具。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) links are provisioned from security analyses of stationary channels, whereas the devices that determine the channel drift between recalibrations. Whether an eavesdropper who cannot alter the channel's own noise gains by following that drift has not been quantified. Adaptive eavesdropping is posed here as a constrained Markov decision process in which the attacker selects one circuit per round while the noise level follows an Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process and the abort condition is a budget over each block of rounds. The value of adaptation is bounded by the best fixed circuit and a dynamic-programming upper bound. The actions are learnt attacks. Whereas Decker et al. trained a parametrised circuit on a fixed gate template against a fixed channel, here the gate structure and rotation angles are searched jointly. This yields circuits compact enough to form a discrete action set, extending the construction to noise models lacking a known template, including the amplitude damping channel. On device-independent E91 under bilateral depolarising noise, a reinforcement-learning attacker raises her Holevo information from $0.135$ for the best fixed circuit to $0.348$ at zero detection, $98\%$ of the upper bound. On BB84 under a drifting bit-flip channel, she exceeds a conservative noise-indexed rule by $0.024$ in fidelity, reaching $99\%$ of the upper bound. Under stationary noise, the attacker's gain from basis asymmetry changes sign between an averaged and a per-basis error-rate constraint. The search, started from random gate sequences, recovers the analytical cloners and the collective-attack key rate, and meets the lower bound of the Winick--Lütkenhaus--Coles objective from above.

</details>


### [162] [Collision models: Markovian and Non-Markovian impurity models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01814)
*Jacob Werner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一类"马尔可夫杂质模型"碰撞模型，用BLP度量证明若p-杂质模型非马尔可夫则(p+1)-杂质模型亦非马尔可夫，存在马尔可夫到非马尔可夫的临界p，数值研究表明增大系统-辅助子或辅助子-辅助子相互作用一般降低临界p。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碰撞模型是研究开放量子系统与引入非马尔可夫性的灵活框架。作者受"信息回流被周期性阻断"的物理场景启发，希望构造一类能刻画马尔可夫与非马尔可夫之间转变的新模型。

Method: 提出"马尔可夫杂质模型"（Markovian impurity models）：在碰撞模型中周期性引入阻断信息回流的杂质。采用Breuer-Laine-Piilo（BLP）非马尔可夫性度量进行分析，并结合数值模拟考察临界参数对相互作用强度的依赖。

Result: 证明单调性：若p-杂质模型非马尔可夫，则对应的(p+1)-杂质模型也非马尔可夫，从而存在一个过程由马尔可夫转变为非马尔可夫的临界p。数值模拟显示，虽然存在例外，但增大系统-辅助子或辅助子-辅助子相互作用强度通常会降低临界p；当辅助子-辅助子相互作用足够弱时，即使没有杂质，动力学仍保持马尔可夫。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一类可通过杂质数目调控马尔可夫性转变的碰撞模型，揭示了相互作用强度对临界p的调控规律，并简要引入了互补的非马尔可夫杂质模型，为开放量子系统非马尔可夫性研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: Collision models provide a flexible framework for studying open quantum systems and introducing non-Markovianity. We present a novel class of collision models, which we term Markovian impurity models, motivated by physical scenarios in which information backflow is periodically blocked. Using the Breuer-Laine-Piilo (BLP) measure of non-Markovianity, we show that if a $p$-impurity model is non-Markovian, then the corresponding $(p+1)$-impurity model is also non-Markovian, implying the existence of a critical $p$ at which the process transitions from Markovian to non-Markovian. Numerical simulations are used to investigate how this critical parameter depends on the system-ancilla and ancilla-ancilla interaction strengths. We find that, altough exceptions exist, increasing either interaction strength generally lowers the critical $p$, while sufficiently weak ancilla-ancilla interactions lead to a regime in which the dynamics remain Markovian even in the absence of impurities. Finally, we briefly introduce the complementary non-Markovian impurity model.

</details>


### [163] [QPI-DeepONet-MAC: A Scalable and Stable Hybrid Classical-Quantum Architecture for Physics-Informed Deep Operator Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01824)
*Said Lantigua,José Valencia,Gilson Giraldi,Renato Portugal,Jonas Maziero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出QPI-DeepONet-MAC混合量子-经典架构，将参数化量子电路通过乘加耦合嵌入PI-DeepONet，并从理论上证明了通用逼近能力、避免贫瘠高原的条件、传感器数量下界及基于量子相干性的正则化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用算子学习求解参数化PDE是AI与物理建模交叉的核心挑战。PI-DeepONet虽将控制方程纳入学习，但在高维下优化困难；量子扩展虽增强表示能力，却受贫瘠高原限制可训练性。因此需要一个兼顾表示能力与可训练性的理论化框架。

Method: 提出QPI-DeepONet-MAC混合量子-经典架构，将参数化量子电路（PQC）经由经典表示与量子期望值的乘加耦合（MAC）集成进PI-DeepONet。理论分析包括：证明通用算子逼近能力；推导量子参数梯度界并给出规避贫瘠高原的显式条件与可训练性缩放准则；推导达到目标精度所需传感器数量下界；提出基于量子相干性与态保真度动力学的信息正则化方案。

Result: 理论上证明该架构保持通用算子逼近能力；给出量子参数梯度界及避免贫瘠高原的显式条件与可训练性缩放判据；得到传感器数量下界，将采样需求与输入函数正则性、域维度和PDE动力学增长直接关联；信息正则化可防止训练中相干性损失并保持量子结构。

Conclusion: 这些结果共同为量子增强的物理信息算子学习奠定理论基础，使QPI-DeepONet-MAC成为求解参数化PDE的可扩展、稳定框架。

Abstract: General operator learning for parametric partial differential equations (PDEs) is a fundamental challenge at the intersection of artificial intelligence and physics-based modeling. Physics-informed Deep Operator Networks (PI-DeepONets) incorporate governing equations into learning, but face optimization difficulties in high dimensions. Quantum extensions offer additional representational capacity, yet their trainability can be hindered by barren plateaus. Here, we introduce QPI-DeepONet-MAC, a hybrid quantum-classical architecture that integrates parameterized quantum circuits into PI-DeepONets through a multiplicative-and-additive coupling (MAC) of classical representations and quantum expectation values. We prove analytically that the proposed architecture retains universal operator approximation capabilities. Furthermore, we derive bounds on quantum parameter gradients, establishing explicit conditions to avoid barren plateaus alongside a scaling criterion for trainability. Additionally, we derive a lower bound on the number of sensors required for a target accuracy, directly linking sampling requirements to input function regularity, domain dimension, and PDE dynamical growth. Finally, we propose an informational regularization scheme based on quantum coherence and state fidelity dynamics to prevent coherence loss and preserve quantum structure during training. Together, these results establish a theoretical foundation for quantum-enhanced physics-informed operator learning and position QPI-DeepONet-MAC as a scalable, stable framework for solving parametric PDEs.

</details>


### [164] [Experimental realization of Wheeler's delayed-choice experiment with dual selections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01832)
*Xiaowan Yang,Xinglei Yu,Liangsheng Li,Xinzhi Zhao,Tianle Zheng,Chengjie Zhang,Chuan-Feng Li,Guang-Can Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 基于纠缠光子实现具有双重选择（两个分束器同时控制）的延迟选择实验，观测单光子的波粒行为，并讨论两分束器处于量子叠加时波粒量子叠加态的设想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Wheeler延迟选择实验通常仅控制Mach-Zehnder干涉仪中第二个分束器的有无。本文旨在将延迟选择推广到对两个分束器同时进行量子控制，以更完整地研究波粒二象性在不同构型下的表现。

Method: 利用纠缠光子实现延迟选择实验；通过路径编码同时控制Mach-Zehnder干涉仪中的两个分束器，每个分束器的存在与缺失由量子态编码决定。

Result: 实验揭示了单光子在不同双分束器构型下的波粒行为，证明了通过量子控制可分束器状态来观测波粒二象性；并进一步分析了两个分束器同时处于存在/缺失量子叠加态的情形。

Conclusion: 该工作将延迟选择从单分束器控制扩展到双分束器双重选择，并预测当两分束器处于量子叠加时，光子将处于波粒量子叠加态，为波粒二象性的量子控制研究提供了新途径。

Abstract: Wheeler's delayed-choice experiment demonstrates wave-particle duality of particles using different experimental configurations of a Mach-Zehnder interferometer. In a quantum version of this experiment, the wave-particle behavior of photons can be observed by controlling presence or absence of the second beam splitter. Here, we implement a delayed-choice experiment with dual selections based on entangled photons, experimentally controlling both of two beam splitters in the Mach-Zehnder interferometer, where the presence and absence of the second beam splitter are simultaneously controlled through path encoding. Our experiment reveals the wave-particle behavior of single photons under different configurations of two beam splitters. We further discuss the scenario when two beam splitters are in a quantum superposition of being present and absent, photons will be in a wave-particle quantum superposition state.

</details>


### [165] [Toward Optimal Circuit Depth for Geometrically Local Hamiltonian Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01839)
*Zhenyu Shen,Yusen Wu,Penghui Yao,Xiao Yuan,Yukun Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为固定维度格点上有限程哈密顿量的数字量子模拟构造了近邻量子线路，将线路深度与门数的多对数开销从polylog降至单个对数：深度$O(T\log(nT/\varepsilon))$、门数$O(nT\log(nT/\varepsilon))$；并证明一致XXZ哈密顿量的无条件线路深度下界$\Omega(\log n/\log\log n)$，与上界仅差双对数因子，达到近最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子模拟的核心目标是理解相互作用量子系统的动力学。对于几何局域哈密顿量，局域性暗示数字模拟应能保留底层动力学的并行性。然而此前Haah、Hastings、Kothari与Low的结果存在polylog多对数开销，如何进一步降线路深度、并判断其是否最优，是本文动机所在。

Method: 构造模拟n量子比特、时间无关、固定维度格点上有限程哈密顿量演化的最近邻量子线路。基于Lieb–Robinson分解的对数尺度分块，利用空间局域性同时控制高精度代价：在每个块内，用一阶乘积公式常数阶误差补偿修正项，使精度达到指数级；修正线路深度仅与块直径成正比，不引入超出空间局域性所需的新渐近深度。此外，对一致XXZ哈密顿量证明无条件线路深度下界。

Result: 上界：对时间$T\ge1$、目标精度$\varepsilon$，线路深度$O(T\log(nT/\varepsilon))$、门数$O(nT\log(nT/\varepsilon))$，把HHKL的多对数开销降为单个对数。下界：固定演化时间、足够小固定误差下，任何最近邻线路模拟一致XXZ动力学需深度$\Omega(\log n/\log\log n)$，与$O(\log n)$上界相差双对数因子，达到近最优。

Conclusion: 结果明确了局域性与精度在决定量子动力学线路复杂度中的作用，表明在该区间内格点哈密顿量模拟的线路深度近最优，并暗示空间受限量子计算的更广泛原理。

Abstract: Understanding the dynamics of interacting quantum systems is a central goal of quantum simulation. For geometrically local Hamiltonians, locality suggests that digital simulation should retain much of the parallelism of the underlying dynamics. For a target accuracy $ε$, we construct a nearest-neighbor quantum circuit that simulates the dynamics of an $n$-qubit, time-independent, finite-range Hamiltonian with normalized local interaction strength on a fixed-dimensional lattice for time $T\ge1$, with circuit depth $O(T\log(nT/ε))$ and gate count $O(nT\log(nT/ε))$. This reduces the polylogarithmic overhead of Haah, Hastings, Kothari, and Low to a single logarithm. On logarithmic-scale blocks from the Lieb--Robinson decomposition, a shallow but highly accurate implementation is essential for reducing the circuit depth. Our key observation is that spatial localization also controls the cost of high precision. In each block, the error of a constant-order product formula is compensated for by a correction, yielding exponential accuracy. The correction can be implemented with depth proportional to the block diameter, introducing no additional asymptotic depth scale beyond that required by spatial localization.
  Furthermore, we prove an unconditional circuit-depth lower bound for uniform XXZ Hamiltonians: at fixed evolution time $T$ in the stated regime and sufficiently small fixed error $ε$, any nearest-neighbor quantum circuit simulating these dynamics requires depth $Ω(\log n/\log\log n)$. This matches our $O(\log n)$ upper bound up to a doubly logarithmic factor, establishing near-optimal circuit depth for lattice Hamiltonian simulation in this regime. Together, these results clarify the role of locality and precision in determining the circuit complexity of quantum dynamics and suggest broader principles for spatially constrained quantum computation.

</details>


### [166] [Trapdoored Clifford Operators and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01848)
*Minki Hhan,Hojune Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"陷门Clifford算子分布",其样本与均匀随机Clifford计算不可区分,但在已知陷门时可近线性时间采样与实现(基于LPN假设变体),并用于加速随机Clifford协议及矩阵/Clifford问题的worst-case到average-case归约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀随机n量子比特Clifford算子的采样与实现受限于Clifford群的规模,复杂度接近二次,成为随机化基准测试、经典影子、量子认证等应用的瓶颈。作者希望在不牺牲"随机性"的前提下绕过这一复杂度壁垒。

Method: 利用密码学手段构造带陷门的Clifford算子分布:样本与均匀随机Clifford计算不可区分,但借助陷门可近线性时间采样与实现。构造基于learning parity with noise(LPN)假设的变体;同时构造有限域上的陷门矩阵,支持矩阵及其逆矩阵的高效乘法(解决Vaikuntanathan-Zamir [SODA'26]开放问题)。构造支持Pauli标签上的快速tableau作用以用于经典模拟,并可优化至多对数深度实现。

Result: 获得基于随机Clifford的更快协议;建立矩阵与Clifford问题(含迭代矩阵乘法、Clifford电路综合)的worst-case到average-case归约。特别地,证明"批处理Clifford电路"的困难性:对多个寄存器施加同一Clifford的综合问题至少与worst-case矩阵乘法一样难,即使仅在随机Clifford的小常数比例上成功;该结论可推广至用通用量子电路进行近似实现的情形。

Conclusion: 带陷门的Clifford算子分布为随机Clifford的高效采样与实现提供了新途径,既加速相关量子协议,又给出Clifford电路综合等问题的困难性证据,并确立批处理Clifford综合与worst-case矩阵乘法之间的归约关系。

Abstract: Random Clifford operators have numerous applications in quantum computing, including randomized benchmarking, classical shadows, and quantum authentication. However, sampling and implementing uniformly random $n$-qubit Clifford incur near-quadratic complexity due to the size of Clifford group.
  We introduce a cryptographic way to overcome these barriers: trapdoored Clifford operator distributions whose samples are computationally indistinguishable from uniformly random Cliffords, yet implementing them can be much faster given the trapdoor. We construct a distribution of trapdoored Clifford operators whose elements can be sampled and implemented in near-linear time under a variant of the learning parity with noise assumption. Our constructions allow fast tableau action on Pauli labels for classical simulation, and also can be optimized to admit polylogarithmic-depth implementation. Along the way, we construct trapdoored matrices over finite fields that support efficient multiplication by both a matrix and its inverse, resolving an open question left by Vaikuntanathan and Zamir [SODA'26].
  We use these constructions to obtain faster protocols based on random Cliffords. We also explore their applications to the worst-case to average-case reductions for matrix and Clifford problems including the iterated matrix multiplication and Clifford circuit synthesis. In particular, we show the hardness of batching Clifford circuits: synthesizing circuits that apply the same Clifford to multiple registers is at least as hard as worst-case matrix multiplication, even when synthesis succeeds on a small constant fraction of random Cliffords. This extends to approximate implementations by general quantum circuits.

</details>


### [167] [Quantum Optimal Transport Barycenters: Existence, Duality, and Gaussian Rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01855)
*Augusto Gerolin,Zhiyi Lin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出量子最优传输（QOT）重心框架，建立存在性与对偶性理论，并针对高斯输入态证明了全局刚性定理：若至少一个高斯输入为忠实态，则重心在所有量子态中唯一且必为高斯态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典Wasserstein重心推广到量子态，为2-量子Wasserstein重心在量子态与量子信道两种表述下提供统一处理，并回答高斯输入是否强制重心为高斯且唯一这一核心问题。

Method: 在可分Hilbert空间上，对可能无界的传输代价建立QOT重心框架及其存在性与对偶性；通过取Caglioti–Golse–Mouhot–Paul与De Palma–Trevisan的2-量子Wasserstein距离对应的典范二次代价算子，将问题化为协方差矩阵上的有限维凸优化；再利用协方差互补松弛下的态重构原理，把最优协方差唯一性提升为量子态唯一性。

Result: 重心问题存在高斯极小元并约化为协方差矩阵的有限维凸优化；在协方差互补松弛下可重构量子态，从而证明全局刚性：至少一个高斯输入为忠实态时，重心在所有量子态中唯一且为高斯态。忠实性是充分非必要条件：纯态族仍确定唯一重心，而部分纯非忠实高斯输入可能允许多个重心。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了2-量子Wasserstein重心的态与信道表述，并给出高斯刚性的完整刻画：协方差最优性唯一性本身不足以保证态唯一性，需借助互补松弛下的态重构予以加强；忠实性为充分条件而非必要条件。

Abstract: We develop a Quantum Optimal Transport (QOT) barycenter framework for quantum states, as an analog of Wasserstein barycenters~\cite{AguCar}, and establish existence and duality results for a broad class of possibly unbounded transport costs on separable Hilbert spaces. Our framework provides, in particular, a unified treatment of $2$-quantum Wasserstein (QW) barycenters in both the quantum-state and quantum-channel formulations by specializing to the canonical quadratic cost operators associated with the $2$-quantum Wasserstein distances of Caglioti--Golse--Mouhot--Paul~\cite{caglioti2021towards,Golse-Mouhot-Paul:2016} and De Palma--Trevisan~\cite{DPaTre19}.
  The central results concern Gaussian input states and, in particular, Gaussian rigidity: whether Gaussian input states force the 2-QW barycenter itself to be Gaussian and uniquely determined when the minimization is taken over all quantum states. We first show that the barycenter problem admits a Gaussian minimizer and reduces to a finite-dimensional convex optimization problem over covariance matrices.
  The main difficulty is that uniqueness of the optimal covariance does not, in general, imply uniqueness of the underlying quantum state. We bridge this gap through a state-reconstruction principle under covariance complementary slackness that upgrades uniqueness of the optimal covariance to uniqueness of the full quantum state, and thereby prove a global rigidity theorem: if at least one Gaussian input is faithful, then the barycenter is unique among all quantum states and is necessarily Gaussian. Faithfulness is sufficient but not necessary: families of pure inputs still determine a unique barycenter, whereas partially pure nonfaithful Gaussian inputs may admit multiple barycenters.

</details>


### [168] [Fourier Symmetrization for Geometric Quantum Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01874)
*Letao Wang,Abdel Lisser,Sreejith Sreekumar,Zeno Toffano*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过傅里叶表示研究量子傅里叶模型（QFM）中对称性如何塑造表达能力，给出对称化系数方差与轨道内方差之和的关系、近似2-design下的偏差界，并提出无需辅助比特的随机化编码，在二维PDE基准上验证对称QFM（QPINN）取得最低平均误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何量子机器学习将对称性引入量子模型，但对称性如何影响模型表达能力、以及如何据此进行有效模型设计仍缺乏充分理解。作者希望从理论上刻画对称性对QFM频谱与表达能力的作用，为设计高效几何量子模型提供指导。

Method: 1) 用傅里叶表示分析量子傅里叶模型（QFM），对称性将频谱组织为轨道，并将同一轨道内的傅里叶系数求和为对称化系数。2) 当QFM的可训练层构成独立的精确2-design时，证明每个对称化系数的方差等于其轨道内各系数方差之和。3) 对具有ε-近似2-design可训练层的单层QFM，给出该恒等式的偏差界，并证明该界对单个傅里叶系数可比既有界指数级更紧。4) 以超八面体群说明轨道增长可缓解对称化系数的表达力消失问题；对初等阿贝尔2-群进行对称化可得到纯多元切比雪夫多项式基函数。5) 提出随机化编码（randomized encoding），无需辅助比特或量子twirling的额外电路深度即可实现不变模型。6) 将模型作为量子物理信息神经网络（QPINN）在二维屏蔽泊松方程和定常粘性Hamilton-Jacobi方程上评估。

Result: 在硬边界约束下，使用精确对称化与随机化编码的QPINN分别在两个基准上取得最低平均误差。理论方面给出了对称化系数方差恒等式、单层近似2-design情形下的偏差界（对单个系数可指数级优于既有界），并展示了超八面体群的轨道增长可缓解表达力消失。

Conclusion: 对称性通过轨道结构系统性地组织QFM的傅里叶频谱，其对称化系数的方差行为可由2-design性质精确刻画；所提随机化编码可低成本实现不变模型，并在PDE求解的QPINN基准中表现最优。这为将对称性纳入量子模型设计提供了理论基础与实用方案。

Abstract: Geometric quantum machine learning incorporates symmetry into quantum models, but how symmetry shapes their expressivity and guides effective model design remains insufficiently understood. We address this question through the Fourier representation of quantum Fourier models (QFMs). Symmetry organizes the frequency spectrum into orbits and sums the Fourier coefficients within each orbit into a symmetrized coefficient. When QFM trainable layers form independent exact 2-designs, the variance of each symmetrized coefficient equals the sum of the coefficient variances in its orbit. For single-layer QFMs with $\varepsilon$-approximate 2-design trainable layers, we bound the deviation from this identity. The resulting bound for individual Fourier coefficients can be exponentially tighter than an existing bound. The hyperoctahedral group provides an example of orbit growth that can mitigate vanishing expressivity of the symmetrized coefficients. Symmetrization of QFMs over an elementary abelian 2-group also yields pure multivariate Chebyshev polynomial basis functions. We introduce randomized encoding, which implements invariant models without ancilla qubits or the additional circuit depth for quantum twirling. We evaluate the models as quantum physics-informed neural networks (QPINNs) on two-dimensional screened Poisson and stationary viscous Hamilton-Jacobi equations. Under hard boundary constraints, QPINNs using exact symmetrization and randomized encoding achieve the lowest mean errors in the two benchmarks, respectively.

</details>


### [169] [Exponential quantum advantages for decoded quantum interferometry in the streaming setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01902)
*Kewen Wu,Guangxu Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在流式（streaming）模型下研究解码量子干涉（DQI）算法，通过Hermite插值与Hasse导数推广最优多项式交集（OPI）问题，给出量子算法与经典算法的空间-近似权衡曲线：量子算法单遍、多对数空间即可满足93%约束，而经典算法即使多遍、无限时间也需多项式空间才能达到76%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DQI在时间维度上已展现对经典算法的优势，但空间（内存）同样是核心资源，尤其容错量子比特开销巨大。作者追问：DQI是否能在内存上带来可无条件证明的量子优势？流式模型为此提供了自然且可证的下界框架。

Method: 1) 用Hermite插值与Hasse导数将OPI推广为：寻找低次多项式以尽可能满足关于其取值与导数的约束；2) 设计DQI算法的流式改编版本，单遍读取输入流，仅用多对数空间、每条流入多对数计算时间；3) 对经典算法建立空间下界：即便允许多项式多遍读取与无限时间，任何达到76%满足度的经典算法仍需多项式空间。

Result: 量子侧：改编的DQI算法满足93%约束，单遍流式、多对数空间、每条目多对数时间。经典侧：任何满足76%约束的经典算法需多项式空间，即使允许多项式多遍与无限时间。二者共同给出可调参数下的完整权衡曲线。

Conclusion: 结果提供了完整的空间-近似权衡曲线，表明DQI在流式设置下具有可证明的量子内存优势，并由此推出DQI对原始OPI问题亦具备可证明的量子优势。

Abstract: Decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) is a polynomial-time quantum algorithm introduced by Jordan et al. (Nature 2025). For a natural optimization problem, known as optimal polynomial intersection (OPI), it achieves approximation guarantees in regimes where all known classical algorithms require exponential time.
  Besides time, space is another central resource: storing and manipulating a massive input can be very challenging, especially when logical qubits carry substantial fault-tolerant implementation overhead. This motivates the following question: does DQI yield quantum advantages in memory, and can we prove it unconditionally?
  We give an affirmative answer to this question in the streaming setting. In particular, we consider a natural generalization of OPI using Hermite interpolation and Hasse derivatives, which asks for a low-degree polynomial satisfying as many constraints on its values and derivatives as possible. As a concrete example, we show
  [Quantum efficiency.] An adaptation of the DQI algorithm produces a polynomial satisfying $93\%$ of the constraints; moreover, it only reads the input stream in one pass, uses polylogarithmic space, and has polylogarithmic computation time per stream entry.
  [Classical hardness.] Any classical algorithm that produces an answer satisfying just $76\%$ of the constraints requires polynomial space, even if it can read the input stream with polynomially many passes and can use unlimited time.
  Our result provides a complete tradeoff curve for the tunable parameters, and implies that DQI has provable quantum advantages for the original OPI problem.

</details>


### [170] [Loss-tolerant distributed lattice surgery using fusion networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01923)
*Felix Burt,Richard Meister,Sheng-Ku Lin,Kuan-Cheng Chen,Michael Hanks,Roberto Bondesan,M. S. Kim,Kin K. Leung*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用ZX演算变换构造混合综合提取协议，将其应用于分布式旋转表面码格点手术，证明若干协议在无局域噪声时可达50%界面擦除阈值，并系统分析了阈值随资源态截断、局域电路噪声与聚变擦除的变化，最终将擦除阈值转换为光子损耗阈值并评估亚阈值性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络化物质量子处理单元是扩展容错量子计算机的有前景途径，但需通过光子链路执行分布式逻辑操作。由于光子损耗和概率性线性光学操作，链路噪声在特征上不同于且强于局域QPU噪声。测量基与聚变基量子计算对损耗和概率性光子操作具有鲁棒性，因此有望在网络QPU接口处补充电路基纠错。

Method: 采用ZX演算变换构造混合综合提取协议，应用于分布式旋转表面码的格点手术。具体包括使用聚变贝尔对的电路基格点手术和基于线性簇态的混合协议。以直线贝尔对接口几何为对照，将合并可观测量接口距离从d+1提升至2d+1，并将垂直可观测量接口距离从⌊(d+1)/2⌋恢复至d。进一步计算截断资源态下的阈值衰减，并映射资源态错误、局域电路噪声与聚变擦除下的可纠正区域，再将擦除阈值转换为光子损耗阈值，利用聚变增强考察亚阈值性能。

Result: 若干协议在无局域噪声时达到50%界面擦除阈值，包括使用聚变贝尔对的电路基格点手术与使用线性簇态的混合协议。混合协议相对直线贝尔对几何显著提升接口距离。当电路与资源态错误率为10⁻³时，局域错误在很大程度上掩盖了接口距离优势；当局域噪声降至10⁻⁴及以下时，线性链协议随擦除率下降改善更快，表明接口距离增强在低局域噪声区有效。

Conclusion: 混合测量/聚变基协议可有效补充网络QPU接口处的电路基纠错，在低局域噪声条件下接口距离增强带来的优势能够充分发挥，为分布式容错量子计算的光子链路接口设计提供了量化依据。

Abstract: Networking matter-based quantum processing units (QPUs) offers a promising route to scaling fault-tolerant quantum computers. This requires distributed logical operations to be performed across photonic links, where noise is characteristically different from and stronger than in local QPUs owing to photon loss and probabilistic linear-optical operations. Measurement- and fusion-based quantum computing are designed to be robust against loss and probabilistic photonic operations, suggesting they could complement circuit-based error correction at the interface between networked QPUs. We use ZX calculus transformations to construct hybrid syndrome extraction protocols and apply them to distributed rotated surface code lattice surgery, demonstrating that several protocols attain a $50\%$ interface-erasure threshold when local noise is absent, including circuit-based lattice surgery using fused Bell pairs and hybrid protocols using linear cluster states. Relative to a straight Bell-pair interface geometry, the hybrid protocols increase the merge-observable interface distance from $d+1$ to $2d+1$ and restore the perpendicular-observable interface distance from $\lfloor(d+1)/2\rfloor$ to $d$. We calculate how the threshold decreases when using truncated resource states and map correctable regions under resource-state errors, local circuit noise, and fusion erasure. We then convert these erasure thresholds into photon-loss thresholds and probe subthreshold performance using fusion boosting. At circuit and resource-state error rates of $10^{-3}$, local errors largely mask the interface-distance advantage. As local noise is reduced to $10^{-4}$ and below, the linear-chain protocols improve more rapidly with decreasing erasure, suggesting that interface distance enhancements are effective in low local noise regimes.

</details>


### [171] [Optimal query complexity for fractional quantum evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01940)
*Anthony Yuezhang Liu,Adam Wesołowski,Jayne Thompson,Mile Gu,Lirandë Pira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究分数次查询问题，证明QSVT上界在渐近意义下最优，确立最优查询复杂度为Θ_τ(1/δ log(1/ε))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 给定未知酉算子U=e^{iH}的oracle访问，分数次查询问题要求实现非整数幂U^t=e^{itH}(0<t<1)，且谱与分支割之间有间隙δ。已有量子奇异值变换(QSVT)给出O((1/δ)log(1/ε))查询的上界，但其是否最优尚不清楚，作者希望给出匹配的下界以确定最优查询复杂度。

Method: 作者将任意N次查询电路归约为以次数O(N)的三角多项式逼近e^{itθ}的问题，并结合Remez不等式建立下界Ω_τ((1/δ)log(1/ε))。此外，作者提出基于构造一个湮灭逼近空间的线性函数的替代下界证明，给出对δ一致的Ω_τ(log(1/ε))下界。

Result: 证明了任意查询算法的匹配下界，得到分数次查询问题的最优查询复杂度为Θ_τ((1/δ)log(1/ε))；并给出对δ一致的Ω_τ(log(1/ε))替代下界。这表明已知的QSVT构造在渐近意义下最优。

Conclusion: 分数次查询问题的最优查询复杂度为Θ_τ((1/δ)log(1/ε))，QSVT构造渐近最优；三角多项式逼近与Remez不等式以及线性泛函湮灭方法为下界证明提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Given oracle access to an unknown unitary $U=e^{iH}$ , the fractional query problem asks how many queries are required to implement a noninteger power $U^t=e^{itH}$, $0<t<1$, when the spectrum is separated from the branch cut by a gap $δ$. Quantum singular value transformation gives an upper bound of $O\!\left(\frac{1}δ\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)$ queries for approximation error $\varepsilon$. We prove a matching lower bound for arbitrary query algorithms. Our argument reduces any $N$-query circuit to the approximation of $e^{itθ}$ by a trigonometric polynomial with degree bounded by $O(N)$, together with Remez inequality. This allows us to establish the lower bound of $Ω_τ\!\left(\frac{1}δ\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)$. Consequently, the optimal query complexity for fractional query problem is $Θ_τ\!\left(\frac{1}δ\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)$, showing that the known QSVT construction is asymptotically optimal. We also give an alternative lower bound proof based on constructing a linear functional that annihilates the approximant space, yielding a $Ω_τ\!\left(\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)$ bound uniform to $δ$.

</details>


### [172] [Universality Sacrifices Reliability in Classical-Quantum Channel Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01941)
*Kaito Watanabe,Masahito Hayashi,Takaya Matsuura,Hao-Chung Cheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究经典-量子信道的通用编码可靠性，证明其在一般情况下无法同时达到信道已知情形下的容量与最优高码率可靠性，并刻画了通用编码的最优可靠性由Petz与sandwiched Rényi散度的差异所决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典信道中通用码可同时达到容量和最优高码率可靠性，作者希望探究这一兼容性在经典-量子信道中是否依然成立，尤其是当信道输出系统存在未知酉旋转时通用编码是否仍能达到信道已知情形下的最优可靠性。

Method: 构造一族经典-量子信道，证明不存在固定编码方案能达到信道已知情形下的最优可靠性；针对酉不变解码器推导可靠性的逆定理界；并构造信道无关的编码器-解码器对，给出高码率区域可达界，使其与该逆定理界匹配，从而刻画最优通用可靠性；借助Petz Rényi散度与sandwiched Rényi散度分别刻画信道已知与通用情形的指数。

Result: 证明经典-量子信道的通用编码无法普遍达到信道已知情形下的最优可靠性；给出酉不变解码器下可严格小于信道已知最优误差指数的逆定理界；构造的通用编解码方案在高码率区达到与逆定理界匹配的可靠性，从而确定最优通用可靠性；指出信道已知与通用指数分别由Petz与sandwiched Rényi散度支配，二者在可交换输出时一致、在不可交换时不同。

Conclusion: 通用性在经典信道中可保持最优可靠性，但在量子信道中会因输出酉旋转的未知性而降低可靠性，揭示了经典-量子信道通用编码任务存在根本性的可靠性代价。

Abstract: Universal channel coding enables communication without a complete description of the channel. For classical channels, universal codes can attain both capacity and the optimal high-rate reliability. We show that this compatibility fails for classical-quantum channels in general; that is, the optimal reliability in the channel-aware scenario is not always achievable with universal coding due to the ignorance of the unitary rotation of the output system. We exhibit a family of classical-quantum channels for which one cannot achieve the channel-aware optimal reliability by a fixed coding scheme. We further derive a converse bound on the reliability for unitary-invariant decoders, a natural assumption for the universal coding scheme, that can be strictly smaller than the optimal channel-aware error exponent. Conversely, we construct a channel-independent encoder-decoder pair and establish a universally achievable bound on the reliability that matches this converse bound in the high-rate regime, thereby characterizing the optimal universal reliability. Specifically, the channel-aware and universal exponents are governed by the Petz and sandwiched Rényi divergences, respectively. These divergences coincide for commuting outputs but differ for noncommuting ones, explaining why universality preserves optimal reliability classically but can reduce it quantumly. Our results showcase the fundamental reliability cost of performing the classical-quantum channel coding task universally.

</details>


### [173] [Hardware-Efficient Ground-State Preparation using Variational Imaginary-Time Majorana Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01954)
*Federico Santona,Manuel G. Algaba,Aeishah Ameera Anuar,Anna M. Wernbacher,Prachi Sharma,Fedor Šimkovic*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出VIME经典预训练算法，将OVQITE扩展到基于单一编译Majorana传播代理图的分子电子结构，并配合压缩tiled酉积态(c-tUPS)拟设，在TLD-1411及并苯系列上以更少资源达到化学精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期容错量子化学亟需紧凑的基态制备电路，现有ADAPT-VQE类变分Majorana传播方法在强关联体系中资源开销大、精度不足。

Method: VIME：扩展算子投影变分量子虚时演化(OVQITE)至分子电子结构的经典预训练算法，使用单一编译的Majorana传播(MP)代理图；并开发压缩tiled酉积态(c-tUPS)拟设以降低经典模拟与量子态制备成本。

Result: 在强关联钌配合物TLD-1411中，最多52量子比特的活性空间计算相对DMRG达到化学精度，能量误差比ADAPT-VMPE小约400倍；在至七并苯(60量子比特)的多参考并苯系列中，VIME匹配或优于ADAPT-VMPE的MP能量精度，CNOT数最多减少26倍，双量子比特深度降低167倍。

Conclusion: 压缩变分拟设的虚时经典预训练是实现资源高效分子基态制备的有前景途径。

Abstract: Compact ground-state preparation circuits are essential for early fault-tolerant quantum chemistry. We introduce Variational Imaginary-time Majorana Evolution (VIME), a classical pre-training algorithm that extends operator-projected variational quantum imaginary-time evolution (OVQITE) to molecular electronic structure using a single compiled Majorana-propagation (MP) surrogate graph. We pair VIME with a compressed tiled Unitary Product State (c-tUPS) ansatz that we develop to reduce both classical simulation and quantum state-preparation costs.
