We compute it inside the formal Riemannian structure of Otto and confirm it directly from the definition of $W_2$, which also shows that no neighbourhood of the uniform measure in $P_2(S^1)$ is CAT(0). The result corrects the widely stated expectation that a Wasserstein space is flat whenever its base manifold is one-dimensional.
  The computation is carried out in the global frame of the tangent bundle of $P^\infty(M)$ induced by the eigenfunctions of the Laplacian of $M$, whose existence was observed by Lott. We prove that it is a Schauder basis of every tangent space and write Otto's metric and its Levi-Civita connection in it. On the circle this gives the metric and connection coefficients of $P^\infty(S^1)$ in closed form, as finite combinations of the Fourier coefficients of the density. A final section puts the frame to work on a computation from dynamics, reading the derivative of the push-forward action of an expanding circle map at its invariant measure as a weighted shift on the Fourier frequencies.

</details>


### [213] [The Heat Kernel of the Hodge Laplacian on an Invariant Subspace of 1-forms on the Hyperbolic Space $\mathbb{H}^{n}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00653)
*Petros Siasos*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文给出了实双曲空间 $\mathbb{H}^{n}$ 上 Hodge Laplacian 限制在 $1$-形式不变子空间上的热核显式公式，建立了跨维度的 Millson 型递推关系，并由此导出尖锐一致估计与长时间渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典标量热核的界已有完善结果，但 $1$-形式（Hodge Laplacian）情形缺乏显式热核公式与尖锐估计，需要将标量情形结果推广到 $1$-形式上。

Method: 利用 warped product（扭曲乘积）分解，将问题约化为特定子空间上的分量算子，从而对奇、偶维数分别得到闭式表达式；并建立连接不同维数热核的 Millson 型递推关系。

Result: 得到奇、偶维数下 $1$-形式分量热核的精确闭式表达式，建立 Millson 型跨维递推关系，并基于显式表示导出尖锐一致估计与长时间渐近行为。

Conclusion: 该工作将实双曲空间上经典标量热核界推广至 $1$-形式，为 Hodge Laplacian 的谱与热核分析提供了显式工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct explicit formulas for the heat kernel of the Hodge Laplacian restricted to an invariant subspace of $1$-forms on the real hyperbolic space $\mathbb{H}^{n}$. Using warped product decompositions, the problem is reduced to component operators on specific subspaces, yielding precise closed-form expressions for both odd and even dimensions. We further establish Millson-type recurrence relations that connect heat kernels across differing dimensions. Based on these explicit representations, we derive sharp uniform estimates and long-time asymptotic behavior, extending classical scalar heat kernel bounds to $1$-forms on real hyperbolic spaces.

</details>


### [214] [Strict Log-Concavity of First Dirichlet Eigenfunctions under Symmetry in $\mathbb{CP}^n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00716)
*Jia-Lin Hsu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了在 $\mathbb{CP}^n$（$n=2,3,4$）中具有凸边界的平滑区域上，关于标准 $U(1)\times U(n-1)$-作用不变的第一个 Dirichlet 正特征函数是严格对数凹的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究复射影空间 $\mathbb{CP}^n$ 中凸区域上 Dirichlet 特征函数的几何性质，特别是对数凹性这一在欧氏空间已有经典结论、但在复流形上尚不清楚的问题。

Method: 利用标准 $U(1)\times U(n-1)$-对称性将问题约化，并针对维数 $n=2,3,4$ 证明第一个 Dirichlet 正特征函数满足严格对数凹性。

Result: 对 $n=2,3,4$，所有具有凸边界且关于 $U(1)\times U(n-1)$-作用不变的平滑域，其第一个 Dirichlet 正特征函数均为严格对数凹。

Conclusion: 在复射影空间的凸区域上，对称性约束下的第一 Dirichlet 特征函数具有严格对数凹性，为复流形上特征函数凸性/凹性研究提供了低维证据，并可能推动更高维情形的推广。

Abstract: We prove that the positive first Dirichlet eigenfunction of every smooth domain with convex boundary in $\mathbb{CP}^n$ invariant under the standard $U(1)\times U(n-1)$-action is strictly log-concave for $n=2,3,4$.

</details>


### [215] [On the Bryant conjecture for minimal hypersurfaces in $S^4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00886)
*Chao Qian,Ya Tao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了单位球面 $S^4$ 中具有常数量曲率且每点有三个不同主曲率的局部极小超曲面 $M^3$ 必局部全等于 Cartan 极小等参超曲面，从而肯定回答了 Bryant 猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bryant 猜想关注 $S^4$ 中极小超曲面的刚性分类问题；此前相关问题（如 Chang 的工作）尚未完全解决，需要证明这类具有常数量曲率和三个不同主曲率的极小超曲面只有 Cartan 等参超曲面这一标准例子。

Method: 关键技术是证明：对每个固定的 $S_0>3$，满足 $|A|^2\equiv S_0$ 的归一化局部图解的四阶 jets 构成的集合 $\mathscr{G}_{S_0}$ 是紧的，其中 $A$ 为形状算子。由此对局部图解进行紧性分析与刚性推导。

Result: 证明了 $M^3$ 必局部全等于 $S^4$ 中的 Cartan 极小等参超曲面，并由此得到 Bryant 猜想的肯定答案。核心是建立了四阶 jets 集合 $\mathscr{G}_{S_0}$ 的紧性。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了 Bryant 猜想的一个正面解决，说明常数量曲率与三个不同主曲率条件下极小超曲面具有强刚性；四阶 jets 紧性方法为同类刚性/分类问题提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We prove that a local minimal hypersurface $M^3$ in the unit sphere $S^4$ with constant scalar curvature and three distinct principal curvatures at every point must be locally congruent to the minimal Cartan isoparametric hypersurface in $S^4$, which provides a positive answer to the Bryant conjecture \cite{ChangLocal}. The key ingredient is to prove that, for each fixed \(S_0>3\), the set $\mathscr{G}_{S_0}$ of fourth-order jets of normalized local graph solutions satisfying \(|A|^2\equiv S_0\) is compact, where \(A\) denotes the shape operator.

</details>


### [216] [Monge-Ampère degenerations and conifold contractions in Fujiki class $\mathcal{C}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00942)
*Riley Guyett,Ryan Mc Gowan*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 对有常双点奇点的Q-Gorenstein正规射影Calabi-Yau簇及其小crepant消解，在例外簇外建立了沿退化度量的统一估计；三维情形下证明度量在Gromov-Hausdorff意义下收敛到正则轨迹的度量完备化，推广了Song关于Candelas-de la Ossa猜想的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Candelas与de la Ossa曾提出关于Calabi-Yau簇奇异点处Ricci平坦度量退化行为的猜想，Song此前在特定情形下进行了研究。本文旨在将相关结果推广到更一般的Q-Gorenstein正规射影Calabi-Yau簇（以常双点为奇点）及其小crepant消解的情形，理解度量在例外簇附近退化时的极限行为。

Method: 设Y为以常双点为奇点的Q-Gorenstein正规射影Calabi-Yau簇，π:X→Y为小crepant消解且X属于Fujiki类。研究给定度量在例外簇外退化时的渐近性质，建立一致估计。对三维情形，进一步分析度量的收敛性，证明其在Gromov-Hausdorff意义下收敛到Y正则轨迹的度量完备化。

Result: 在例外轨迹外建立了退化度量的统一估计，刻画了度量在奇点附近的渐近行为；当Y为三维时，证明了度量在Gromov-Hausdorff意义下收敛到Y正则轨迹的度量完备化。

Conclusion: 本文的结果推广了Song关于Candelas-de la Ossa猜想的研究，将相关结论扩展到更一般的Q-Gorenstein Calabi-Yau簇及Fujiki类中的小crepant消解情形，深化了对Calabi-Yau簇奇异点处度量退化与极限空间结构的理解。

Abstract: Given a $\mathbb{Q}$-Gorenstein normal projective Calabi-Yau variety $Y$ with ordinary double points as singularities and a small crepant resolution $π\colon X\to Y$ with $X$ in the Fujiki class $\mathcal{C}$, we establish uniform estimates away from the exceptional locus where the given metrics may degenerate. When $Y$ is a threefold, we prove that these metrics also converge in a Gromov-Hausdorff sense to the metric completion of $Y$ on the regular locus. In particular, our results extend the work of Song on a conjecture of Candelas and de la Ossa.

</details>


### [217] [Parabolic Monge-Ampère equations on a family of Calabi-Yau manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01202)
*Jiyuan Han*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在一致Skoda估计下证明了抛物型Monge–Ampère方程的一致$L^\infty$估计，并将其应用于穿孔圆盘上一族极化Calabi–Yau流形上的Kähler–Ricci流，得到沿流的一致Skoda估计、一致直径界与Gromov–Hausdorff预紧性，并给出Li–Tosatti直径估计的替代证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究退化族上Kähler–Ricci流的一致估计与收敛性，需要处理初始测度与给定测度的一致Skoda估计，并寻求对已有直径估计的独立验证。

Method: 证明抛物型Monge–Ampère方程在关于初始与给定测度的一致Skoda估计下的一致$L^\infty$估计；将该估计用于穿孔圆盘上极化Calabi–Yau流形族的Kähler–Ricci流；通过插值论证导出沿流的一致Skoda估计。

Result: 得到沿该族Kähler–Ricci流的一致Skoda估计、一致直径界以及Gromov–Hausdorff预紧性；并由此给出Li–Tosatti直径估计的替代证明。

Conclusion: 一致Skoda估计可作为获得抛物型Monge–Ampère方程一致$L^\infty$估计的有效工具，进而统一处理Kähler–Ricci流族的一致几何界与紧性，并复现已有直径结果。

Abstract: We prove a uniform $L^\infty$-estimate for the parabolic Monge--Ampère equation under uniform Skoda estimates with respect to the initial and prescribed measures. We apply this estimate to Kähler--Ricci flows on a family of polarized Calabi--Yau manifolds over the punctured disk. An interpolation argument gives uniform Skoda estimates along the flows. We also establish uniform diameter bounds and Gromov--Hausdorff precompactness for this family of flows. In particular, we obtain an alternative proof of the diameter estimate by Li-Tosatti.

</details>


### [218] [Algebraic limits of Hermitian-Yang-Mills connections on Kähler surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01221)
*Keshu Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文给出Kähler曲面上有界能量Hermitian-Yang-Mills联络的Hörmander $L^2$-估计的统一几何方法，将Uhlenbeck收敛实现为解析平坦族中的代数收敛，并应用于连续性定理与$\\mu$-半稳定层的界性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hörmander $L^2$-估计与Uhlenbeck收敛是研究Hermitian-Yang-Mills联络及模空间的核心工具，但既有方法缺乏统一几何框架，且收敛性与代数几何之间的联系（解析族中的代数收敛）尚不清晰，导致模空间紧化与有界性等问题难以处理。

Method: 发展一套几何化方法，对Kähler曲面上具有有界能量的Hồrmander型联络统一实施$L^2$-估计；利用该估计将光滑HYM联络的Uhlenbeck收敛构造为解析平坦族内部的代数收敛。

Result: 建立了统一几何框架下的$L^2$-估计；实现了Uhlenbeck收敛到解析平坦族中代数收敛的对应；证明了光滑可平滑族的连续性定理，以及固定拓扑下$\\mu$-半稳定层的有界性结果。

Conclusion: 该几何框架统一并深化了HYM联络分析理论，架起微分几何收敛与代数几何收敛之间的桥梁，并对模空间问题具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: We develop a geometric way to uniformly carry out Hörmander's $L^2$-estimates for Hermitian-Yang-Mills connections with bounded energy on Kähler surfaces. We then show the Uhlenbeck convergence of smooth HYM connections on Kähler surfaces can be realized as algebraic convergence inside an analytic flat family. As applications, we prove a continuity theorem for smoothable families and a boundedness result for $μ$-semistable sheaves with fixed topology. We also discuss potential applications for the moduli problem.

</details>


### [219] [Riemannian Structures on Quotients in Elastic Diffeology via Q-Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01346)
*Yusuke Shiobara*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 沿Q-charts（Blohmann弹性可下降的一类subduction）证明弹性微分几何的Riemann结构（垂直/水平/全联络、度规、Levi-Civita联络、测地线）可双向下降，并完全确定无理环面Tor_α的Riemann几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Miyamoto引入Q-charts后，Blohmann的弹性微分学可沿其下降，但弹性微分学中的Riemannian结构（联络、度规、测地线等）是否也沿Q-charts下降尚不清楚，且无理环面等商空间的Riemann几何缺乏完整刻画。

Method: 利用迭代切丛T^nX及其纤维积T_kX是沿φ的相应丛的拉回这一关键事实，建立Y上结构与X上Ψ(φ)-不变结构之间的双射；对微分离散群主作用的商，提升测地线并研究完备测地流下降。

Result: Y上结构（垂直/水平/全联络、Riemann度规、Levi-Civita联络、测地线）与X上Ψ(φ)-不变结构一一对应，且无挠性、有效性、平坦性、满性、Levi-Civita性质与测地线方程双向保持；商情形下可整体提升测地线，弹性空间类对商封闭，完备测地流可下降。对无理环面Tor_α= R/(Z+αZ)，由dt诱导的one-form ϑ使得每个Riemann度规是ϑ²的正常数倍，垂直联络构成单参数平坦有效联络族，其中唯一成员为所有度规的唯一Levi-Civita联络，该联络测地流完备但诱导伪距离恒为零。

Conclusion: 弹性微分学的Riemannian apparatus沿Q-charts可双向下降，商结构性质良好；无理环面是完备测地流与退化伪距离并存的典型例子。

Abstract: Following Miyamoto, we consider Q-charts, a class of subductions $φ\colon X\to Y$ of diffeological spaces along which Blohmann's elasticity descends. We show that the Riemannian apparatus of elastic diffeology---vertical, horizontal and full connections, Riemannian metrics, Levi-Civita connections and geodesics---also descends along Q-charts. More precisely, structures on $Y$ correspond bijectively to structures on $X$ that are invariant under the pseudogroup $Ψ(φ)$ of fibre-preserving transitions, and torsion-freeness, effectivity, flatness, fullness, the Levi-Civita property and the geodesic equation are preserved in both directions. The key input is that the iterated tangent bundles $T^nX$ and their fibre products $T_kX$ are the pullbacks along $φ$ of the corresponding bundles over $Y$.
  For quotients by principal actions of diffeologically discrete groups, we lift geodesics globally, prove that the class of elastic spaces on which $\R$ is a curve object is closed under such quotients, and show that complete geodesic flows descend. As an application, we determine the Riemannian geometry of the irrational tori $\Tor_α=\R/(\Z+α\Z)$ completely. Let $\vartheta$ be the one-form on $\Tor_α$ induced by $dt$ on $\R$. Every Riemannian metric is a positive constant multiple of $\vartheta^2$, and the vertical connections form a one-parameter family of flat effective connections, a single member of which is the unique Levi-Civita connection of every metric. This connection has a complete geodesic flow, yet the induced pseudo-distance vanishes identically.

</details>


### [220] [Rigidity of volume-minimizing free boundary hypersurfaces in high dimensional Riemannian manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01392)
*Sanghun Lee*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在$(n+1)$维黎曼流形中研究体积最小化自由边界超曲面，建立了体积估计，并在等号情形下证明了流形的刚性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究稳定极小自由边界超曲面的体积估计及其等号情形下的刚性，是几何分析中的重要问题，有助于理解流形内蕴曲率（数量曲率）与边界外在几何（第二基本形式）对内部极小超曲面体积的约束关系。

Method: 针对$(n+1)$维黎曼流形$M$中的稳定极小自由边界超曲面，利用数量曲率与边界第二基本形式构造体积估计；并在此基础上分析等号成立条件以推导刚性结论。

Result: 得到了用$M$的数量曲率和$\partial M$的第二基本形式表示的稳定极小自由边界超曲面体积估计；在相应等号情形下证明了$M$的刚性结果。

Conclusion: 体积估计与刚性结果揭示了流形数量曲率及边界第二基本形式对稳定极小自由边界超曲面体积的控制作用，等号情形对应流形的特定刚性几何结构。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish a volume estimate and an associated rigidity result for volume-minimizing free boundary hypersurfaces in an $(n+1)$-dimensional Riemannian manifold $M$. First, we derive a volume estimate for stable minimal free boundary hypersurfaces in terms of the scalar curvature of $M$ and the second fundamental form of the boundary $\partial M$. We then prove rigidity results of $M$ in the corresponding equality cases.

</details>


### [221] [The sphere is the only closed surface satisfying the fixed-width Archimedean property](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01550)
*Mijia Lai*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明：在 R³ 中，对某一固定宽度 h，若连通光滑闭嵌入曲面的两平行平面间区域面积恒等于 2πh，则该曲面必为单位球面。即阿基米德球带面积性质在单一固定宽度下即可刻画单位球。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿基米德发现单位球介于两平行平面（间距为 h）之间的区域面积为 2πh，这一性质是球的经典刻画特征之一。作者希望探究该性质是否在弱化条件下仍能唯一刻画单位球——即无需对所有宽度成立，仅需对某一个固定宽度成立即可。

Method: 在 R³ 中连通、光滑、闭、嵌入曲面的范围内，施加条件：对某个固定宽度 h，任意两平行平面（均与曲面相交且间距为 h）所截出的区域面积恒为 2πh，进而证明满足此条件的曲面必为单位球面。

Result: 证明了该阿基米德球带面积性质在单一固定宽度 h 下即可刻画单位球：满足条件的连通光滑闭嵌入曲面必为单位球面。

Conclusion: 阿基米德球带面积性质是一个强刻画条件，只需一个固定宽度即足以唯一确定单位球，无需对所有宽度成立。

Abstract: Among Archimedes' many celebrated discoveries is a striking fact: the region of the unit sphere between two parallel planes, both meeting the sphere and separated by a distance $h$, has area $2πh$. We prove that, among connected smooth closed embedded surfaces in $\mathbb R^3$, this property characterizes the unit sphere for a single fixed width.

</details>


### [222] [Solvable Algebraic Generalized Ricci Solitons in low dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01608)
*Lorenzo Mucciante*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文证明可解李代数上代数广义Ricci孤立子的新结构结果，并给出低维分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究可解李代数上代数广义Ricci孤立子的结构性质与低维存在性。

Method: 利用代数广义Ricci孤立子的结构定理，分析阿贝尔李代数的一维扩张，并按等距与缩放对三维李代数及四维可解幺模李代数上的解进行分类。

Result: 所有非平凡可解代数广义Ricci孤立子必为扩张型；给出阿贝尔李代数一维扩张上存在性的必要条件；完成三维李代数和四维可解幺模李代数上代数广义Ricci孤立子的分类。

Conclusion: 刻画了可解情形下代数广义Ricci孤立子的扩张性质，并在低维给出完整分类。

Abstract: We prove new structural results for algebraic generalized Ricci solitons on solvable Lie algebras and we discuss the existence of examples in low dimensions. We establish that all non-trivial solvable algebraic generalized Ricci solitons must be expanding and give necessary conditions for the existence of such solitons on one-dimensional extensions of abelian Lie algebras. Finally, we classify algebraic generalized Ricci solitons on three-dimensional Lie algebras, and algebraic generalized Ricci solitons on four-dimensional solvable unimodular Lie algebras, up to isometry and scaling.

</details>


### [223] [Finite Time Singularities of the Kähler Ricci Flow on Compact Kähler Surfaces are of Type I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01643)
*Yeyun Xu,Linfeng Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在紧致Kähler曲面上为有限时间体积坍缩的Kähler–Ricci流建立了I型曲率界，无需对称性假设，初始Kähler类也不需为有理类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体积坍缩情形下Kähler–Ricci流的曲率行为此前缺乏一般性刻画，现有结果往往依赖对称性假设或初始Kähler类为有理类的限制；本文旨在去除这些限制，建立更普适的I型曲率界。

Method: 证明结合了局部辛拓扑方法与梯度Kähler–Ricci收缩孤子的分类；利用Bamler的紧性与结构理论构造论证所需的收缩极限。

Result: 在紧致Kähler曲面上，对有限时间体积坍缩的Kähler–Ricci流证明了I型曲率界，且不施加对称性假设、初始Kähler类不必为有理类。

Conclusion: 该结果将I型曲率界的适用范围推广到更一般的体积坍缩Kähler–Ricci流，表明局部辛拓扑、收缩孤子分类与Bamler紧性结构理论相结合是研究坍缩流奇点行为的有效途径。

Abstract: We establish a Type I curvature bound for finite-time volume-collapsing Kähler--Ricci flows on compact Kähler surfaces. No symmetry assumption is imposed, and the initial Kähler class need not be rational. The proof combines local symplectic topology with the classification of gradient Kähler--Ricci shrinking solitons. Bamler's compactness and structure theory provides the shrinking limits used in the argument.

</details>


### [224] [Classification of quadratically pinched translators in higher codimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01713)
*Debora Impera,Michele Rimoldi,Francesco Ruatta*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究任意余维数平均曲率流平移孤子，在第二基本形式二次挤压条件下证明Andrews-Baker阈值处的余维数约化定理，并给出刚性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进以往由古代平均曲率流余维数约化结果所得的低维挤压范围，并在无熵假设下获得刚性分类。

Method: 纯椭圆方法：将平移孤子视为加权极小子流形，结合漂移Laplace算子的Simons型恒等式、精细梯度估计与Omori-Yau极大值原理。

Result: 若完备n维连通平移孤子满足|B|^2 ≤ (4/(3n)-ε0)|H|^2，则为仿射n-平面，或处处|H|>0且含于(n+1)维仿射子空间；在更强挤压|B|^2 ≤ (c_n-ε0)|H|^2（c_n=min{4/(3n),1/(n-2)}，n=2时c_n=2/3）下，孤子为仿射平面或含于(n+1)维仿射子空间的bowl孤子。

Conclusion: 建立了平移孤子在二次挤压下的余维数约化与刚性定理，无需熵假设，改进了低维情形已知结果。

Abstract: We study complete translating solitons for the mean curvature flow in arbitrary codimension under quadratic pinching of the second fundamental form. We prove a codimension reduction theorem at the Andrews-Baker threshold: if a complete $n$-dimensional connected translator isometrically immersed in $(\mathbb{R}^{n+p},\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle)$ satisfies \[ |B|^2\leq \left(\frac{4}{3n}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)|H|^2 \] for some $\varepsilon_{0}>0$, then either it is an affine $n$-plane or $|H|>0$ everywhere and the translator is contained in an $(n+1)$-dimensional affine subspace. In particular, this improves in low dimensions the pinching range previously obtained from codimension reduction results for ancient mean curvature flows. Our proof is purely elliptic: viewing translators as weighted minimal submanifolds, we combine Simons-type identities for the drift Laplacian with refined gradient estimates and an Omori-Yau maximum principle. As a consequence, under the stronger pinching condition \[ |B|^2\leq (c_n-\varepsilon_{0})|H|^2,\qquad c_n=\min\left\{\frac{4}{3n},\frac{1}{n-2}\right\} \] for $n\geq3$, with $c_n=2/3$ when $n=2$, we obtain a rigidity theorem: the translator is either an affine plane or a bowl soliton contained in an $(n+1)$-dimensional affine subspace. No entropy assumption is required.

</details>


### [225] [Hessian manifolds of positive constant Hessian sectional curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01737)
*Hakobi Sakamoto*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在正曲率情形下分类了常Hessian截面曲率的Hessian流形标准模型，方法是在具哈密顿作用的Kähler流形商流形上引入Hessian结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hessian几何被视为Kähler几何的实类比，Hessian截面曲率对应Kähler几何中的全纯截面曲率。Furuhata与Kurose（2013）已分类非正曲率情形的常Hessian截面曲率标准模型，但正曲率情形仍是公开问题，本文旨在解决该问题。

Method: 在配备阿贝尔李群合适哈密顿作用的Kähler流形的商流形上引入Hessian结构，并将标准模型实现为此类商流形，以此完成正曲率情形的分类。

Result: 完成了正曲率情形下常Hessian截面曲率Hessian流形标准模型的分类。

Conclusion: 正曲率情形的标准模型可通过具哈密顿作用的Kähler流形商流形实现并分类，从而补全了常Hessian截面曲率标准模型的完整分类。

Abstract: Hessian structure on a manifold is a geometric structure consisting of a pair $(\nabla,g)$ of an affine connection $\nabla$ and a Riemannian metric $g$ satisfying certain compatibility conditions. It is also known as a dually flat structure in information geometry. Hessian geometry is considered to be a real analogue of Kähler geometry; in particular, Hessian sectional curvature corresponds to holomorphic sectional curvature in Kähler geometry. The standard models of Hessian manifolds with constant Hessian sectional curvature were classified by Furuhata and Kurose (2013) in the nonpositive curvature case, whereas the positive curvature case is open. In this paper, we classify the standard models in the positive curvature case. For this end, we introduce Hessian structure on quotient manifolds of Kähler manifolds equipped with suitable Hamiltonian actions of abelian Lie groups and realizing the standard models as such quotient manifolds.

</details>


### [226] [Quasi-contact metric structures from Sasaki-Einstein metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02073)
*Beniamino Cappelletti-Montano,Antonio De Nicola*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文首次构造了维数≥5的奇数维非接触的拟接触度量结构，并在5维给出含Killing特征向量场的单参数族及S^5上的齐性例子，同时给出Ricci曲率判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟接触度量结构与接触度量结构的关系尚不清楚，此前缺乏非接触的拟接触度量结构实例，作者希望揭示二者的差异并刻画其判别条件。

Method: 在任意奇数维（≥5）显式构造非接触的拟接触度量结构；在5维通过对Sasaki-Einstein SU(2)-结构进行形变，构造具Killing特征向量场的单参数族；并研究具Killing特征向量场的拟接触度量结构，用Ricci曲率给出K-contact判据。

Result: 得到≥5奇数维首批非接触的拟接触度量结构；在5维得到一参数族例子，并在S^5上获得紧致齐性例子；建立了拟接触度量结构为K-contact的Ricci曲率判据。

Conclusion: 拟接触度量结构严格推广了接触度量结构，二者在≥5维确有本质区别；Killing特征向量场下的Ricci曲率条件可判定K-contact性，S^5提供了具体紧致齐性实例。

Abstract: We construct the first examples of quasi-contact metric structures that are not contact metric in every odd dimension greater than or equal to $5$. In dimension $5$, we construct a one-parameter family of such examples with a Killing characteristic vector field by deforming Sasaki-Einstein $\mathrm{SU}(2)$-structures. This yields compact, homogeneous examples on $S^5$. In arbitrary dimensions, we investigate quasi-contact metric structures with a Killing characteristic vector field and derive a Ricci curvature criterion for the structure to be K-contact.

</details>


### [227] [The Morse index of prismatic free boundary minimal surfaces in the unit ball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02078)
*Hung Tran*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文精确计算了一类具棱柱群对称性的零亏格自由边界极小曲面的Morse指标（=2b）与零维数（=3），并给出指标形式负空间在对称群下的表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球内自由边界极小曲面的Morse指标与零维数极少被精确计算，除赤道圆盘和临界悬链面外基本未知，作者希望借助对称性精确确定一大类曲面的指标与零维数。

Method: 利用有限群表示论，对面积与能量构造等变比较（equivariant comparison），并借助圆域按对称类型统计Teichmüller方向。

Result: 证明该零亏格b边界分量FBMS的Morse指标为2b、零维数为3，并确定指标形式负空间作为对称群表示的分解；给出具旋转或多面体对称性曲面的指标下界。

Conclusion: 结果涵盖KKMS的b-noids及大b时Folha–Pacard–Zolotareva的零亏格曲面，是除赤道圆盘与临界悬链面外首批指标被精确确定的球内FBMS。

Abstract: For integer $b\ge3$, let $\Sig\subset\Ball$ be an embedded free boundary minimal surface (FBMS) of genus zero with $b$ boundary components. Suppose that $\Sig$ is invariant under the prismatic group of order $4b$, the rotation of order $b$ permutes the boundary components cyclically, and the reflection in the equatorial plane preserves each of them. We show that the Morse index of $\Sig$ is $2b$ and its nullity is three; moreover, we determine the negative space of the index form as a representation of the symmetry group. Examples include the $b$-noids of Karpukhin, Kusner, McGrath, and Stern and, for large $b$, the genus-zero surfaces of Folha, Pacard, and Zolotareva. To our knowledge, apart from the equatorial disc and the critical catenoid, these are the first FBMS in the ball whose Morse indices are known precisely. There are also lower bounds for the index of surfaces with rotational or polyhedral symmetry. The main new ingredient is an equivariant comparison, using the representation theory of finite groups, between area and energy, in which the Teichmüller directions are counted by symmetry type using circle domains.

</details>


### [228] [Uniqueness of the soliton vector field of a shrinking gradient Kähler-Ricci soliton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02164)
*Ronan J. Conlon,Alix Deruelle*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 完备收缩梯度Kähler-Ricci孤子在其孤子向量场的选取上，在双全纯映射推前意义下具有唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kähler-Ricci孤子作为Ricci流的自相似解，其孤子向量场刻画了度量演化的方向。然而同一孤子度量可能对应不同的孤子向量场，厘清这种非唯一性程度对于孤子的分类与刚性研究至关重要。

Method: 对完备收缩梯度Kähler-Ricci孤子的孤子向量场进行唯一性分析，借助双全纯映射的推前（pushforward）建立不同孤子向量场之间的等价关系。

Result: 证明了：若两个孤子向量场对应同一完备收缩梯度Kähler-Ricci孤子，则它们之间至多相差双全纯映射的推前。

Conclusion: 完备收缩梯度Kähler-Ricci孤子的孤子向量场在双全纯推前意义下唯一，为该类孤子的刚性及分类提供了基础性结论。

Abstract: We establish the uniqueness of the soliton vector field of a complete shrinking gradient Kähler-Ricci soliton up to pushforward by biholomorphism.

</details>


### [229] [Ahlfors--Weill Extension, Asymptotically Conformal Curves and Epstein--Poincaré Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02171)
*Ming Hong Tee*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文构造局部拟反射,修正Ahlfors经典估计,证明渐近共形曲线所围区域具有渐近相似的双曲与拟双曲度量,并结合Epstein曲面的几何解释得出互补区域的Epstein-Poincaré曲面在曲线附近任意接近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ahlfors-Weill构造通过Epstein曲面给出了Schwarzian范数小于1/2的单叶函数的拟共形延拓,而渐近共形曲线情形下统一化映射可延拓到更大的圆盘,需借助Loewner理论验证其拟共形性。作者希望在该延拓框架下构造局部拟反射,并用以改进经典估计。

Method: 构造一个具有此类延拓性质的局部拟反射;利用该局部拟反射修正Ahlfors的经典估计;并将该延拓通过Epstein曲面进行几何重释。

Result: 证明由渐近共形曲线所围的区域具有渐近相似的双曲度量和拟双曲度量;并证明互补区域的Epstein-Poincaré曲面在曲线附近彼此任意接近。

Conclusion: 局部拟反射为研究渐近共形曲线提供了有效工具,既统一了度量渐近相似性的估计,也从Epstein曲面角度刻画了边界附近的几何接近性。

Abstract: The Ahlfors--Weill construction explicitly extends a univalent function $f$ with Schwarzian norm less than $1/2$ to a quasiconformal homeomorphism of the Riemann sphere, an extension that can be understood geometrically through Epstein surfaces. For asymptotically conformal curves, the uniformization map can be extended to a larger disk, and Loewner theory can be used to verify that this extension is quasiconformal. In this paper, we construct a local quasireflection with such an extension. Using this local quasireflection, we modify classical estimates of Ahlfors and show that domains bounded by asymptotically conformal curves have asymptotically similar hyperbolic and quasihyperbolic metrics. Furthermore, by combining these estimates with a geometric reinterpretation of the extension through Epstein surfaces, we show that the Epstein-Poincaré surfaces of the complementary domains are arbitrarily close to one another near the curve.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [230] [Quantum hierarchies of the ILW and BO equations and the DR cycles on the moduli spaces of curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01841)
*Alexandr Buryak,Abner Hernandez Rodriguez*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子ILW可积层级与其极限（KdV与BO）之间的关系，提出一个将量子ILW层级的BO极限与Nazarov–Sklyanin量子层级相联系的猜想，并给出多种验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前P. Rossi与第一作者基于任意上同调场论和稳定曲线模空间上的double ramification（DR）循环，构造了一大类量子层级，其特例给出Intermediate Long Wave（ILW）方程的量子化。ILW有两个著名极限：KdV方程与Benjamin–Ono（BO）方程，而BO的量子层级已由Nazarov–Sklyanin构造。作者希望厘清量子ILW层级的极限与既有Nazarov–Sklyanin层级之间的关系。

Method: 以DR循环构造的量子ILW层级为出发点，考察其向BO极限的退化，并将该极限与Nazarov–Sklyanin量子层级进行对比，提出描述二者关系的猜想。

Result: 提出一个联系量子ILW层级极限与Nazarov–Sklyanin层级的猜想。

Conclusion: 通过多种检验支持该猜想，表明量子ILW层级的BO极限与Nazarov–Sklyanin量子层级之间存在预期的一致性关系。

Abstract: In an earlier paper of P. Rossi and the first author, a construction of a wide class of quantum hierarchies was presented, with the input data given by an arbitrary cohomological field theory. A crucial role in the construction is played by the double ramification (DR) cycles on the moduli spaces of stable curves. As a special case, one gets a quantization of the hierarchy of the Intermediate Long Wave (ILW) equation. The ILW equation has two famous limits: the Korteweg--de Vries equation and the Benjamin--Ono (BO) equation, and for the latter one a quantum hierarchy was constructed by Nazarov and Sklyanin. In our paper, we propose a conjecture describing a relation between a limit of the quantum ILW hierarchy and the Nazarov--Sklyanin hierarchy and present various checks to support it.

</details>


### [231] [Closed-form evaluation of the cyclic four-electron atomic correlation integral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00036)
*Jonas Matuzas*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了四电子循环Slater积分（Bonham分类中的case V）在正指数下的有限实数解析表达式，权重不超过三，一般结果为十八个块的显式和，仅涉及对数、双对数和三对数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四电子Slater积分是量子化学与多体物理中的基本量，但其循环拓扑（case V）此前缺乏一个无需根式、广义多重对数、围道规定或未求极限的完全有限解析表达式，作者旨在填补这一空白并建立相应解析理论。

Method: 通过将循环四电子Slater积分化为权重至多三的多重对数组合，作者构造出由十八个块组成的显式求和公式，并将所有对数参数取正、非平凡多重对数参数限制在(-1,1)。同时采用三条独立数值路径互验，并用精确二进分数值计算经典公式作为高精度证书，另对极限律、二面体对称性与重排律进行解析证明。

Result: 一般答案为十八个块组成的显式和，仅含对数、双对数与三对数；等指数情形化简为I(λ,λ,λ,λ)=(4π)^4λ^{-4}[Li_3(1/4)/2-7ζ(3)/24+π^2 log2/12-2log^3 2/3]，是仅在2和3处分歧的权重三周期；三套独立数值方法在四组指数元组上吻合到至少32位小数，精确二进法给出八个100位证书，三组已发表标定值逐位复现；并证明小/大指数极限律、二面体对称性与三不等价循环排布下的严格重排律。

Conclusion: 该公式适用于四循环拓扑，成功给出有限实数权重三解析表达并实现高精度数值验证，但不评估两种带弦（chorded）拓扑。

Abstract: For positive exponents $α_1,\ldots,α_4$ we evaluate the cyclic four-electron Slater integral, the topology recorded as case V in Bonham's 1965 classification, in a finite real expression of weight at most three. The general answer is an explicit sum of eighteen blocks involving only logarithms, dilogarithms and trilogarithms of rational functions of the exponents; all logarithm arguments are positive and all nonconstant polylogarithm arguments may be placed in $(-1,1)$, so no radical, generalized polylogarithm, contour prescription or unevaluated limit is required. At equal exponents the value collapses to $I(λ,λ,λ,λ)=(4π)^4λ^{-4}[\mathrm{Li}_3(1/4)/2-7ζ(3)/24+π^2\log 2/12-2\log^3 2/3]$, a weight-three period over $\mathbb{Q}$ ramified only at 2 and 3. Three independently implemented numerical routes agree at four exponent tuples to at least 32 decimal places, an exact-dyadic evaluation of the classical formula gives eight 100-place certificates, and three published four-electron calibration values are reproduced to every printed digit. We also prove the small- and large-exponent limit laws with closed-form coefficients, dihedral symmetry, and a strict rearrangement law for the three inequivalent cyclic placements of four exponents. The formula applies to the four-cycle and does not evaluate the two chorded topologies.

</details>


### [232] [Quantum Reverse Hypercontractivity for KMS-Symmetric Quantum Markov Semigroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00242)
*Jinsong Wu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究原始KMS对称量子Markov半群的量子反向超压缩性，构造了在p=1/2处p-对数Sobolev常数消失及当p→0⁺时趋于−∞的例子，证明了在(0,1)上不存在一致正反向超压缩速率，并在1/2<p≤q<1及退相干扰动、图基情形下给出了正向结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究原始KMS对称量子Markov半群的量子反向超压缩性是否在整个区间(0,1)上具有一致的正速率，并刻画其适用参数范围与充分条件。

Method: 构造原始KMS对称量子Markov半群的显式反例，包括p-对数Sobolev常数在p=1/2处消失的情形以及相关的图基例子（p→0⁺时常数趋于−∞）；同时通过退相干扰动与图基半群分析，给出反向超压缩性的充分时间估计。

Result: 构造的反例表明(0,1)上不存在正的一致反向超压缩速率；但每个原始KMS对称量子Markov半群在1/2<p≤q<1上满足量子反向超压缩性并具有显式充分时间；对任意δ>0，足够强的退相干扰动在[δ,1]上满足一致正对数Sobolev不等式，从而在δ<p≤q<1（0<δ<1/2）上成立反向超压缩性；对于n≥3的连通图，图基KMS对称量子Markov半群在δ<p≤q<1（0<δ<1）上成立反向超压缩性。

Conclusion: 量子反向超压缩性在接近p=1/2及p→0⁺时可能失效，无法在(0,1)上获得一致正速率；但在1/2<p≤q<1区间以及退相干扰动、连通图（n≥3）等条件下，反向超压缩性仍可成立并具有定量时间估计。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct primitive KMS-symmetric quantum Markov semigroups for which the $p$-logarithmic Sobolev constant vanishes at $p=1/2$ and a correlated graph-based example for which the $p$-logarithmic Sobolev constant tends to $-\infty$ as $p\to 0^+$. These examples obstruct a positive uniform reverse-hypercontractivity rate on $(0,1)$. By contrast, we show that every primitive KMS-symmetric quantum Markov semigroup satisfies quantum reverse hypercontractivity for $1/2< p\leq q < 1$ with explicit sufficient times. For each fixed $δ>0$, a sufficiently strong dephasing perturbation satisfies a uniform positive logarithmic Sobolev inequality on $[δ, 1]$ and hence quantum reverse hypercontractivity for $δ< p\leq q<1$ with fixed $0<δ<1/2$. Furthermore, we establish reverse hypercontractivity for graph-based KMS-symmetric quantum Markov semigroups for $δ< p\leq q< 1$ with fixed $0<δ<1$ for connected graphs when $n \geq 3$.

</details>


### [233] [Complete Factorial Asymptotics and Integrable Structure of Generalized Charlier Recurrence Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00396)
*Mahouton Norbert Hounkonnou*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究广义Charlier多项式关于离散权 ρ_μ(k)=μ^k/(k!)^2 的首一递推系数，证明了偏差 β_n-n 与 μ-γ_n 的超代数阶渐近，并给出精确连接公式 c_n∼J_n(2√μ) 及因子式修正项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对离散权 μ^k/(k!)^2 对应的广义Charlier多项式，其首一递推系数 β_n、γ_n 偏离平凡值 β_n≈n、γ_n≈μ 的修正量此前缺乏完整渐近刻画。作者希望揭示这些偏差的真实阶与显式主项，并理解其与可积结构（离散Painlevé、Toda、Hankel行列式）的联系。

Method: 1) 采用参数化 β_n-n=√μ c_n c_{n+1}，γ_n=μ(1-c_n^2)，将递推系数问题化为满足离散Painlevé II方程的正序列 (c_n)。2) 在 c_n=0 附近线性化，得到离散Bessel方程，其recessive解为 J_n(2√μ)。3) 证明比值 c_n/J_n(2√μ) 收敛到有限极限 L(μ)，结合Bessel比较与基于Hankel行列式扇区分解的全局分析，定出 L(μ)=1。4) 另用Hankel行列式的离散对数气体解释，证明 Δ_n(μ)=μ^{n(n-1)/2}T_n(μ) 及扇区分解 T_n(μ)=1+Σ_{q=1}^n S_q(μ)，并估计 S_q(μ)∼e^μ μ^q/(q!)^2。

Result: 得到精确连接公式 c_n∼J_n(2√μ) (n→∞)，进而 β_n-n∼μ^{n+1}/(n!(n+1)!)，μ-γ_n∼μ^{n+1}/(n!)^2，表明偏差超越一切代数阶，出现在尺度 μ^n/(n!)^2。同时获得 Hankel 行列式的因子分解与扇区贡献渐近 S_q(μ)∼e^μ μ^q/(q!)^2，独立导出因子式修正。

Conclusion: Bessel比较与扇区分解两种方法相互印证，揭示了离散Painlevé动力学、Toda形变与Hankel-分拆表示之间的丰富可积结构，并完整刻画了广义Charlier递推系数的因子式渐近行为。

Abstract: We study the monic recurrence coefficients of the generalized Charlier polynomials associated with the discrete weight
  \[
  ρ_μ(k)
  =
  \frac{μ^k}{(k!)^2},
  \qquad
  k\in\mathbb N_0,
  \qquad
  μ>0.
  \]
  We establish complete factorial asymptotics for the deviations $β_n-n$ and $μ-γ_n$, proving that they are beyond all algebraic orders and appear at the scale $μ^{n}/(n!)^2$. The key step is a parametrization $β_n-n=\sqrtμ\,c_nc_{n+1}$, $γ_n=μ(1-c_n^2)$, which reduces the problem to a positive sequence $(c_n)$ satisfying a discrete Painlevé II equation.
  Linearization around $c_n=0$ yields a discrete Bessel equation whose recessive solution is $J_n(2\sqrtμ)$. We prove that the ratio $c_n/J_n(2\sqrtμ)$ converges to a finite limit $L(μ)$, which we identify exactly as $L(μ)=1$ by combining Bessel comparison with a global analysis based on the sector decomposition of the associated Hankel determinant. This yields the exact connection formula
  \[
  c_n \sim J_n(2\sqrtμ), \qquad n\to\infty,
  \]
  and consequently the precise asymptotics
  \[
  β_n-n\sim \frac{μ^{n+1}}{n!(n+1)!}, \qquad
  μ-γ_n\sim \frac{μ^{n+1}}{(n!)^2}.
  \]
  We further develop a complementary approach based on Hankel determinants interpreted as a discrete logarithmic gas. We show that the Hankel determinant factorizes as $Δ_n(μ)=μ^{n(n-1)/2}T_n(μ)$ and admits a sector decomposition $T_n(μ)=1+\sum_{q=1}^{n}S_q(μ)$ with sector contributions independent of $n$. We establish $S_q(μ)\sim e^μμ^q/(q!)^2$, providing an independent derivation of the factorial corrections. Together, the Bessel comparison and sector decomposition reveal a rich integrable structure linking discrete Painlevé dynamics, Toda deformations, and Hankel--partition representations.

</details>


### [234] [On spectrum of the harmonic Kronig-Penney model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00879)
*J. Gibbons,A. P. Veselov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究穿衣服链理论所启发的量子力学Kronig-Penney模型的谐振修正的光谱性质，证明在特殊情形下基态可显式给出，且所有谱隙等长，其端点可表示为相应Weber-Hermite函数的零点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究受穿衣服链（dressing chain）理论的启发，旨在探究谐振修正下量子力学Kronig-Penney模型的光谱性质，以理解此类周期势模型的能带结构与谱隙特征。

Method: 以穿衣服链理论为动机，对量子力学Kronig-Penney模型进行谐振修正，分析其光谱性质；在特殊情形下求解基态，并利用Weber-Hermite函数的零点刻画谱隙端点。

Result: 在特殊情形下，相应的基态可以显式地给出；并且所有谱隙具有相同的长度，其端点可表示为相应Weber-Hermite函数的零点。

Conclusion: 谐振修正的Kronig-Penney模型在特殊情形下具有可显式求解的基态与等长谱隙结构，谱隙端点与Weber-Hermite函数零点相关联，揭示了该模型与穿衣服链理论之间的联系。

Abstract: We study the spectral properties of the harmonic modification of the quantum mechanical Kronig-Penney model motivated by the theory of the dressing chain. In particular, we show that in the special cases the corresponding ground states can be given explicitly and all spectral gaps have the same length with the ends expressed as zeros of the corresponding Weber-Hermite functions.

</details>


### [235] [The Buchholz Algebra from the Universal Resolvent Algebra--Particle Structure, Gibbs States, and Closed-Path Expansions without Fock Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01622)
*Yoshitsugu Sekine*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文不依赖Fock表示构造了Buchholz代数，将其实现为规范固定预解代数的本征粒子截断商的有界逆向极限，并赋予等距粒子度坐标；其有限矩阵角具备典范迹、KMS态与迹范数乘积公式，以及为路径积分表示奠基的闭路展开；已知Fock空间描述与适当的准局域子代数作为实现出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Buchholz代数（及相关的预解代数）通常依赖Fock空间表示来定义与刻画，这限制了其构造的普适性与代数内在性。作者希望给出一个不依赖Fock表示的、纯代数/算子代数式的构造，从而揭示其更本质的结构（如粒子截断商、迹与KMS态等），并为路径积分表示提供代数基础。

Method: 以规范固定预解代数的本征粒子截断商为基本构件，取有界逆向极限构造Buchholz代数；引入等距粒子度坐标；在其有限矩阵角上定义典范迹与KMS态，推导迹范数乘积公式，并建立闭路展开。

Result: 所构造的代数无需求助Fock表示；其有限矩阵角具有典范迹、KMS态与迹范数乘积公式；闭路展开为路径积分表示奠定基础；已知的Fock空间描述与适当的准局域子代数均可作为该构造的具体实现（realization）出现。

Conclusion: Buchholz代数可脱离Fock表示、以内蕴的粒子截断商逆向极限方式构造，Fock描述仅是其中一种实现；该框架统一了迹、KMS态、乘积公式与闭路展开，为路径积分表示提供了代数前提。

Abstract: We construct the Buchholz algebra without a Fock representation, as the bounded inverse limit of intrinsic particle-cutoff quotients of the gauge-fixed resolvent algebra, with isometric particle-degree coordinates. Its finite matrix corners carry canonical traces, KMS states, and trace-norm product formulas, together with closed-path expansions that lay the groundwork for path-integral representations. The known Fock-space description and a proper quasilocal subalgebra arise as realizations.

</details>


### [236] [Quadratic symmetry algebras and Sturm algebraization of superintegrable systems with magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01869)
*Shams Ara,Md Fazlul Hoque,Ian Marquette*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究了轴对称磁场中两个三维量子超可积系统，通过二次代数/变形振子、抛物分离后的sl(2)代数化，以及分离前完整Sturm问题的sl(3)代数化，给出三种互补代数描述与精确谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示同一多重可分超可积系统在不同处理阶段可涌现出不同的隐藏代数结构与精确/准精确可解性。

Method: 对两个轴对称磁场模型构造积分并导出含Casimir的二次代数，用有限维变形振子表示求谱；在圆抛物坐标下分离得到六次双合流Heun方程，将其表述为以物理能量为参数、分离常数为对角的Sturm本征值问题，经规范变换实现sl(2)代数化并化为有限三对角矩阵；进一步构造分离前完整哈密顿量的Sturm表示，以反平方耦合为Sturm本征值，固定轴向量后将双变量Sturm哈密顿量及非中心积分纳入U(sl(3))。

Result: 三种方法给出相同谱：二次代数/变形振子表示、抛物分离后的sl(2)代数化、完整Sturm问题的sl(3)代数化；固定轴向积分后两个原分类中不同的磁场哈密顿量化为同一奇异2:1振子正规形，但有效参数嵌入不同。

Conclusion: 两个磁场系统尽管原分类不同，固定轴向积分后在Sturm层面共享sl(3)结构，但并不意味原系统等价；表明多重可分超可积系统中不同阶段可呈现不同隐藏代数结构及精确或准精确可解性。

Abstract: We study two three-dimensional quantum superintegrable systems in nonvanishing axially symmetric magnetic fields. For each model the integrals generate a quadratic algebra with a Casimir, and finite-dimensional deformed-oscillator representations yield algebraic spectra. The same spectra are recovered by separation of variables. In circular parabolic coordinates the separated equations are sextic biconfluent-Heun equations with finite polynomial sectors. We formulate them as Sturm eigenvalue problems with the physical energy as a parameter and the separation constant diagonalized. After gauge transformation, each equation admits an $sl(2)$ algebraization and reduces to a finite tridiagonal matrix. We also construct a pre-separation Sturm representation of each complete Hamiltonian, taking the inverse-square coupling as the Sturm eigenvalue. The transformed integrals remain commuting and, after fixing the axial integral, changing variables and gauge rotating, the two-variable Sturm Hamiltonian and its non-central integrals belong to the universal enveloping algebra $U(sl(3))$. We thus obtain three complementary algebraic descriptions: the quadratic-algebra/deformed-oscillator representation of the original Hamiltonian, the $sl(2)$ algebraization after parabolic separation, and the $sl(3)$ algebraization of the complete Sturm problem before separation. Although the two magnetic Hamiltonians are distinct in the original classification, fixing the axial integral reduces them to the same singular 2:1 oscillator normal form with different effective parameter embeddings. This explains the common $sl(3)$ structure without implying equivalence of the original magnetic systems. These results show how distinct hidden algebraic structures and exact or quasi-exact solvability can emerge at different stages of a multiseparable superintegrable system.

</details>


### [237] [Superintegrability of a resonant ghostly tri-Hamiltonian model: polynomial first integrals and transcendental completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01901)
*Andreas Fring,Ian Marquette*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一个具有不定动能的三维共振Hamiltonian的经典与量子超可积性，及其同一流的三种Poisson描述。多项式首次积分环除三个对合二次Hamiltonian外，还含两个四次本原生成元；一个八次关系使环的超越次数为四。结合分支自由的超越不变量，系统在多项式与有理类中为极小超可积，在允许超越积分时于不变量开集上为极大超可积；Weyl量子化给出三套Poisson实现中的精确高阶微分对称性及非局域量子对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典与量子超可积系统的研究主要集中于正定动能情形，而不定动能、共振三维系统及其多种Poisson描述的完整首次积分结构与功能秩分类尚不清楚；本文旨在厘清此类系统在多项式、有理与超越函数类中的可积层次，并建立其量子对应。

Method: 研究一个三维共振Hamiltonian在三种Poisson描述下的经典动力学；分析多项式首次积分环的生成元（三个二次Hamiltonian与两个四次本原生成元）及其八次关系；求解完整首次积分方程以确定所有有理首次积分；引入分支自由的超越不变量；采用Weyl量子化构造精确高阶微分对称性并寻找第五不变量的非局域量子对应。

Result: 多项式不变量环由三个二次Hamiltonian与两个四次本原生成元生成，满足一个八次关系，超越次数为四；每个有理首次积分都是H1,H2,H3,I4^o的有理函数，有理不变量域的功能秩为四；分支自由超越不变量在Δ>0的不变量开集上把一般光滑功能秩提升到五；Weyl量子化在所有三种Poisson实现中给出精确高阶微分对称性，分支自由的第五经典不变量具有稠定非局域量子对应。

Conclusion: 该系统在多项式与有理函数类中为极小超可积，而在允许超越积分时于不变量域Δ>0上为极大超可积；Weyl量子化提供了该超越完备化的具体量子实现。

Abstract: We study classical and quantum superintegrability of a resonant three-dimensional Hamiltonian with indefinite kinetic energy and three Poisson descriptions of the same flow. Besides three commuting quadratic Hamiltonians, the polynomial first-integral ring contains two primitive degree-four generators, $I_4^{\rm o}$ and $I_4^{\rm e}$, of odd and even momentum parity. A single octic relation leaves the polynomial invariant ring with transcendence degree four. Solving the complete first-integral equation shows that every rational first integral is a rational function of $H_1,H_2,H_3,I_4^{\rm o}$, so that the rational invariant field likewise has generic functional rank four. A branch-free transcendental invariant raises the generic smooth functional rank to five on the invariant open set $Δ>0$. The system is therefore minimally superintegrable in the polynomial and rational classes and maximally superintegrable on this invariant domain when transcendental integrals are admitted. Weyl quantisation yields exact higher-order differential symmetries in all three Poisson realisations. The branch-free fifth classical invariant also admits a densely defined nonlocal quantum counterpart, providing a concrete quantum realisation of the transcendental completion.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [238] [Self-Orthogonal $τ$-Tilting Modules and Tilting Modules III: Finitistic Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00105)
*Xiaojin Zhang,Panyue Zhou*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究Artin代数上τ-tilting模的自正交猜想与有限维数猜想之间的关系，证明有限little finitistic dimension加有限自扩张消失范围迫使τ-tilting模成为经典1-tilting模，并给出两个猜想间的蕴含关系及radical cube zero情形的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: τ-tilting理论中，自正交τ-tilting模何时等同于经典tilting模是一个核心问题，且与同调猜想（有限维数猜想）密切相关。作者希望建立自正交τ-tilting猜想与finitistic dimension猜想之间的桥梁，从而借助已知结果推进该猜想在特定代数类中的验证。

Method: 设A为Artin代数，T为τ-tilting右A-模，令C=A/Ann_A(T)。利用有限little finitistic dimension条件与T的自扩张消失的有限范围，通过同调代数与τ-tilting理论的模论论证进行刻画；并通过猜想的蕴含论证建立两猜想间的联系。

Result: 证明若C^op的little finitistic dimension有限，且T的自扩张消失（Ext^i(T,T)=0）的范围有限，则T必为经典1-tilting模。进一步证明：若finitistic dimension猜想对所有Artin代数成立，则自正交τ-tilting猜想对所有Artin代数成立；反之，自正交τ-tilting猜想的反例将给出finitistic dimension猜想的一个反例。

Conclusion: 两猜想之间存在单向蕴含与反例对应关系，自正交τ-tilting猜想在radical cube zero的Artin代数上成立。结果深化了τ-tilting理论与有限维数猜想之间的联系。

Abstract: Let $A$ be an Artin algebra and let $T$ be a $τ$-tilting right $A$-module. Put $C=A/{\rm Ann}_A(T)$. We prove that finite little finitistic dimension of $C^{\rm op}$, together with a finite range of self-extension vanishing for $T$, forces $T$ to be a classical $1$-tilting module. We also show that if the finitistic conjecture holds for all Artin algebras then the self-orthogonal $τ$-tilting conjecture holds for all Artin algebras. Moreover, a counterexample to the self-orthogonal $τ$-tilting conjecture will give a counterexample to the finitistic dimension conjecture. As an application, the self-orthogonal $τ$-tilting conjecture holds for Artin algebras with radical cube zero.

</details>


### [239] [A characterization of triangulated structures induced by premutation triples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00135)
*Panyue Zhou*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Compat条件(MT4)是Iitsuka预三角结构成为三角结构的充要条件，并给出反例回答Iitsuka的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Iitsuka引入premutation triple作为各类mutation构造的统一框架，并证明商范畴Z/[I]具有典范pretriangulated结构，但该结构何时成为triangulated结构尚需刻画。本文旨在给出这一刻画并考察相关兼容条件和分类问题。

Method: 在extriangulated category中研究premutation triple (S,Z,V)，设I=S∩Z=Z∩V，证明Compat条件(MT4)对该结构成为triangulated既是充分也是必要的；对concentric twin cotorsion pairs，证明Nakaoka构造triangulated quotient时使用的兼容条件与(MT4)等价；构造显式的reducible triple作为反例。

Result: (MT4)精确刻画了Iitsuka典范pretriangulated结构成为triangulated的时机；对concentric twin cotorsion pairs，Nakaoka的兼容条件等价于(MT4)；构造出一个不是mutation triple的reducible triple。

Conclusion: (MT4)是triangulated商结构出现的充要条件，统一了Iitsuka与Nakaoka框架下的兼容性条件；反例表明reducible triple未必是mutation triple，对Iitsuka提出的问题给出否定回答。

Abstract: Let $(\mathcal{S},\mathcal{Z},\mathcal{V})$ be a premutation triple in an extriangulated category. Set $\mathcal{I}=\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{Z}=\mathcal{Z}\cap\mathcal{V}$. Iitsuka introduced premutation triples as a unified framework for various mutation constructions and showed that the quotient category $\mathcal{Z}/[\mathcal{I}]$ admits a canonical pretriangulated structure. In this paper, we prove that the compatibility condition $(\mathrm{MT4})$ is not only sufficient but also necessary for this structure to be triangulated. Therefore, $(\mathrm{MT4})$ precisely characterizes when Iitsuka's canonical pretriangulated structure becomes triangulated. Furthermore, we prove that, for concentric twin cotorsion pairs, the compatibility condition used by Nakaoka in the construction of triangulated quotients is equivalent to $(\mathrm{MT4})$. Finally, we construct an explicit reducible triple which is not a mutation triple. This gives a negative answer to a question posed by Iitsuka.

</details>


### [240] [Explicit Transgression Primitives on Flag Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00300)
*Greg Weiler*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文构造了旗流形 $K/T$ 上相对超渡原语 $\alpha_j$，显式给出 $d_\mathfrak{m}\alpha_j = -p_j(F_\mathfrak{t})$ 的闭合公式，并揭示 $j\ge 3$ 时循环迹对称性破缺、以及用 Koszul DGA 消解余不变量代数恢复 Poincaré 多项式与 Euler 特征的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Borel 定理以非构造方式给出 $H^*(K/T;\mathbb{C})\cong S(\mathfrak{h}^\vee)/I_W$，说明特征型 $p_j(F_\mathfrak{t})$ 的上同调消失，但从 Weil 代数 $W(\mathfrak{k})$ 投影得到的 $K$ 上闭原语无法下降到 $K/T$。作者希望显式构造这些原语的相对超渡版本，从而把消隐关系构造化、并理解其随 $j$ 增长的结构演化。

Method: 在相对微分复形上构造 $\alpha_j\in(\Lambda^{2j-1}\mathfrak{m}^\vee)^T\cong {}^K\Omega^{2j-1}(K/T)$；引入连接 $\theta_s=\theta_\mathfrak{t}+s\theta_\mathfrak{m}$，得到曲率分解 $F_s=(1-s)(F_\mathfrak{t}-s\theta_\mathfrak{m}^2)$；利用非交换字和与 Euler Beta 积分展开，导出对一切 $j\ge 1$ 的通用闭式。对 $A_2$（$\mathfrak{sl}_3(\mathbb{C})$）情形用 $T$-不变上链对应闭环进行几何刻画，并以 Koszul DGA 极小消解 $K_\bullet(p_2,\dots,p_k)$ 处理余不变量代数。

Result: 得到对所有 $j\ge 1$ 成立的显式原语公式，并刻画其结构转变：$\alpha_2=-\frac{1}{3}\mathrm{tr}(\theta_\mathfrak{m}^3)$ 无曲率项，$\alpha_3$ 对 $F_\mathfrak{t}$ 线性，而 $j=4$ 时 $[F_\mathfrak{t},\theta_\mathfrak{m}^2]\neq 0$ 使循环迹对称性破裂为独立项链，产生不可约不变量 $\mathrm{tr}(\theta_\mathfrak{m}\wedge F_\mathfrak{t}\wedge\theta_\mathfrak{m}^2\wedge F_\mathfrak{t})$。在 $A_2$ 情形，$\alpha_2=\Omega_--\Omega_+$ 为三角形有向差，$d_\mathfrak{m}$ 是边碎裂算子并见证 $c_2(E)$ 的恰当性；在 $k=4$ 处 Specht 分解出现重数障碍，而 Koszul DGA 消解无矩阵秩歧义地恢复 $P_{K/T}(t)=\prod_{j=2}^k\frac{1-t^{2j}}{1-t^2}$ 与 $\chi(K/T)=k!$。

Conclusion: 相对超渡原语为 Borel 定理的消隐关系提供了构造性证明，并显示出原语复杂度随次数递增而对称性逐步破缺的层级结构。Koszul DGA 消解克服了特征标方法在 Specht 分解中的重数障碍，干净地给出旗流形的 Poincaré 多项式与 Euler 特征。

Abstract: Borel's theorem presents the flag manifold cohomology as the coinvariant algebra $H^*(K/T; \mathbb{C}) \cong S(\mathfrak{h}^\vee)/I_W$, establishing the vanishing of characteristic forms $p_j(F_\mathfrak{t}) = \mathrm{tr}(F_\mathfrak{t}^j)$ non-constructively; projection from the Weil algebra $W(\mathfrak{k})$ yields closed primitives on $K$ failing to descend.
  We construct relative transgression primitives $α_j \in (Λ^{2j-1}\mathfrak{m}^\vee)^T \cong {}^KΩ^{2j-1}(K/T)$ satisfying $d_\mathfrak{m} α_j = -p_j(F_\mathfrak{t})$ on $K/T$. Via connections $θ_s = θ_\mathfrak{t} + sθ_\mathfrak{m}$, the curvature factors as $F_s = (1-s)(F_\mathfrak{t} - sθ_\mathfrak{m}^2)$; expanding via non-commutative word sums and Euler's Beta integral yields a universal closed formula for all $j \ge 1$.
  These primitives exhibit a structural transition: $α_2 = -\frac{1}{3}\mathrm{tr}(θ_\mathfrak{m}^3)$ is curvature-free, $α_3$ is linear in $F_\mathfrak{t}$, while at $j = 4$, $[F_\mathfrak{t}, θ_\mathfrak{m}^2] \neq 0$ shatters cyclic trace symmetry into independent necklaces, producing the irreducible invariant $\mathrm{tr}(θ_\mathfrak{m} \wedge F_\mathfrak{t} \wedge θ_\mathfrak{m}^2 \wedge F_\mathfrak{t})$.
  On the $A_2$ root lattice ($\mathfrak{sl}_3(\mathbb{C})$), $T$-invariant cochains correspond to closed loops, $α_2 = Ω_- - Ω_+$ is the oriented difference of triangles, and $d_\mathfrak{m}$ acts as an edge-fracturing operator witnessing the exactness of $c_2(E)$. While character theory encounters a multiplicity barrier at $k=4$ in Specht decompositions, a minimal Koszul DGA resolution $K_\bullet(p_2, \dots, p_k)$ resolves the coinvariant algebra, recovering the Poincaré polynomial $P_{K/T}(t) = \prod_{j=2}^k \frac{1-t^{2j}}{1-t^2}$ and Euler characteristic $χ(K/T) = k!$ without matrix-rank ambiguities.

</details>


### [241] [Magnitude of module categories and special biserial algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00413)
*Haruhisa Enomoto*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了Børve、Horiatakis和Kalck提出的关于模范畴magnitude的猜想：在代数闭域上，模范畴的magnitude至少等于单模个数，等号成立当且仅当代数为特殊双列代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Børve、Horiatakis和Kalck将表示有限代数模范畴的magnitude定义为不可分解模之间Hom维数矩阵之逆的元素和，并证明其等于Auslander–Reiten箭图的欧拉特征（顶点、箭头与网格的交替计数），进而猜想该magnitude不小于单模个数，且等号恰在特殊双列代数时成立。本文旨在证明这一猜想。

Method: 对表示有向代数，建立模范畴、去掉给定单模合成因子的子范畴以及其理想商三者magnitude之间的关系；利用Ringel和Vossieck的hammocks理论，将上述理想商化为有限偏序集表示范畴并计算其magnitude；通过给标准形代数的分次化构造，将一般情形归约到表示有向情形。

Result: 在代数闭域上证明了该猜想成立：模范畴的magnitude至少为单模个数，且等号成立当且仅当代数为特殊双列代数；同时给出了表示有向代数情形下相关范畴与理想商magnitude的关系，并计算了有限偏序集表示范畴的magnitude。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了Børve–Horiatakis–Kalck猜想，揭示了模范畴magnitude与单模个数之间的不等式关系及等号刻画的代数结构意义，并为相关范畴magnitude的计算提供了方法。

Abstract: Børve, Horiatakis, and Kalck defined the magnitude of the module category of a representation-finite algebra as the sum of the entries of the inverse of the matrix of Hom dimensions between indecomposable modules. They showed it is an Euler characteristic, the alternating count of vertices, arrows, and meshes of the Auslander--Reiten quiver, and conjectured that it is at least the number of simple modules, with equality precisely for special biserial algebras. We prove this conjecture over an algebraically closed field. For a representation-directed algebra, we relate the magnitudes of the module category, the subcategory of modules without a given simple composition factor, and the ideal quotient by it. By the theory of hammocks of Ringel and Vossieck, this quotient is equivalent to a category of representations of a finite poset, whose magnitude we compute. The general case reduces to the representation-directed case through algebras obtained by grading a standard form.

</details>


### [242] [Big Finitistic Dimensions of Radical-Cube-Zero Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00433)
*Liang Chen*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 构造一个十维代数 $A$（$\operatorname{rad}^3A=0$），使 $\operatorname{findim}A=2$、$\operatorname{Findim}A=\infty$、$\operatorname{Findim}A^{\mathrm{op}}=0$，从而给出对大有限维数猜想（big finitistic dimension conjecture）的反例，并系统刻画相关模类与维数向量，证明该反例类的野性与纯内射模存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大有限维数猜想（small/large finitistic dimension conjecture）长期是有限维代数表示论的核心问题。本文旨在构造 $\operatorname{findim}$ 有限而 $\operatorname{Findim}$ 无限的代数，从而否定大有限维数猜想，并进一步理解该类代数上投射维数受限模的结构与分类。

Method: 在任意域上显式构造十维代数 $A$，满足 $(\operatorname{rad}A)^3=0$；用显式的逆 syzygy（inverse-syzygy）构造产生每个正有限投射维数的可数生成右模，逐步确定完整投射覆盖核，并用非零 $\mathrm{Ext}$ 探测精确解消长度。对投射维数至多一的、可嵌入投射模根中的模，在不加生成限制下用可逆线性算子进行分类；确定投射维数二的有限维模的全部维数向量并分类达到尖锐维数界者（含全部十四维投射维数二模）。对任意 Artin 代数给出通过根湮灭理想与合适角（corners）递归控制有限投射解消的充分判据；对有限维初等 radical-cube-zero 代数，证明两顶点各含一 loop 情形的 $\operatorname{Findim}$ 有限性，并给出界为一或二的乘法映射判据。最后通过精确表示嵌入证明投射维数二类的野性，并在任意域上构造该类的超可分解纯内射模。

Result: 得到十维代数 $A$，$\operatorname{findim}A=2$，$\operatorname{Findim}A=\infty$，$\operatorname{Findim}(A^{\mathrm{op}})=0$，否证大有限维数猜想；显式逆 syzygy 构造给出每个正有限投射维数的可数生成右模；分类了维数至多一且嵌入投射模根的模（用可逆线性算子）；确定投射维数二模的全部维数向量及达到尖锐维数界者（含所有十四维投射维数二模）；对任意 Artin 代数给出递归充分判据；在两顶点各含一 loop 的初等 radical-cube-zero 情形证明 $\operatorname{Findim}$ 有限并给出界一或二的乘法映射判据；证明投射维数二类野性，并在任意域上得到该类的超可分解纯内射模。

Conclusion: 大有限维数猜想不成立：存在 $\operatorname{rad}^3=0$ 的十维代数使其大有限维数无限而小有限维数为二，且对偶代数大有限维数为零。该反例类结构高度受限但仍具有野性表示型，并蕴含超可分解纯内射模；同时本文提供了一系列关于 Artin 代数与初等 radical-cube-zero 代数投射维数界的判据与精细分类结果。

Abstract: We construct, over any field, a ten-dimensional algebra $A$ with $(\operatorname{rad}A)^3=0$ such that $\operatorname{findim}A=2$, $\operatorname{Findim}A=\infty$, and $\operatorname{Findim}(A^{\mathrm{op}})=0$. An explicit inverse-syzygy construction gives countably generated right modules of every positive finite projective dimension, disproving the big finitistic dimension conjecture. At each step we identify the full projective-cover kernel, and nonzero Ext detects the exact resolution length. For this algebra, we classify the modules of projective dimension at most one that embed in radicals of projective modules, with no generation restriction, in terms of invertible linear operators. We determine all dimension vectors of finite-dimensional modules of projective dimension two and classify those attaining the sharp dimension bounds, including all fourteen-dimensional modules of projective dimension two. For arbitrary Artin algebras, we give recursive sufficient criteria that control finite projective resolutions through radical-annihilated ideals and suitable corners. For finite-dimensional elementary radical-cube-zero algebras, we prove finiteness of the big finitistic dimension in the two-vertex case with one loop at each vertex and obtain multiplication-map criteria for bounds of one or two. Finally, an exact representation embedding yields wildness of the projective-dimension-two class and, over every field, a superdecomposable pure-injective module in that class.

</details>


### [243] [Endomorphism algebras of Gorenstein-projective modules over string algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00937)
*Yu-Zhe Liu,Zheng Xin*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文将弦代数的Gorenstein-投射支撑τ-倾斜对刻画为一类极大好集合，构造有限相容图；在G条件下给出End_A(T)的带标签瓦片曲面模型，并证明A表示有限当且仅当其所有基本Gorenstein-投射模T的自同态代数表示有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究弦代数的Gorenstein-投射τ-倾斜理论，寻找其组合与几何刻画，并建立原代数与其Gorenstein-投射模自同态代数的表示有限性之间的联系。

Method: 将Gorenstein-投射支撑τ-倾斜对等同于一 distinguished class of maximal good collections，给出有限相容图；在G条件下为End_A(T)构造带标签的瓦片曲面模型（labelled tiled-surface model）。

Result: 1）Gorenstein-投射支撑τ-倾斜对与极大好集合之间的对应及有限相容图；2）G条件下每个非零基本Gorenstein-投射模T的End_A(T)都有显式带标签瓦片曲面模型；3）G条件下A表示有限 ⟺ 对所有基本Gorenstein-投射模T，End_A(T)表示有限。

Conclusion: 该工作为弦代数的Gorenstein-投射τ-倾斜对提供了组合与曲面模型的统一描述，并将表示有限性从弦代数传递到其Gorenstein-投射模的自同态代数。

Abstract: Let $A$ be a string algebra. We identify its Gorenstein-projective support $τ$-tilting pairs with a distinguished class of maximal good collections and give a finite compatibility graph for them. Under the G-condition, we construct an explicit labelled tiled-surface model for $\mathrm{End}_A(T)$ for every nonzero basic Gorenstein-projective module $T$. Under the same condition, we show that $A$ is representation-finite if and only if $\End_A(T)$ is representation-finite for every basic Gorenstein-projective module $T$.

</details>


### [244] [Homology and higher-order intermediate categories for proper abelian subcategories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01155)
*Zheng-Gang Wu,Xianhui Fu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 在不依赖 t-结构的前提下，于具 ℓ+1 层扩展闭真阿贝尔子范畴的有限扩展窗口上建立自然长正合同调序列，并证明该界是最优的；在 Krull-Schmidt 情形下用相容序列分类稳定的中间子范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上长正合同调序列的构造往往依赖 t-结构等额外框架。本文试图去除这一依赖，在更弱条件下（仅需负自扩张在若干次数上消失）得到同调序列，并确定该条件的界是否可达最优。

Method: 在有限扩展窗口上，考虑 ℓ+1 层扩展闭真阿贝尔子范畴构成的序列；假设负自扩张在次数 1,…,ℓ+1 上消失，直接构造自然长正合同调序列，并给出该界的统一最优性证明；在 Krull-Schmidt 范畴框架内，借助相容序列对取纯同调层稳定的中间子范畴进行分类。

Result: 建立了不依赖 t-结构的自然长正合同调序列，并证明了所给界的统一最优性；在 Krull-Schmidt 情形下完成了对纯同调层稳定的中间子范畴的分类。

Conclusion: 长正合同调序列的构造可在弱于 t-结构的一般框架下实现，且所需消失条件在界上不可改进；纯同调层稳定性与相容序列提供了中间子范畴的完整分类工具。

Abstract: Without needing a $t$-structure, we establish natural long exact homology sequences on finite extension windows with $\ell+1$ layers of extension-closed proper abelian subcategories, assuming vanishing of negative self-extensions in degrees $1,\ldots,\ell+1$, and prove uniform sharpness of this bound. In the Krull-Schmidt setting, we classify intermediate subcategories stable under taking pure homology layers by compatible sequences.

</details>


### [245] [Central algebraic cohomology of Galois gerbes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01159)
*Taeyeoup Kang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 研究Galois gerbes的中心代数上同调，给出 Abel化上同调 的纤维积公式，并在数域上通过笛卡尔方块由 Abel化与实局部化还原完整上同调集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Galois gerbes 的中心代数上同调在局部域与数域上具有重要性，但一般系数（连通约化群与不一定有限或连通的代数中心子群）下的结构尚需刻画，特别是与 Kaletha 刚性 gerbe 的比较以及 Kottwitz gerbe 的特例。

Method: 对特征为零的局部域和数域上的 Galois gerbes，取连通约化群及代数中心子群为系数；对存在来自 Kaletha 刚性 gerbe 的比较态射的 gerbes，利用有限中心商簇的代数基本群与从 band 到中心子群的同态，推导 Abel 化上同调的纤维积公式；在数域情形，借助笛卡尔方块将完整上同调集与其 Abel 化及实局部化联系；并对 Kottwitz 局部与整体 gerbes 进行特化。

Result: 对允许比较态射的 gerbes 得到 Abel 化上同调的纤维积公式；当 band 为有限型时该比较存在。数域上无需比较假设，完整上同调集可由其 Abel 化与实局部化经笛卡尔方块还原。非阿基米德局部域与全虚数域上 Abel 化为双射。公式可特化到 Kottwitz 局部与整体 gerbes。

Conclusion: 建立了 Galois gerbes 中心上同调的纤维积与笛卡尔方块结构，刻画了 Abel 化的双射范围，并将结果统一应用于 Kottwitz gerbes，为相关上同调计算提供框架。

Abstract: We study central algebraic cohomology of Galois gerbes over local fields of characteristic zero and number fields, with coefficients in a connected reductive group and an algebraic central subgroup, not necessarily finite or connected. For gerbes admitting a comparison morphism from Kaletha's rigid gerbe, we obtain fiber product formulas for abelianized cohomology in terms of algebraic fundamental groups of finite central quotients and homomorphisms from the band to the central subgroup. Such comparisons exist whenever the band is of finite type. Over number fields, independently of the comparison hypothesis, the full cohomology set is recovered from its abelianization and its real localizations through a cartesian square. Over non-Archimedean local fields and totally imaginary number fields, abelianization is bijective. We also specialize the formulas to Kottwitz's local and global gerbes.

</details>


### [246] [Harmonic Norms on Quiver Representations over Non-Archimedean Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01694)
*Oren Ben-Bassat*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 证明了任意完备非阿基米德域上有限箭图的每一多重稳定表示都容许HKKP意义下的分裂调和范数，回答了HKKP的一个公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HKKP在其预印本中提出：多重稳定表示是否容许分裂调和范数。此前结果通常要求域球形完备或对值群、箭图施加限制，本文旨在去掉这些限制。

Method: 利用Moret-Bailly闭像定理获得与 destabilizing 子对象的统一分离；对范数权重作有限估计得到稳定表示的线性强制性界，从而在完备CAT(0)范数空间上得到扩展HKKP势的极小值；通过一阶变分计算将分裂极小元等同于HKKP调和范数；用严格支撑铰链将分裂达到性归约为带任意实半径标签的箭图半稳定归约；对提升分裂作小扰动使逆扩张非分裂，并用有限斜率-秩下降证明终止。

Result: 每个极小值都由具有相同箭头奇异剖面的分裂范数达到，且对值群或箭图无任何限制；非分裂极小元示例展示了在一、二半径修正下的分裂达到性；双参数Berkovich解析族分离了多重稳定性、半稳定性与所选分裂范数的调和性。

Conclusion: 在任意完备非阿基米德域上，多重稳定表示的分裂调和范数存在性得到完整证明，消除了球形完备性及值群、箭图限制，并给出了分裂达到性与非分裂极小元的刻画。

Abstract: Let $K$ be any complete non-Archimedean field, not necessarily spherically complete. We prove that every polystable representation of a finite quiver over $K$ admits a split harmonic norm in the sense of Haiden--Katzarkov--Kontsevich--Pandit (HKKP), answering a question from their preprint \emph{Towards Categorical Kähler Geometry}. Moret-Bailly's closed-image theorem gives a uniform separation from representations with destabilizing subobjects. A finite estimate on norm weights then yields a linear coercivity bound for stable representations and hence a minimum of the extended HKKP potential on the complete CAT(0) space of all norms. A direct first-variation computation identifies split minimizers with HKKP harmonic norms. Strict supporting hinges reduce split attainment to semistable reduction for quivers with arbitrary real radius labels. Small changes of the lifted splitting make each reversed extension nonsplit; a finite slope--rank descent proves termination. Consequently, every minimum in the completed norm space is attained by a split norm with the same arrow singular profiles, without restrictions on the value group or the quiver. Nonsplit minimizers illustrate split attainment in one and two radius modifications, and a two-parameter Berkovich analytic family separates polystability, semistability, and harmonicity of a chosen split norm.

</details>


### [247] [Rotation fixed points of undecorated braid varieties and fusion rings of affine Lie algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02034)
*Yuma Mizuno*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了与Coxeter元素幂次对应的辫子簇上，由字循环旋转诱导的Zamolodchikov变换（有限阶自等价）的不动点栈的粗模空间同构于相应仿射Lie代数的融合环的谱。该结果可推广到twisted Coxeter元素，从而以该方式获得所有仿射Lie代数的融合环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辫子簇与自等价的研究是代数群和表示论中的重要课题，尤其是与Coxeter元素相关的结构。Zamolodchikov变换作为字循环旋转诱导的自等价，其不动点性质可能揭示辫子簇与仿射Lie代数融合环之间的深层联系，因此作者希望建立这一同构关系。

Method: 设G为单连通半单代数群，考虑与Coxeter元素幂次关联的无装饰辫子簇。在其上定义由字循环旋转诱导的有限阶自等价——Zamolodchikov变换，然后研究该自等价的不动点栈的粗模空间，并证明它与相应仿射Lie代数的融合环的谱同构。方法进一步推广到twisted Coxeter元素的情形。

Result: 证明了Zamolodchikov变换的不动点栈的粗模空间同构于对应仿射Lie代数融合环的谱。该结果对twisted Coxeter元素同样成立，并且所有仿射Lie代数的融合环都可以通过这种方式得到。

Conclusion: 辫子簇上的Zamolodchikov变换与仿射Lie代数融合环之间存在深刻的同构关系，且这一构造具有普遍性，可覆盖所有仿射Lie代数的融合环。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a simply connected semisimple algebraic group. On the (undecorated) braid variety associated with a power of a Coxeter element, there is a finite-order autoequivalence, called the Zamolodchikov transformation, induced by cyclic rotation of words. In this paper, we show that the coarse moduli space of the fixed-point stack of this autoequivalence is isomorphic to the spectrum of the fusion ring of the corresponding affine Lie algebra. The above results also extend to twisted Coxeter elements, and the fusion rings of all affine Lie algebras are obtained in this way.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [248] [Sectional category versus module sectional category for retractive maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00089)
*Paul-Eugene Parent*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 构造了一个满足同伦收缩的单连通有理有限型空间映射，其msecat(p)=2小于secat(p)=3，从而推翻Carrasquel–Vera关于收缩映射的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Carrasquel–Vera曾提出关于收缩映射（retractive maps）的猜想，推测相关范畴不变量之间存在某种不等式关系。本文旨在检验并否定该猜想。

Method: 在单连通、有理有限型的有理空间之间构造一个映射p，使其满足同伦收缩条件，并计算出msecat(p)=2与secat(p)=3。目标空间取为一个含11个胞腔的单连通十二维CW复形的有理化，且为有理双曲型。

Result: 所构造的映射满足msecat(p)=2<secat(p)=3，与Carrasquel–Vera猜想所预言的不等式相矛盾。

Conclusion: 该反例表明Carrasquel–Vera关于收缩映射的猜想不成立，即对带同伦收缩的映射而言，msecat与secat之间未必存在猜想所述的关系。

Abstract: We construct a map between simply connected rational spaces of finite rational type which admits a homotopy retraction and satisfies $msecat(p)=2<secat(p)=3$. The target is the rationalization of a simply connected twelve-dimensional CW complex with eleven cells, and is rationally hyperbolic. This disproves Carrasquel--Vera's conjecture for retractive maps.

</details>


### [249] [A homotopical enhancement of Neisendorfer's algebraic models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01350)
*Bruno Stonek*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文对Neisendorfer关于有理单纯集、交换微分分次代数、余交换微分分次余代数与微分分次李代数之间同伦范畴等价的结论给出了$\infty$-范畴增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Neisendorfer的经典等价在相应连通性、幂零性与有限型假设下成立，但缺乏高阶/$\infty$-范畴层面的刻画；本文旨在将其提升到$\infty$-范畴框架。

Method: 证明线性对偶是弱有限型连通cdgcs与cdgas之间的部分Quillen等价；并证明Quillen的Chevalley–Eilenberg伴随在cdgcs与dgls之间是部分crossed derivable等价，其中crossed derivable等价为本文新引入的概念，用以公理化该伴随的行为。

Result: 建立了线性对偶的部分Quillen等价性与Chevalley–Eilenberg伴随的部分crossed derivable等价性，从而在$\infty$-范畴层面刻画了这些模型范畴之间的关系。

Conclusion: 给出Neisendorfer等价的一个$\infty$-范畴增强，并通过新概念crossed derivable等价精确描述Chevalley–Eilenberg伴随的性质。

Abstract: We give an $\infty$-categorical enhancement of Neisendorfer's equivalences between the homotopy categories of rational simplicial sets, commutative dg algebras (cdgas), cocommutative dg coalgebras (cdgcs) and dg Lie algebras (dgls), where these objects have respective hypotheses of connectedness, nilpotency and finite type. We do so by proving that linear duality is a partial Quillen equivalence between weak finite type connected cdgcs and cdgas, and that Quillen's Chevalley--Eilenberg adjunction is a partial crossed derivable equivalence between cdgcs and dgls, a concept we introduce in this paper to axiomatize precisely the behavior of this adjunction.

</details>


### [250] [Mumford--Morita--Miller classes in generalised cohomology theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01888)
*Oscar Randal-Williams*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文将 Mumford–Morita–Miller 类的定义从通常的普通上同调推广到任意复定向上同调理论中，并对其展开初步研究，重点关注由此产生的新现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mumford–Morita–Miller 类传统上定义在黎曼曲面模空间的普通上同调中。复定向上同调理论提供了更一般的框架，将这类特征类推广到此框架下有望揭示普通上同调中观察不到的新结构与现象，值得系统考察。

Method: 利用复定向上同调理论的 Thom 同构与形式群律等工具，将 MMM 类重新定义于任意复定向上同调理论中的黎曼曲面模空间上，并对其性质展开分析。

Result: 在任意复定向上同调理论下给出了 MMM 类的定义，并指出推广过程中出现的、区别于普通上同调情形的新现象。

Conclusion: MMM 类可在复定向上同调理论框架中自然推广，该推广带来值得进一步研究的新现象，为后续深入探索奠定基础。

Abstract: The Mumford--Morita--Miller classes on the moduli space of Riemann surfaces may be defined not only in ordinary cohomology but in any complex-oriented cohomology theory. We begin their investigation, emphasising the new phenomena that arise.

</details>


### [251] [Stable Complex Structures On Real Vector Bundles Over Connected Sums Of Quaternionic Projective Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02147)
*Souvik Mandal*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 通过分析复化映射的像，用Pontryagin类刻画了$M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$上具有稳定复结构的实向量丛，并证明其稳定几乎复结构存在当且仅当$n=2$且$\ell-m$为偶数，从而对一切$n\ge3$否定几乎复结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定四元数射影空间连通和$M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$上（稳定）几乎复结构的存在性，并推广Sato–Suzuki 1974年的部分结果至所有$n\ge3$。

Method: 分析复化映射$c:\widetilde{KO}(M^{4n}_{\ell,m})\to\widetilde{K}(M^{4n}_{\ell,m})$的像，以Pontryagin类为工具刻画容许稳定复结构的实向量丛。

Result: $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$稳定几乎复当且仅当$n=2$且$\ell-m$为偶数；对任意$n\ge3$，$M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$不具有几乎复结构。

Conclusion: 完整解决了$n\ge3$情形下几乎复结构的不存在性，并加强为稳定几乎复结构的不存在性。

Abstract: For integers $n\ge 2$ and integers $\ell,m\ge 0$, not both zero, let $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}=\ell\,\mathbb{HP}^{n}\,\#\,m\,\overline{\mathbb{HP}^{n}}$ denote the connected sum of $\ell$ copies of the quaternionic projective space $\mathbb{HP}^{n}$ and $m$ copies of $\mathbb{HP}^{n}$ endowed with the opposite orientation. By analysing the image of the complexification map $c\,\colon\,\widetilde{KO}(M^{4n}_{\ell,m})\to\widetilde{K}(M^{4n}_{\ell,m})$, we characterise, in terms of Pontryagin classes, the real vector bundles over $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$ admitting a stable complex structure, and deduce that $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$ is stably almost complex if and only if $n=2$ and $\ell-m$ is even. Consequently no $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$ with $n\ge 3$ admits an almost complex structure. Sato and Suzuki asserted the non-existence of almost complex structures on $M^{4n}_{\ell,m}$ in 1974 for $3\le n\le 10$, except possibly when $n=3$ and $\ell=3m+1$; we establish it for all $n\ge 3$, and in the stronger form of stable almost complex structures.

</details>
