Method: 从含外部电流的Proca理论出发进行量子化处理，明确总守恒电流为外部电流与Proca场内部电流之和；研究电荷产生过程导致的Proca场因果形成过程，构造并分析对应的非平衡量子态；将该非平衡态与相应静态Proca场态进行对比；重点分析外部电流瞬时开启情形，分别在点电荷产生与扩展电荷产生两种设定下求解量子动力学。

Result: 所得非平衡量子态表现为一个膨胀的Proca场并伴随一个移动前沿，与相应静态Proca场态存在根本性差异；在点电荷产生问题中发现了一种引人注目的冲击波结构；同时给出了扩展电荷产生问题的讨论结果；并系统阐述了含外部电流Proca理论的量子化及一般量子动力学结论。

Conclusion: 含外部电流的Proca理论可通过总电流守恒（外部电流＋内部电流）自洽地量子化，其电荷不守恒特性与纵向场的量子描述得以统一。电荷产生引发的Proca场因果形成产生具有移动前沿的膨胀非平衡态，与静态态本质不同；尤其在外电流瞬时开启情形下，点电荷产生呈现冲击波结构，扩展电荷产生亦有相应描述。

Abstract: We consider the Proca theory of a real massive vector field coupled to an external current. This theory allows for non-conservation of the charge associated with the external current. It is so because the total conserved current is given by the sum of the external current and the internal current associated with the Proca field, neither of which is required to be conserved separately. Moreover, this theory admits the quantum description of a longitudinal field generated by a charge distribution. We explore both features by studying the quantum dynamics underlying the causal formation of the Proca field following the charge-creation process. The resulting non-equilibrium quantum state exhibits an expanding Proca field accompanied by a moving front. We compare this state with the one representing the corresponding static Proca field and identify fundamental differences between these states. The quantization of the Proca theory in the presence of an external current is discussed in detail. General results concerning the quantum dynamics of this theory are presented. Particular emphasis is placed on the problem of the instantaneous switching-on of an otherwise static external current. An intriguing shockwave structure is found in the point-charge creation problem, the extended-charge creation problem is also discussed.

</details>


### [291] [M1 Radiative Transitions in the $B_c$ System after the $B_c^\ast(1S)$ Observation: Radial-Node Cancellation and Quark-Line Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00138)
*Bing-Dong Wan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 基于相对论Salpeter波函数研究$B_c$介子1S和2S态的M1辐射跃迁，给出$B_c^*(1S)\to B_c(1S)\gamma$宽度并揭示阻碍跃迁中的径向相消机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ATLAS对$B_c^*(1S)$介子的观测确定了基态超精细劈裂，从而确定了$B_c^*(1S)\to B_c(1S)\gamma$的相空间。借此契机，需系统研究$B_c$系统1S与2S态的M1辐射跃迁，理解允许与阻碍跃迁的振幅结构差异。

Method: 采用相对论Salpeter波函数（校准至测量的基态质量），计算四个1S和2S态的M1辐射跃迁；并进行夸克线分辨（quark-line-resolved）分析，分别考察$b$发射与$\bar c$贡献。

Result: 得到$Γ[B_c^*(1S)\to B_c(1S)γ]=5.24\times10^{-2}$ keV。允许跃迁无径向抵消；而阻碍的$2S\to1S$振幅是径向量子节点上约二十倍相消后的残差。夸克线分辨分析显示交叉阻碍道存在强烈不对称：$b$发射贡献经历$\mathcal O(10^2)$的内部相消，而$\bar c$贡献仍保持在$\mathcal O(20)$。非符号线强度表明较小的$b$流来源于增强的相消而非更小的非符号贡献。

Conclusion: 阻碍跃迁的微小振幅源于显著的径向相消而非本征的小贡献；未测量的$B_c^*(2S)$质量仍是限制$2S\to2S$辐射宽度计算的主要不确定性来源。

Abstract: The recent ATLAS observation of the $B_c^*(1S)$ meson fixes the ground-state hyperfine splitting and thereby determines the phase space for $B_c^*(1S)\to B_c(1S)γ$. We study four $1S$ and $2S$ M1 radiative transitions using relativistic Salpeter wave functions calibrated to the measured ground-state masses, obtaining $Γ[B_c^*(1S)\to B_c(1S)γ]=5.24\times10^{-2}$~keV. The allowed transitions show no radial cancellation, whereas the hindered $2S\to1S$ amplitudes are residuals of an approximately twenty-fold cancellation across the radial node. A quark-line-resolved analysis shows a strong asymmetry in the crossed hindered channel: the $b$-emission contribution undergoes an $\mathcal O(10^2)$ internal cancellation while the $\bar c$ contribution remains at $\mathcal O(20)$. The corresponding unsigned line strengths show that the small $b$-current is generated by enhanced cancellation rather than by a smaller unsigned contribution. The unmeasured $B_c^*(2S)$ mass remains the main limitation on the $2S\to2S$ radiative width.

</details>


### [292] [Dark-State Interference in an Effective Confined Three-Level Color System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00146)
*S. D. Campos*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文用QCD启发的有效模型，将量子光学中的暗态干涉（EIT）类比移植到三态禁闭色动力学体系，在马尔可夫GKSL主方程框架下证明两低能Q Qbar态与胶子激发杂化态间可形成相干叠加，导致稳态探针磁化率虚部被强烈压制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子光学中的暗态干涉与相干布居囚禁机制能否在禁闭色动力学（QCD）体系中找到对应的有效描述，从而在量子光学与非微扰QCD之间建立可控的理论联系，并解释受限色动力学中的"透明"现象。

Method: 构建QCD启发的三态有效模型：两个低能Q Qbar态|1>、|2>与一个胶子激发杂化态|H>；在未微扰哈密顿量中引入静态色电场通量管背景，用含时色电扰动以Λ型构型耦合|1>、|2>与|H>；采用马尔可夫Gorini-Kossakowski-Sudarshan-Lindblad（GKSL）主方程处理退相干；在双"光子"共振条件下求解稳态探针磁化率Im χ_CE，并加入唯象非阿贝尔修正到杂化态能量。

Result: 在两"光子"共振下，系统形成与|H>解耦的相干叠加（暗态），当低态退相干趋于零时，稳态探针磁化率Im χ_CE被强烈压制，出现透明窗口；唯象非阿贝尔修正主要使透明窗口发生移动和重塑，而非产生干涉本身。

Conclusion: 该模型并非介质不透明度的第一性原理QCD计算，而是量子光学暗态干涉与受限色动力学之间的可控有效桥梁，说明EIT类相干效应可在禁闭QCD有效描述中类比实现。

Abstract: In this work, a QCD-inspired effective model describes dark-state interference in a confined three-state system with two low-energy $Q\overline{Q}$ states, $|1\rangle$ and $|2\rangle$, and a gluon-excited hybrid state, $|H\rangle$. A stationary chromoelectric flux-tube background enters the unperturbed Hamiltonian, while a time-dependent chromoelectric perturbation couples $|1\rangle$ and $|2\rangle$ to $|H\rangle$ in a $Λ$-type configuration. Under two-photon\footnote{We retain standard terms like ``two-photon'' from the EIT literature only by mathematical analogy, but they do not refer to electromagnetic photons but to the effective frequencies and energy mismatches of chromoelectric perturbations and gluonic excitations that connect confined states.} resonance and within a Markovian Gorini-Kossakowski-Sudarshan-Lindblad master equation, the system forms a coherent superposition that decouples from $|H\rangle$, strongly suppressing the steady-state probe susceptibility $\operatorname{Im}χ_{\text{CE}}$ as lower-state decoherence vanishes. A phenomenological non-Abelian correction to the hybrid energy mainly shifts and reshapes the transparency window rather than creating the interference itself. The model is not a first-principles QCD calculation of medium opacity but a controlled effective link between dark-state interference in quantum optics and reduced confined color dynamics.

</details>


### [293] [Thermal Higgsino Dark Matter in GmSUGRA for the LZ Event and Solar-Neutrino Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00226)
*Imtiaz Khan,Waqas Ahmed,Tianjun Li,Shabbar Raza,Ali Muhammad*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在GmSUGRA边界条件下的MSSM中研究了热Higgsino暗物质，数值扫描给出与Planck兼容的Higgsino分支，其中子态劈裂从数百keV连续延伸到MeV量级；LZ高反冲事件偏好的0.3–0.4 MeV区间被太阳俘获限制压低，但更大劈裂（约0.85、1.10、1.34 MeV）的分支仍然可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LUX–ZEPLIN实验报告了高反冲事例，随后IceCube数据给出了太阳中微子限制，需要对GUT-motivated超对称构造中的热Higgsino暗物质进行重新检验，并澄清这些数据究竟是排除还是仅选择Higgsino参数空间的特定部分。

Method: 在MSSM框架下采用GmSUGRA非普适软破缺关系；Higgs部门使用NUHM2型参数化，以μ和m_A为输入；进行数值扫描，考察热Higgsino质量和中性态劈裂（数百keV至MeV），并与Planck、LZ高反冲解释及太阳俘获重新解释的限制进行对比。

Result: 存在一个与Planck兼容的热Higgsino分支，其中性态劈裂连续分布；劈裂约为0.3–0.4 MeV的部分与LZ事例的运动学区间重叠，但对满密度热Higgsino而言被当前太阳俘获重新解释所压低；同一高能标分支在更大劈裂处依然存在，代表性谱包括0.85、1.10和1.34 MeV，均超出所引用的太阳俘获限制。

Conclusion: LZ高反冲解释偏好的质量和亚MeV劈裂可以在GUT-motivated超对称构造中动力学地实现，无需将neutralino压缩作为弱电标度输入；综合数据选择的是同一GmSUGRA Higgsino分支的不同部分，而非排除热Higgsino构造本身。

Abstract: We study the thermal Higgsino dark matter in the Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model (MSSM) with Generalized Minimal Supergravity (GmSUGRA) boundary conditions after the high-recoil event reported by the LUX--ZEPLIN experiment and the subsequent solar-neutrino limits inferred from IceCube data. The Higgs sector is implemented in a NUHM2-type parametrization with $μ$ and $m_A$ as inputs, while the supersymmetry breaking soft terms obey the non-universal GmSUGRA relations. The numerical scan contains a Planck-compatible Higgsino branch near the thermal mass, with the neutral-state splitting extending continuously from the few-hundred-keV region to the MeV scale. The part of this branch with $\dmchi\simeq0.3$--$0.4$ MeV overlaps the kinematic interval relevant to the LZ event, but is disfavored by the current solar-capture reinterpretations for a full-density thermal Higgsino. The same high-scale branch persists at larger splittings, including representative spectra with $\dmchi\simeq0.85$, $1.10$, and $1.34$ MeV, beyond the quoted solar-capture limits. Our results demonstrate that the mass and sub-MeV splitting favored by the LZ high-recoil interpretation can be realized dynamically within a GUT-motivated supersymmetric construction, without imposing the neutralino compression as a weak-scale input. The combined data therefore select different parts of a common GmSUGRA Higgsino branch rather than excluding the thermal Higgsino construction itself.

</details>


### [294] [Three-Body Holonomy and $SU(3)\to SU(2)\times U(1)$ Symmetry Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00235)
*Tadashi Yoshikawa*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用三体完整群（holonomy）选择常规 $SU(3)\to SU(2)\times U(1)$ 破缺方向的可玩具机制：通过三粒子一维少体系统在相对空间中的闭合循环，构造循环三态质量算符，在 $\theta_3=0$ 时谱分裂为 $3\to2+1$，诱导质量算符正比于 Cartan 生成元 $T_8$，其交换子为 $SU(2)\times U(1)$，并在类 trinification 嵌入下复现标准模型超荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文动机在于解释规范对称性破缺方向的选择问题：常规的 $SU(3)\to SU(2)\times U(1)$ 破缺方向通常是被假设的，而作者希望展示三体完整群可以自然地选出这一方向，从而为破缺方向的起源提供一种几何/拓扑机制。

Method: 方法上，作者利用三粒子系统：在移除质心坐标后，这是具有二维相对空间的最小一维少体系统，允许在成对接触由匹配条件编码时绕三重合点形成闭合循环。由此构造一个循环三态质量算符 $M^{[k]}=M_0+2\Delta\cos((\theta_3+2\pi k)/3)$，$k=0,1,2$。在 $\theta_3=0$ 时分析其谱分裂及诱导质量算符与 Cartan 生成元 $T_8$ 的关系，并在类 trinification 嵌入中考察左右扇区完整群对超荷组合的影响。

Result: 结果表明：在 $\theta_3=0$ 时质量谱按 $3\to2+1$ 分裂，诱导质量算符正比于 Cartan 生成元 $T_8$，因此其交换子为 $SU(2)\times U(1)$。在类 trinification 嵌入中，左右扇区不同的完整群所保留的超荷组合能够复现通常 trinification 物质分配下的标准模型超荷。

Conclusion: 结论是：该构造提供了一个选择破缺方向的可玩具机制，说明三体完整群可选出常规 $SU(3)\to SU(2)\times U(1)$ 方向；但实现秩降低和真空选择仍需要额外的动力学，因此该机制并非完整的对称性破缺理论。

Abstract: We show that a three-body holonomy can select the conventional $SU(3)\to SU(2)\times U(1)$ breaking direction. Three particles are special in the present construction: after removal of the center-of-mass coordinate they are the minimal one-dimensional few-body system with a two-dimensional relative space, allowing a closed cycle around the triple-coincidence point once pairwise contacts are encoded by matching conditions. A cyclic three-state mass operator gives \[ M^{[k]}=M_0+2Δ\cos\!\left(\frac{θ_3+2πk}{3}\right),\qquad k=0,1,2. \] At $θ_3=0$ the spectrum splits as $3\to2+1$, and the induced mass operator is proportional to the Cartan generator $T_8$. Its commutant is therefore $SU(2)\times U(1)$. In a trinification-like embedding, distinct left- and right-sector holonomies leave the hypercharge combination which reproduces the Standard-Model hypercharges for the usual trinification matter assignment. The construction is a toy mechanism for selecting the breaking direction; additional dynamics are required for rank reduction and vacuum selection.

</details>


### [295] [Fractional anomalous determinants and the chiral phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00472)
*Robert D. Pisarski*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 构建了一个"超越朗道"的综合模型：高温下瞬子气体给出整数幂反常行列式；低于手征相变温度时，假定拓扑荷Q取分数值（整数乘以1/N_c），导致手征有效拉氏量中出现分数幂反常行列式，从而将Witten-Veneziano项推广至任意N_f/N_c，并给出对相变阶数与格点QCD检验的预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高温下瞬子呈稀薄气体，QCD类理论中反常U(1)_A对称性破缺由反常行列式的整数幂给出，拓扑荷Q为整数。作者希望突破朗道相变理论的框架（"beyond Landau"），将Witten-Veneziano项从仅适用于小N_f/N_c的情形推广到任意N_f/N_c，并探讨手征相变的阶数及其可检验性。

Method: 作者构造一个显式"超越朗道"的综合模型（syncretic model）。手征极限下：温度高于手征相变温度T_χ时，仅整数幂的反常行列式出现；低于T_χ时，参照't Hooft等人的思路，假定拓扑荷Q为分数值，即整数乘以1/N_c（N_c为颜色数），由此在手征有效拉氏量中引入分数幂的反常行列式。该推广对N_f个简并味的情形给出任意N_f/N_c下的有效描述。

Result: 该模型下手征相变一般为二级相变；例外情形有两种：单味时可能为交叉（crossover），三味时可能为弱一级相变。这些预言可通过格点QCD用2+1味进行检验：在T_χ到约2T_χ之间，比较π^a与a_0^a传播子之差的（已知）温度依赖性与奇异夸克手征凝聚。对一到四味简并味，也可在T_χ附近做类似测量。

Conclusion: 分数拓扑荷的假设使得反常行列式的分数幂进入手征有效拉氏量，将Witten-Veneziano项推广至任意N_f/N_c，并给出相变阶数的具体预言（一般二级，单味可能交叉，三味可能弱一级）。作者还提出了一个对称相中重子数的算符，为格点QCD检验提供了可行方向。

Abstract: At high temperature instantons form a dilute gas, so in QCD-like theories the breaking of the anomalous $U(1)_A$ symmetry is given by integral powers of the anomalous determinant, $\sim (\det Φ)^{Q}$, where $Φ\sim \overline{q}_L q_R$ is bilinear in the quark fields, and with untwisted boundary conditions, the topological charge, $Q$, is an integer. A syncretic model is constructed, which is manifestly "beyond Landau". In the chiral limit, at temperatures above the chiral phase transition, $T > T_χ$, only integral powers of the anomalous determinant appear. Below $T_χ$, following 't Hooft et al. I assume that the topological charge $Q$ is fractional, as an integer times $1/N_c$, where $N_c$ is the number of colors. I suggest that consequently, fractional powers of the anomalous determinant appear in the chiral effective Lagrangian. For $N_f$ degenerate flavors, this generalizes the Witten-Veneziano term, valid for small $N_f/N_c$, to arbitrary $N_f/N_c$. In this model the chiral phase transition is generically of second order. The two exceptions are for one flavor, where it is probably crossover, and three flavors, where it could well be weakly first order. This can be tested in lattice QCD with $2+1$ flavors by comparing the (known) temperature dependence of the difference of the $π^a$ and $a_0^a$ propagators, to the chiral condensate of the strange quark, between $T_χ$ and $\sim 2 \, T_χ$. Analogous measurements are possible for one to four degenerate flavors about $T_χ$. Lastly, I propose an operator for baryon number in the symmetric phase.

</details>


### [296] [Full-event anomaly detection for new physics searches at the Electron-Ion Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00308)
*Dimitrios Athanasakos,Sebastian Grieninger,Hongkai Liu,Tymothy Mangan,Felix Ringer,Robert Szafron*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究利用机器学习与事件全粒子信息，在未来电子-离子对撞机(EIC)上开展共振异常检测。采用弱监督学习增强不变质量搜寻灵敏度，并构建条件全事件生成器提供背景参考，同时评估无监督图自编码器在极低信号占比下的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来EIC为寻找超出标准模型的新物理提供新机遇。传统搜寻依赖高层面观测量和特定信号假设，而共振异常检测可利用事件完整粒子信息，在无需训练时识别单个信号事件的前提下提升灵敏度，因此有必要系统评估该类方法在EIC独特运动学条件下的可行性与增益。

Method: 以新中性规范玻色子和重中性轻子作为不同电子-喷注拓扑的示例信号；利用事件全粒子内容开展弱监督学习，仅依赖不变质量信息增强搜寻灵敏度，并避免训练中标记单个信号事件。将结果与高层面观测量对比。进一步构建条件全事件生成器，通过在LHC喷注上预训练的模型迁移适配生成EIC背景事件，作为学习到的背景参考。另考虑完全无监督的图自编码器方法。

Result: 与高层面观测量对比显示，电子运动学对信号-背景分离贡献显著，而粒子间关联提供额外灵敏度。在信号占比扫描中，使用学习到的背景参考实现明显信号增强，验证了该方法在EIC的概念可行性。完全无监督图自编码器带来适度信号增强，并在信号占比过小、弱监督失效时仍保持灵敏度。

Conclusion: 结果支持将全事件异常检测纳入EIC物理项目，并为发展适配EIC独特运动学的方法指明了方向。

Abstract: The future Electron-Ion Collider (EIC) will offer new opportunities to search for physics beyond the Standard Model. We study resonant anomaly detection using machine learning and the full particle content of an event. We consider a new neutral gauge boson and a heavy neutral lepton as illustrative signals with different electron-jet topologies. Weakly supervised learning uses this event information to enhance the sensitivity of an invariant-mass search without identifying individual signal events during training. Comparisons with high-level observables show that the electron kinematics account for an important part of the separation, while correlations among the particles provide further sensitivity. We also construct a conditional full-event generator by adapting a model pretrained on LHC jets to produce EIC background events. A scan over signal fractions demonstrates substantial signal enhancement using the learned background reference, providing a proof of concept for this approach at the EIC. Finally, we consider a fully unsupervised graph autoencoder that provides modest signal enhancement but retains sensitivity at signal fractions too small for effective weak supervision. Our results motivate full-event anomaly detection as part of the EIC physics program and identify directions for developing methods suited to its distinct kinematics.

</details>


### [297] [Primordial Black Hole and Gravitational Wave by Peaked Cosmic Perturbations from Axion Curvaton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00457)
*Keisuke Harigaya,Lian-Tao Wang,Jiangyi Yi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 轴子场的暴胀动力学可产生峰值位于中等波数的扰动，经curvaton机制生成具有三角形谱的引力波与PBH；PBH可构成全部暗物质并伴随小尺度蓝倾等曲率扰动，引力波红外斜率与暗物质等曲率扰动幅度反相关，提供多信使检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究轴子暴胀动力学能否产生峰值处于中等波数的扰动，并以此为种子通过curvaton机制同时生成引力波与PBH，从而寻找可观测的多信使信号。

Method: 计算轴子暴胀演化产生的扰动谱，并通过curvaton机制推导引力波与PBH谱；在计算PBH丰度时，正确处理曲率扰动与密度扰动之间的非线性关系以及curvaton产生的原初非高斯性。

Result: 引力波谱呈三角形，其紫外与红外斜率由轴子暴胀演化决定且可作为自由参数；PBH可解释全部暗物质，同时在小尺度上伴随蓝倾等曲率扰动；引力波谱红外斜率与暗物质等曲率扰动幅度呈反相关。

Conclusion: 该模型可通过引力波谱红外斜率与暗物质等曲率扰动的反相关关系进行多信使检验，为轴子-curvaton场景提供可观测判别依据。

Abstract: We demonstrate that an axion field can develop perturbations peaked at an intermediate wavenumber through its inflationary dynamics. We compute the resultant spectrum of gravitational waves (GWs) and primordial black holes (PBHs) generated through the curvaton mechanism. The GW spectrum exhibits a triangular shape, with UV and IR slopes determined by the axion's inflationary evolution and treatable as free parameters. In computing the PBH abundance, we properly account for the nonlinear relation between curvature and density perturbations and the primordial non-Gaussianity generated by the curvaton. The PBHs can account for all of dark matter, accompanied by blue-tilted isocurvature perturbations at small scales. The IR slope of the GW spectrum is anti-correlated with the magnitude of the dark matter isocurvature perturbations, offering a multi-messenger test of the model.

</details>


### [298] [QCD axion misalignment during reheating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00458)
*Disha Bandyopadhyay,Debasish Borah,Nayan Das*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在慢滚暴胀结束后延长再加热时期，通过真空错位与动能错位机制产生QCD轴子暗物质，并在三种微扰再加热情形下给出与观测暗物质丰度一致的参数空间及探测前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QCD轴子作为暗物质候选者，其丰度产生通常依赖错位机制，而再加热阶段的宇宙演化历史（尤其是暴胀子衰变方式）会显著改变轴子丰度，因此需要系统研究再加热情形对轴子暗物质产生的影响。

Method: 假设暴胀前Peccei-Quinn对称性已破缺，并采用再加热期间的单项式暴胀子势，将研究分为三种微扰再加热情形：暴胀子衰变为(i)一对类SM玻色子，(ii)一对类SM费米子，或(iii)仅衰变为一对重右手马约拉纳中微子（其主导能量密度后衰变至SM末态）。分别对每种情形研究真空错位与动能错位机制下的QCD轴子遗存。

Result: 针对再加热、单项式暴胀子势与错位机制的不同组合，识别出与观测暗物质遗存密度一致并满足其他宇宙学与实验室限制的参数空间，并评估了轴子探测实验与引力波实验对当前允许参数空间的探测能力。

Conclusion: 再加热历史与暴胀子衰变模式对QCD轴子暗物质的产生有重要影响，综合真空与动能错位机制可给出一致参数空间，且未来轴子探测与引力波实验有望对该参数空间进行检验。

Abstract: We study the production of QCD axion dark matter (DM) via vacuum and kinetic misalignment mechanisms during an extended reheating period after the end of slow-roll inflation. With pre-inflationary Peccei-Quinn breaking and considering a monomial inflaton potential during reheating, we classify our study into three different perturbative reheating scenarios in which the inflaton decays into (i) a pair of Standard Model (SM)-like bosons, (ii) a pair of SM-like fermions, or (iii) exclusively into a pair of heavy right-handed neutrinos, which eventually decays into the SM final states after dominating the energy density of the Universe for an intermediate epoch. For each of these reheating scenarios, we study the details of QCD axion relic separately via vacuum and kinetic misalignment mechanisms. For different combinations of reheating, monomial inflaton potential and misalignment mechanisms, we identify the parameter space consistent with the observed DM relic density while satisfying other cosmological and laboratory constraints. We also check the scope of probing the currently allowed parameter space at axion-detection and gravitational wave (GW) experiments.

</details>


### [299] [ALP-Mediated Dark Matter at Low Reheating Temperature and the Belle II Excess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00460)
*Joel Jones-Pérez,Luca Merlo,Javier Silva-Malpartida*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 重新评估Belle II的B⁺→K⁺νν̄异常在类轴子粒子介导热暗物质框架下的解释，引入新的质量微分测量与两个可见衰变搜索，发现标准宇宙学仅边缘兼容，而低温柔 reheating 场景可显著改善兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Belle II报告B⁺→K⁺νν̄存在2.7σ超出标准模型的过量，此前被解释为类轴子粒子介导的热暗物质候选者所致，需用更完整的实验数据重新检验该解释的可行性。

Method: 采用同一数据集的新质量微分测量，并纳入此前分析遗漏的两个可见衰变搜索来重新评估；进而考虑暗物质在低温重加热时期冻结退出、伴随熵注入稀释遗迹丰度的宇宙学场景。

Result: 标准宇宙学历史下的预测与过量仅边缘兼容，仅存于狭窄参数窗口；低温柔reheating场景因需更小耦合以匹配观测暗物质密度，开辟出显著改善模型与Belle II过量兼容性的参数区域。

Conclusion: 通过低重加热时代的熵注入稀释机制，类轴子暗物质模型与Belle II过量之间的兼容性得到显著提升，为解释该异常提供了更可行的宇宙学框架。

Abstract: Belle II has reported a $2.7σ$ excess in $B^+\to K^+ν\barν$ over its Standard Model prediction, recently interpreted as arising from an axion-like particle mediating a thermal dark matter candidate. We reassess this interpretation using a new mass-differential measurement of the same data set and adding two visible-decay searches that earlier analyses omitted. The resulting bounds leave the predictions from a standard cosmological history only marginally compatible with the excess, viable in a narrow window of parameter space. Thus, we consider a situation where dark matter freezes out during a low-temperature reheating era, featuring entropy injection diluting the relic abundance. This requires smaller couplings compared to those in standard cosmology in order to match the observed dark matter density, opening a region of parameter space where the compatibility of the model with the Belle II excess is significantly improved.

</details>


### [300] [The Great Wall of the Standard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00461)
*Yang Bai,Yiming Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了标准模型规范群整体结构 $[SU(3)_c\times SU(2)_L\times U(1)_Y]/\Gamma$ 所对应的残余一形式对称性在热圆紧致化下产生的离散零形式对称性自发破缺所形成的畴壁的张力与宽度，并建立热速度相关单尺度模型追踪其网络演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型规范群的整体形式（即 $\Gamma\subseteq\mathbb{Z}_6$ 的选取）尚未被实验确定。对于 $\Gamma=\mathbb{Z}_p$，理论具有残余整体电一形式对称性 $\mathbb{Z}_{6/p}$；热圆紧致化后其自发破缺可产生宇宙学畴壁。作者希望探究这些畴壁是否可作为探测标准模型规范群整体结构的宇宙学探针。

Method: 作者计算了电弱标度以下 $\mathbb{Z}_{6/p}$ 畴壁的温度相关张力与宽度；建立了依赖温度的热速度相关单尺度模型（thermal velocity-dependent one-scale model），其中纳入温度相关的表面张力、惯性与等离子体拖曳，用于追踪畴壁网络的演化。

Result: 除 $\Gamma=\mathbb{Z}_6$ 外，所有商群均存在此类畴壁；在作者基准参数下，其厚度在再电离时期可达千米量级。对于足够早的形成时间，基准给出的壁能量分数在大爆炸核合成开始时约为 $10^{-14}$ 量级，并在更晚时期进一步压低。不确定性来自表面自由能与物理壁能量的关系，以及晚期等离子体演化和结构形成。

Conclusion: 尽管能量分数很小，这些宏观热界面（"标准模型长城"）仍可能探测标准模型规范群的整体结构，因而值得针对其宇宙学信号开展专门搜寻。

Abstract: The global form of the Standard Model (SM) gauge group, $[SU(3)_c\times SU(2)_L\times U(1)_Y]/Γ$, with $Γ\subseteq\mathbb{Z}_6$, remains undetermined. For $Γ=\mathbb{Z}_p$, the SM possesses a residual global electric one-form symmetry $\mathbb{Z}_{6/p}$. Compactification on the thermal circle yields an ordinary discrete zero-form symmetry whose spontaneous breaking can produce cosmological domain walls. We calculate the temperature-dependent tension and width of the $\mathbb{Z}_{6/p}$ domain walls below the electroweak scale, which exist for every quotient except $Γ=\mathbb{Z}_6$, finding that their thickness can reach the kilometer scale around reionization in our benchmarks. These macroscopic thermal interfaces, if produced, would constitute the "Great Walls" of the Standard Model. We develop a phenomenological thermal velocity-dependent one-scale model to follow their network evolution, incorporating temperature-dependent surface tension, inertia, and plasma drag. For sufficiently early formation, our benchmarks give an estimated wall energy fraction of order $10^{-14}$ near the onset of Big Bang nucleosynthesis, with further suppression at later times. Uncertainties arise from relating surface free energy to physical wall energy and from late-time plasma evolution and structure formation. Despite their small energy fraction, these walls could probe the global structure of the SM gauge group, motivating dedicated searches for their cosmological signatures.

</details>


### [301] [Quantum Information in High-Energy Physics: a top subject](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00467)
*Yoav Afik,Juan Ramón Muñoz de Nova*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述量子信息技术在高能物理中的应用，以顶夸克为相对论量子比特范例，研究顶夸克对产生中的纠缠，并介绍量子层析实验方案及ATLAS与CMS的观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息与高能物理的交叉研究日益兴起，需要系统梳理该领域的技术实现路径与代表性成果，并为后续研究提供新的视角。

Method: 以顶夸克作为相对论量子比特的范例，分析高能对撞机（如LHC）中顶-反顶夸克产生过程中的纠缠现象，并提出针对顶夸克对的量子层析实验方案。

Result: 所提出的量子层析方案最终促成ATLAS与CMS合作组的纠缠观测，这是迄今最高能量下的纠缠观测；相关概念与技术已发展成一个丰富的研究领域。

Conclusion: 量子信息方法可有效应用于高能物理，顶夸克纠缠研究开创了QI与HEP交叉的新领域，并取得实验验证。

Abstract: As a work selected for the Frontiers of Science Award, we present in the Proceedings of the ICBS an overview of how the techniques from the field of Quantum Information (QI) can be implemented in High-Energy Physics (HEP), including also some novel insights. We focus on the paradigmatic case of the top quark, a drosophila of a relativistic qubit. We study the presence of entanglement, perhaps the most genuine feature of Quantum Mechanics, in top-antitop quark production in high-energy colliders such as the LHC. We also analyze our experimental proposal of a quantum tomography protocol for the top-antitop quark pair, eventually leading to the entanglement observations by the ATLAS and CMS collaborations, which constituted the highest-energy observations of entanglement ever. We discuss how the concepts and techniques developed in our work have effectively spawned an already vast and rich field of QI in HEP.

</details>


### [302] [Glueballs and fractional anomalous determinants at nonzero $θ$, and the decays of the X(2370)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00484)
*Francesco Giacosa,Shahriyar Jafarzade,Győző Kovács,Péter Kovács,Robert D. Pisarski,Fabian Rennecke*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 利用分数反常行列式，通过与标量和赝标量胶球的耦合，构建了非零 θ 角下 SU(3) 规范理论的迹反常与轴反常模型，并应用于纯规范理论及 QCD 中 X(2370) 衰变的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非零 θ 角下 SU(3) 规范理论的迹反常与轴反常，并解释 BESIII 观测到的 0^{-+} 胶球候选态 X(2370) 的多重衰变道，检验相关模型。

Method: 采用分数反常行列式（fractional anomalous determinants），引入其与标量、赝标量胶球的耦合来构造反常模型；在纯规范理论中计算真空能的 θ 依赖与标量胶球质量，并估算 θ=π 一阶相变时拓扑荷密度的跃变；在 QCD 中将赝标量胶球与分数反常行列式耦合以描述衰变道。

Result: 纯规范理论中，重现了格点结果给出的真空能 θ 依赖与标量胶球质量，并估算出 θ=π 一阶相变的拓扑荷密度跃变；在 QCD 中，该模型描述了 X(2370) 全部四个三赝标量衰变道（测得的 0^{-+} 胶球候选态）。

Conclusion: 分数反常行列式耦合模型能统一描述非零 θ 角反常效应及 X(2370) 的衰变，其中 ηηη 与 ηηη' 衰变率的测量可为该模型提供严格检验。

Abstract: Using fractional anomalous determinants, we construct a model of the trace and axial anomalies of a $SU(3)$ gauge theory at nonzero $θ$ angle through the couplings to scalar and pseudoscalar glueballs. In the pure gauge theory, we reproduce the $θ$ dependence of the vacuum energy and the scalar-glueball mass from the lattice, and estimate the jump in the topological charge density for the first-order transition at $θ= π$. In QCD, the coupling of a pseudoscalar glueball to fractional anomalous determinants describes all of the four three-pseudoscalar decay channels of the $0^{-+}$ glueball candidate $X(2370)$ measured by the BESIII collaboration. Measuring the decay rates for $ηηη$ and $ηηη'$ can provide a stringent test of the model.

</details>


### [303] [A massive right-handed sterile neutrino in three-body ${\bar B}\to D^*\ell^-{\bar N}_R$ decays - a Monte Carlo simulation study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00488)
*Bhubanjyoti Bhattacharya,Thomas E. Browder,Lucien M. Cremaldi,Horacio Crotte Ledesma,Nilakshi Das,Alakabha Datta,Tejhas Kapoor,Kumar Pandey,Alexei Sibidanov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文开发了基于EvtGen的蒙特卡洛事件生成器，用于模拟$\bar{B}\to D^*\ell^-\bar{N}_R$过程，并验证其在$q^2$、缺失质量及角分布等可观测量上与解析结果一致，进而讨论Belle II对右手中微子的搜寻灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 右手中微子（RHN）是超出标准模型物理的重要候选粒子，而$\bar{B}\to D^*\ell^-\bar{N}_R$衰变为其提供了一种有前景的实验探寻通道；然而缺乏能够模拟该过程的蒙特卡洛工具，限制了实验搜索的开展。

Method: 基于EvtGen框架开发了可模拟超出标准模型过程的新型蒙特卡洛事件生成器，用于模拟$\bar{B}\to D^*\ell^-\bar{N}_R$（$\ell=e,\mu$）的微分衰变分布，并将蒙特卡洛结果与解析结果在$q^2$、缺失质量及$A_{FB}$、$S_3$、$S_5$等角分布特征上进行对比验证。

Result: 蒙特卡洛模拟结果在多个运动学变量（$q^2$、缺失质量）和角分布特征（$A_{FB}$、$S_3$、$S_5$等）上与解析结果一致，验证了模型中模拟实现的正确性。

Conclusion: 该蒙特卡洛生成器将助力对右手中微子的新实验搜索，并支撑在Belle II上开展相关实验分析的可行性研究。

Abstract: We investigate the differential decay distributions of $\bar{B}\to D^*\ell^-{\bar N}_R $, where $N_R$ is a heavy right-handed neutrino (RHN) and $\ell = e, μ$. We employ a newly developed Monte Carlo event generator based on the EvtGen framework that can simulate beyond-the-Standard-Model processes. We validate the implementation of the model by comparing the Monte-Carlo results with analytical results for several kinematic variables such as $q^2$ and missing mass, as well as signatures in the angular distributions such as $A_{FB}$, $S_{3}$, $S_{5}$, etc. This MC Generator will enable new experimental searches for RHNs. We discuss the sensitivity of various observables to the presence of an RHN and the feasibility of an experimental analysis at Belle II.

</details>


### [304] [Unified description of longitudinal and transverse parton dynamics in cold nuclear matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00514)
*Weiyao Ke*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在SCET_G框架下统一描述了冷核物质中的纵向动量演化与横动量展宽，将介质诱导能量损失与BFKL型快度演化结合，用同一组冷核参数解释了SIDIS和Drell-Yan的主要核修正，并给出EIC下TMD强子多重数预测及单点能量关联子的讨论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷核物质中纵向动量演化（能量损失）与横动量展宽长期被分开处理，缺乏统一描述；LPM干涉使两者在有限核内耦合，需要一个能同时刻画多尺度演化的理论框架，并为EIC等未来实验提供可检验预言。

Method: 采用含Glauber胶子相互作用的软共线有效理论（SCET_G）。将依赖碰撞参数的能损的RG演化与碰撞核的BFKL型快度演化结合，并引入运动学约束和跑动耦合改进。用统一的冷核物质参数拟合/描述SIDIS与Drell-Yan的核修正，并外推至EIC的TMD强子多重数与DIS单点能量关联子。

Result: 同一组冷核参数可同时描述半单举DIS和Drell-Yan的主要核修正模式，并给出EIC下TMD强子多重数预言。对DIS中单点能量关联子，能量加权压制了末态领头能损贡献；在所研究的EIC运动学区间，残余动力学修正很小，核修正主要由初态核部分子分布主导。

Conclusion: SCET_G可统一处理冷核物质中的纵向能损与横动量展宽，LPM干涉导致两者通过被观测尺度耦合；该框架具有良好的普适性与预言能力。对能量关联子而言，能量加权使其对末态能损不敏感，EIC相关运动学下的核效应主要来自初态nPDF。

Abstract: We present a unified description of longitudinal momentum evolution and transverse-momentum broadening in cold nuclear matter using soft collinear effective theory with Glauber gluon interactions (SCET$_{\rm G}$). In a finite nucleus, Landau--Pomeranchuk--Migdal interference couples medium-induced energy loss to the transverse scale resolved by the observable. Our framework combines RG evolution of impact-parameter-dependent energy loss with BFKL-type rapidity evolution of the collision kernel, improved by kinematical constraints and running coupling. With a common set of cold-nuclear-matter parameters, it accounts for the main nuclear modification patterns in semi-inclusive DIS and Drell--Yan production and provides predictions for TMD hadron multiplicities at the EIC. We also discuss the one-point energy correlator in DIS, whose energy weighting suppresses the leading final-state energy-loss contribution. In the EIC kinematics studied, the residual dynamical corrections are small, leaving the nuclear modification dominated by the initial-state nuclear parton distributions.

</details>


### [305] [Charged scalar portal dark matter with $U(1)_Y$ non-restoration and gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00522)
*Debasish Borah,Dhruv Ringe,Indrajit Saha*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 通过引入带电单态标量门户，可复活标量单态暗物质的"沙漠"参数区，并在早期宇宙实现超荷对称性非恢复及强一阶相变，从而联系暗物质、重子不对称与引力波信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小标量单态暗物质经希格斯门户湮灭，受直接探测实验严格限制，仅在希格斯共振附近和TeV附近两个狭窄质量区间可行，中间留下大片"沙漠"参数区。作者希望复活该沙漠区域，并同时解释早期宇宙超荷对称性非恢复及重子不对称起源。

Method: 在最小标量单态暗物质模型中加入一个带电单态标量作为新门户。计算该带电标量对暗物质湮灭与单圈直接探测速率的贡献，分析其在高温下获得非零真空期望值所导致的U(1)_Y超荷对称性非恢复，并研究电荷破缺真空通过强一阶相变恢复到标准希格斯真空的过程。

Result: 带电标量门户可复活沙漠区，同时使单圈直接探测速率仍处于当前及未来实验可及范围。参数空间子集中，电荷破缺真空经强一阶相变恢复为标准希格斯真空，可通过轻子生成机制产生宇宙重子不对称。满足暗物质遗迹丰度、直接探测、对称性非恢复及其他实验限制的大部分参数空间，可被LISA、BBO、DECIGO等未来引力波探测器探测。

Conclusion: 该最小扩展同时容纳了暗物质、超荷对称性非恢复、重子不对称与可探测的引力波信号，是一个多现象学自洽且有预言性的场景。

Abstract: We explore the possibility of reviving scalar singlet dark matter (DM) with a minimal extension by a charged singlet scalar which also leads to hypercharge non-restoration in the early Universe. The minimal scalar singlet DM annihilating via the Higgs portal is tightly constrained by direct-detection experiments, leaving a desert between two narrow regions of the DM mass near the Higgs resonance and around a TeV. The addition of a charged scalar portal revives this desert region while keeping the one-loop direct-detection rate within reach of current and future experiments. A non-zero vacuum expectation value of the charged scalar at high temperature can also drive $U(1)_Y$ hypercharge symmetry non-restoration in the early Universe. In a subset of the parameter space, the charge-breaking minimum gets restored to the standard Higgs minimum via a strong first-order phase transition, making it a viable scenario for the generation of baryon asymmetry of the Universe via leptogenesis. A large part of the parameter space consistent with DM relic, direct-detection, symmetry non-restoration and other experimental bounds can be probed at upcoming GW detectors such as LISA, BBO and DECIGO.

</details>


### [306] [Inelastic Dark Matter from a Higher-Dimensional Operator: The LUX-ZEPLIN High-Recoil Event and Thermal Relic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00645)
*Debajyoti Choudhury,Jaydeb Das,Divya Sachdeva*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种非弹性散射解释，用单维度-6算符的费米暗物质扩展模型，通过微小软对称破缺项实现吸热跃迁并压低弹性散射，在无需微调的情况下同时解释LZ数据和遗迹丰度，暗物质质量约在(0.2,1)TeV范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期LUX-ZEPLIN(LZ)数据在约248 keV处出现核反冲事件，这激励了暗物质优先在大反冲能量处散射的情景，需要寻找相应的理论解释。

Method: 构建一个简单扩展模型：费米暗物质场通过单个维度-6算符与标准模型相互作用，并引入微小软对称破缺项以促进吸热跃迁，同时自然压低弹性散射。

Result: 在无需任何微调的情况下，该模型能够同时解释LZ数据与观测到的遗迹丰度，对应暗物质质量在约(0.2,1)TeV范围。

Conclusion: 非弹性散射解释为LZ大反冲事件提供了一种可行的理论框架，可在自然参数区间内统一解释实验信号与宇宙学遗迹丰度。

Abstract: The recent LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) data shows a nuclear-recoil event around $248\,\mathrm{keV}$, motivating scenarios in which dark matter (DM) preferentially scatters at large recoil energies. We investigate an inelastic scattering interpretation in terms of a simple extension with a fermionic DM field interacting with the Standard Model through a single dimension-6 operator. A tiny soft symmetry breaking term facilitates an endothermic transition while naturally suppressing elastic scatterings. Without any fine tuning, a simultaneous explanation of the LZ data and the observed relic density can be effected for a DM mass in the $\sim (0.2,1)$ TeV range.

</details>


### [307] [Neutrino trident process at a muon collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00723)
*Reinaldo Francener,Victor P. Goncalves*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究利用TeV级缪子对撞机直线段缪子衰变产生的高准直高能中微子束，通过前向中微子探测器探测稀有中微子三联过程（trident），并评估其对Z'玻色子等新物理的敏感度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TeV级缪子对撞机可产生高准直高能中微子束，近期研究表明可用于高精度中微子物理测量。作者希望进一步探索该束流在发现稀有中微子三联过程方面的潜力，以用于标准模型高精度检验和新物理搜索。

Method: 研究缪子对撞机产生的中微子三联过程，该过程源于中微子携带的弱玻色子与靶核库仑场中光子的融合。在基线10 kg中微子探测器中，针对末态带电轻子的不同味道组合，计算三联过程的年发生率，并以此评估其物理潜力。以L_μ−L_τ模型预言的Z'规范玻色子为例，推导对该新玻色子的投影敏感度。

Result: 在基线10 kg中微子探测器中，针对不同味道的末态带电轻子对，每年可获得量级为O(10^3)的高中微子三联过程发生率。基于此，推导出对L_μ−L_τ模型Z'玻色子的投影敏感度，结果表明缪子对撞机处的中微子探测器将显著扩展当前已排除的参数空间区域。

Conclusion: 缪子对撞机配合前向中微子探测器可高效观测稀有中微子三联过程，实现标准模型高精度检验并搜索新物理，尤其能显著提升对L_μ−L_τ模型Z'玻色子的探测能力。

Abstract: A TeV-scale muon collider enables the production of a highly collimated high-energy neutrino flux by muon decay in the straight sections of the collider ring. Recent studies have demonstrated that these neutrinos can be used for high-precision measurements in neutrino physics with a dedicated far-forward neutrino detector. In this study, we investigate the potentiality of the muon collider to discover the rare neutrino trident process, which is characterized by the fusion between a weak boson from the neutrino and a photon from the Coulomb field of the nucleus target. Our results indicate large neutrino trident rates per year [$\mathcal{O}(10^3)$] in a baseline 10 kg neutrino detector for a configuration of different flavour pairs of charged leptons in the final state, which will allow high-precision tests of the Standard Model and searches for New Physics. As a first example, we investigate the impact of the a new gauge boson $Z'$, as predicted by the $L_μ- L_τ$ model, and derive the projected sensitivity to this new gauge boson. Our findings show that a neutrino detector at a muon collider will significantly extend the current excluded regions of the parameter space.

</details>


### [308] [Systematic Bayesian investigation of the (2+1)-flavor QCD phase transition in a holographic model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01104)
*Liqiang Zhu,Xun Chen,Kai Zhou,Hanzhong Zhang,Mei Huang,Enke Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 构建贝叶斯全息QCD模型，用LQCD数据标定EMD模型参数，预测QCD相图临界终点（CEP）位置及置信区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将全息QCD模型与格点QCD（LQCD）数据系统结合，利用贝叶斯推断定量约束模型参数并纳入误差，从而对QCD相图CEP位置给出可靠预测，弥补传统全息建模缺乏数据标定与不确定性量化的不足。

Method: 以Einstein-Maxwell-Dilaton（EMD）框架为基础构建全息QCD模型，纳入零化学势下的LQCD数据（熵、声速平方、重子数磁化率）及其误差；采用贝叶斯推断框架标定模型参数，并在最大后验（MAP）估计下研究(2+1)味QCD在零/有限化学势下的热力学性质并预测CEP。

Result: MAP估计下CEP位于(T, μ)_MAP = (0.119, 0.615) GeV；68% CL区间为(T, μ) = (0.1176–0.1204, 0.59–0.63) GeV，95% CL区间为(0.1172–0.1206, 0.58–0.63) GeV。与其他理论模型预测的对比验证了该方法的稳健性与预测能力。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了全息建模的新型分析框架，并为难物质在极端条件下的相变研究提供了理论洞见。

Abstract: In this study, we construct a bayesian holographic QCD model by integrating the Einstein-Maxwell-Dilaton (EMD) framework with lattice quantum chromodynamics (QCD) data at zero chemical potential, specifically entropy, the square of the speed of sound, and baryon number susceptibilities, while systematically incorporating error estimates from the lattice QCD (LQCD) results. Leveraging a bayesian inference framework, we first achieve a precise calibration of the model parameters, then perform a comprehensive investigation into the thermodynamic properties of \((2+1)\)-flavor QCD at both zero and finite chemical potentials, and finally provide a prediction for the location of the critical end point (CEP) in the QCD phase diagram. Our results indicate that, under the maximum a posteriori (MAP) estimation, the CEP is located at $(T, μ)_{\mathrm{MAP}} = (0.119, 0.615)\mathrm{GeV}$. Furthermore, we provide the predicted regions for the CEP at $68\%$ and $95\%$ confidence levels (CL), yielding $(T, μ)_{68\% \mathrm{CL}} = (0.1176\text{--}0.1204,0.59\text{--}0.63)\mathrm{GeV}$ and $(T, μ)_{95\% \mathrm{CL}} = (0.1172\text{--}0.1206,0.58\text{--}0.63)\mathrm{GeV}$, respectively. A thorough comparison with predictions from other theoretical models validates the robustness and predictive power of our approach. This work not only establishes a novel analytical framework for holographic modeling but also provides valuable theoretical insights into the phase transitions of strongly interacting matter under extreme conditions.

</details>


### [309] [Analyticity Bootstrap of Feynman Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01154)
*Xiang Li,Dao-Ming Mu,Yan-Qing Ma*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出解析性自举方法确定费曼积分约化，利用泰勒展开区域有限性约束约化系数，可显著降低辅助质量流法中微分方程构造成本，甚至无需IBP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费曼积分约化（尤其是多变量情形）通常依赖大量IBP求解，计算成本高；需要一种更高效、少依赖IBP的约化方法。

Method: 基于维度正则化费曼积分在任意运动学点存在泰勒展开区域这一观察，利用泰勒区域有限性对约化系数（运动学变量的有理函数）施加约束；写出与奇点结构相容的最一般拟设，仅用少量IBP样本或渐近展开确定剩余参数，系统发展单变量与多变量情形。

Result: 给出单变量和多变量情形下的系统方法及多个显式算例；证明主积分的微分方程决定全部解析性约束，反之约束也有助于构造微分方程；在辅助质量流方法中可大幅降低DE构造成本，甚至完全无需IBP即可确定。

Conclusion: 解析性自举方法能高效确定费曼积分约化，揭示了微分方程与解析性约束的等价/互补关系，为减少IBP依赖与降低DE构造代价提供了新途径。

Abstract: We propose an analyticity bootstrap method to determine the reduction of Feynman integrals (FIs). The key observation is that, at any kinematic point, a dimensionally regularized FI possesses a Taylor expansion region (analytic region), and the finiteness of the Taylor region strongly constrains the integral reduction coefficients, which are rational functions of the kinematic variables. By writing the most general ansatz compatible with the singularity structure and then fixing the remaining parameters with only a few integration-by-parts (IBP) samples or asymptotic expansions, one can obtain the full reduction. We develop the method systematically in both single-variable and multi-variable cases, with many explicit examples. Interestingly, the differential equations (DEs) of the master integrals determine all these analyticity constraints and, conversely, the constraints facilitate the construction of the DEs. In particular, we show that the DEs in the auxiliary mass flow method can be constructed at significantly reduced cost and can even be fully determined without using IBP.

</details>


### [310] [Generalized fluctuation-dissipation theorem and Einstein relation in rotating equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01797)
*Shuo Fang,Shi Pu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文从精确的相空间KMS关系推导出旋转热平衡中矢量场的广义涨落耗散定理（FDT），揭示了旋转导致的张量贡献、广义爱因斯坦关系、连接零模与非旋转阻尼系数的爱因斯坦型关系，以及修正的细致平衡关系，为研究旋转量子物质提供微观平衡约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转量子物质（如重夸克偶素轨道极化）的微观平衡约束尚不清晰，尤其在矢量场情形下，传统标量热因子的FDT不足以描述旋转诱导的旋度效应，需要建立更广义的FDT框架以约束输运与极化行为。

Method: 从精确的相空间Kubo-Martin-Schwinger（KMS）关系出发，对旋转热平衡中的矢量场推导广义涨落耗散定理；在局域输运极限下，展开至热涡度二阶，分析对称动量扩散与耗散拖曳的谱信息关系，并推导零模与阻尼系数的爱因斯坦型关系及修正的细致平衡关系。

Result: 得到含旋转诱导张量贡献的FDT，超越标量热因子；局域输运极限下得到含旋转的模型无关广义爱因斯坦关系，二阶热涡度下对称动量扩散依赖超出旋转依赖耗散拖曳的低频谱信息；发现连接谱函数零模与非旋转阻尼系数的新爱因斯坦型关系，零模构成重夸克偶素轨道极化的新机制；修正的细致平衡关系其一阶涡旋修正由跃迁极化决定，且不显式依赖浴模型。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了研究旋转量子物质的微观平衡约束框架，所揭示的FDT、爱因斯坦关系及细致平衡修正对凝聚态、高能核物理等领域中旋转量子物质的研究具有普适意义。

Abstract: We derive a generalized fluctuation-dissipation theorem (FDT) for vector fields in rotating thermal equilibrium from an exact phase-space Kubo-Martin-Schwinger (KMS) relation. The resulting FDT contains rotation-induced tensorial contributions beyond a scalar thermal factor. In the local transport limit, we obtain a model-independent generalized Einstein relation with rotation in which, through second order in thermal vorticity, symmetric momentum diffusion depends on low-frequency spectral information beyond that encoded in the rotation-dependent dissipative drag. Remarkably, we find a new Einstein-type relation linking zero modes in the spectral function to the nonrotating drag coefficient. These zero modes underlie a new mechanism for the orbital polarization of heavy quarkonium. The same FDT constraint yields a modified detailed-balance relation whose first-order vortical correction is governed by transition polarization, without explicit dependence on the bath model. Our findings establish microscopic equilibrium constraints for studying rotating quantum matter in various fields.

</details>


### [311] [Parton-shower and fixed-order QCD effects in Higgs boson production in weak-boson fusion and its decays to bottom quarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01347)
*Arnd Behring*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究高阶QCD修正对弱玻色子融合产生希格斯玻色子并随后衰变为底夸克对的影响，发现固定阶计算在真实相空间截断下出现大幅修正，而采用部分子簇射匹配的描述可改善微扰收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确描述弱玻色子融合（VBF）产生希格斯玻色子并衰变为底夸克对的过程对LHC精确测量至关重要，但高阶QCD修正与相空间截断的相互作用尚未被充分理解。

Method: 在固定阶QCD计算中引入现实的fiducial截断，分析其对希格斯产生与衰变子过程辐射修正的影响；并采用与部分子簇射匹配的方式来描述衰变过程。

Result: 固定阶计算中观察到大幅修正，来源于特定fiducial截断与希格斯衰变子过程辐射修正之间的相互影响；切换到部分子簇射匹配描述后，在最低阶即能捕捉到相当大一部分修正，并改善微扰收敛性。

Conclusion: 部分子簇射匹配的衰变描述对于VBF希格斯产生及b夸克对衰变过程的精确计算更为可靠，能提升微扰稳定性和预测精度。

Abstract: We study the impact of higher-order QCD corrections on Higgs production via weak-boson fusion with subsequent decays of the Higgs boson to bottom-quark pairs. We consider realistic fiducial cuts and observe large corrections in a fixed-order calculation. They arise from the interplay of the particular fiducial cuts considered and the radiative corrections to the Higgs decay subprocess. Switching to a parton-shower-matched description of the decay captures a significant portion of the corrections already at the lowest order and improves the perturbative convergence of the calculation.

</details>


### [312] [Gravitational D-form factors of the nucleon and pion with mixing between two-quark and four-quark states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01396)
*Mamiya Kawaguchi,Yao Ma,Yong-Liang Ma*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在扩展线性sigma模型中研究核子与π介子的引力D形状因子，发现介子混合与手征不变质量对核子D形状因子的抑制作用难以区分，而π介子D形状因子可提供额外分辨信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核子D形状因子与核子质量分解相关，格点QCD给出其数值，但不同模型中导致该数值压低（抑制）的微观机制不同，需辨别介子混合效应与手征不变质量效应。

Method: 采用同时包含双夸克态、四夸克态及核子场的扩展线性sigma模型（eLSM），在标量介子主导图像下计算核子与π介子的引力D形状因子，并与宇称双重态模型（PDM）及格点QCD结果对比。

Result: eLSM中介子混合压低核子D形状因子，与格点QCD一致；PDM中核子质量的手征不变分量也产生定量相近的压低。手征极限下π介子D形状因子前向极限值在eLSM与PDM中均为-1，但介子混合改变其动量转移依赖，而手征不变质量不直接影响π介子D形状因子。

Conclusion: 仅凭核子D形状因子难以区分介子混合与手征不变质量的效应，联合分析核子与π介子D形状因子有助于深入理解核子质量分解及介子的夸克组成。

Abstract: We investigate the gravitational $D$-form factors of the nucleon and pion within an extended linear sigma model (eLSM) that incorporates both two-quark and four-quark states, as well as nucleon fields. Within the scalar-meson-dominance picture, we find that meson mixing between two-quark and four-quark states suppresses the nucleon $D$-form factor and leads to agreement with the lattice-QCD results. Interestingly, a quantitatively similar suppression was previously found in the parity doublet model (PDM) as a consequence of a nonzero chiral invariant component of the nucleon mass. This suggests that the effects of meson mixing and the chiral invariant mass are difficult to distinguish from the nucleon $D$-form factor alone. We therefore examine the pion $D$-form factor in the chiral limit. While its value in the forward limit remains fixed at $-1$ in both the eLSM and the PDM, meson mixing modifies its momentum-transfer dependence. In contrast, the chiral invariant mass does not directly affect the pion $D$-form factor. These results suggest that a combined analysis of the nucleon and pion $D$-form factors can provide further insight into the mass decomposition of the nucleon and the quark composition of mesons.

</details>


### [313] [Probing an up-phobic Higgs at colliders: the role of $b$-associated production in the democratic 3HDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01412)
*Dipankar Das,Teesha Khandelwal,Ipsita Saha*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在up-phobic极限下常规重希格斯产生机制被压低，b-associated产生成为主要通道；民主三希格斯二重态模型(3HDM)可实现该极限，现有b-associated搜索对其参数空间给出最强约束，未来对撞机可探测更多区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规重希格斯产生机制在某些希格斯sector中可能被压低，需要寻找替代的主要产生通道及可实现该情形的B SM框架，并对该情景给出实验约束与未来探测前景。

Method: 提出并论证民主三希格斯二重态模型(democratic 3HDM)作为可实现该极限的B SM框架；在该up-phobic极限下分析b-associated产生为主导的机制；利用现有b-associated搜索数据推导对3HDM参数空间的约束，并对未来对撞机搜索做投影。

Result: 在up-phobic极限下，b-associated产生通道成为主要产生机制；现有b-associated搜索是LHC对该3HDM情景最敏感的探针，并给出相应参数空间约束；未来对撞机搜索投影显示可探测模型up-phobic参数空间的更多区域。

Conclusion: 民主3HDM提供了可实现重希格斯常规产生机制被压低的可行B SM框架，此时b-associated产生占主导，现有及未来对撞机b-associated搜索可有效约束和探测其up-phobic参数空间。

Abstract: We show that there exists a theoretically well-motivated Higgs sector in which the conventional heavy-Higgs production mechanisms are suppressed. In this limit, the $b$-associated production channel assumes the role of the primary production mechanism. We demonstrate that the democratic three Higgs-doublet model (3HDM) provides a viable beyond the SM (BSM) framework in which this limit can be realized. We further show that existing $b$-associated searches provide the most sensitive LHC probe of this 3HDM scenario in the up-phobic limit and derive the corresponding constraints on its parameter space. We also present projections for future collider searches, demonstrating their potential to probe further regions of the model's up-phobic parameter space.

</details>


### [314] [Mechanisms of $D \bar{D}$ pair production in $e^{+} e^{-} \to e^{+} e^{-} D \bar{D}$ and $AA \to AA D \bar{D}$ reactions and production of exotic charmonia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01420)
*Antoni Szczurek,Izabela Babiarz,Piotr Lebiedowicz,Wolfgang Schäfer*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了 $e^+e^-$ 碰撞中 $D\bar{D}$ 对的产生机制，认为 Belle 和 BaBar 观测到的 $M_{D^0\bar{D}^0}=3.8$ GeV 宽鼓包主要来自连续谱贡献，而非 $\chi_{c0}(3860)$ 共振，并给出了 $\chi_{c2}(3930)\to D\bar{D}$ 的分支比及 Belle II 与 LHC 的相关预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对 Belle 和 BaBar 在 $M_{D^0\bar{D}^0}=3.8$ GeV 处观测到的宽鼓包，需澄清其来源是连续谱机制还是宽 $\chi_{c0}(3860)$ 共振；同时 $\chi_{c2}(3930)$ 作为 $P$ 波粲偶素候选者，其衰变分支比尚不明确，需要理论与实验对比确定。

Method: 考虑 $D^0\bar{D}^0$ 连续谱机制中的 $t/u$ 道 $D^*$ 矢量介子交换，$D^*D\gamma$ 耦合常数由 $D^*\to D\gamma$ 衰变导出；对 $D^+D^-$ 道主要考虑 $D^\pm$ 交换。同时研究 $\chi_{c0}(3860)$ 与 $\chi_{c2}(3930)$ 的共振产生，并将模型结果与 $\Gamma(\chi_{c2}(2P)\to\gamma\gamma)=0.544$ keV（对应 Buchmüller-Tye 势）及 BaBar 数据进行比较。

Result: $D^0\bar{D}^0$ 道连续谱贡献相对较大，$D^+D^-$ 道显著较小，表明 3.8 GeV 宽鼓包源于连续谱而非宽 $\chi_{c0}(3860)$ 共振；$\chi_{c2}(3930)$ 是首个激发 $P$ 波粲偶素 $\chi_{c2}(2P)$ 的良好候选者，其可衰变至两个 $D\bar{D}$ 道，拟合得到 $B(\chi_{c2}(3930)\to D\bar{D})=0.58\pm0.13$。此外给出了 Belle II 运动学下若干变量的微分分布，并给出 LHC 上超周边重离子反应 $PbPb\to PbPb D\bar{D}$（低不变质量 $M_{D\bar{D}}<4.3$ GeV）的预言。

Conclusion: 3.8 GeV 处的宽鼓包具有连续谱起源，而非宽 $\chi_{c0}(3860)$ 共振；$\chi_{c2}(3930)$ 可解释为 $\chi_{c2}(2P)$ 并给出其 $D\bar{D}$ 分支比，研究结果可为 Belle II 及 LHC 超周边碰撞实验提供检验与预言。

Abstract: We discuss mechanisms of $D \bar{D}$ pair production in $e^+ e^-$ collisions. We consider the $D^0 {\bar D}^0$-continuum mechanism with the $t/u$-channel $D^*$ vector-meson exchanges, where the $D^* D γ$ coupling constants are derived from the $D^* \to D γ$ decays. For the $D^+ D^-$ channel, we consider mainly the $D^{\pm}$ exchanges. We find a relatively large continuum contribution for the $D^0 {\bar D}^0$ channel and a significantly smaller one for $D^+ D^-$, concluding that the broad bump at $M_{D^0 {\bar D}^0} = 3.8$ GeV observed by the Belle and BaBar Collaborations has a continuum origin rather than corresponding to the broad $χ_{c0}(3860)$ resonance. We also investigate the resonant production of $D \bar{D}$ pairs, including $χ_{c0}(3860)$ and $χ_{c2}(3930)$. The $χ_{c2}(3930)$ meson is a good candidate for the first excited $P$-wave charmonium $χ_{c2}(2P)$ state. This resonance can decay into both $D \bar{D}$ channels; however, its branching fractions are not well known at present. Comparing our model result with $Γ(χ_{c2}(2P) \to γγ) = 0.544$ keV (corresponding to the Buchmüller-Tye potential) to the BaBar data, we find $B(χ_{c2}(3930) \to D \bar{D}) = 0.58 \pm 0.13$. We present results for differential distributions in several variables for Belle II kinematics. Finally, we shortly mention predictions for the ultraperipheral heavy-ion reaction $Pb Pb \to Pb Pb D \bar{D}$ for low invariant mass ($M_{D \bar{D}} < 4.3$ GeV) at the LHC.

</details>


### [315] [Radiative corrections to the subprocess of annihilation of quark-antiquark pair of prompt photon production in proton-proton collisions at NICA energies $\sqrt{\mathbf{s}}$= 10 GeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01552)
*Mohsun R. Alizada,Azar I. Ahmadov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对NICA能区质子-质子碰撞中即时光子产生的夸克-反夸克湮灭过程，开展了QCD与QED辐射修正（qq̄→qq̄γ、qq̄→ggγ、qq̄→gγγ）的理论与数值研究，揭示了截面、双自旋不对称度及极化效应规律，并验证了FeynCalc与PYTHIA 8.316的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低碰撞能量与低横动量下辐射修正显著，而NICA能区即时光子产生中上述辐射修正过程尚缺乏系统研究，需明确各修正通道的相对贡献及其极化依赖性。

Method: 使用FeynCalc进行严格领头阶解析计算，得到微分截面与双自旋不对称度ALL；并在PYTHIA 8.316中对三个辐射修正过程（qq̄→qq̄γ、qq̄→ggγ、qq̄→gγγ）进行建模与数值模拟，对比中心运动学区域结果。

Result: 辐射修正在低√s和小光子横动量pT下显著。截面关系为σ(qq̄→ggγ)>σ(qq̄→qq̄γ)>σ(qq̄→gγγ)；微分截面关系为dσ(qq̄→qq̄γ)/dpT²>dσ(qq̄→gγγ)/dpT²>dσ(qq̄→ggγ)/dpT²。质子极化对三个过程影响几乎相同，上述关系依然成立。FeynCalc解析结果与PYTHIA 8.316模拟在中心运动学区域高度一致。

Conclusion: 该研究系统刻画了NICA能区即时光子产生中QCD/QED辐射修正的行为，给出了不同修正通道的截面排序与极化效应特征，并证实了解析计算与PYTHIA模拟的良好吻合。

Abstract: In this work, a comprehensive theoretical and numerical study of the quantum-chromodynamic and quantum-electrodynamic radiative corrections: 1. $q\bar{q} \rightarrow q\bar{q}γ$, 2. $q\bar{q} \rightarrow ggγ$, and 3. $q\bar{q} \rightarrow gγγ$ to the annihilation of a quark-antiquark pair $q\bar{q} \rightarrow gγ$ of prompt photon production in proton-proton collisions at NICA energies $\sqrt{s}$ = 10 GeV was performed. The differential cross-sections and double spin asymmetries $A_{LL}$ of the radiative corrections were investigated analytically using the FeynCalc and modulated in PYTHIA 8.316. Radiative corrections are significant at low collision energies of protons $\sqrt{s}$ and small transverse momentum of photons $p_{T}$. It was shown that radiative corrections to the annihilation of a quark-antiquark pair process satisfy the following relations:
  $$ 2. σ_{\sqrt{s}}(q\bar{q} \rightarrow ggγ) > 1. σ_{\sqrt{s}}(q\bar{q} \rightarrow q\bar{q}γ) > 3. σ_{\sqrt{s}}(q\bar{q} \rightarrow gγγ) $$ $$ 1. \frac{dσ(q\bar{q} \rightarrow q\bar{q}γ)}{dp_{T}^{2}} >
  3.
  \frac{dσ(q\bar{q} \rightarrow gγγ)}{dp_{T}^{2}} >
  2. \frac{dσ(q\bar{q} \rightarrow ggγ)}{dp_{T}^{2}} $$
  The polarization of colliding protons has almost the same effect on processes 1. $q\bar{q} \rightarrow q\bar{q}γ$, 2. $q\bar{q} \rightarrow ggγ$, and 3. $q\bar{q} \rightarrow gγγ$ and in this case above indicated relation is also satisfied.
  A direct comparison between strict leading order analytical calculations in FeynCalc and PYTHIA 8.316 modulations revealed excellent agreement in the central kinematic regions.

</details>


### [316] [From the LZ Event to Grand Unification: Heavy Higgsinos and Proton Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01613)
*Shihwen Hor,Natsumi Nagata,Tsutomu T. Yanagida*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LZ事件被解释为很重的inelastic Higgsino暗物质（~10^5 GeV），虽加剧规范耦合统一挑战，但在包含GUT阈值修正的最小SU(5)中仍可满足统一条件，并令dimension-five质子衰变p→K⁺ν̄处于下一代实验探测范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ事件重新激发了inelastic Higgsino暗物质的兴趣。弱电标度Higgsino虽能简单解释该事件，但与IceCube基于太阳中暗物质俘获与湮灭的束缚相冲突；要回避该冲突需很重的Higgsino（质量约10^5 GeV、中性态质量劈裂约400 keV），进而要求gaugino质量约10^7 GeV。如此重的超对称谱通常被认为不利于超对称大统一：会恶化规范耦合统一、降低统一标度，可能导致过快的质子衰变。本文即重新审视这一预期。

Method: 在最小超对称SU(5)框架下，纳入GUT标度的阈值修正，重新分析规范耦合统一条件，并考察其对SU(5)规范玻色子质量、色三重态Higgs质量以及质子衰变寿命的影响，同时讨论能产生所需超对称质量谱的超对称破缺方案。

Result: 结果表明，当adjoint-Higgs自耦合较小时，统一条件允许SU(5)规范玻色子保持足够重，从而压低dimension-six质子衰变；同时色三重态Higgs质量可保持在常规GUT标度附近，M_H_C~10^16 GeV，尤其当gluino显著重于wino时。在该参数区域，dimension-five衰变模式p→K⁺ν̄的寿命可落入下一代质子衰变实验（Hyper-Kamiokande、JUNO、DUNE）的探测范围。

Conclusion: 很重的inelastic Higgsino谱在最小超对称SU(5)中并不必然破坏统一：GUT阈值修正可使规范玻色子足够重以抑制dimension-six衰变，并保持色三重态Higgs接近常规GUT标度，同时给出下一代实验可探测的p→K⁺ν̄寿命。文中还讨论了可实现该谱的超对称破缺情形。

Abstract: The recently reported LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) event has renewed interest in inelastic Higgsino dark matter. While a weak-scale Higgsino provides a simple interpretation of the event, such a scenario is in tension with the IceCube bound from dark-matter capture and annihilation in the Sun. This tension can be avoided for a much heavier Higgsino, with a mass of order $10^5$ GeV and a neutral-state mass splitting of $\simeq 400$ keV, which in turn points to gaugino masses around $10^7$ GeV. Such a heavy supersymmetric spectrum is often thought to be unfavorable for supersymmetric grand unified theories (GUTs), since it tends to worsen gauge coupling unification and lower the unification scale, potentially leading to excessively rapid proton decay. In this work, we revisit this expectation in minimal supersymmetric SU(5), taking into account the GUT-scale threshold corrections. We show that the resulting unification conditions allow the SU(5) gauge bosons to remain sufficiently heavy when the adjoint-Higgs self-coupling is small, thereby suppressing dimension-six proton decay. At the same time, the color-triplet Higgs mass can remain near the conventional GUT scale, $M_{H_C}\sim 10^{16}$ GeV, particularly when the gluino is significantly heavier than the wino. Remarkably, in this region the dimension-five decay mode $p\to K^+\barν$ can have a lifetime within the reach of next-generation proton-decay searches, including Hyper-Kamiokande, JUNO, and DUNE. We also discuss supersymmetry-breaking scenarios that can give rise to the required supersymmetric mass spectrum.

</details>


### [317] [Overlapping Subcritical Bubbles: Free Energy and Lifetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01629)
*Guangshang Chen,Yang Xiao,Jin Min Yang,Yang Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究弱一级相变中亚临界气泡间的空间重叠对其演化的影响，发现重叠降低自由能代价并延长气泡寿命，可能产生集体效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚临界气泡在弱一级相变中可形成可观测的群体，但通常被当作孤立涨落处理。作者质疑相邻亚临界气泡间的空间重叠是否会改变其演化，这一基本假设此前未被系统检验。

Method: 结合两种手段：针对复合高斯型气泡分布进行解析自由能计算，并采用Langevin模拟研究气泡重叠构型。

Result: 重叠会降低自由能代价，并总体延长亚临界气泡的寿命；寿命增强可能反过来影响其丰度。

Conclusion: 气泡重叠可产生集体效应，因此应纳入亚临界气泡群体的动力学描述中。

Abstract: Subcritical bubbles can form an appreciable population during weak first-order phase transitions, but are usually treated as isolated fluctuations. This raises the question of whether spatial overlap between neighboring subcritical bubbles can modify their evolution. We address this question by combining analytic free-energy calculations for composite Gaussian profiles with Langevin simulations of overlapping configurations. We find that the overlap lowers the free-energy cost and generally increases the lifetime of subcritical bubbles, with the enhanced persistence potentially feeding back on their abundance. Such overlap can therefore generate collective effects and should be incorporated into kinetic descriptions of subcritical-bubble populations.

</details>


### [318] [Spin relaxation of heavy quarks from momentum diffusion in the quark-gluon plasma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01791)
*Ankit Kumar,Sourav Dey,Vinod Chandra,Amaresh Jaiswal*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 通过建立耦合的动量-自旋Fokker-Planck方程，将重夸克扩散的随机动量反冲经由Thomas进动与自旋弛豫联系起来，并定量估计了粲夸克和底夸克的自旋弛豫时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重夸克自旋动力学可作为探测重夸克与夸克-胶子等离子体相互作用的潜在探针，但此前缺乏将扩散过程与自旋转动联系起来的系统理论框架。

Method: 从Thomas-Bargmann-Michel-Telegdi方程与描述重夸克动量的Langevin方程出发，推导出新的耦合自旋-动量Fokker-Planck方程；在重夸克极限下积分掉动量自由度，得到关于自旋取向的转动Fokker-Planck方程，从而建立Thomas进动对自旋弛豫时间τ_s的贡献与动量扩散系数κ的直接关系。

Result: 非相对论极限下得到τ_s=2τ_p(m_Q/T)^2（τ_p为动量弛豫时间）；基于格点QCD、强耦合估计、T-matrix计算和重味唯象学给出的κ值，计算了粲夸克和底夸克的Thomas进动自旋弛豫时间贡献。相对论运动学修正仅带来适度变化，不确定度主要来自κ。

Conclusion: 所给出的结果仅代表Thomas进动对重夸克自旋弛豫的贡献，与色磁场的直接耦合提供了另一条额外通道。

Abstract: Heavy-quark spin dynamics in the quark-gluon plasma provides a potential probe of the interaction between heavy quarks and the medium. We develop a stochastic framework in which the random momentum kicks responsible for heavy-quark diffusion also induce spin rotation through Thomas precession. Starting from the Thomas-Bargmann-Michel-Telegdi equation and a Langevin equation for heavy-quark momentum, we derive a novel coupled spin-momentum Fokker-Planck equation. In the heavy-quark limit, where momentum equilibrates faster than spin, the momentum degrees of freedom can be integrated out, yielding a rotational Fokker-Planck equation for the spin orientation. This establishes a direct relation between the Thomas precession contribution to the spin-relaxation time $τ_{s}$ and the heavy-quark momentum-diffusion coefficient $κ$. In the non-relativistic limit, we find $τ_s=2τ_p(m_Q/T)^2$, with $τ_p$ being the momentum relaxation time. Using recent determinations of $κ$ from lattice QCD, strong-coupling estimates, $T$-matrix calculations, and heavy-flavor phenomenology, we obtain the corresponding Thomas precession contribution to spin relaxation times for charm and bottom quarks. Relativistic kinematics modify the result by only a modest amount over the temperature range relevant for heavy-ion collisions, while the uncertainty is dominated by that of $κ$. We emphasize that the result represents the Thomas precession contribution to heavy-quark spin relaxation; direct coupling to the chromomagnetic field provides an additional channel.

</details>


### [319] [Probing the neutrino mass through semileptonic decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01808)
*Claire Chevallier*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出通过半轻子衰变的前后不对称性来检验有质量中微子假设：该观测量对无质量中微子严格为零，若中微子有质量且通过左手算符相互作用则非零；对右手相互作用，即使右手中微子无质量该量也显著偏离零。作者以赝标量介子衰变为另一赝标量介子的过程进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种能够区分无质量中微子与有质量中微子、并辨别左手与右手相互作用的可观测检验手段。

Method: 分析半轻子衰变中的前后不对称性相关观测量，并以一个赝标量介子衰变为另一个赝标量介子为具体算例进行显式计算。

Result: 该前后不对称性相关参量在无质量中微子情形下恒为零；当有质量中微子通过左手算符与标准模型场耦合时变为非零；在右手相互作用情形下，即便右手中微子无质量，该量也明显不为零。

Conclusion: 半轻子衰变的精细分析可作为检验有质量中微子及区分左右手相互作用的有效探针，赝标量介子衰变实例印证了该判别量的可行性。

Abstract: We argue that a detailed analysis of semileptonic decays can test the possibility of a massive neutrino. The key observable, related to the forward-backward asymmetry, is exactly zero for a massless neutrino but becomes non-zero if the neutral lepton is heavy and interacts with Standard Model fields via left-handed operators. For right-handed interactions, this quantity differs significantly from zero even for a massless right-handed neutrino. We demonstrate this explicitly using the example of a pseudoscalar meson decaying into another pseudoscalar meson.

</details>


### [320] [Near Threshold Kaluza-Klein Graviton Decays and the Dark Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01825)
*Kevin Langhoff*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: KK引力子在阈值附近的塔内衰变为纯d波，衰变率∝p^5而非暗维度文献假设的s波∝p；平直暗维度下KK引力子不能作为暗物质，且其冻结产生MeV伽马射线可限制再加热温度，结合BBN与CMB下限要求额外维度半径比距离猜想偏好值小约三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗维度文献通常假设KK引力子塔内衰变为s波（Γ∝p），并据此将KK引力子作为暗物质候选者。本文旨在检验该假设在阈值附近是否成立，并评估其对暗物质、再加热温度及额外维度半径的物理后果。

Method: 计算阈值附近KK引力子塔内衰变的角动量性质与衰变率标度；在平直暗维度框架下分析KK引力子的冻结产生（freeze-in）丰度及其产生的MeV伽马射线通量；结合大爆炸核合成（BBN）与宇宙微波背景（CMB）对再加热温度的下限进行约束。

Result: 阈值附近塔内衰变为纯d波，Γ∝p^5，而非s波的Γ∝p。在平直暗维度中，这排除了KK引力子作为暗物质候选者。即使假设其他暗物质来源，KK引力子的不可约冻结产生丰度会产生MeV伽马射线，从而给出再加热温度的上限；结合BBN与CMB给出的再加热温度下限，要求额外维度半径R远小于距离猜想偏好值，约小三个数量级，即R~Λ^{-1/4}。

Conclusion: 暗维度文献中关于KK引力子s波衰变及暗物质候选者的假设不成立。修正后的d波衰变率带来可观测的MeV伽马射线信号，并对再加热温度与额外维度半径施加严格限制，使半径比距离猜想自然预期值小约三个数量级。

Abstract: Near threshold, intra-tower decays of Kaluza-Klein (KK) gravitons are pure $d$-wave, with rate $Γ\propto p^5$ in the decay products momentum $p$, rather than the $s$-wave $Γ\propto p$ assumed in the dark dimension literature. In a flat dark dimension this excludes KK gravitons as a dark matter candidate. Even if some other source of dark matter is assumed, the irreducible freeze-in abundance of KK gravitons produce MeV gamma rays which allows one to set upper limits on the reheating temperature; additionally using the lower bound on the reheating temperature from big-bang nucleosynthesis and the cosmic microwave background requires the radius of the extra dimension to be almost three orders of magnitude smaller than the distance conjecture would naively prefer, $R\sim Λ^{-1/4}$.

</details>


### [321] [An optimal strategy for experimental searches in high-mass dilepton production at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01965)
*J. Alcaraz Maestre,O. M. Carretero*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于次领头阶QCD精度重加权的方法，用于LHC高不变质量双轻子产生中新物理效应的最优研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LHC高不变质量双轻子产生中的新物理效应需要高精度且灵活的理论工具，而完整精确生成各种新物理情景的模拟样本计算成本高昂。

Method: 采用在量子色动力学次领头阶精度下精确的重加权（reweighting）程序。唯一需要的计算昂贵输入是标准模型pp→V(*)→ℓ⁺ℓ⁻（V=Z,γ*）样本，该样本需在同等精度下生成并对高不变质量相空间有充分统计覆盖。

Result: 该方法适用于任意q q̄ ℓ⁺ℓ⁻接触相互作用情景，无需假设底层结构和耦合形式；也适用于寻找s道产生的中等至大宽度新中性共振态，通过同一初始样本即可方便地改变质量、耦合或宽度等输入参数。

Conclusion: 该方法可高效、精确且灵活地研究LHC双轻子高不变质量区域的新物理，避免为每种情景重复生成昂贵样本。

Abstract: We present an optimal method for studying new physics effects in high-mass dilepton production at the LHC. This method relies on a reweighting procedure that is precise to the next-to-leading order in quantum chromodynamics. The only computationally expensive input required is a standard model $\mathrm{pp\rightarrow V^{(*)}\rightarrow\ell^+\ell^-}$ sample, with $\mathrm{V=Z,γ^*}$, generated at the same level of precision and with sufficient statistical coverage of the phase space at high invariant masses. The proposed method is suitable for any $\mathrm{q\overline{q}\ell^+\ell^-}$ contact-interaction scenario, regardless of the assumed underlying structure and couplings. It is also ideal for searching for medium-to-large-width new neutral resonances produced in the s-channel, as changes to the input values for mass, coupling, or width can be easily implemented using the same initial generated samples.

</details>


### [322] [Confronting $K^-d$ dynamics with kaonic atom measurements within a novel unitary framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01977)
*Albert Feijoo,Àngels Ramos,Juan Torres-Rincon*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文用Faddeev方程固定中心近似构建幺正的K⁻d三体振幅，并借助SIDDHARTA-2最新测量重新标定BCN模型参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低能反kaon-核子（K̄N）相互作用中的同位旋矢量分量约束较差，需要新的实验信息。常规方法缺少幺正性约束，且未系统考虑耦合道、电荷交换及同位旋破缺效应，因此需要一致的理论框架来利用最新的kaonic氢与kaonic氘实验数据。

Method: 以手征K̄N相互作用模型（OR与BCN）为输入，在Faddeev方程的固定中心近似下构建K⁻d三体振幅，通过纳入相干多次散射过程实现幺正约束；将幺正化推广到含电荷交换的耦合道并引入物理粒子质量以包含同位旋破缺效应；进而求散射长度与有效力程，并用bootstrap分析检验BCN模型与SIDDHARTA-2最新的kaonic氢、kaonic氘测量数据的相容性。

Result: 给出了低能K⁻d振幅的一致描述并确定了散射长度和有效力程；bootstrap分析表明BCN参数化需要重新调整，且该调整在同位旋敏感的多个K̄N散射可观测量中产生明显效应。

Conclusion: 新的kaonic原子测量为更好地约束K̄N相互作用中约束较差的同位旋矢量分量提供了额外信息，凸显了此类实验对改进手征K̄N模型的重要价值。

Abstract: We study the low-energy interaction of negative kaons with deuterons using chiral $\bar{K}N$ interaction models (OR and BCN) as input. The three-body $K^-d$ amplitude is constructed within the Fixed-Center Approximation to the Faddeev equations, where the unitary constraints missing in the conventional approach are formally implemented with the incorporation of coherent multiple-scattering processes. The unitary procedure is extended to coupled channels including charge-exchange processes, as well as isospin-breaking effects through the use of physical particle masses. This provides a consistent description of the low-energy $K^-d$ amplitude and allows for the determination of the scattering length and the effective range. The resulting framework is then used to assess the compatibility of the BCN $\bar{K}N$ interaction model with the recent SIDDHARTA-2 measurements of kaonic hydrogen and kaonic deuterium through a bootstrap analysis. The procedure leads to a readjustment of the BCN parameterization which produces visible effects in several isospin-sensitive $\bar{K}N$ scattering observables, hence highlighting the additional information provided by the new kaonic-atom measurements to better constrain the poorly known isovector component of the ${\bar K}N$ interaction.

</details>


### [323] [Effective field theory of scalar glueballs: Form factors and interaction radii](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01988)
*Adamu Issifu,Orlando Oliveira,Tobias Frederico*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于Yang-Mills胶子凝聚建立标量胶球相互作用的手征规范不变有效场论，从相干态真空推导有效拉氏量，给出归一化标量胶球形子，预测基态胶球有效相互作用半径r_int=0.28 fm，与格点结果高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量胶球（特别是f_0(1710)共振）的动力学描述需要连接有效场论与第一性原理格点计算。现有方法缺乏系统性桥梁，且相互作用半径等关键量的理论预测与格点数据对比不足，本文旨在构建规范不变的有效场论框架以填补这一空白。

Method: 以Yang-Mills胶子凝聚为基础，采用相干态表述构建Yang-Mills真空，推导标量胶球及其与胶子相互作用的有效拉氏量；将满足交叉对称的截断四胶子Green函数投影到色单态J^{PC}=0^{++}通道，得到归一化标量胶球形子，其可分解为普适运动学结构与内禀胶球函数；并纳入第一激发态0^{++}胶球修正。

Result: 形式体系无参数地预测基态标量胶球（对应f_0(1710)共振）的有效相互作用半径为r_int=√6/m_φ=0.28 fm，与近期格点Yang-Mills测得的质量-半径r=0.263(31) fm 高度吻合；归一化形子的动量依赖与格点Yang-Mills引力形子数据一致，f_0(1710)给出最佳整体拟合；纳入第一激发态0^{++}胶球后对相互作用半径产生适度修正（r_int^*=0.240 fm），同时给出稳定且运动学稳健的形子修正。

Conclusion: 该框架为标量胶球动力学的有效场论描述与第一性原理格点Yang-Mills计算之间提供了系统性桥梁，验证了胶子凝聚相干态方法描述胶球相互作用的有效性。

Abstract: We develop a gauge-invariant effective field theory for scalar glueball interactions based on the Yang-Mills gluon condensate. Starting from a coherent-state formulation of the Yang-Mills vacuum, we derive an effective Lagrangian for the scalar glueball and its interactions with gluons. The resulting crossing-symmetric amputated four-gluon Green's function is projected onto the color-singlet $J^{PC}=0^{++}$ channel, yielding a normalized scalar glueball form factor that factorizes into universal kinematic structures and an intrinsic glueball function. The formalism predicts a parameter-free effective interaction radius for the ground-state scalar glueball with resonance $f_0(1710)$, $r_{\rm int}=\sqrt{6}/m_φ=0.28$ fm, which is in excellent agreement with recent lattice Yang-Mills determinations of the mass-radius ($r=0.263(31)$ fm). The predicted momentum dependence of the normalized form factor is consistent with lattice Yang-Mills gravitational form factor data, with the $f_0(1710)$ candidate providing the best overall fit and $r_{\rm int}$. Inclusion of the first excited $0^{++}$ glueball produces modest corrections to the interaction radius ($r_{\rm int}^*=0.240$ fm) while yielding stable and kinematically robust modifications of the form factor. This framework provides a systematic bridge between effective field theory and first-principles lattice Yang-Mills calculations of scalar glueball dynamics.

</details>


### [324] [Electron and monopole properties in a dark matter model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02083)
*Jacob García-Moreno,Vicente Vento*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文将QED与电弱理论分别扩展为引入暗扇区（含暗光子与暗磁单极子）的模型，通过两扇区间的混合相互作用使轻子与磁单极子转化为双荷子，并分析电子在两模型中的可观测效应，指出暗光子质量对可观测性起关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁单极子自提出以来用于对称化麦克斯韦方程组，但至今无实验证据。近期有工作提出带无质量暗光子与暗磁单极子的QED推广模型，可给出研究磁单极物理的新途径，作者希望将该思路推广至电弱理论并探讨可观测性。

Method: 在电弱理论中引入含大质量暗光子与暗磁单极子的暗扇区，并加入两扇区之间的混合相互作用项；随后对QED模型与电弱模型分别推导电子（轻子）转化为双荷子的效应，并分析相关实验后果，重点考察暗光子质量的影响。

Result: 在引入混合相互作用后，轻子与磁单极子转化为双荷子；电子由此获得新的行为特征，成为探测磁单极物理的途径。暗光子的质量对可观测性具有重要影响，不同质量情形下电子的实验信号不同。

Conclusion: 将暗扇区及混合相互作用推广到电弱理论是可行的，电子可作为研究磁单极物理的探针，暗光子质量是决定可观测性的关键参数。

Abstract: Monopoles have been a subject of much theoretical and experimental research since they were proposed to symmetrize Maxwell's equations. No experimental signature of their existence has been detected. A model generalizing QED with dark massless photons and dark monopoles was proposed not long ago. In this model, by introducing a mixing interaction between the two sectors, leptons and monopoles are transformed into into dyons. This particular behavior of the electrons determines a new path to study the physics of monopoles. Here we generalize the Electroweak theory in the same way by incorporating a dark sector with massive photons and monopoles, and analyze experimental consequences for electrons in both models. The mass of the dark photon plays an important role regarding observability.

</details>


### [325] [A consistent $\mathcal{O}(v^2)$ NRQCD description of decays and high-$P_T$ prompt production of charmonia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02096)
*Valerio Bertone,Jean-Philippe Lansberg,Kate Lynch,Tanjona R. Rabemananjara*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用LHC高横动量数据，在NRQCD框架下至$\mathcal{O}(v^2)$阶全球拟合六个长程矩阵元(LDMEs)，成功描述了$J/\psi$、$\psi(2S)$及$\chi_c$的大$P_T$强子产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验$\mathcal{O}(v^2)$ NRQCD能否统一描述$J/\psi$、$\psi(2S)$和$\chi_c$在高横动量区间的强子产生，并确定六个长程矩阵元。

Method: 采用NLO+NLL领头幂次碎裂框架，注入每个粲偶素的衰变宽度，拟合ATLAS与CMS的$\psi(2S)/J/\psi$产额比及CMS在$\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV的$\psi(2S)$极化数据，并结合直接与来自$\chi_c$、$\psi(2S)$的feed-down贡献。

Result: 拟合所得LDMEs能出色预言CMS和ATLAS直至$P_T=360$ GeV的$J/\psi$截面数据以及CMS的$J/\psi$极化数据。

Conclusion: 与产生及衰变数据的高度一致性表明，$\mathcal{O}(v^2)$ NRQCD足以描述大$P_T$下$J/\psi$、$\psi(2S)$和$\chi_c$的强子产生。

Abstract: We present a global determination of the six long-distance matrix elements (LDMEs) within non-relativistic QCD (NRQCD) appearing in the production of prompt $J/ψ$ up to $\mathcal{O}(v^2)$ using high-$P_T$ LHC data for direct and feed-down contributions from $χ_c$ and $ψ(2S)$. Using a leading-power fragmentation framework at NLO+NLL, we fit $ψ(2S)/J/ψ$ yield ratios from ATLAS and CMS and $ψ(2S)$ CMS polarization data at $\sqrt{s} = 13$ TeV, injecting one decay width per charmonium. The fit LDMEs provide excellent predictions of the CMS and ATLAS cross-section data up to $P_T=360$ GeV for the $J/ψ$, as well as the $J/ψ$ polarization data from CMS. The obtained excellent agreement with production and decay data demonstrates that $\mathcal{O}(v^2)$ NRQCD is sufficient to describe $J/ψ$, $ψ(2S)$, and $χ_c$ hadroproduction at large $P_T$.

</details>


### [326] [The Scalar MSW Effect: Compact analytical formulas for neutrino oscillations with large matter effects, including $μ-μ$ and $τ-τ$ dominant matter potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02107)
*Sandhya Choubey,Andreas Lund*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展Jacobi对角化方法，通过特定欧拉旋转参数化PMNS矩阵，推导出标准物质效应及标量非标准相互作用（SNSI）下的三味中微子振荡有效参数映射公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确且简洁的三味中微子物质振荡概率公式对理解未来实验现象学至关重要，而现有方法难以处理奇异物质势（如SNSI）情形。

Method: 扩展Jacobi对角化方法，利用特定排列的欧拉旋转对混合矩阵U_PMNS进行参数化；先重访标准物质效应以验证推广方法，再应用于标量非标准相互作用（SNSI），推导三种单对角耦合情形下的有效参数映射。

Result: 推导出的公式表明所有对角SNSI耦合均可产生标量MSW（SMSW）效应，即类似MSW效应的能量无关共振增强；针对多个当代和未来实验评估了SNSI公式精度，δm_ee情形精度极佳，δm_μμ和δm_ττ情形因需忽略A_CC而精度较差，并通过一阶微扰修正予以改善。

Conclusion: 该Jacobi对角化扩展方法可有效推导奇异物质势下的简洁精确有效参数映射，所揭示的SMSW效应及精度评估为未来中微子实验的现象学研究提供了工具与参考。

Abstract: Deriving simple and accurate three-flavor neutrino oscillation probability formulas in matter is critical for understanding the phenomenology of upcoming experiments. In this article, we extend the Jacobi diagonalization method for deriving effective parameter mappings for neutrino oscillations in matter. The key insight behind the extension is to purposefully parameterize the mixing matrix, $U_{\text{PMNS}}$, using particular arrangements of Euler rotations. This novel extension allows for deriving simple and accurate effective mixing parameter mappings for exotic types of matter potentials. First, we revisit the Jacobi diagonalization method for neutrino oscillations with standard matter effects and use this to demonstrate our generalization. Subsequently, we apply the Jacobi diagonalization method to scalar non-standard interactions (SNSI) and derive effective parameter mappings for all three single diagonal coupling cases. The resulting formulas reveal that all diagonal SNSI couplings could give rise to a scalar MSW (SMSW) effect, that is an energy independent resonant enhancement analogous to the MSW effect. Finally, we study the accuracy of the derived SNSI formulas for various contemporary and upcoming experiments. For $δm_{ee}$, the accuracy is excellent for all considered experiments. For $δm_{μμ}$ and $δm_{ττ}$ the accuracy is worse since $A_{CC}$ must be neglected, and first order perturbative corrections are applied to amend this.

</details>


### [327] [Drell-Yan lepton pair production from low to high transverse momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02169)
*Benoît Assi,Enrico Bothmann,John Campbell,Christian Gütschow,Stefan Höche,Wan-Li Ju,Max Knobbe,Marek Schönherr,Jesse Thaler,Michael L. Wagman*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种粒子级Drell-Yan轻子对产生计算技术，在低至中等横动量区域达到N³LO和近似N⁴LL'精度，在Z+4、5、6喷注时采用树图精度，高横动量区域达到近似NLO电弱精度；Collins-Soper核来自连续极限格点QCD；高精度理论结果通过最大熵原理的严格正事件权重重铸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确的Drell-Yan轻子对产生预测对LHC高精度测量至关重要，需要在宽运动学范围内同时兼顾微扰与非微扰效应，并将高精度理论结果转化为可用于事件生成的严格正权重。

Method: 采用QCD微扰计算：低至中等横动量区域实现N³LO与近似N⁴LL'；Z+4/5/6喷注采用树图精度；高横动量采用近似NLO电弱精度。Collins-Soper核取自连续极限格点QCD结果。基于最大熵原理的信息论方法将高精度理论结果重铸为严格正事件权重。

Result: 在低至中横动量达到N³LO及近似N⁴LL'精度，Z+4/5/6喷注为树图精度，高横动量达到近似NLO电弱精度；Collins-Soper核由连续极限格点QCD获得；高精度结果被成功转为严格正事件权重。

Conclusion: 该技术实现了多精度层级与格点QCD非微扰输入的粒子级Drell-Yan计算，并通过最大熵方法保证事件权重严格为正，适用于高精度事件生成与实验比较。

Abstract: We present a technique for computing Drell-Yan lepton pair production at particle-level that achieves N$^3$LO and approximate N$^4$LL$'$ accuracy at small to moderate transverse momentum, tree-level precision for $Z+4$, $5$, and $6$ jets, and approximate NLO EW precision at high transverse momentum. A crucial non-perturbative input, the Collins-Soper kernel, is obtained from continuum-limit lattice QCD results. High-precision theory results are recast into strictly positive event weights using an information-theoretic approach based on the maximum entropy principle.

</details>
